
FILE crates/xynoos-aura/examples/completion.rs
use std::time::Instant;
use xynoos_aura::{
    physical::{fixture, Policy},
    selector::Selector,
};
fn main() -> Result<(), Box<dyn std::error::Error>> {
    println!("trace,seed,policy,selector,refaults,migrations,stall_ns,restore_ns,cpu_ns,io_bytes,physical_bytes,backing_bytes,evaluations,rejections,no_eligible,oscillations,event_ns,feasible,capacity_rejections,budget_rejections,no_feasible,no_policy_migration,peak_total,metadata,resident_raw,resident_compressed");
    for kind in 0..9 {
        for seed in [1u64, 42, 2026] {
            let mut rng = seed;
            let trace: Vec<_> = (0..512)
                .map(|i| {
                    rng ^= rng << 13;
                    rng ^= rng >> 7;
                    rng ^= rng << 17;
                    let page = match kind {
                        0 => i % 4,
                        2 => (i / 32 % 4) * 4 + i % 4,
                        6 => {
                            if i % 32 < 24 {
                                0
                            } else {
                                (rng % 16) as usize
                            }
                        }
                        7 => i % 16,
                        _ => (rng % 16) as usize,
                    };
                    let pressure = if kind == 3 {
                        (i * 1000 / 511) as u16
                    } else if kind == 7 {
                        if i % 2 == 0 {
                            690
                        } else {
                            710
                        }
                    } else {
                        800
                    };
                    (page, pressure)
                })
                .collect();
            for policy in [Policy::Lru, Policy::CompressionFirst, Policy::Aura] {
                for mode in [
                    "historical",
                    "bounded",
                    "round_robin",
                    "journal",
                    "accounting_only",
                    "stall_only",
                    "combined",
                ] {
                    let mut s = fixture(policy);
                    if kind == 4 {
                        for p in &mut s.pages {
                            p.compressed_bytes = 4114;
                        }
                    }
                    if kind == 5 {
                        for p in &mut s.pages {
                            p.compressed_bytes = 512;
                        }
                    }
                    if kind == 8 {
                        for p in &mut s.pages {
                            p.confidence_permille = 100;
                        }
                    }
                    let calibration = [
                        (27, 718, 1978),
                        (39, 727, 4412),
                        (1381, 3236, 2612),
                        (4114, 1354, 1977),
                        (1390, 1567, 2816),
                    ];
                    for (i, p) in s.pages.iter_mut().enumerate() {
                        let c = calibration[if kind == 4 {
                            3
                        } else if kind == 5 {
                            0
                        } else {
                            i % 5
                        }];
                        p.compressed_bytes = c.0;
                        p.compress_ns = c.1;
                        p.decompress_ns = c.2;
                    }
                    s.state.stall_budget_override = Some(s.state.physical_budget_bytes);
                    if mode == "accounting_only" || mode == "combined" {
                        s.state.physical_budget_bytes = u64::MAX;
                    }
                    if mode == "stall_only" || mode == "combined" {
                        s.state.stall_raw_only = true;
                    }
                    let mut inspections = xynoos_aura::selector::SearchStats::default();
                    let mut sel = Selector::default();
                    let mut recency = [0u64; 16];
                    let mut elapsed = 0u128;
                    for (tick, &(page, pressure)) in trace.iter().enumerate() {
                        recency[page] = tick as u64 + 1;
                        let start = Instant::now();
                        let order: Vec<_> = (0..16).collect();
                        let candidate = match mode {
                            "historical" => (0..16).min_by_key(|&i| (recency[i], i)).unwrap(),
                            "round_robin" => tick % 16,
                            _ => {
                                let (candidate, stats) =
                                    sel.state_aware(&s, page, pressure, &order)?;
                                inspections.evaluations += stats.evaluations;
                                inspections.rejected += stats.rejected;
                                inspections.feasible += stats.feasible;
                                inspections.capacity += stats.capacity;
                                inspections.budget += stats.budget;
                                inspections.no_feasible += stats.no_feasible;
                                inspections.no_policy_migration += stats.no_policy_migration;
                                candidate.unwrap_or(page)
                            }
                        };
                        if mode == "journal" {
                            s.event_journal(page, pressure, candidate)?;
                        } else {
                            s.event_candidate(page, pressure, candidate)?;
                        }
                        if mode == "historical" || mode == "round_robin" {
                            inspections.evaluations += 1;
                            if s.metrics.last_feasible_mask == 0 {
                                inspections.rejected += 1;
                                inspections.no_feasible += 1;
                            } else {
                                inspections.feasible += 1;
                            }
                            inspections.capacity += s.metrics.last_capacity_rejections;
                            inspections.budget += s.metrics.last_budget_rejections;
                        }
                        elapsed += start.elapsed().as_nanos();
                    }
                    let m = &s.metrics;
                    println!("{kind},{seed},{policy:?},{mode},{},{},{},{},{},{},{},{},{},{},{},{},{elapsed},{},{},{},{},{},{},{},{},{}",m.refaults,m.migrations,m.stall_ns,m.restore_ns,m.cpu_ns,m.io_bytes,xynoos_aura::physical::physical_usage(&s.state)?,s.state.used_bytes[2],inspections.evaluations,inspections.rejected,sel.no_candidate,m.oscillations,inspections.feasible,inspections.capacity,inspections.budget,inspections.no_feasible,inspections.no_policy_migration,m.peak_total_bytes,s.state.metadata_bytes,s.state.used_bytes[0],s.state.used_bytes[1]);
                }
            }
        }
    }
    Ok(())
}


FILE crates/xynoos-aura/examples/fallible.rs
//! Eksperimen allocator terpisah, single-thread; tidak dipakai library.
use std::{sync::atomic::Ordering, time::Instant};
use xynoos_aura::{
    physical::{fixture, Policy, Simulator},
    selector::Selector,
};
#[path = "support/tracker010.rs"]
mod tracker;
use tracker::*;
#[global_allocator]
static ALLOCATOR: Counting = Counting;
fn make(n: usize) -> Simulator {
    let base = fixture(Policy::Aura);
    let mut pages = Vec::new();
    for i in 0..n {
        let mut p = base.pages[i % 16].clone();
        p.id = i as u64;
        pages.push(p);
    }
    let mut state = base.state.clone();
    state.used_bytes = [n as u64 * 4096, 0, 0];
    state.capacity_bytes[0] = n as u64 * 4096;
    state.metadata_bytes = n as u64 * 80;
    state.physical_budget_bytes = state.used_bytes[0] + state.metadata_bytes;
    state.capacity_bytes[1] = n as u64 * 4096;
    state.capacity_bytes[2] = n as u64 * 8192;
    Simulator::new(state, pages, base.engine).expect("fixture valid")
}
fn run(
    n: usize,
    seed: u64,
    mode: &str,
    sample: u8,
    kind: usize,
) -> Result<(), Box<dyn std::error::Error>> {
    let mut s = make(n);
    for p in &mut s.pages {
        if kind == 4 {
            p.compressed_bytes = 4114;
        }
        if kind == 5 {
            p.compressed_bytes = 27;
        }
        if kind == 8 {
            p.confidence_permille = 0;
        }
    }
    let mut selector = Selector::default();
    let order: Vec<_> = (0..n).collect();
    let trace: Vec<_> = (0..128)
        .map(|i| {
            (
                match kind {
                    0 => i % n.min(4),
                    2 => (i / 16 * 4 + i % 4) % n,
                    6 => {
                        if i % 16 < 12 {
                            0
                        } else {
                            (i * 17 + seed as usize) % n
                        }
                    }
                    7 => i % n,
                    _ => (i * 17 + seed as usize) % n,
                },
                if kind == 3 {
                    (i * 1000 / 127) as u16
                } else if kind == 7 {
                    if i % 2 == 0 {
                        690
                    } else {
                        710
                    }
                } else {
                    800
                },
            )
        })
        .collect();
    let (mut inspection, mut eval) = (0u128, 0u64);
    tracker::begin();
    let start = Instant::now();
    for &(page, pressure) in &trace {
        if mode == "fast" {
            let (_, stats, _) =
                selector.fast_event_measured(&mut s, page, pressure, &order, &mut inspection)?;
            eval += stats.evaluations;
        } else {
            let t = Instant::now();
            let (candidate, stats) = selector.state_aware(&s, page, pressure, &order)?;
            inspection += t.elapsed().as_nanos();
            eval += stats.evaluations;
            if mode == "journal" {
                s.event_journal(page, pressure, candidate.unwrap_or(page))?;
            } else {
                s.event_candidate(page, pressure, candidate.unwrap_or(page))?;
            }
        }
    }
    let elapsed = start.elapsed().as_nanos();
    ENABLED.store(false, Ordering::SeqCst);
    let (a, b, d) = (
        ALLOCS.load(Ordering::Relaxed),
        BYTES.load(Ordering::Relaxed),
        FREES.load(Ordering::Relaxed),
    );
    let m = &s.metrics;
    if sample > 0 {
        println!("{n},{seed},{mode},{sample},{elapsed},{inspection},{eval},{},{},{},{},{},{},{},{},{},{a},{b},{d},{},{},{},{}",if mode=="fast"{128}else{128*(n+1)},if mode=="fast"{128}else if mode=="journal"{128*2*n}else{128*(2*n+1)},m.refaults,m.migrations,m.stall_ns,m.cpu_ns,m.io_bytes,xynoos_aura::physical::physical_usage(&s.state)?,m.peak_total_bytes,LIVE.load(Ordering::Relaxed),LIVE_BYTES.load(Ordering::Relaxed),PEAK.load(Ordering::Relaxed),kind);
    }
    drop(s);
    assert_eq!(LIVE.load(Ordering::Relaxed), 0);
    assert!(!INVALID.load(Ordering::Relaxed));
    Ok(())
}
fn main() -> Result<(), Box<dyn std::error::Error>> {
    tracker::validate_register();
    let old = std::hint::black_box(Box::new([1u8; 8]));
    tracker::begin();
    drop(old);
    assert_eq!(FREES.load(Ordering::Relaxed), 0);
    let mut test = Vec::with_capacity(4);
    test.extend_from_slice(&[2u8; 4]);
    test.reserve(128);
    assert!(PEAK.load(Ordering::Relaxed) >= test.capacity() as u64);
    ENABLED.store(false, Ordering::SeqCst);
    drop(test);
    assert_eq!(LIVE.load(Ordering::Relaxed), 0);
    println!("groups,seed,mode,sample,event_ns,inspection_ns,evaluations,estimated_access_replays,estimated_vec_clones,refaults,migrations,stall_ns,cpu_ns,io_bytes,physical_bytes,peak_modeled_bytes,alloc_count,alloc_bytes,dealloc_count,live_count,live_bytes,peak_live_bytes,trace");
    for kind in 0..9 {
        for n in [1, 16, 64] {
            for seed in [1, 42, 2026] {
                let mut modes = ["clone", "journal", "fast"];
                modes.rotate_left((n + seed as usize) % 3);
                for mode in modes {
                    for sample in 0..4 {
                        run(n, seed, mode, sample, kind)?;
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }
    Ok(())
}


FILE crates/xynoos-aura/examples/fastpath.rs
use std::time::Instant;
use xynoos_aura::{
    physical::{fixture, Policy},
    selector::Selector,
};
fn main() -> Result<(), Box<dyn std::error::Error>> {
    println!("trace,seed,policy,selector,refaults,migrations,stall_ns,restore_ns,cpu_ns,io_bytes,physical_bytes,backing_bytes,evaluations,rejections,no_eligible,oscillations,event_ns,inspection_ns,sample,feasible,capacity_rejections,budget_rejections,no_feasible,no_policy_migration,peak_total,metadata,resident_raw,resident_compressed");
    for kind in 0..9 {
        for seed in [1u64, 42, 2026] {
            let mut rng = seed;
            let trace: Vec<_> = (0..512)
                .map(|i| {
                    rng ^= rng << 13;
                    rng ^= rng >> 7;
                    rng ^= rng << 17;
                    let page = match kind {
                        0 => i % 4,
                        2 => (i / 32 % 4) * 4 + i % 4,
                        6 => {
                            if i % 32 < 24 {
                                0
                            } else {
                                (rng % 16) as usize
                            }
                        }
                        7 => i % 16,
                        _ => (rng % 16) as usize,
                    };
                    let pressure = if kind == 3 {
                        (i * 1000 / 511) as u16
                    } else if kind == 7 {
                        if i % 2 == 0 {
                            690
                        } else {
                            710
                        }
                    } else {
                        800
                    };
                    (page, pressure)
                })
                .collect();
            for policy in [Policy::Lru, Policy::CompressionFirst, Policy::Aura] {
                let mut modes = ["historical", "bounded", "journal", "fast"];
                modes.rotate_left((seed as usize) % 4);
                for mode in modes {
                    for sample in 0..4 {
                        let mut s = fixture(policy);
                        if kind == 4 {
                            for p in &mut s.pages {
                                p.compressed_bytes = 4114;
                            }
                        }
                        if kind == 5 {
                            for p in &mut s.pages {
                                p.compressed_bytes = 512;
                            }
                        }
                        if kind == 8 {
                            for p in &mut s.pages {
                                p.confidence_permille = 100;
                            }
                        }
                        let calibration = [
                            (27, 718, 1978),
                            (39, 727, 4412),
                            (1381, 3236, 2612),
                            (4114, 1354, 1977),
                            (1390, 1567, 2816),
                        ];
                        for (i, p) in s.pages.iter_mut().enumerate() {
                            let c = calibration[if kind == 4 {
                                3
                            } else if kind == 5 {
                                0
                            } else {
                                i % 5
                            }];
                            p.compressed_bytes = c.0;
                            p.compress_ns = c.1;
                            p.decompress_ns = c.2;
                        }
                        s.state.stall_budget_override = Some(s.state.physical_budget_bytes);
                        if mode == "accounting_only" || mode == "combined" {
                            s.state.physical_budget_bytes = u64::MAX;
                        }
                        if mode == "stall_only" || mode == "combined" {
                            s.state.stall_raw_only = true;
                        }
                        let mut inspections = xynoos_aura::selector::SearchStats::default();
                        let mut sel = Selector::default();
                        let mut recency = [0u64; 16];
                        let mut elapsed = 0u128;
                        let mut inspection_ns = 0u128;
                        for (tick, &(page, pressure)) in trace.iter().enumerate() {
                            recency[page] = tick as u64 + 1;
                            let start = Instant::now();
                            let order: Vec<_> = (0..16).collect();
                            if mode == "fast" {
                                let (_, stats, _) = sel.fast_event_measured(
                                    &mut s,
                                    page,
                                    pressure,
                                    &order,
                                    &mut inspection_ns,
                                )?;
                                inspections.evaluations += stats.evaluations;
                                inspections.rejected += stats.rejected;
                                inspections.feasible += stats.feasible;
                                inspections.capacity += stats.capacity;
                                inspections.budget += stats.budget;
                                inspections.no_feasible += stats.no_feasible;
                                inspections.no_policy_migration += stats.no_policy_migration;
                            } else {
                                let candidate = if mode == "historical" {
                                    (0..16).min_by_key(|&i| (recency[i], i)).unwrap()
                                } else {
                                    let inspection_start = Instant::now();
                                    let (candidate, stats) =
                                        sel.state_aware(&s, page, pressure, &order)?;
                                    inspection_ns += inspection_start.elapsed().as_nanos();
                                    inspections.evaluations += stats.evaluations;
                                    inspections.rejected += stats.rejected;
                                    inspections.feasible += stats.feasible;
                                    inspections.capacity += stats.capacity;
                                    inspections.budget += stats.budget;
                                    inspections.no_feasible += stats.no_feasible;
                                    inspections.no_policy_migration += stats.no_policy_migration;
                                    candidate.unwrap_or(page)
                                };
                                if mode == "journal" {
                                    s.event_journal(page, pressure, candidate)?;
                                } else {
                                    s.event_candidate(page, pressure, candidate)?;
                                }
                            }
                            if mode == "historical" || mode == "round_robin" {
                                inspections.evaluations += 1;
                                if s.metrics.last_feasible_mask == 0 {
                                    inspections.rejected += 1;
                                    inspections.no_feasible += 1;
                                } else {
                                    inspections.feasible += 1;
                                }
                                inspections.capacity += s.metrics.last_capacity_rejections;
                                inspections.budget += s.metrics.last_budget_rejections;
                            }
                            elapsed += start.elapsed().as_nanos();
                        }
                        let m = &s.metrics;
                        if sample > 0 {
                            println!("{kind},{seed},{policy:?},{mode},{},{},{},{},{},{},{},{},{},{},{},{},{elapsed},{inspection_ns},{sample},{},{},{},{},{},{},{},{},{}",m.refaults,m.migrations,m.stall_ns,m.restore_ns,m.cpu_ns,m.io_bytes,xynoos_aura::physical::physical_usage(&s.state)?,s.state.used_bytes[2],inspections.evaluations,inspections.rejected,sel.no_candidate,m.oscillations,inspections.feasible,inspections.capacity,inspections.budget,inspections.no_feasible,inspections.no_policy_migration,m.peak_total_bytes,s.state.metadata_bytes,s.state.used_bytes[0],s.state.used_bytes[1]);
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }
    Ok(())
}


FILE crates/xynoos-aura/examples/reliability.rs
//! Eksperimen allocator terpisah, single-thread; tidak dipakai library.
use std::{sync::atomic::Ordering, time::Instant};
use xynoos_aura::{
    physical::{fixture, Policy, Simulator},
    selector::Selector,
};
#[path = "support/tracker.rs"]
mod tracker;
use tracker::*;
#[global_allocator]
static ALLOCATOR: Counting = Counting;
fn make(n: usize) -> Simulator {
    let base = fixture(Policy::Aura);
    let mut pages = Vec::new();
    for i in 0..n {
        let mut p = base.pages[i % 16].clone();
        p.id = i as u64;
        pages.push(p);
    }
    let mut state = base.state.clone();
    state.used_bytes = [n as u64 * 4096, 0, 0];
    state.capacity_bytes[0] = n as u64 * 4096;
    state.metadata_bytes = n as u64 * 80;
    state.physical_budget_bytes = state.used_bytes[0] + state.metadata_bytes;
    state.capacity_bytes[1] = n as u64 * 4096;
    state.capacity_bytes[2] = n as u64 * 8192;
    Simulator::new(state, pages, base.engine).expect("fixture valid")
}
fn run(
    n: usize,
    seed: u64,
    mode: &str,
    sample: u8,
    kind: usize,
) -> Result<(), Box<dyn std::error::Error>> {
    let mut s = make(n);
    for p in &mut s.pages {
        if kind == 4 {
            p.compressed_bytes = 4114;
        }
        if kind == 5 {
            p.compressed_bytes = 27;
        }
        if kind == 8 {
            p.confidence_permille = 0;
        }
    }
    let mut selector = Selector::default();
    let order: Vec<_> = (0..n).collect();
    let trace: Vec<_> = (0..128)
        .map(|i| {
            (
                match kind {
                    0 => i % n.min(4),
                    2 => (i / 16 * 4 + i % 4) % n,
                    6 => {
                        if i % 16 < 12 {
                            0
                        } else {
                            (i * 17 + seed as usize) % n
                        }
                    }
                    7 => i % n,
                    _ => (i * 17 + seed as usize) % n,
                },
                if kind == 3 {
                    (i * 1000 / 127) as u16
                } else if kind == 7 {
                    if i % 2 == 0 {
                        690
                    } else {
                        710
                    }
                } else {
                    800
                },
            )
        })
        .collect();
    let (mut inspection, mut eval) = (0u128, 0u64);
    tracker::begin();
    let start = Instant::now();
    for &(page, pressure) in &trace {
        if mode == "fast" {
            let (_, stats, _) =
                selector.fast_event_measured(&mut s, page, pressure, &order, &mut inspection)?;
            eval += stats.evaluations;
        } else {
            let t = Instant::now();
            let (candidate, stats) = selector.state_aware(&s, page, pressure, &order)?;
            inspection += t.elapsed().as_nanos();
            eval += stats.evaluations;
            if mode == "journal" {
                s.event_journal(page, pressure, candidate.unwrap_or(page))?;
            } else {
                s.event_candidate(page, pressure, candidate.unwrap_or(page))?;
            }
        }
    }
    let elapsed = start.elapsed().as_nanos();
    ENABLED.store(false, Ordering::SeqCst);
    let (a, b, d) = (
        ALLOCS.load(Ordering::Relaxed),
        BYTES.load(Ordering::Relaxed),
        FREES.load(Ordering::Relaxed),
    );
    let m = &s.metrics;
    if sample > 0 {
        println!("{n},{seed},{mode},{sample},{elapsed},{inspection},{eval},{},{},{},{},{},{},{},{},{},{a},{b},{d},{},{},{},{}",if mode=="fast"{128}else{128*(n+1)},if mode=="fast"{128}else if mode=="journal"{128*2*n}else{128*(2*n+1)},m.refaults,m.migrations,m.stall_ns,m.cpu_ns,m.io_bytes,xynoos_aura::physical::physical_usage(&s.state)?,m.peak_total_bytes,LIVE.load(Ordering::Relaxed),LIVE_BYTES.load(Ordering::Relaxed),PEAK.load(Ordering::Relaxed),kind);
    }
    drop(s);
    assert_eq!(LIVE.load(Ordering::Relaxed), 0);
    assert!(!INVALID.load(Ordering::Relaxed));
    Ok(())
}
fn main() -> Result<(), Box<dyn std::error::Error>> {
    let old = std::hint::black_box(Box::new([1u8; 8]));
    tracker::begin();
    drop(old);
    assert_eq!(FREES.load(Ordering::Relaxed), 0);
    let mut test = Vec::with_capacity(4);
    test.extend_from_slice(&[2u8; 4]);
    test.reserve(128);
    assert!(PEAK.load(Ordering::Relaxed) >= test.capacity() as u64);
    ENABLED.store(false, Ordering::SeqCst);
    drop(test);
    assert_eq!(LIVE.load(Ordering::Relaxed), 0);
    println!("groups,seed,mode,sample,event_ns,inspection_ns,evaluations,estimated_access_replays,estimated_vec_clones,refaults,migrations,stall_ns,cpu_ns,io_bytes,physical_bytes,peak_modeled_bytes,alloc_count,alloc_bytes,dealloc_count,live_count,live_bytes,peak_live_bytes,trace");
    for kind in 0..9 {
        for n in [1, 16, 64] {
            for seed in [1, 42, 2026] {
                let mut modes = ["clone", "journal", "fast"];
                modes.rotate_left((n + seed as usize) % 3);
                for mode in modes {
                    for sample in 0..4 {
                        run(n, seed, mode, sample, kind)?;
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }
    Ok(())
}


FILE crates/xynoos-aura/examples/research.rs
//! Eksperimen terbatas: 9 trace \xd7 3 seed \xd7 3 policy \xd7 512 event.
use std::{hint::black_box, time::Instant};
use xynoos_aura::placement::{fixture, Policy};
fn main() -> Result<(), Box<dyn std::error::Error>> {
    println!("trace,seed,policy,refaults,restore_ns,stall_ns,migrations,compressions,cpu_ns,io_bytes,peak_ram_bytes,peak_compressed_bytes,peak_backing_bytes,fallbacks,oscillations,event_execution_ns,decision_probe_ns,profile_bytes");
    for (kind, name) in [
        "interactive",
        "background",
        "switching",
        "pressure",
        "entropy",
        "compressible",
        "bursty",
        "adversarial",
        "unreliable",
    ]
    .iter()
    .enumerate()
    {
        for seed in [1u64, 42, 2026] {
            let mut rng = seed;
            let trace: Vec<_> = (0..512)
                .map(|i| {
                    rng ^= rng << 13;
                    rng ^= rng >> 7;
                    rng ^= rng << 17;
                    let page = match kind {
                        0 => i % 4,
                        1 => (rng % 16) as usize,
                        2 => (i / 32 % 4) * 4 + i % 4,
                        6 => {
                            if i % 32 < 24 {
                                0
                            } else {
                                (rng % 16) as usize
                            }
                        }
                        7 => i % 16,
                        _ => (rng % 16) as usize,
                    };
                    let pressure = match kind {
                        3 => (i * 1000 / 511) as u16,
                        7 => {
                            if i % 2 == 0 {
                                690
                            } else {
                                710
                            }
                        }
                        _ => 800,
                    };
                    (page, pressure)
                })
                .collect();
            for policy in [Policy::Lru, Policy::CompressionFirst, Policy::Aura] {
                let mut s = fixture(policy);
                if kind == 4 {
                    for p in &mut s.pages {
                        p.compressed_bytes = 4114;
                    }
                }
                if kind == 5 {
                    for p in &mut s.pages {
                        p.compressed_bytes = 512;
                    }
                }
                if kind == 8 {
                    for p in &mut s.pages {
                        p.confidence_permille = 100;
                    }
                }
                let mut event_ns = 0u128;
                let mut decision_ns = 0u128;
                for &(page, pressure) in &trace {
                    let start = Instant::now();
                    s.event(black_box(page), pressure)?;
                    event_ns += start.elapsed().as_nanos();
                    let mut probe = s.engine.clone();
                    let start = Instant::now();
                    black_box(probe.decide(&s.state, &s.pages[page], s.tick)?);
                    decision_ns += start.elapsed().as_nanos();
                }
                let elapsed = event_ns;
                let m = &s.metrics;
                println!(
                    "{name},{seed},{policy:?},{},{},{},{},{},{},{},{},{},{},{},{},{elapsed},{decision_ns},{}",
                    m.refaults,
                    m.restore_ns,
                    m.stall_ns,
                    m.migrations,
                    m.compressions,
                    m.cpu_ns,
                    m.io_bytes,
                    m.peak_bytes[0],
                    m.peak_bytes[1],
                    m.peak_bytes[2],
                    m.fallbacks,
                    m.oscillations,
                    std::mem::size_of::<xynoos_aura::placement::PageGroupProfile>()
                );
            }
        }
    }
    Ok(())
}


FILE crates/xynoos-aura/examples/scalability.rs
//! Eksperimen allocator terpisah, single-thread; tidak dipakai library.
use std::{
    alloc::{GlobalAlloc, Layout, System},
    sync::atomic::{AtomicBool, AtomicU64, Ordering},
    time::Instant,
};
use xynoos_aura::{
    physical::{fixture, Policy, Simulator},
    selector::Selector,
};
struct Counting;
static ENABLED: AtomicBool = AtomicBool::new(false);
static ALLOCS: AtomicU64 = AtomicU64::new(0);
static BYTES: AtomicU64 = AtomicU64::new(0);
static FREES: AtomicU64 = AtomicU64::new(0);
// SAFETY: semua operasi diteruskan ke System dengan pointer/layout yang sama;
// counter atomik tidak mengalokasikan dan benchmark berjalan satu thread.
unsafe impl GlobalAlloc for Counting {
    unsafe fn alloc(&self, l: Layout) -> *mut u8 {
        let p = unsafe { System.alloc(l) };
        if !p.is_null() && ENABLED.load(Ordering::Relaxed) {
            ALLOCS.fetch_add(1, Ordering::Relaxed);
            BYTES.fetch_add(l.size() as u64, Ordering::Relaxed);
        }
        p
    }
    unsafe fn dealloc(&self, p: *mut u8, l: Layout) {
        if ENABLED.load(Ordering::Relaxed) {
            FREES.fetch_add(1, Ordering::Relaxed);
        }
        unsafe { System.dealloc(p, l) }
    }
    unsafe fn realloc(&self, p: *mut u8, l: Layout, n: usize) -> *mut u8 {
        let q = unsafe { System.realloc(p, l, n) };
        if !q.is_null() && ENABLED.load(Ordering::Relaxed) {
            ALLOCS.fetch_add(1, Ordering::Relaxed);
            BYTES.fetch_add(n as u64, Ordering::Relaxed);
            FREES.fetch_add(1, Ordering::Relaxed);
        }
        q
    }
}
#[global_allocator]
static ALLOCATOR: Counting = Counting;
fn make(n: usize) -> Simulator {
    let base = fixture(Policy::Aura);
    let mut pages = Vec::new();
    for i in 0..n {
        let mut p = base.pages[i % 16].clone();
        p.id = i as u64;
        pages.push(p);
    }
    let mut state = base.state.clone();
    state.used_bytes = [n as u64 * 4096, 0, 0];
    state.capacity_bytes[0] = n as u64 * 4096;
    state.metadata_bytes = n as u64 * 80;
    state.physical_budget_bytes = state.used_bytes[0] + state.metadata_bytes;
    state.capacity_bytes[1] = n as u64 * 4096;
    state.capacity_bytes[2] = n as u64 * 8192;
    Simulator::new(state, pages, base.engine).expect("fixture valid")
}
fn run(n: usize, seed: u64, mode: &str, sample: u8) -> Result<(), Box<dyn std::error::Error>> {
    let mut s = make(n);
    let mut selector = Selector::default();
    let order: Vec<_> = (0..n).collect();
    let trace: Vec<_> = (0..128)
        .map(|i| {
            (
                (i * 17 + seed as usize) % n,
                if i % 3 == 0 { 690 } else { 800 },
            )
        })
        .collect();
    let (mut inspection, mut eval) = (0u128, 0u64);
    ALLOCS.store(0, Ordering::Relaxed);
    BYTES.store(0, Ordering::Relaxed);
    FREES.store(0, Ordering::Relaxed);
    ENABLED.store(true, Ordering::SeqCst);
    let start = Instant::now();
    for &(page, pressure) in &trace {
        if mode == "fast" {
            let (_, stats, _) =
                selector.fast_event_measured(&mut s, page, pressure, &order, &mut inspection)?;
            eval += stats.evaluations;
        } else {
            let t = Instant::now();
            let (candidate, stats) = selector.state_aware(&s, page, pressure, &order)?;
            inspection += t.elapsed().as_nanos();
            eval += stats.evaluations;
            if mode == "journal" {
                s.event_journal(page, pressure, candidate.unwrap_or(page))?;
            } else {
                s.event_candidate(page, pressure, candidate.unwrap_or(page))?;
            }
        }
    }
    let elapsed = start.elapsed().as_nanos();
    ENABLED.store(false, Ordering::SeqCst);
    let (a, b, d) = (
        ALLOCS.load(Ordering::Relaxed),
        BYTES.load(Ordering::Relaxed),
        FREES.load(Ordering::Relaxed),
    );
    let m = &s.metrics;
    if sample > 0 {
        println!("{n},{seed},{mode},{sample},{elapsed},{inspection},{eval},{},{},{},{},{},{},{},{},{},{a},{b},{d}",if mode=="fast"{128}else{128*(n+1)},if mode=="fast"{128}else if mode=="journal"{128*2*n}else{128*(2*n+1)},m.refaults,m.migrations,m.stall_ns,m.cpu_ns,m.io_bytes,xynoos_aura::physical::physical_usage(&s.state)?,m.peak_total_bytes);
    }
    Ok(())
}
fn main() -> Result<(), Box<dyn std::error::Error>> {
    ALLOCS.store(0, Ordering::Relaxed);
    BYTES.store(0, Ordering::Relaxed);
    FREES.store(0, Ordering::Relaxed);
    ENABLED.store(true, Ordering::SeqCst);
    let check = std::hint::black_box(Box::new([7u8; 17]));
    assert_eq!(check[0], 7);
    drop(check);
    ENABLED.store(false, Ordering::SeqCst);
    assert!(
        ALLOCS.load(Ordering::Relaxed) >= 1
            && FREES.load(Ordering::Relaxed) >= 1
            && BYTES.load(Ordering::Relaxed) >= 17
    );
    println!("groups,seed,mode,sample,event_ns,inspection_ns,evaluations,estimated_access_replays,estimated_vec_clones,refaults,migrations,stall_ns,cpu_ns,io_bytes,physical_bytes,peak_modeled_bytes,alloc_count,alloc_bytes,dealloc_count");
    for n in [1, 4, 8, 16, 32, 64] {
        for seed in [1, 42, 2026] {
            let mut modes = ["clone", "journal", "fast"];
            modes.rotate_left((n + seed as usize) % 3);
            for mode in modes {
                for sample in 0..4 {
                    run(n, seed, mode, sample)?;
                }
            }
        }
    }
    Ok(())
}


FILE crates/xynoos-aura/examples/selector_research.rs
use std::time::Instant;
use xynoos_aura::{
    physical::{fixture, Policy},
    selector::Selector,
};
fn main() -> Result<(), Box<dyn std::error::Error>> {
    println!("trace,seed,policy,selector,refaults,migrations,stall_ns,restore_ns,cpu_ns,io_bytes,physical_bytes,backing_bytes,evaluations,rejections,no_eligible,oscillations,event_ns");
    for kind in 0..9 {
        for seed in [1u64, 42, 2026] {
            let mut rng = seed;
            let trace: Vec<_> = (0..512)
                .map(|i| {
                    rng ^= rng << 13;
                    rng ^= rng >> 7;
                    rng ^= rng << 17;
                    let page = match kind {
                        0 => i % 4,
                        2 => (i / 32 % 4) * 4 + i % 4,
                        6 => {
                            if i % 32 < 24 {
                                0
                            } else {
                                (rng % 16) as usize
                            }
                        }
                        7 => i % 16,
                        _ => (rng % 16) as usize,
                    };
                    let pressure = if kind == 3 {
                        (i * 1000 / 511) as u16
                    } else if kind == 7 {
                        if i % 2 == 0 {
                            690
                        } else {
                            710
                        }
                    } else {
                        800
                    };
                    (page, pressure)
                })
                .collect();
            for policy in [Policy::Lru, Policy::CompressionFirst, Policy::Aura] {
                for mode in ["historical", "bounded", "round_robin"] {
                    let mut s = fixture(policy);
                    if kind == 4 {
                        for p in &mut s.pages {
                            p.compressed_bytes = 4114;
                        }
                    }
                    if kind == 5 {
                        for p in &mut s.pages {
                            p.compressed_bytes = 512;
                        }
                    }
                    if kind == 8 {
                        for p in &mut s.pages {
                            p.confidence_permille = 100;
                        }
                    }
                    let mut sel = Selector::default();
                    let mut recency = [0u64; 16];
                    let mut elapsed = 0u128;
                    for (tick, &(page, pressure)) in trace.iter().enumerate() {
                        recency[page] = tick as u64 + 1;
                        let mut eligible = [true; 16];
                        eligible[page] = false;
                        let candidate = match mode {
                            "bounded" => sel.next(&eligible)?.ok_or("no eligible")?,
                            "round_robin" => tick % 16,
                            _ => (0..16).min_by_key(|&i| (recency[i], i)).ok_or("empty")?,
                        };
                        let start = Instant::now();
                        s.event_candidate(page, pressure, candidate)?;
                        elapsed += start.elapsed().as_nanos();
                    }
                    let m = &s.metrics;
                    println!("{kind},{seed},{policy:?},{mode},{},{},{},{},{},{},{},{},{},{},{},{},{elapsed}",m.refaults,m.migrations,m.stall_ns,m.restore_ns,m.cpu_ns,m.io_bytes,xynoos_aura::physical::physical_usage(&s.state)?,s.state.used_bytes[2],sel.evaluations,sel.rejected,sel.no_candidate,m.oscillations);
                }
            }
        }
    }
    Ok(())
}


FILE crates/xynoos-aura/examples/support/tracker.rs
//! Register pointer berbatas tanpa dereferensi atau alokasi dalam callback.
use std::{
    alloc::{GlobalAlloc, Layout, System},
    sync::atomic::{AtomicBool, AtomicU64, AtomicUsize, Ordering},
};
pub struct Counting;
pub static ENABLED: AtomicBool = AtomicBool::new(false);
pub static ALLOCS: AtomicU64 = AtomicU64::new(0);
pub static BYTES: AtomicU64 = AtomicU64::new(0);
pub static FREES: AtomicU64 = AtomicU64::new(0);
pub static LIVE: AtomicU64 = AtomicU64::new(0);
pub static LIVE_BYTES: AtomicU64 = AtomicU64::new(0);
pub static PEAK: AtomicU64 = AtomicU64::new(0);
pub static INVALID: AtomicBool = AtomicBool::new(false);
static LOCK: AtomicBool = AtomicBool::new(false);
const N: usize = 4096;
static PTR: [AtomicUsize; N] = [const { AtomicUsize::new(0) }; N];
static SIZE: [AtomicUsize; N] = [const { AtomicUsize::new(0) }; N];
fn lock() {
    while LOCK
        .compare_exchange(false, true, Ordering::Acquire, Ordering::Relaxed)
        .is_err()
    {
        std::hint::spin_loop();
    }
}
fn insert(p: usize, n: usize) {
    for offset in 0..N {
        let i = ((p >> 4) + offset) % N;
        let old = PTR[i].load(Ordering::Relaxed);
        if old == 0 || old == usize::MAX {
            PTR[i].store(p, Ordering::Relaxed);
            SIZE[i].store(n, Ordering::Relaxed);
            ALLOCS.fetch_add(1, Ordering::Relaxed);
            BYTES.fetch_add(n as u64, Ordering::Relaxed);
            LIVE.fetch_add(1, Ordering::Relaxed);
            let live = LIVE_BYTES.fetch_add(n as u64, Ordering::Relaxed) + n as u64;
            PEAK.fetch_max(live, Ordering::Relaxed);
            return;
        }
    }
    INVALID.store(true, Ordering::Relaxed);
}
fn remove(p: usize) -> bool {
    for offset in 0..N {
        let i = ((p >> 4) + offset) % N;
        let old = PTR[i].load(Ordering::Relaxed);
        if old == 0 {
            return false;
        }
        if old == p {
            PTR[i].store(usize::MAX, Ordering::Relaxed);
            LIVE.fetch_sub(1, Ordering::Relaxed);
            LIVE_BYTES.fetch_sub(SIZE[i].load(Ordering::Relaxed) as u64, Ordering::Relaxed);
            FREES.fetch_add(1, Ordering::Relaxed);
            return true;
        }
    }
    false
}
/// Dipanggil hanya ketika tidak ada objek scope terdahulu yang masih hidup.
pub fn begin() {
    assert_eq!(LIVE.load(Ordering::Relaxed), 0);
    for p in &PTR {
        p.store(0, Ordering::Relaxed);
    }
    for c in [&ALLOCS, &BYTES, &FREES, &LIVE, &LIVE_BYTES, &PEAK] {
        c.store(0, Ordering::Relaxed);
    }
    INVALID.store(false, Ordering::Relaxed);
    ENABLED.store(true, Ordering::SeqCst);
}
// SAFETY: pointer/Layout diteruskan identik ke System; register hanya menyimpan
// alamat numerik dan ukuran, tidak pernah mendereferensi pointer. Callback tanpa heap/logging.
unsafe impl GlobalAlloc for Counting {
    unsafe fn alloc(&self, l: Layout) -> *mut u8 {
        let p = unsafe { System.alloc(l) };
        if !p.is_null() {
            lock();
            if ENABLED.load(Ordering::Relaxed) {
                insert(p as usize, l.size());
            }
            LOCK.store(false, Ordering::Release);
        }
        p
    }
    unsafe fn dealloc(&self, p: *mut u8, l: Layout) {
        lock();
        remove(p as usize);
        LOCK.store(false, Ordering::Release);
        unsafe { System.dealloc(p, l) }
    }
    unsafe fn realloc(&self, p: *mut u8, l: Layout, n: usize) -> *mut u8 {
        lock();
        let q = unsafe { System.realloc(p, l, n) };
        if !q.is_null() {
            let tracked = remove(p as usize);
            if tracked || ENABLED.load(Ordering::Relaxed) {
                insert(q as usize, n);
            }
        }
        LOCK.store(false, Ordering::Release);
        q
    }
}


FILE crates/xynoos-aura/examples/support/tracker010.rs
//! Register pointer berbatas tanpa dereferensi atau alokasi dalam callback.
use std::{
    alloc::{GlobalAlloc, Layout, System},
    sync::atomic::{AtomicBool, AtomicU64, AtomicUsize, Ordering},
};
pub struct Counting;
pub static ENABLED: AtomicBool = AtomicBool::new(false);
pub static ALLOCS: AtomicU64 = AtomicU64::new(0);
pub static BYTES: AtomicU64 = AtomicU64::new(0);
pub static FREES: AtomicU64 = AtomicU64::new(0);
pub static LIVE: AtomicU64 = AtomicU64::new(0);
pub static LIVE_BYTES: AtomicU64 = AtomicU64::new(0);
pub static PEAK: AtomicU64 = AtomicU64::new(0);
pub static INVALID: AtomicBool = AtomicBool::new(false);
pub static EPOCH: AtomicU64 = AtomicU64::new(0);
static LOCK: AtomicBool = AtomicBool::new(false);
const N: usize = 4096;
static PTR: [AtomicUsize; N] = [const { AtomicUsize::new(0) }; N];
static SIZE: [AtomicUsize; N] = [const { AtomicUsize::new(0) }; N];
fn lock() {
    while LOCK
        .compare_exchange(false, true, Ordering::Acquire, Ordering::Relaxed)
        .is_err()
    {
        std::hint::spin_loop();
    }
}
fn insert(p: usize, n: usize) {
    for offset in 0..N {
        let i = ((p >> 4) + offset) % N;
        let old = PTR[i].load(Ordering::Relaxed);
        if old == 0 || old == usize::MAX {
            PTR[i].store(p, Ordering::Relaxed);
            SIZE[i].store(n, Ordering::Relaxed);
            ALLOCS.fetch_add(1, Ordering::Relaxed);
            BYTES.fetch_add(n as u64, Ordering::Relaxed);
            LIVE.fetch_add(1, Ordering::Relaxed);
            let live = LIVE_BYTES.fetch_add(n as u64, Ordering::Relaxed) + n as u64;
            PEAK.fetch_max(live, Ordering::Relaxed);
            return;
        }
    }
    INVALID.store(true, Ordering::Relaxed);
}
fn remove(p: usize) -> bool {
    for offset in 0..N {
        let i = ((p >> 4) + offset) % N;
        let old = PTR[i].load(Ordering::Relaxed);
        if old == 0 {
            return false;
        }
        if old == p {
            PTR[i].store(usize::MAX, Ordering::Relaxed);
            LIVE.fetch_sub(1, Ordering::Relaxed);
            LIVE_BYTES.fetch_sub(SIZE[i].load(Ordering::Relaxed) as u64, Ordering::Relaxed);
            FREES.fetch_add(1, Ordering::Relaxed);
            return true;
        }
    }
    false
}
fn record_realloc(old: usize, new: usize, n: usize) {
    if new != 0 {
        let tracked = remove(old);
        if tracked || ENABLED.load(Ordering::Relaxed) {
            insert(new, n);
        }
    }
}
/// Dipanggil hanya ketika tidak ada objek scope terdahulu yang masih hidup.
pub fn try_begin() -> bool {
    lock();
    if LIVE.load(Ordering::Relaxed) != 0 {
        LOCK.store(false, Ordering::Release);
        return false;
    }
    for p in &PTR {
        p.store(0, Ordering::Relaxed);
    }
    for c in [&ALLOCS, &BYTES, &FREES, &LIVE, &LIVE_BYTES, &PEAK] {
        c.store(0, Ordering::Relaxed);
    }
    INVALID.store(false, Ordering::Relaxed);
    EPOCH.fetch_add(1, Ordering::Relaxed);
    ENABLED.store(true, Ordering::SeqCst);
    LOCK.store(false, Ordering::Release);
    true
}
pub fn begin() {
    assert!(try_begin(), "scope lama masih memiliki objek hidup");
}
/// Uji register metadata murni: alamat sintetis tidak pernah diteruskan ke System.
pub fn validate_register() {
    ENABLED.store(false, Ordering::SeqCst);
    begin();
    lock();
    insert(16, 7);
    let a = remove(16);
    let b = remove(16);
    insert(16, 9);
    let c = remove(16);
    insert(16, 7);
    record_realloc(16, 16, 13);
    let same_address =
        LIVE.load(Ordering::Relaxed) == 1 && LIVE_BYTES.load(Ordering::Relaxed) == 13;
    record_realloc(16, 0, 100);
    let failed = LIVE_BYTES.load(Ordering::Relaxed) == 13;
    record_realloc(16, 32, 17);
    remove(32);
    let reused = a && !b && c && same_address && failed && LIVE.load(Ordering::Relaxed) == 0;
    for i in 1..=N {
        insert(i * 16, 1);
    }
    insert((N + 1) * 16, 1);
    let full = INVALID.load(Ordering::Relaxed) && LIVE.load(Ordering::Relaxed) == N as u64;
    for i in 1..=N {
        remove(i * 16);
    }
    LOCK.store(false, Ordering::Release);
    ENABLED.store(false, Ordering::SeqCst);
    assert!(reused && full);
    assert_eq!(LIVE.load(Ordering::Relaxed), 0);
    begin();
    ENABLED.store(false, Ordering::SeqCst);
}
// SAFETY: pointer/Layout diteruskan identik ke System; register hanya menyimpan
// alamat numerik dan ukuran, tidak pernah mendereferensi pointer. Callback tanpa heap/logging.
unsafe impl GlobalAlloc for Counting {
    unsafe fn alloc(&self, l: Layout) -> *mut u8 {
        let p = unsafe { System.alloc(l) };
        if !p.is_null() {
            lock();
            if ENABLED.load(Ordering::Relaxed) {
                insert(p as usize, l.size());
            }
            LOCK.store(false, Ordering::Release);
        }
        p
    }
    unsafe fn dealloc(&self, p: *mut u8, l: Layout) {
        lock();
        remove(p as usize);
        LOCK.store(false, Ordering::Release);
        unsafe { System.dealloc(p, l) }
    }
    unsafe fn realloc(&self, p: *mut u8, l: Layout, n: usize) -> *mut u8 {
        lock();
        let q = unsafe { System.realloc(p, l, n) };
        record_realloc(p as usize, q as usize, n);
        LOCK.store(false, Ordering::Release);
        q
    }
}


FILE crates/xynoos-aura/examples/tracker_validation.rs
#[path = "support/tracker010.rs"]
mod tracker;
use std::sync::{atomic::Ordering, Arc, Barrier};
use tracker::*;
#[global_allocator]
static ALLOCATOR: Counting = Counting;
fn main() {
    validate_register();
    let old = std::hint::black_box(Box::new([1u8; 8]));
    begin();
    let epoch = EPOCH.load(Ordering::Relaxed);
    drop(old);
    assert_eq!(FREES.load(Ordering::Relaxed), 0);
    let mut v = Vec::<u8>::with_capacity(16);
    v.extend_from_slice(&[1; 16]);
    assert!(!try_begin());
    assert_eq!(EPOCH.load(Ordering::Relaxed), epoch);
    v.reserve(4096);
    assert_eq!(LIVE_BYTES.load(Ordering::Relaxed), v.capacity() as u64);
    ENABLED.store(false, Ordering::SeqCst);
    drop(v);
    assert_eq!(LIVE.load(Ordering::Relaxed), 0);
    let mut old = Vec::<u8>::with_capacity(4);
    old.extend_from_slice(&[1; 4]);
    begin();
    old.reserve(8192);
    assert_eq!(LIVE_BYTES.load(Ordering::Relaxed), old.capacity() as u64);
    ENABLED.store(false, Ordering::SeqCst);
    drop(old);
    assert_eq!(LIVE.load(Ordering::Relaxed), 0);
    begin();
    let z = Box::new(());
    drop(std::hint::black_box(z));
    assert_eq!(LIVE.load(Ordering::Relaxed), 0);
    ENABLED.store(false, Ordering::SeqCst);
    // Semua thread dibuat sebelum scope; attribution adalah global, bukan per-thread.
    let start = Arc::new(Barrier::new(5));
    let done = Arc::new(Barrier::new(5));
    let mut workers = Vec::new();
    for _ in 0..4 {
        let a = start.clone();
        let b = done.clone();
        workers.push(std::thread::spawn(move || {
            a.wait();
            for _ in 0..100 {
                let mut data = std::hint::black_box(Vec::with_capacity(16));
                data.extend_from_slice(&[3u8; 16]);
                data.reserve(128);
                assert_eq!(data[0], 3);
                drop(data);
            }
            b.wait();
        }));
    }
    begin();
    start.wait();
    done.wait();
    ENABLED.store(false, Ordering::SeqCst);
    for w in workers {
        w.join().unwrap();
    }
    assert_eq!(LIVE.load(Ordering::Relaxed), 0);
    assert_eq!(LIVE_BYTES.load(Ordering::Relaxed), 0);
    assert!(!INVALID.load(Ordering::Relaxed));
    assert!(ALLOCS.load(Ordering::Relaxed) >= 800);
    println!("PASS: epoch, pra/pasca scope, realloc tracked/untracked, reuse/tombstone/table-full, ZST, 4 thread x100 operasi. Realloc same-address diuji metadata register; System tidak dijamin memilih alamat sama.");
}


FILE crates/xynoos-aura/examples/typed_research.rs
//! Eksperimen allocator terpisah, single-thread; tidak dipakai library.
use std::{sync::atomic::Ordering, time::Instant};
use xynoos_aura::{
    physical::{fixture, Policy, Simulator},
    selector::Selector,
};
#[path = "support/tracker010.rs"]
mod tracker;
use tracker::*;
#[global_allocator]
static ALLOCATOR: Counting = Counting;
fn make(n: usize) -> Simulator {
    let base = fixture(Policy::Aura);
    let mut pages = Vec::new();
    for i in 0..n {
        let mut p = base.pages[i % 16].clone();
        p.id = i as u64;
        pages.push(p);
    }
    let mut state = base.state.clone();
    state.used_bytes = [n as u64 * 4096, 0, 0];
    state.capacity_bytes[0] = n as u64 * 4096;
    state.metadata_bytes = n as u64 * 80;
    state.physical_budget_bytes = state.used_bytes[0] + state.metadata_bytes;
    state.capacity_bytes[1] = n as u64 * 4096;
    state.capacity_bytes[2] = n as u64 * 8192;
    Simulator::new(state, pages, base.engine).expect("fixture valid")
}
fn run(
    n: usize,
    seed: u64,
    mode: &str,
    sample: u8,
    kind: usize,
) -> Result<(), Box<dyn std::error::Error>> {
    let mut s = make(n);
    for p in &mut s.pages {
        if kind == 4 {
            p.compressed_bytes = 4114;
        }
        if kind == 5 {
            p.compressed_bytes = 27;
        }
        if kind == 8 {
            p.confidence_permille = 0;
        }
    }
    let mut selector = Selector::default();
    let order: Vec<_> = (0..n).collect();
    let trace: Vec<_> = (0..128)
        .map(|i| {
            (
                match kind {
                    0 => i % n.min(4),
                    2 => (i / 16 * 4 + i % 4) % n,
                    6 => {
                        if i % 16 < 12 {
                            0
                        } else {
                            (i * 17 + seed as usize) % n
                        }
                    }
                    7 => i % n,
                    _ => (i * 17 + seed as usize) % n,
                },
                if kind == 3 {
                    (i * 1000 / 127) as u16
                } else if kind == 7 {
                    if i % 2 == 0 {
                        690
                    } else {
                        710
                    }
                } else {
                    800
                },
            )
        })
        .collect();
    let (mut inspection, mut eval) = (0u128, 0u64);
    tracker::begin();
    let start = Instant::now();
    for &(page, pressure) in &trace {
        if mode == "fast" {
            let (_, stats, _) =
                selector.fast_event_measured(&mut s, page, pressure, &order, &mut inspection)?;
            eval += stats.evaluations;
        } else {
            let t = Instant::now();
            let (candidate, stats) = selector.state_aware(&s, page, pressure, &order)?;
            inspection += t.elapsed().as_nanos();
            eval += stats.evaluations;
            if mode == "journal" {
                s.event_journal(page, pressure, candidate.unwrap_or(page))?;
            } else {
                s.event_candidate(page, pressure, candidate.unwrap_or(page))?;
            }
        }
    }
    let elapsed = start.elapsed().as_nanos();
    ENABLED.store(false, Ordering::SeqCst);
    let (a, b, d) = (
        ALLOCS.load(Ordering::Relaxed),
        BYTES.load(Ordering::Relaxed),
        FREES.load(Ordering::Relaxed),
    );
    let m = &s.metrics;
    if sample > 0 {
        println!("{n},{seed},{mode},{sample},{elapsed},{inspection},{eval},{},{},{},{},{},{},{},{},{},{a},{b},{d},{},{},{},{}",if mode=="fast"{128}else{128*(n+1)},if mode=="fast"{128}else if mode=="journal"{128*2*n}else{128*(2*n+1)},m.refaults,m.migrations,m.stall_ns,m.cpu_ns,m.io_bytes,xynoos_aura::physical::physical_usage(&s.state)?,m.peak_total_bytes,LIVE.load(Ordering::Relaxed),LIVE_BYTES.load(Ordering::Relaxed),PEAK.load(Ordering::Relaxed),kind);
    }
    drop(s);
    assert_eq!(LIVE.load(Ordering::Relaxed), 0);
    assert!(!INVALID.load(Ordering::Relaxed));
    Ok(())
}
fn main() -> Result<(), Box<dyn std::error::Error>> {
    tracker::validate_register();
    let old = std::hint::black_box(Box::new([1u8; 8]));
    tracker::begin();
    drop(old);
    assert_eq!(FREES.load(Ordering::Relaxed), 0);
    let mut test = Vec::with_capacity(4);
    test.extend_from_slice(&[2u8; 4]);
    test.reserve(128);
    assert!(PEAK.load(Ordering::Relaxed) >= test.capacity() as u64);
    ENABLED.store(false, Ordering::SeqCst);
    drop(test);
    assert_eq!(LIVE.load(Ordering::Relaxed), 0);
    println!("groups,seed,mode,sample,event_ns,inspection_ns,evaluations,estimated_access_replays,estimated_vec_clones,refaults,migrations,stall_ns,cpu_ns,io_bytes,physical_bytes,peak_modeled_bytes,alloc_count,alloc_bytes,dealloc_count,live_count,live_bytes,peak_live_bytes,trace");
    for kind in [0, 4, 7, 8] {
        for n in [1, 16, 64] {
            for seed in [1, 42, 2026] {
                let mut modes = ["clone", "journal", "fast"];
                modes.rotate_left((n + seed as usize) % 3);
                for mode in modes {
                    for sample in 0..4 {
                        run(n, seed, mode, sample, kind)?;
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }
    Ok(())
}


FILE crates/xynoos-aura/examples/validation.rs
//! Eksperimen terbatas: 9 trace \xd7 3 seed \xd7 3 policy \xd7 512 event.
use std::{hint::black_box, time::Instant};
use xynoos_aura::physical::{fixture, Policy};
fn main() -> Result<(), Box<dyn std::error::Error>> {
    println!("trace,seed,policy,refaults,restore_ns,stall_ns,migrations,compressions,cpu_ns,io_bytes,peak_ram_bytes,peak_compressed_bytes,peak_backing_bytes,fallbacks,oscillations,event_execution_ns,decision_probe_ns,profile_bytes,physical_bytes,peak_physical_bytes,temporary_bytes,capacity_violations,resident_compressed,backing_bytes");
    for (kind, name) in [
        "interactive",
        "background",
        "switching",
        "pressure",
        "entropy",
        "compressible",
        "bursty",
        "adversarial",
        "unreliable",
    ]
    .iter()
    .enumerate()
    {
        for seed in [1u64, 42, 2026] {
            let mut rng = seed;
            let trace: Vec<_> = (0..512)
                .map(|i| {
                    rng ^= rng << 13;
                    rng ^= rng >> 7;
                    rng ^= rng << 17;
                    let page = match kind {
                        0 => i % 4,
                        1 => (rng % 16) as usize,
                        2 => (i / 32 % 4) * 4 + i % 4,
                        6 => {
                            if i % 32 < 24 {
                                0
                            } else {
                                (rng % 16) as usize
                            }
                        }
                        7 => i % 16,
                        _ => (rng % 16) as usize,
                    };
                    let pressure = match kind {
                        3 => (i * 1000 / 511) as u16,
                        7 => {
                            if i % 2 == 0 {
                                690
                            } else {
                                710
                            }
                        }
                        _ => 800,
                    };
                    (page, pressure)
                })
                .collect();
            for variant in [
                "lru",
                "compression",
                "aura",
                "old_fallback",
                "no_hysteresis",
                "no_cooldown",
                "no_confidence",
                "no_cost",
                "round_robin",
                "legacy_params",
            ] {
                let policy = match variant {
                    "lru" => Policy::Lru,
                    "compression" => Policy::CompressionFirst,
                    _ => Policy::Aura,
                };
                let mut s = fixture(policy);
                if kind == 4 {
                    for p in &mut s.pages {
                        p.compressed_bytes = 4114;
                    }
                }
                if kind == 5 {
                    for p in &mut s.pages {
                        p.compressed_bytes = 512;
                    }
                }
                if kind == 8 {
                    for p in &mut s.pages {
                        p.confidence_permille = 100;
                    }
                }
                s.state.metadata_bytes = 16 * 48;
                s.state.physical_budget_bytes = 65536 + 16 * 48;
                s.metrics.peak_physical_bytes = s.state.physical_budget_bytes;
                if variant == "legacy_params" {
                    s.engine.fallback_recency = false;
                }
                if variant == "old_fallback" {
                    s.engine.fallback_recency = false;
                }
                if variant == "no_hysteresis" {
                    s.engine.config.exit_pressure = 699;
                }
                if variant == "no_cooldown" {
                    s.engine.config.cooldown_ticks = 0;
                }
                if variant == "no_confidence" {
                    s.engine.config.confidence_min = 0;
                }
                if variant == "no_cost" {
                    s.engine.use_cost = false;
                }
                if variant != "legacy_params" {
                    for p in &mut s.pages {
                        let entropy = kind == 4 || (kind != 5 && p.id % 2 == 1);
                        p.compressed_bytes = if entropy { 4114 } else { 27 };
                        p.compress_ns = if entropy { 1355 } else { 718 };
                        p.decompress_ns = if entropy { 1977 } else { 1978 };
                    }
                }
                let mut recency = [0u64; 16];
                let mut event_ns = 0u128;
                let mut decision_ns = 0u128;
                for (tick, &(page, pressure)) in trace.iter().enumerate() {
                    recency[page] = tick as u64 + 1;
                    let candidate = if variant == "round_robin" || variant == "legacy_params" {
                        if variant == "legacy_params" {
                            (tick + 1) % 16
                        } else {
                            tick % 16
                        }
                    } else {
                        (0..16).min_by_key(|&i| (recency[i], i)).unwrap()
                    };
                    let start = Instant::now();
                    s.event_candidate(black_box(page), pressure, candidate)?;
                    event_ns += start.elapsed().as_nanos();
                    let mut probe = s.engine.clone();
                    let start = Instant::now();
                    black_box(probe.decide(&s.state, &s.pages[page], s.tick)?);
                    decision_ns += start.elapsed().as_nanos();
                }
                let elapsed = event_ns;
                let m = &s.metrics;
                println!(
                    "{name},{seed},{variant},{},{},{},{},{},{},{},{},{},{},{},{},{elapsed},{decision_ns},{},{},{},{},0,{},{}",
                    m.refaults,
                    m.restore_ns,
                    m.stall_ns,
                    m.migrations,
                    m.compressions,
                    m.cpu_ns,
                    m.io_bytes,
                    m.peak_bytes[0],
                    m.peak_bytes[1],
                    m.peak_bytes[2],
                    m.fallbacks,
                    m.oscillations,
                    std::mem::size_of::<xynoos_aura::physical::PageGroupProfile>(),
                    xynoos_aura::physical::physical_usage(&s.state)?,m.peak_physical_bytes,m.peak_temporary_bytes,s.state.used_bytes[1],s.state.used_bytes[2]
                );
            }
        }
    }
    Ok(())
}


FILE crates/xynoos-aura/src/cooperative.rs
//! Policy for an explicitly cooperative, private corpus. No process targeting.

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub enum Choice {
    Keep,
    Compress,
    BackOff,
}

/// The retention signal is a private application budget, never synthetic host PSI.
/// Two observations are supplied by the owned-workload state machine.
pub fn choose(sustained_retention: bool, cpu_or_io_busy: bool, cooling: bool) -> Choice {
    if cpu_or_io_busy || cooling {
        Choice::BackOff
    } else if sustained_retention {
        Choice::Compress
    } else {
        Choice::Keep
    }
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;
    #[test]
    fn real_choice_requires_retention_and_admits_backoff() {
        assert_eq!(choose(false, false, false), Choice::Keep);
        assert_eq!(choose(true, false, false), Choice::Compress);
        assert_eq!(choose(true, true, false), Choice::BackOff);
        assert_eq!(choose(true, false, true), Choice::BackOff);
    }
}


FILE crates/xynoos-aura/src/lib.rs
#![forbid(unsafe_code)]
use xynoos_core::{CostEstimate, MemoryPressure, StorageTier};
pub trait DecisionPolicy {
    fn decide(&self, pressure: MemoryPressure, cost: CostEstimate) -> StorageTier;
}
pub struct Baseline;
impl DecisionPolicy for Baseline {
    fn decide(&self, pressure: MemoryPressure, _cost: CostEstimate) -> StorageTier {
        match pressure {
            MemoryPressure::Low => StorageTier::Memory,
            MemoryPressure::Moderate | MemoryPressure::High => StorageTier::Compressed,
        }
    }
}
#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;
    #[test]
    fn deterministic() {
        let c = CostEstimate {
            latency_ns: 1,
            bytes: 4,
        };
        for _ in 0..10 {
            assert_eq!(
                Baseline.decide(MemoryPressure::Moderate, c),
                StorageTier::Compressed
            );
            assert_eq!(
                Baseline.decide(MemoryPressure::High, c),
                StorageTier::Compressed
            );
            assert_eq!(Baseline.decide(MemoryPressure::Low, c), StorageTier::Memory);
        }
    }
}
