FILE crates/xynoos-aura/src/selector.rs
//! Selector deterministik dengan himpunan eligibility eksogen yang sama antar policy.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub enum Error {
    Empty,
    TooMany,
    CounterOverflow,
}
impl std::fmt::Display for Error {
    fn fmt(&self, f: &mut std::fmt::Formatter<'_>) -> std::fmt::Result {
        write!(f, "{self:?}")
    }
}
impl std::error::Error for Error {}
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Default, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub struct Selector {
    cursor: usize,
    pub evaluations: u64,
    pub rejected: u64,
    pub no_candidate: u64,
}
impl Selector {
    /// Memeriksa maksimal 64 kandidat; mutasi terbatas pada state selector.
    pub fn next(&mut self, eligible: &[bool]) -> Result<Option<usize>, Error> {
        if eligible.is_empty() {
            return Err(Error::Empty);
        }
        if eligible.len() > 64 {
            return Err(Error::TooMany);
        }
        let mut next = self.clone();
        for offset in 0..eligible.len() {
            let i = (next.cursor + offset) % eligible.len();
            next.evaluations = next
                .evaluations
                .checked_add(1)
                .ok_or(Error::CounterOverflow)?;
            if eligible[i] {
                next.cursor = (i + 1) % eligible.len();
                *self = next;
                return Ok(Some(i));
            }
            next.rejected = next.rejected.checked_add(1).ok_or(Error::CounterOverflow)?;
        }
        next.no_candidate = next
            .no_candidate
            .checked_add(1)
            .ok_or(Error::CounterOverflow)?;
        next.cursor = (next.cursor + 1) % eligible.len();
        *self = next;
        Ok(None)
    }
}
#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;
    #[test]
    fn progress_and_ties() {
        let mut s = Selector::default();
        let mask = [false, true, false, true];
        assert_eq!(s.next(&mask), Ok(Some(1)));
        assert_eq!(s.next(&mask), Ok(Some(3)));
        assert_eq!(s.next(&mask), Ok(Some(1)));
        assert_eq!(s.evaluations, 6);
        assert_eq!(s.rejected, 3);
    }
    #[test]
    fn empty_and_overflow_atomic() {
        let mut s = Selector::default();
        assert_eq!(s.next(&[]), Err(Error::Empty));
        assert_eq!(s.next(&[true; 65]), Err(Error::TooMany));
        s.evaluations = u64::MAX;
        let before = s.clone();
        assert_eq!(s.next(&[true]), Err(Error::CounterOverflow));
        assert_eq!(s, before);
    }
    #[test]
    fn no_candidate_and_changing_mask() {
        let mut s = Selector::default();
        assert_eq!(s.next(&[false; 4]), Ok(None));
        assert_eq!(s.no_candidate, 1);
        let mut seen = [false; 4];
        for _ in 0..4 {
            seen[s.next(&[true; 4]).unwrap().unwrap()] = true;
        }
        assert!(seen.into_iter().all(|x| x));
    }
}

use crate::physical::{Decision, Simulator};
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Default, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub struct SearchStats {
    pub evaluations: u64,
    pub rejected: u64,
    pub feasible: u64,
    pub capacity: u64,
    pub budget: u64,
    pub no_feasible: u64,
    pub no_policy_migration: u64,
}
impl Selector {
    /// Preview memakai event clone yang sama dengan commit, termasuk layanan akses.
    /// Tidak mengubah simulator; urutan seluruh grup tetap diperiksa agar counters adil.
    pub fn state_aware(
        &mut self,
        sim: &Simulator,
        index: usize,
        pressure: u16,
        order: &[usize],
    ) -> Result<(Option<usize>, SearchStats), crate::physical::Error> {
        use crate::physical::Error;
        if order.len() != sim.pages.len() || order.is_empty() || order.len() > 64 {
            return Err(Error::InvalidInput);
        }
        let mut seen = [false; 64];
        for &i in order {
            if i >= order.len() || seen[i] {
                return Err(Error::InvalidInput);
            }
            seen[i] = true;
        }
        let mut stats = SearchStats::default();
        let mut chosen = None;
        for offset in 0..order.len() {
            let i = order[(self.cursor + offset) % order.len()];
            let mut trial = sim.clone();
            let d = trial.event_candidate(index, pressure, i)?;
            stats.evaluations += 1;
            stats.capacity += trial.metrics.last_capacity_rejections;
            stats.budget += trial.metrics.last_budget_rejections;
            if trial.metrics.last_feasible_mask != 0 {
                stats.feasible += 1
            } else {
                stats.rejected += 1;
            }
            if chosen.is_none()
                && matches!(
                    d,
                    Decision::StoreRaw | Decision::StoreCompressed | Decision::MoveToBacking
                )
            {
                chosen = Some(i);
            }
        }
        if stats.feasible == 0 {
            stats.no_feasible = 1;
        }
        if chosen.is_none() {
            stats.no_policy_migration = 1;
        }
        self.cursor = (self.cursor + 1) % order.len();
        Ok((chosen, stats))
    }
}
#[cfg(test)]
mod aware_tests {
    use super::*;
    use crate::physical::{fixture, Policy};
    #[test]
    fn checks_are_pure_and_bounded() {
        let mut sim = fixture(Policy::CompressionFirst);
        for p in &mut sim.pages {
            p.eligible = false;
        }
        let before = sim.clone();
        let mut selector = Selector::default();
        let (chosen, stats) = selector
            .state_aware(&sim, 0, 800, &(0..16).collect::<Vec<_>>())
            .unwrap();
        assert_eq!(chosen, None);
        assert_eq!(stats.evaluations, 16);
        assert_eq!(stats.rejected, 16);
        assert_eq!(stats.no_feasible, 1);
        assert_eq!(sim, before);
    }
    #[test]
    fn multiple_rejections_progress() {
        let mut sim = fixture(Policy::CompressionFirst);
        sim.tick = 10;
        for p in &mut sim.pages {
            p.eligible = false;
        }
        sim.pages[14].eligible = true;
        let mut selector = Selector::default();
        let (chosen, stats) = selector
            .state_aware(&sim, 0, 800, &(0..16).collect::<Vec<_>>())
            .unwrap();
        assert_eq!(chosen, Some(14));
        assert!(stats.rejected >= 15);
        let before = selector.clone();
        assert!(selector
            .state_aware(&sim, 99, 800, &(0..16).collect::<Vec<_>>())
            .is_err());
        assert_eq!(selector, before);
    }
}

// Hanya Prepared yang dapat diinspeksi; hanya CommitReady dapat dipublikasikan.
struct Prepared {
    simulation: Simulator,
    limits: (u64, u64),
}
struct CommitReady {
    simulation: Simulator,
    decision: Decision,
}
impl Prepared {
    fn new(
        original: &Simulator,
        index: usize,
        pressure: u16,
    ) -> Result<Self, crate::physical::Error> {
        let mut simulation = original.try_clone_metadata()?;
        let limits = simulation.prepare_access(index, pressure)?;
        Ok(Self { simulation, limits })
    }
    fn inspect(
        &mut self,
        index: usize,
    ) -> Result<(Decision, crate::physical::Metrics), crate::physical::Error> {
        self.simulation.inspect_prepared(index, self.limits)
    }
    fn validate(mut self, index: usize) -> Result<CommitReady, crate::physical::Error> {
        let decision = self.simulation.complete_candidate(index, self.limits)?;
        Ok(CommitReady {
            simulation: self.simulation,
            decision,
        })
    }
}
impl CommitReady {
    fn publish(self, original: &mut Simulator) -> Decision {
        *original = self.simulation;
        self.decision
    }
}

impl Selector {
    /// Satu clone transaksi dan satu layanan akses, tanpa Vec clone dalam loop kandidat.
    ///
    /// Prepared adalah detail private dan tidak dapat dipublikasikan oleh caller:
    /// ```compile_fail
    /// use xynoos_aura::selector::Prepared;
    /// ```
    pub fn fast_event(
        &mut self,
        sim: &mut Simulator,
        index: usize,
        pressure: u16,
        order: &[usize],
    ) -> Result<(Option<usize>, SearchStats, Decision), crate::physical::Error> {
        self.fast_event_inner(sim, index, pressure, order, None)
    }
    pub fn fast_event_measured(
        &mut self,
        sim: &mut Simulator,
        index: usize,
        pressure: u16,
        order: &[usize],
        inspection_ns: &mut u128,
    ) -> Result<(Option<usize>, SearchStats, Decision), crate::physical::Error> {
        self.fast_event_inner(sim, index, pressure, order, Some(inspection_ns))
    }
    fn fast_event_inner(
        &mut self,
        sim: &mut Simulator,
        index: usize,
        pressure: u16,
        order: &[usize],
        inspection_ns: Option<&mut u128>,
    ) -> Result<(Option<usize>, SearchStats, Decision), crate::physical::Error> {
        self.fast_event_hook(sim, index, pressure, order, inspection_ns, |_| Ok(()))
    }
    fn fast_event_hook<F: FnMut(u8) -> Result<(), crate::physical::Error>>(
        &mut self,
        sim: &mut Simulator,
        index: usize,
        pressure: u16,
        order: &[usize],
        inspection_ns: Option<&mut u128>,
        mut hook: F,
    ) -> Result<(Option<usize>, SearchStats, Decision), crate::physical::Error> {
        use crate::physical::Error;
        if order.len() != sim.pages.len() || order.is_empty() || order.len() > 64 {
            return Err(Error::InvalidInput);
        }
        let mut seen = [false; 64];
        for &i in order {
            if i >= order.len() || seen[i] {
                return Err(Error::InvalidInput);
            }
            seen[i] = true;
        }
        hook(0)?;
        let mut prepared = Prepared::new(sim, index, pressure)?;
        hook(1)?;
        let inspection_start = inspection_ns.as_ref().map(|_| std::time::Instant::now());
        let mut stats = SearchStats::default();
        let mut chosen = None;
        for offset in 0..order.len() {
            let i = order[(self.cursor + offset) % order.len()];
            let (d, metrics) = prepared.inspect(i)?;
            hook(2)?;
            stats.evaluations += 1;
            stats.capacity += metrics.last_capacity_rejections;
            stats.budget += metrics.last_budget_rejections;
            if metrics.last_feasible_mask != 0 {
                stats.feasible += 1;
            } else {
                stats.rejected += 1;
            }
            if chosen.is_none()
                && matches!(
                    d,
                    Decision::StoreRaw | Decision::StoreCompressed | Decision::MoveToBacking
                )
            {
                chosen = Some(i);
            }
        }
        if let (Some(output), Some(start)) = (inspection_ns, inspection_start) {
            *output += start.elapsed().as_nanos();
        }
        if stats.feasible == 0 {
            stats.no_feasible = 1;
        }
        if chosen.is_none() {
            stats.no_policy_migration = 1;
        }
        hook(3)?;
        let ready = prepared.validate(chosen.unwrap_or(index))?;
        hook(4)?;
        let decision = ready.publish(sim);
        self.cursor = (self.cursor + 1) % order.len();
        Ok((chosen, stats, decision))
    }
}
#[cfg(test)]
mod fast_tests {
    use super::*;
    use crate::physical::{fixture, Error, Policy};
    fn reference(
        sel: &mut Selector,
        s: &mut Simulator,
        index: usize,
        p: u16,
        o: &[usize],
    ) -> Result<(Option<usize>, SearchStats, Decision), Error> {
        let mut next = sel.clone();
        let (chosen, stats) = next.state_aware(s, index, p, o)?;
        let d = s.event_candidate(index, p, chosen.unwrap_or(index))?;
        *sel = next;
        Ok((chosen, stats, d))
    }
    #[test]
    fn multi_event_equivalence() {
        for n in [1, 16, 64] {
            for policy in [Policy::Aura, Policy::Lru, Policy::CompressionFirst] {
                let mut a = fixture(policy);
                if n == 1 {
                    a.pages.truncate(1);
                    a.state.used_bytes = [4096, 0, 0];
                } else if n == 64 {
                    let template = a.pages.clone();
                    for i in 16..64 {
                        let mut p = template[i % 16].clone();
                        p.id = i as u64;
                        a.pages.push(p);
                    }
                    a.state.used_bytes = [64 * 4096, 0, 0];
                    a.state.capacity_bytes[0] = 64 * 4096;
                    a.state.physical_budget_bytes = 64 * 4096 + a.state.metadata_bytes;
                }
                let mut b = a.clone();
                let mut x = Selector::default();
                let mut y = x.clone();
                let mut rng = 42u64;
                let order: Vec<_> = (0..n).rev().collect();
                for tick in 0..256 {
                    rng ^= rng << 13;
                    rng ^= rng >> 7;
                    rng ^= rng << 17;
                    let index = if tick % 31 == 0 { 99 } else { rng as usize % n };
                    let pressure = if tick % 2 == 0 { 690 } else { 800 };
                    assert_eq!(
                        reference(&mut x, &mut a, index, pressure, &order),
                        y.fast_event(&mut b, index, pressure, &order)
                    );
                    assert_eq!(a, b);
                    assert_eq!(x, y);
                }
            }
        }
    }
    #[test]
    fn six_sizes_budget_and_fallback() {
        for n in [1usize, 4, 8, 16, 32, 64] {
            for mode in 0..4 {
                let base = fixture(Policy::Aura);
                let mut pages = Vec::new();
                for i in 0..n {
                    let mut p = base.pages[i % 16].clone();
                    p.id = i as u64;
                    if mode == 2 {
                        p.confidence_permille = 0;
                    }
                    pages.push(p);
                }
                let mut state = base.state.clone();
                state.used_bytes = [n as u64 * 4096, 0, 0];
                state.capacity_bytes[0] = state.used_bytes[0];
                state.capacity_bytes[1] = n as u64 * 4096;
                state.capacity_bytes[2] = n as u64 * 8192;
                state.metadata_bytes = n as u64 * 80;
                state.physical_budget_bytes = state.used_bytes[0] + state.metadata_bytes;
                if mode == 1 {
                    state.cpu_budget_ns = 0;
                    state.io_budget_bytes = 0;
                }
                if mode == 3 {
                    state.temporary_budget_bytes = 0;
                }
                let mut a = Simulator::new(state, pages, base.engine).unwrap();
                let mut b = a.clone();
                let mut x = Selector::default();
                let mut y = x.clone();
                let order: Vec<_> = (0..n).collect();
                for i in 0..128 {
                    let idx = i % n;
                    let pressure = if i % 2 == 0 { 690 } else { 800 };
                    assert_eq!(
                        reference(&mut x, &mut a, idx, pressure, &order),
                        y.fast_event(&mut b, idx, pressure, &order)
                    );
                    assert_eq!(a, b);
                    assert_eq!(x, y);
                }
            }
        }
    }
    #[test]
    fn prepared_is_private_until_validated_publication() {
        let mut original = fixture(Policy::Aura);
        let before = original.clone();
        let mut prepared = Prepared::new(&original, 0, 800).unwrap();
        prepared.inspect(0).unwrap();
        prepared.inspect(4).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(original, before);
        let ready = prepared.validate(4).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(original, before);
        let mut expected = before.clone();
        let d = expected.event_candidate(0, 800, 4).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(ready.publish(&mut original), d);
        assert_eq!(original, expected);
    }
    #[test]
    fn failures_atomic() {
        for case in 0..5 {
            let mut a = fixture(Policy::Aura);
            match case {
                0 => a.tick = u64::MAX,
                1 => a.pages[0].accesses = u64::MAX,
                2 => a.metrics.migrations = u64::MAX,
                3 => a.engine.config.capacity_ns_per_byte = u64::MAX,
                _ => a.metrics.stall_ns = u64::MAX,
            };
            let mut b = a.clone();
            let before = a.clone();
            let mut x = Selector::default();
            let mut y = x.clone();
            let o: Vec<_> = (0..16).collect();
            let r = reference(&mut x, &mut a, 0, 800, &o);
            assert!(r.is_err());
            assert_eq!(r, y.fast_event(&mut b, 0, 800, &o));
            assert_eq!(a, b);
            assert_eq!(b, before);
            assert_eq!(x, y);
        }
    }
    #[test]
    fn invalid_order_and_empty() {
        let mut a = fixture(Policy::Aura);
        let before = a.clone();
        let mut s = Selector::default();
        assert!(s.fast_event(&mut a, 0, 800, &[0; 16]).is_err());
        assert_eq!(a, before);
    }
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod injected_tests {
    use super::*;
    use crate::physical::{fixture, Error, Policy};
    #[test]
    fn stage_errors_rollback() {
        for stage in 0..5 {
            let mut s = fixture(Policy::Aura);
            let before = s.clone();
            let mut selector = Selector::default();
            let initial = selector.clone();
            let order: Vec<_> = (0..16).collect();
            let r = selector.fast_event_hook(&mut s, 0, 800, &order, None, |phase| {
                if phase == stage {
                    Err(Error::Capacity)
                } else {
                    Ok(())
                }
            });
            assert_eq!(r, Err(Error::Capacity));
            assert_eq!(s, before);
            assert_eq!(selector, initial);
        }
    }
    #[test]
    fn unwind_before_commit_rollback() {
        for stage in 0..5 {
            let mut s = fixture(Policy::Aura);
            let before = s.clone();
            let mut selector = Selector::default();
            let initial = selector.clone();
            let order: Vec<_> = (0..16).collect();
            let result = std::panic::catch_unwind(std::panic::AssertUnwindSafe(|| {
                selector.fast_event_hook(&mut s, 0, 800, &order, None, |phase| {
                    assert_ne!(phase, stage, "injection unwind");
                    Ok(())
                })
            }));
            assert!(result.is_err());
            assert_eq!(s, before);
            assert_eq!(selector, initial);
        }
    }
    #[test]
    fn targeted_reservation_atomic() {
        let mut s = fixture(Policy::Aura);
        let before = s.clone();
        let mut selector = Selector::default();
        let initial = selector.clone();
        let order: Vec<_> = (0..16).collect();
        let r = selector.fast_event_hook(&mut s, 0, 800, &order, None, |phase| {
            if phase == 0 {
                Err(Error::AllocationReservation)
            } else {
                Ok(())
            }
        });
        assert_eq!(r, Err(Error::AllocationReservation));
        assert_eq!(s, before);
        assert_eq!(selector, initial);
    }
    #[test]
    fn controlled_allocation_rejection() {
        let mut s = fixture(Policy::Aura);
        let before = s.clone();
        let mut selector = Selector::default();
        let order: Vec<_> = (0..16).collect();
        let r = selector.fast_event_hook(&mut s, 0, 800, &order, None, |phase| {
            if phase == 0 {
                let mut v = Vec::<u8>::new();
                v.try_reserve_exact(usize::MAX)
                    .map_err(|_| Error::Capacity)?;
            }
            Ok(())
        });
        assert_eq!(r, Err(Error::Capacity));
        assert_eq!(s, before);
    }
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod reservation_tests {
    use super::*;
    use crate::physical::{fixture, Error, Policy};
    #[test]
    fn injected_reservation() {
        let s = fixture(Policy::Aura);
        let before = s.clone();
        let r = s.try_clone_metadata_with(|_, n| {
            assert_eq!(n, 16);
            Err(Error::AllocationReservation)
        });
        assert_eq!(r, Err(Error::AllocationReservation));
        assert_eq!(s, before);
        assert_eq!(
            s.try_clone_metadata_with(|_, _| Ok(())),
            Err(Error::AllocationReservation)
        );
    }
    #[test]
    fn reserve_overflow_is_distinct() {
        let s = fixture(Policy::Aura);
        let r = s.try_clone_metadata_with(|pages, _| {
            pages
                .try_reserve_exact(usize::MAX)
                .map_err(|_| Error::AllocationReservation)
        });
        assert_eq!(r, Err(Error::AllocationReservation));
        assert_eq!(s.try_clone_metadata().unwrap(), s);
    }
    #[test]
    fn same_candidate_and_access() {
        let mut a = fixture(Policy::Aura);
        let mut b = a.clone();
        let mut x = Selector::default();
        let mut y = x.clone();
        let order: Vec<_> = (0..16).collect();
        for tick in 0..128 {
            let p = tick % 16;
            let (c, stats) = x.state_aware(&a, p, 800, &order).unwrap();
            let d = a.event_candidate(p, 800, c.unwrap_or(p)).unwrap();
            assert_eq!(y.fast_event(&mut b, p, 800, &order).unwrap(), (c, stats, d));
            assert_eq!(a, b);
            assert_eq!(x, y);
        }
    }
}


FILE crates/xynoos-bench/src/main.rs
#![forbid(unsafe_code)]
use std::{collections::HashMap, error::Error, hint::black_box, process::Command, time::Instant};
use xynoos_hypermemory::PageStore;
const N: usize = 256;
const SIZE: usize = 4096;
const RUNS: usize = 32;
fn dataset(kind: &str) -> Vec<Vec<u8>> {
    let mut seed = 0x12345678u32;
    (0..N)
        .map(|p| {
            (0..SIZE)
                .map(|i| {
                    seed ^= seed << 13;
                    seed ^= seed >> 17;
                    seed ^= seed << 5;
                    let k = if kind == "mixed" {
                        ["zero", "pattern", "structured", "entropy"][p % 4]
                    } else {
                        kind
                    };
                    match k {
                        "zero" => 0,
                        "pattern" => (i % 13) as u8,
                        "structured" if i % 64 < 48 => ((i / 64 + p) % 16) as u8,
                        _ => seed as u8,
                    }
                })
                .collect()
        })
        .collect()
}
fn rss(key: &str) -> Option<usize> {
    std::fs::read_to_string("/proc/self/status")
        .ok()?
        .lines()
        .find(|l| l.starts_with(key))?
        .split_whitespace()
        .nth(1)?
        .parse()
        .ok()
}
fn cpu_ns() -> Option<u64> {
    std::fs::read_to_string("/proc/self/schedstat")
        .ok()?
        .split_whitespace()
        .next()?
        .parse()
        .ok()
}
fn main() -> Result<(), Box<dyn Error>> {
    let args: Vec<_> = std::env::args().collect();
    if args.get(1).map(String::as_str) == Some("--mvp") {
        if args.len() != 3 {
            return Err("usage: xynoos-bench --mvp baseline|observe|simulate".into());
        }
        let mode = match args[2].as_str() {
            "baseline" => xynoos_engine::Mode::Baseline,
            "observe" => xynoos_engine::Mode::Observe,
            "simulate" => xynoos_engine::Mode::Simulate,
            _ => return Err("invalid MVP benchmark mode".into()),
        };
        let report =
            xynoos_engine::replay::run(&xynoos_engine::replay::default_trace(), mode, true)?;
        println!("{}", serde_json::to_string(&report)?);
        return Ok(());
    }
    if args.len() == 1 {
        println!("dataset,backend,run,insert_batch_ns_per_page,get_batch_ns_per_page,codec_compress_mib_s,codec_decompress_mib_s,payload_bytes,capacity_bytes,metadata_bytes,map_capacity,raw_pages,compressed_pages,rss_before_kib,rss_loaded_kib,peak_kib,cpu_ns");
        for (index, kind) in ["zero", "pattern", "structured", "entropy", "mixed"]
            .iter()
            .enumerate()
        {
            let backends = ["raw", "lz4", "adaptive"];
            for i in 0..3 {
                let backend = backends[(i + index) % 3];
                let output = Command::new(std::env::current_exe()?)
                    .args([kind, backend])
                    .output()?;
                if !output.status.success() {
                    return Err(String::from_utf8_lossy(&output.stderr).into_owned().into());
                }
                print!("{}", String::from_utf8(output.stdout)?);
            }
        }
        return Ok(());
    }
    let kind = &args[1];
    if args.len() != 3
        || !["zero", "pattern", "structured", "entropy", "mixed"].contains(&kind.as_str())
        || !["raw", "lz4", "adaptive"].contains(&args[2].as_str())
    {
        return Err("usage: xynoos-bench [DATASET raw|lz4|adaptive] | --mvp MODE".into());
    }
    let backend = &args[2];
    let pages = dataset(kind);
    // Pembuatan dataset di luar seluruh interval pengukuran.
    let before = rss("VmRSS:");
    let cpu_start = cpu_ns();

    for run in 0..RUNS + 4 {
        // Codec-only; alokasi output termasuk, checksum/store/verifikasi tidak termasuk.
        let start = Instant::now();
        let blocks: Vec<_> = pages
            .iter()
            .map(|p| lz4_flex::block::compress(black_box(p)))
            .collect();
        let ct = start.elapsed().as_secs_f64();
        let start = Instant::now();
        let decoded: Vec<_> = blocks
            .iter()
            .map(|p| lz4_flex::block::decompress(black_box(p), SIZE))
            .collect::<Result<_, _>>()?;
        let dt = start.elapsed().as_secs_f64();
        assert_eq!(decoded, pages);
        drop(decoded);
        drop(blocks);
        let mut outputs = Vec::with_capacity(N);
        let (it, gt, accounting, loaded) = if backend != "raw" {
            let mut s = if backend == "adaptive" {
                PageStore::adaptive(SIZE, N, usize::MAX)?
            } else {
                PageStore::with_capacity(N)?
            };
            let mut ids = Vec::with_capacity(N);
            let start = Instant::now();
            for p in &pages {
                ids.push(s.insert(black_box(p))?);
            }
            let it = start.elapsed().as_nanos() as f64 / N as f64;
            let st = s.stats();
            let accounting = (
                st.stored_bytes,
                st.buffer_capacity_bytes,
                st.metadata_bytes_lower_bound,
                st.hash_map_capacity,
                st.raw_pages,
                st.compressed_pages,
            );
            let loaded = rss("VmRSS:");
            let start = Instant::now();
            for id in ids {
                outputs.push(s.get(black_box(id))?);
            }
            let gt = start.elapsed().as_nanos() as f64 / N as f64;
            (it, gt, accounting, loaded)
        } else {
            let mut s = HashMap::new();
            let start = Instant::now();
            for (id, p) in pages.iter().enumerate() {
                s.insert(id, black_box(p).clone());
            }
            let it = start.elapsed().as_nanos() as f64 / N as f64;
            let accounting = (
                N * SIZE,
                s.values().map(Vec::capacity).sum(),
                N * std::mem::size_of::<(usize, Vec<u8>)>(),
                s.capacity(),
                N,
                0,
            );
            let loaded = rss("VmRSS:");
            let start = Instant::now();
            for id in 0..N {
                outputs.push(black_box(s.get(&id).ok_or("ID hilang")?).clone());
            }
            let gt = start.elapsed().as_nanos() as f64 / N as f64;
            (it, gt, accounting, loaded)
        };
        assert_eq!(outputs, pages);
        black_box(outputs);
        if run >= 4 {
            let cpu = cpu_ns()
                .zip(cpu_start)
                .map(|(a, b)| a.saturating_sub(b))
                .unwrap_or(0);
            println!(
                "{kind},{backend},{},{it:.2},{gt:.2},{:.2},{:.2},{},{},{},{},{},{},{},{},{},{}",
                run - 4,
                (N * SIZE) as f64 / 1048576.0 / ct,
                (N * SIZE) as f64 / 1048576.0 / dt,
                accounting.0,
                accounting.1,
                accounting.2,
                accounting.3,
                accounting.4,
                accounting.5,
                before.unwrap_or(0),
                loaded.unwrap_or(0),
                rss("VmHWM:").unwrap_or(0),
                cpu
            );
        }
    }
    Ok(())
}


FILE crates/xynoos-cli/src/bin/xynoos-mvp.rs
//! Distribution entry point: observe, owned-buffer simulation and read-only recovery.
#![forbid(unsafe_code)]
#[path = "../mvp.rs"]
mod mvp;

fn main() -> Result<(), Box<dyn std::error::Error>> {
    let args: Vec<String> = std::env::args().skip(1).collect();
    if args.is_empty() || args == ["help"] {
        println!("xynoos-mvp observe [--count N] [--interval-ms N] [--human]\nxynoos-mvp simulate [--trace FILE] [--journal NEW_FILE] [--human]\nxynoos-mvp recovery [--journal FILE] [--human]");
        return Ok(());
    }
    if !matches!(
        args.first().map(String::as_str),
        None | Some("help" | "observe" | "simulate" | "recovery")
    ) {
        return Err("distribution permits only observe, simulate and read-only recovery".into());
    }
    mvp::run(&args)
}


FILE crates/xynoos-cli/src/main.rs
use std::{
    error::Error,
    io::{self, Write},
    path::{Path, PathBuf},
    time::Duration,
};
use xynoos_pressureiq::{
    collect, csv_row, sample, validate_sampling, Error as PressureError, CSV_HEADER,
};
mod mvp;
fn main() -> Result<(), Box<dyn Error>> {
    let args: Vec<_> = std::env::args().skip(1).collect();
    if args.first().map(String::as_str) == Some("mvp") {
        return mvp::run(&args[1..]);
    }
    if args.is_empty() {
        let mut store = xynoos_hypermemory::PageStore::with_capacity(1)?;
        let page = vec![0; 4096];
        let id = store.insert(&page)?;
        assert_eq!(store.get(id)?, page);
        println!("Halaman {:?}: {:?}", id, store.stats());
        return Ok(());
    }
    if args.first().map(String::as_str) == Some("shadow") {
        return shadow_command(&args);
    }
    if args.first().map(String::as_str) == Some("system") {
        return system_command(&args);
    }
    if args.len() < 2 || args[0] != "pressure" {
        return Err("Gunakan: pressure snapshot | pressure sample [--interval-ms N] [--count N] [--output FILE]".into());
    }
    if args[1] == "snapshot" && (args.len() == 2 || args.len() == 3 && args[2] == "--human") {
        let s = collect();
        if args.len() == 3 {
            println!("PressureIQ \u2014 sumber /proc, pengambilan berurutan");
            match &s.psi.result {
                Ok(p) => println!(
                    "PSI some avg10: {:.2}% ; total: {} \xb5s",
                    p.some.avg10, p.some.total_us
                ),
                Err(e) => println!("PSI: {e}"),
            }
            match &s.mem.result {
                Ok(m) => println!(
                    "MemTotal: {:?} byte; MemAvailable: {:?} byte",
                    m.bytes.get("MemTotal"),
                    m.bytes.get("MemAvailable")
                ),
                Err(e) => println!("MemInfo: {e}"),
            }
            match &s.vm.result {
                Ok(v) => println!("VMStat: {} counter tersedia", v.counters.len()),
                Err(e) => println!("VMStat: {e}"),
            }
            println!(
                "Parsial: {}; durasi snapshot: {} ns",
                s.partial(),
                s.elapsed_ns
            );
        } else {
            println!("{CSV_HEADER}\n{}", csv_row(&s));
        }
        if s.partial() {
            eprintln!(
                "Pengumpulan parsial: PSI={:?}; meminfo={:?}; vmstat={:?}",
                s.psi.result.as_ref().err(),
                s.mem.result.as_ref().err(),
                s.vm.result.as_ref().err()
            );
        }
        return Ok(());
    }
    if args[1] != "sample" {
        return Err("Perintah pressure tidak valid".into());
    }
    let (mut count, mut ms, mut output) = (10u32, 1000u64, None);
    let mut i = 2;
    while i < args.len() {
        if i + 1 >= args.len() {
            return Err("Parameter membutuhkan nilai".into());
        }
        match args[i].as_str() {
            "--count" => count = args[i + 1].parse()?,
            "--interval-ms" => ms = args[i + 1].parse()?,
            "--output" => output = Some(PathBuf::from(&args[i + 1])),
            _ => return Err("Parameter tidak dikenal".into()),
        }
        i += 2
    }
    let interval = Duration::from_millis(ms);
    validate_sampling(count, interval)?;
    let mut writer: Box<dyn Write> = match output {
        Some(path) => {
            let parent = path
                .parent()
                .filter(|p| !p.as_os_str().is_empty())
                .unwrap_or(Path::new("."));
            let resolved = std::fs::canonicalize(parent)?;
            if resolved.starts_with("/proc")
                || resolved.starts_with("/sys")
                || resolved.starts_with("/dev")
                || path.exists() && std::fs::canonicalize(&path)?.starts_with("/proc")
            {
                return Err("Output harus berupa file biasa di luar filesystem sistem".into());
            }
            Box::new(
                std::fs::OpenOptions::new()
                    .write(true)
                    .create_new(true)
                    .open(path)?,
            )
        }
        None => Box::new(io::stdout()),
    };
    writeln!(writer, "{CSV_HEADER}")?;
    sample(count, interval, |s| {
        if s.partial() {
            eprintln!(
                "Pengumpulan parsial: PSI={:?}; meminfo={:?}; vmstat={:?}",
                s.psi.result.as_ref().err(),
                s.mem.result.as_ref().err(),
                s.vm.result.as_ref().err()
            );
        }
        writeln!(writer, "{}", csv_row(s)).map_err(|e| PressureError::Io(e.kind().to_string()))
    })?;
    Ok(())
}

fn system_command(args: &[String]) -> Result<(), Box<dyn Error>> {
    use xynoos_pressureiq::system::{collect_system, cpu_utilization, disk_delta};
    if args.len() >= 3 && args[1] == "snapshot" && args[2] == "--json" {
        let mut disk = None;
        let mut freshness_ms = 5000u64;
        let mut i = 3;
        while i < args.len() {
            let value = args.get(i + 1).ok_or("Missing option value")?;
            match args[i].as_str() {
                "--disk" => disk = Some(value.as_str()),
                "--freshness-ms" => freshness_ms = value.parse()?,
                _ => return Err("Unknown option".into()),
            }
            i += 2;
        }
        let s = collect_system();
        let contract = xynoos_pressureiq::system::contract::build(
            &s,
            None,
            disk,
            std::time::Instant::now(),
            Duration::from_millis(freshness_ms),
        );
        println!("{}", serde_json::to_string(&contract)?);
        return Ok(());
    }
    if args.len() == 2 && args[1] == "snapshot" {
        let s = collect_system();
        println!("CPU: {:?}\nCPU PSI: {:?}\nMemori: {:?}\nMemory PSI: {:?}\nVMStat: {:?}\nIO PSI: {:?}\nDisk: {:?}\nCgroup v2: {:?}\nFrekuensi CPU kHz: {:?}\nLoad 1 menit: {:?}\nDRM: {:?}\nThermal: {:?}\nPower: {:?}\nGPU utilisasi/video decode/akselerasi: tidak tersedia",s.cpu,s.cpu_psi,s.memory.mem.result,s.memory.psi.result,s.memory.vm.result,s.io_psi,s.disks,s.cgroup,s.cpu_frequency_khz,s.load_one,s.gpu,s.thermal,s.power);
        return Ok(());
    }
    if args.len() < 2 || args[1] != "sample" {
        return Err("Gunakan system snapshot atau system sample --count N --interval-ms N".into());
    }
    let (mut count, mut ms) = (5u32, 1000u64);
    let mut disk_name: Option<&str> = None;
    let mut json = false;
    let mut freshness_ms = 5000u64;
    let mut i = 2;
    while i < args.len() {
        if args[i] == "--json" {
            json = true;
            i += 1;
            continue;
        }
        if i + 1 >= args.len() {
            return Err("Nilai parameter hilang".into());
        }
        match args[i].as_str() {
            "--count" => count = args[i + 1].parse()?,
            "--interval-ms" => ms = args[i + 1].parse()?,
            "--disk" => disk_name = Some(&args[i + 1]),
            "--freshness-ms" => freshness_ms = args[i + 1].parse()?,
            _ => return Err("Parameter tidak dikenal".into()),
        }
        i += 2
    }
    let interval = Duration::from_millis(ms);
    validate_sampling(count, interval)?;
    if !json {
        println!("timestamp_unix_ms,elapsed_ms,cpu_util_pct,logical_cpus,mem_available_bytes,cpu_psi_some_pct,io_psi_some_pct,cgroup_cpu_usage_us,cgroup_memory_bytes,cgroup_read_bytes,cgroup_write_bytes,disk_major,disk_minor,disk_read_delta_bytes,disk_write_delta_bytes,gpu_cards,thermal_sensors,power_supplies,snapshot_ns,memory_snapshot_ns,memory_psi_some_pct,pgfault_total,pgmajfault_total");
    }
    let mut previous: Option<xynoos_pressureiq::system::SystemSnapshot> = None;
    for k in 0..count {
        if k > 0 {
            std::thread::sleep(interval)
        }
        let s = collect_system();
        if json {
            let contract = xynoos_pressureiq::system::contract::build(
                &s,
                previous.as_ref(),
                disk_name,
                std::time::Instant::now(),
                Duration::from_millis(freshness_ms),
            );
            println!("{}", serde_json::to_string(&contract)?);
            previous = Some(s);
            continue;
        }
        let elapsed = previous
            .as_ref()
            .and_then(|p| s.monotonic.checked_duration_since(p.monotonic));
        let cpu = previous
            .as_ref()
            .zip(s.cpu.as_ref().ok())
            .and_then(|(p, c)| {
                p.cpu
                    .as_ref()
                    .ok()
                    .and_then(|old| elapsed.and_then(|d| cpu_utilization(old, c, d).ok()))
            });
        // No disk is representative by default: the caller must choose one.
        let disk = disk_name.and_then(|name| {
            s.disks
                .as_ref()
                .ok()
                .and_then(|v| v.iter().find(|d| d.name == name))
        });
        let delta = previous
            .as_ref()
            .zip(disk)
            .filter(|_| elapsed.is_some_and(|d| !d.is_zero()))
            .and_then(|(p, d)| {
                p.disks
                    .as_ref()
                    .ok()
                    .and_then(|v| {
                        v.iter()
                            .find(|x| x.major == d.major && x.minor == d.minor && x.name == d.name)
                    })
                    .and_then(|old| disk_delta(old, d).ok())
            });
        let opt = |v: Option<u64>| v.map(|n| n.to_string()).unwrap_or_default();
        let cg = s.cgroup.as_ref().ok();
        let io = cg.and_then(|c| c.io_bytes.as_ref().ok());
        let ts = s
            .timestamp
            .duration_since(std::time::UNIX_EPOCH)?
            .as_millis();
        println!(
            "{},{},{},{},{},{},{},{},{},{},{},{},{},{},{},{},{},{},{},{},{},{},{}",
            ts,
            opt(elapsed.map(|d| d.as_millis() as u64)),
            cpu.map(|v| format!("{v:.3}")).unwrap_or_default(),
            opt(s.cpu.as_ref().ok().map(|v| v.logical_cpus as u64)),
            opt(s
                .memory
                .mem
                .result
                .as_ref()
                .ok()
                .and_then(|m| m.bytes.get("MemAvailable").copied())),
            s.cpu_psi
                .as_ref()
                .ok()
                .map(|p| p.some.avg10.to_string())
                .unwrap_or_default(),
            s.io_psi
                .as_ref()
                .ok()
                .map(|p| p.some.avg10.to_string())
                .unwrap_or_default(),
            opt(cg.and_then(|c| c.cpu_usage_us.as_ref().ok().copied())),
            opt(cg.and_then(|c| c.memory_current_bytes.as_ref().ok().copied())),
            opt(io.map(|x| x.0)),
            opt(io.map(|x| x.1)),
            opt(disk.map(|d| d.major as u64)),
            opt(disk.map(|d| d.minor as u64)),
            opt(delta.map(|d| d.0)),
            opt(delta.map(|d| d.1)),
            s.gpu
                .iter()
                .filter(|x| x.source.ends_with("/device/vendor"))
                .count(),
            s.thermal
                .iter()
                .filter(|x| x.source.ends_with("/temp"))
                .count(),
            s.power
                .iter()
                .filter(|x| x.source.ends_with("/type"))
                .count(),
            s.elapsed_ns,
            s.memory.elapsed_ns,
            s.memory
                .psi
                .result
                .as_ref()
                .ok()
                .map(|p| p.some.avg10.to_string())
                .unwrap_or_default(),
            opt(s
                .memory
                .vm
                .result
                .as_ref()
                .ok()
                .and_then(|v| v.counters.get("pgfault").copied())),
            opt(s
                .memory
                .vm
                .result
                .as_ref()
                .ok()
                .and_then(|v| v.counters.get("pgmajfault").copied()))
        );
        previous = Some(s);
    }
    Ok(())
}

fn shadow_command(args: &[String]) -> Result<(), Box<dyn Error>> {
    if args.len() != 3 {
        return Err("usage: shadow COUNT INTERVAL_MS".into());
    }
    let count: u32 = args[1].parse()?;
    let interval = Duration::from_millis(args[2].parse()?);
    validate_sampling(count, interval)?;
    let mut engine = xynoos_decision::Engine::default();
    for i in 0..count {
        if i > 0 {
            std::thread::sleep(interval);
        }
        let snapshot = xynoos_pressureiq::system::collect_system();
        let target = snapshot.cgroup.as_ref().ok().map(|c| c.path.as_path());
        let decision = engine.evaluate(&snapshot, std::time::Instant::now(), target);
        println!("{}", serde_json::to_string(&decision)?);
    }
    Ok(())
}


FILE crates/xynoos-cli/src/mvp.rs
use std::{
    error::Error,
    fs::OpenOptions,
    io::{Read, Write},
    os::unix::fs::OpenOptionsExt,
    path::Path,
    time::{Duration, Instant},
};
use xynoos_engine::{Engine, Mode};

const HELP: &str = "mvp observe [--count 1..60] [--interval-ms 100..60000] [--human]\nmvp simulate [--trace FILE] [--journal NEW_FILE] [--human]\nmvp controlled [--authorize] [--human] (always refuses physical writes)\nmvp recovery [--journal FILE] [--human] (read-only; never replays)";
pub fn run(args: &[String]) -> Result<(), Box<dyn Error>> {
    let command = args.first().map(String::as_str).unwrap_or("help");
    if command == "help" && args.len() <= 1 {
        println!("{HELP}");
        return Ok(());
    }
    let mut human = false;
    let mut count = 1u32;
    let mut interval = 1000u64;
    let mut trace_path = None;
    let mut journal_path = None;
    let mut authorized = false;
    let mut seen = std::collections::BTreeSet::new();
    let mut i = 1;
    while i < args.len() {
        let key = args[i].as_str();
        if !seen.insert(key) {
            return Err("duplicate option".into());
        }
        match key {
            "--human" => human = true,
            "--authorize" if command == "controlled" => authorized = true,
            "--count" | "--interval-ms" if command == "observe" => {
                i += 1;
                let value = args.get(i).ok_or("missing sampling value")?;
                if key == "--count" {
                    count = value.parse()?;
                } else {
                    interval = value.parse()?;
                }
            }
            "--trace" if command == "simulate" => {
                i += 1;
                trace_path = Some(args.get(i).ok_or("missing trace path")?);
            }
            "--journal" if command == "simulate" || command == "recovery" => {
                i += 1;
                journal_path = Some(args.get(i).ok_or("missing journal path")?);
            }
            _ => return Err(format!("unsupported option; {HELP}").into()),
        }
        i += 1;
    }
    match command {
        "observe" | "controlled" => {
            xynoos_pressureiq::validate_sampling(count, Duration::from_millis(interval))?;
            let mut engine = Engine::default();
            for n in 0..count {
                if n > 0 {
                    std::thread::sleep(Duration::from_millis(interval));
                }
                let snapshot = xynoos_pressureiq::system::collect_system();
                let started = Instant::now();
                let mode = if command == "observe" {
                    Mode::Observe
                } else {
                    Mode::Controlled
                };
                let plan = engine.evaluate(&snapshot, started, mode, authorized, false);
                let decision_ns = started.elapsed().as_nanos();
                let contract = xynoos_pressureiq::system::contract::build(
                    &snapshot,
                    None,
                    None,
                    Instant::now(),
                    Duration::from_secs(5),
                );
                if human {
                    println!("PressureIQ \u2192 AURA: {:?}; {:?}; {:?}. Kernel writes locked. No workload buffers changed. Collection {} ns; decision {} ns.",
                        plan.action, plan.reason, plan.outcome, snapshot.elapsed_ns, decision_ns);
                } else {
                    let stdout = std::io::stdout();
                    let mut output = std::io::BufWriter::new(stdout.lock());
                    xynoos_engine::observation::write(&mut output, &plan, decision_ns, &contract)?;
                    output.write_all(b"\n")?;
                    output.flush()?;
                }
            }
            if command == "controlled" {
                return Err("physical mutation admission denied: exclusive delegation and physical recovery are NOT VERIFIED".into());
            }
        }
        "simulate" => {
            let trace = match trace_path {
                Some(path) => xynoos_engine::replay::Trace::parse(&read_trace(Path::new(path))?)?,
                None => xynoos_engine::replay::default_trace(),
            };
            // The command itself explicitly authorizes work on private simulated pages.
            let report = if let Some(path) = journal_path {
                let mut journal = xynoos_engine::recovery::Journal::create(Path::new(path))?;
                xynoos_engine::replay::run_with_evidence(&trace, Mode::Simulate, true, |entry| {
                    journal.append(entry)
                })?
            } else {
                xynoos_engine::replay::run(&trace, Mode::Simulate, true)?
            };
            if human {
                println!("SIMULATION: {} trace steps; {} safety refusals; {} private-buffer verifications; zero kernel mutations.", report.rows.len(), report.safety_refusals, report.owned_buffer_verifications);
                for row in &report.rows {
                    println!(
                        "step {}: {:?} / {:?}; stored payload {}/{} bytes; verified={}",
                        row.step,
                        row.plan.action,
                        row.plan.reason,
                        row.workload.stored_payload_bytes,
                        row.workload.original_bytes,
                        row.workload.byte_verification_passed
                    );
                }
                println!("Actual userspace work, synthetic pressure. No desktop performance benefit established.");
            } else {
                println!("{}", serde_json::to_string(&report)?);
            }
        }
        "recovery" => {
            let journal = journal_path
                .map(|p| xynoos_engine::recovery::inspect_file(Path::new(p)))
                .transpose()?;
            let report = serde_json::json!({
                "schema_version":1, "read_only":true, "physical_mutation_available":false,
                "automatic_recovery_available":false, "physical_recovery":"not_implemented_locked",
                "manual_intervention_required_for_physical_state":true,
                "capabilities":xynoos_linux_control::discover(), "journal":journal,
                "explanation":"Preserve unresolved records. Fresh exact invocation, descriptor identities, expected values, worker termination and exclusive ownership are required before any separately engineered recovery. This command never restores or deletes kernel state."
            });
            if human {
                println!("{}", serde_json::to_string_pretty(&report)?);
            } else {
                println!("{}", serde_json::to_string(&report)?);
            }
        }
        _ => return Err(HELP.into()),
    }
    Ok(())
}
fn read_trace(path: &Path) -> Result<Vec<u8>, Box<dyn Error>> {
    // Symlinks are rejected, and named pipes cannot block this CLI.
    let file = OpenOptions::new()
        .read(true)
        .custom_flags(libc::O_NOFOLLOW | libc::O_NONBLOCK)
        .open(path)?;
    if !file.metadata()?.is_file() {
        return Err("trace must be a regular file".into());
    }
    let mut bytes = Vec::new();
    file.take(65537).read_to_end(&mut bytes)?;
    Ok(bytes)
}


FILE crates/xynoos-cli/tests/mvp.rs
use std::process::Command;
fn cli(args: &[&str]) -> std::process::Output {
    Command::new(env!("CARGO_BIN_EXE_xynoos-cli"))
        .args(args)
        .output()
        .unwrap()
}
#[test]
fn full_observe_command_is_structured_and_read_only() {
    let o = cli(&["mvp", "observe", "--count", "1"]);
    assert!(o.status.success(), "{:?}", o);
    let v: serde_json::Value = serde_json::from_slice(&o.stdout).unwrap();
    assert_eq!(v["kernel_mutations"], 0);
    assert_eq!(v["owned_buffer_mutations"], 0);
    assert_eq!(v["evidence"], "live_read_only");
    assert_eq!(v["plan"]["mode"], "observe");
    assert!(v["telemetry"]["readings"]["memory_psi"]["source"].is_string());
    assert_eq!(v["plan"]["kernel_write_available"], false);
}
#[test]
fn full_simulation_command_exercises_real_policy_and_store() {
    let o = cli(&["mvp", "simulate"]);
    assert!(o.status.success());
    let v: serde_json::Value = serde_json::from_slice(&o.stdout).unwrap();
    assert_eq!(v["kernel_mutations"], 0);
    assert_eq!(v["telemetry"], "deterministic_replay");
    assert_eq!(v["safety_refusals"], 2);
    assert_eq!(v["rows"][2]["plan"]["action"], "compress_owned_pages");
    assert!(
        v["rows"][2]["workload"]["compressed_pages"]
            .as_u64()
            .unwrap()
            > 0
    );
}
#[test]
fn physical_admission_cannot_be_enabled_by_authorize_or_environment() {
    for args in [
        &["mvp", "controlled"][..],
        &["mvp", "controlled", "--authorize"][..],
    ] {
        let o = Command::new(env!("CARGO_BIN_EXE_xynoos-cli"))
            .args(args)
            .env("XYNOOS_ALLOW_KERNEL_MUTATION", "1")
            .output()
            .unwrap();
        assert!(!o.status.success());
        let v: serde_json::Value = serde_json::from_slice(&o.stdout).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(v["kernel_mutations"], 0);
        assert_eq!(v["plan"]["outcome"], "refused");
    }
}
#[test]
fn recovery_has_no_replay_option_and_cli_rejects_invalid_bounds() {
    let o = cli(&["mvp", "recovery"]);
    assert!(o.status.success());
    let v: serde_json::Value = serde_json::from_slice(&o.stdout).unwrap();
    assert_eq!(v["automatic_recovery_available"], false);
    assert_eq!(v["physical_mutation_available"], false);
    for args in [
        vec!["mvp", "observe", "--count", "61"],
        vec!["mvp", "observe", "--interval-ms", "0"],
        vec!["mvp", "simulate", "--authorize"],
        vec!["mvp", "recovery", "--replay"],
        vec!["mvp", "observe", "--count", "1", "--count", "2"],
        vec!["mvp", "simulate", "--trace", "/dev/null"],
    ] {
        assert!(!cli(&args).status.success());
    }
    assert!(cli(&["mvp", "observe", "--human"]).status.success());
    assert!(cli(&["mvp", "simulate", "--human"]).status.success());
}

#[test]
fn distribution_excludes_controlled_mode_and_journal_cli_preserves_evidence() {
    let binary = env!("CARGO_BIN_EXE_xynoos-mvp");
    assert!(!Command::new(binary)
        .args(["controlled", "--authorize"])
        .output()
        .unwrap()
        .status
        .success());
    let dir = std::env::temp_dir().join(format!("xynoos-mvp-cli-{}", std::process::id()));
    std::fs::create_dir(&dir).unwrap();
    let path = dir.join("journal.jsonl");
    let o = Command::new(binary)
        .args(["simulate", "--journal"])
        .arg(&path)
        .output()
        .unwrap();
    assert!(o.status.success(), "{:?}", o);
    let before = std::fs::read(&path).unwrap();
    let o = Command::new(binary)
        .args(["recovery", "--journal"])
        .arg(&path)
        .output()
        .unwrap();
    assert!(o.status.success());
    let v: serde_json::Value = serde_json::from_slice(&o.stdout).unwrap();
    assert_eq!(v["journal"]["status"], "simulation_restored");
    assert_eq!(std::fs::read(&path).unwrap(), before);
    assert!(!Command::new(binary)
        .args(["simulate", "--journal"])
        .arg(&path)
        .output()
        .unwrap()
        .status
        .success());
    std::fs::remove_dir_all(dir).unwrap();
}

#[test]
fn bounded_file_output_and_no_overwrite() {
    let name = format!(
        "xynoos-pressureiq-{}-{}.csv",
        std::process::id(),
        std::time::SystemTime::now()
            .duration_since(std::time::UNIX_EPOCH)
            .unwrap()
            .as_nanos()
    );
    let path = std::env::temp_dir().join(name);
    let executable = env!("CARGO_BIN_EXE_xynoos-cli");
    let run = || {
        Command::new(executable)
            .args([
                "pressure",
                "sample",
                "--count",
                "2",
                "--interval-ms",
                "100",
                "--output",
            ])
            .arg(&path)
            .output()
            .unwrap()
    };
    assert!(run().status.success());
    let contents = std::fs::read_to_string(&path).unwrap();
    assert_eq!(contents.lines().count(), 3);
    assert!(!run().status.success());
    assert_eq!(std::fs::read_to_string(&path).unwrap(), contents);
    std::fs::remove_file(path).unwrap();
}

        .args(["system", "sample", "--count", "2", "--interval-ms", "100"])
        .output()
        .unwrap();

    let rows: Vec<_> = s.lines().collect();
    assert_eq!(rows.len(), 3);
    let n = rows[0].split(',').count();
    assert_eq!(rows[1].split(',').count(), n);
    assert_eq!(rows[2].split(',').count(), n);
    assert_eq!(rows[1].split(',').nth(2), Some(""));
}

FILE crates/xynoos-decision/examples/cgroup_probe.rs
//! Read-only self-cgroup probe; no isolation is asserted.
use std::{
    thread,
    time::{Duration, Instant},
};
use xynoos_pressureiq::{cgroup_pressure, workload};
fn main() -> Result<(), Box<dyn std::error::Error>> {
    let id = workload::identify(std::process::id())?;
    let mut engine = xynoos_decision::WorkloadEngine::default();
    let mut previous = None;
    for _ in 0..2 {
        let start = Instant::now();
        let s = cgroup_pressure::collect(&id)?;
        let d = engine.evaluate(&s, &xynoos_decision::WorkloadScope::Shared, Instant::now());
        println!("identity={:?} cpu={:?} memory={:?} io={:?} isolation={:?} decision={:?} collection_us={}", s.cgroup, s.cpu.result, s.memory.result, s.io.result, s.isolation_check.result, d, start.elapsed().as_micros());
        if let Some(a) = previous.as_ref() {
            for name in ["cpu", "memory", "io"] {
                println!("{name} delta={:?}", cgroup_pressure::delta(a, &s, name));
            }
        }
        previous = Some(s);
        thread::sleep(Duration::from_millis(200));
    }
    Ok(())
}


FILE crates/xynoos-decision/examples/optimization_experiment.rs
//! Bounded read-only repeatability experiment; JSON lines on stdout.
use std::{
    fs::{self, OpenOptions},
    io::{Read, Write},
    path::Path,
    process::{Child, Command, Stdio},
    sync::atomic::{AtomicBool, Ordering},
    time::{Duration, Instant},
};
use xynoos_pressureiq::{cgroup_pressure, system, workload};
struct Guard(Child);
impl Drop for Guard {
    fn drop(&mut self) {
        let _ = self.0.kill();
        let _ = self.0.wait();
    }
}
fn worker(kind: &str) -> Result<(), Box<dyn std::error::Error>> {
    let start = Instant::now();
    let mut data = vec![
        0u8;
        if kind == "memory" {
            32 * 1024 * 1024
        } else {
            4096
        }
    ];
    let path = std::env::temp_dir().join(format!("xynoos-017-{}", std::process::id()));
    let mut file = if kind == "io" {
        let f = OpenOptions::new()
            .write(true)
            .create_new(true)
            .open(&path)?;
        fs::remove_file(&path)?;
        Some(f)
    } else {
        None
    };
    let mut n = 1u64;
    let mut written = 0usize;
    while start.elapsed() < Duration::from_millis(1200) {
        match kind {
            "cpu" => {
                for _ in 0..100_000 {
                    n = n.wrapping_mul(1664525).wrapping_add(1013904223);
                }
                std::hint::black_box(n);
            }
            "memory" => {
                for v in data.iter_mut().step_by(4096) {
                    *v = v.wrapping_add(1);
                }
                std::hint::black_box(&data);
            }
            "io" => {
                if written < 8 * 1024 * 1024 {
                    file.as_mut().unwrap().write_all(&data)?;
                    written += data.len();
                } else {
                    std::thread::sleep(Duration::from_millis(10));
                }
            }
            "idle" => std::thread::sleep(Duration::from_millis(10)),
            _ => return Err("unknown workload".into()),
        }
    }
    Ok(())
}
static CANCELLED: AtomicBool = AtomicBool::new(false);

extern "C" fn cancel_handler(_: libc::c_int) {
    CANCELLED.store(true, Ordering::Relaxed);
}

fn install_cancellation() -> Result<(), String> {
    // SAFETY: sigaction is initialized before use and the handler only stores an atomic flag.
    unsafe {
        let mut action: libc::sigaction = std::mem::zeroed();
        action.sa_sigaction = cancel_handler as *const () as usize;
        libc::sigemptyset(&mut action.sa_mask);
        for signal in [libc::SIGINT, libc::SIGTERM] {
            if libc::sigaction(signal, &action, std::ptr::null_mut()) != 0 {
                return Err(format!(
                    "install signal handler: {}",
                    std::io::Error::last_os_error()
                ));
            }
        }
    }
    Ok(())
}

fn cancellation_check() -> Result<(), String> {
    if CANCELLED.load(Ordering::Relaxed) {
        Err("cancelled".into())
    } else {
        Ok(())
    }
}

// On failure return after a bounded interval; the caller must preserve the target.
fn stop_and_reap(child: &mut Child) -> Result<(), String> {
    if child
        .try_wait()
        .map_err(|e| format!("child status: {e}"))?
        .is_some()
    {
        return Ok(());
    }
    let kill_error = child.kill().err();
    let deadline = Instant::now() + Duration::from_secs(2);
    loop {
        match child.try_wait() {
            Ok(Some(_)) => return Ok(()),
            Err(e) => {
                return Err(format!(
                    "cannot verify child reaping: {e}; kill error: {kill_error:?}"
                ))
            }
            Ok(None) if Instant::now() >= deadline => {
                return Err(format!(
                    "child termination not verified; kill error: {kill_error:?}"
                ))
            }
            Ok(None) => std::thread::sleep(Duration::from_millis(10)),
        }
    }
}

// The worker blocks on a pipe before allocating memory or performing work.
fn gated_memory_worker(passes: usize) -> Result<(), Box<dyn std::error::Error>> {
    let mut release = [0u8; 1];
    std::io::stdin().read_exact(&mut release)?;
    if release != [1] {
        return Err("invalid release".into());
    }
    let mut memory = vec![0u8; 32 * 1024 * 1024];
    for _ in 0..passes {
        for byte in memory.iter_mut().step_by(4096) {
            *byte = byte.wrapping_add(1);
        }
        std::hint::black_box(&memory);
    }
    Ok(())
}

// Observation only: two identical bounded processes; no policy is applied.
fn memory_repeatability() -> Result<(), Box<dyn std::error::Error>> {
    for repeat in 0..3 {
        for sampled in if repeat % 2 == 0 {
            [false, true]
        } else {
            [true, false]
        } {
            let mut competitor = Guard(
                Command::new(std::env::current_exe()?)
                    .arg("gated-memory-competitor")
                    .stdin(Stdio::piped())
                    .spawn()?,
            );
            let mut worker = Guard(
                Command::new(std::env::current_exe()?)
                    .arg("gated-memory-worker")
                    .stdin(Stdio::piped())
                    .spawn()?,
            );
            let mut sample_ns = 0;
            let mut pressure_before = None;
            if sampled {
                let t = Instant::now();
                pressure_before = system::collect_system()
                    .memory
                    .psi
                    .result
                    .ok()
                    .map(|p| p.some.total_us);
                sample_ns += t.elapsed().as_nanos();
            }
            competitor
                .0
                .stdin
                .take()
                .ok_or("competitor pipe")?
                .write_all(&[1])?;
            let start = Instant::now();
            worker
                .0
                .stdin
                .take()
                .ok_or("worker pipe")?
                .write_all(&[1])?;
            let status = loop {
                if let Some(status) = worker.0.try_wait()? {
                    break status;
                }
                if start.elapsed() > Duration::from_secs(10) {
                    return Err("worker timeout".into());
                }
                std::thread::sleep(Duration::from_millis(1));
            };
            let elapsed = start.elapsed().as_nanos();
            if !status.success() {
                return Err("worker failed".into());
            }
            let competitor_running_at_finish = competitor.0.try_wait()?.is_none();
            let mut pressure_after = None;
            if sampled {
                let t = Instant::now();
                pressure_after = system::collect_system()
                    .memory
                    .psi
                    .result
                    .ok()
                    .map(|p| p.some.total_us);
                sample_ns += t.elapsed().as_nanos();
            }
            // Both workers have fixed work; wait only until the same bounded deadline.
            loop {
                if let Some(status) = competitor.0.try_wait()? {
                    if !status.success() {
                        return Err("competitor failed".into());
                    }
                    break;
                }
                if start.elapsed() > Duration::from_secs(10) {
                    return Err("competitor timeout".into());
                }
                std::thread::sleep(Duration::from_millis(1));
            }
            println!(
                "{}",
                serde_json::json!({"record":"memory_repeatability", "repeat":repeat,
                "sampled":sampled, "worker_passes":128, "competitor_passes":512,
                "memory_bytes_per_process":33554432, "completion_ns":elapsed,
                "competitor_running_at_worker_finish":competitor_running_at_finish,
                "collection_ns":sample_ns, "visible_system_memory_psi_some_total_before_us":pressure_before,
                "visible_system_memory_psi_some_total_after_us":pressure_after,
                "kernel_intervention":false, "performance_effect":"NOT ESTABLISHED"})
            );
        }
    }
    Ok(())
}

const MIN_AVAILABLE_BYTES: u64 = 200 * 1024 * 1024;

fn require_available_memory(bytes: Option<u64>) -> Result<u64, String> {
    match bytes {
        Some(value) if value >= MIN_AVAILABLE_BYTES => Ok(value),
        Some(value) => Err(format!("MemAvailable {value} below 200 MiB minimum")),
        None => Err("MemAvailable unavailable".into()),
    }
}

fn available_memory(snapshot: &system::SystemSnapshot) -> Result<u64, String> {
    require_available_memory(
        snapshot
            .memory
            .mem
            .result
            .as_ref()
            .ok()
            .and_then(|m| m.bytes.get("MemAvailable").copied()),
    )
}

fn preflight() -> Result<(), Box<dyn std::error::Error>> {
    let start = Instant::now();
    let snapshot = system::collect_system();
    let available = snapshot
        .memory
        .mem
        .result
        .as_ref()
        .ok()
        .and_then(|m| m.bytes.get("MemAvailable").copied());
    let identity = workload::identify(std::process::id())?;
    let cgroup = cgroup_pressure::collect(&identity);
    let caps = xynoos_linux_control::discover();
    let cgroup_path = cgroup
        .as_ref()
        .ok()
        .map(|c| c.cgroup.path.display().to_string());
    println!(
        "{}",
        serde_json::json!({"record":"preflight", "pid1":fs::read_to_string("/proc/1/comm").ok().map(|s|s.trim().to_string()),
        "cgroup_membership":fs::read_to_string("/proc/self/cgroup").ok(), "cgroup_path":cgroup_path,
        "cgroup_error":cgroup.err().map(|e|format!("{e:?}")), "capabilities":caps,
        "mem_available_bytes":available, "memory_gate":require_available_memory(available),
        "controlled_mode":"DISABLED: transient-unit ownership and controller activation not verified",
        "elapsed_ns":start.elapsed().as_nanos()})
    );
    Ok(())
}

fn validate_parent_state(
    procs: &str,
    controllers: &str,
    enabled: &str,
) -> Result<(), &'static str> {
    if !procs.trim().is_empty() {
        return Err("parent contains processes");
    }
    if !controllers.split_whitespace().any(|c| c == "memory") {
        return Err("memory controller unavailable");
    }
    if enabled
        .split_whitespace()
        .any(|c| !["cpu", "memory", "pids"].contains(&c))
    {
        return Err("unexpected enabled controller");
    }
    Ok(())
}

// Read-only service verification. It is not authorization to activate controllers.
fn manager_preflight(unit: &str) -> Result<(), Box<dyn std::error::Error>> {
    if !unit.starts_with("xynoos-018d-")
        || !unit.ends_with(".service")
        || !unit
            .bytes()
            .all(|c| c.is_ascii_alphanumeric() || c == b'-' || c == b'.')
    {
        return Err("invalid transient unit name".into());
    }
    let binding = xynoos_linux_control::systemd_identity::Binding::discover(unit)?;
    binding.revalidate()?;
    let membership = fs::read_to_string("/proc/self/cgroup")?;
    let path =
        system::parse_cgroup_path(&membership, &fs::read_to_string("/proc/self/mountinfo")?)?;
    if path.file_name().and_then(|s| s.to_str()) != Some("manager") {
        return Err("manager subgroup mismatch".into());
    }
    let parent = path.parent().ok_or("no delegated parent")?;
    let inspection = xynoos_linux_control::transaction::inspect_manager_parent(parent)?;
    let mut controller_session =
        xynoos_linux_control::controller::ControllerInspectionSession::new(binding)?;
    let readiness = controller_session.inspect()?;
    let memory = available_memory(&system::collect_system());
    let parent_state = validate_parent_state(
        if inspection.parent_empty {
            ""
        } else {
            "populated"
        },
        &inspection.controllers,
        &inspection.enabled,
    );
    let eligible = parent_state.is_ok() && memory.is_ok();
    println!(
        "{}",
        serde_json::json!({"binding":controller_session.binding_diagnostic(), "binding_revalidation":"passed", "controller_session":readiness, "record":"manager_preflight", "unit":unit, "manager_pid":std::process::id(),
        "manager_path":path, "parent_path":parent, "inspection":inspection, "parent_state":parent_state,
        "memory_gate":memory, "eligible_for_further_validation":eligible,
        "controller_activation":"DISABLED", "kernel_control_write":false})
    );
    // Keep the exact MainPID/binding alive until the external observer has pinned
    // its read-only evidence. EOF/timeout is a failure, never implicit approval.
    if std::env::var("XYNOOS_CLEANUP_HANDSHAKE").as_deref() == Ok("1") {
        std::io::stdout().flush()?;
        let mut poll = libc::pollfd {
            fd: 0,
            events: libc::POLLIN,
            revents: 0,
        };
        // SAFETY: poll points to one initialized pollfd; this only waits on stdin.
        if unsafe { libc::poll(&mut poll, 1, 15_000) } != 1 {
            return Err("cleanup observer handshake timeout".into());
        }
        let mut release = [0u8; 1];
        std::io::stdin().read_exact(&mut release)?;
        if release != *b"1" {
            return Err("cleanup observer handshake rejected".into());
        }
    }
    if !eligible {
        return Err("delegation preflight declined".into());
    }
    Ok(())
}

#[allow(dead_code)]
fn controlled(parent: &Path) -> Result<(), Box<dyn std::error::Error>> {
    use xynoos_linux_control::{
        transaction::{Backend, DisposableCgroup, Outcome},
        Action,
    };
    install_cancellation()?;
    cancellation_check()?;
    let snapshot = system::collect_system();
    available_memory(&snapshot)?;
    cancellation_check()?;
    let mut group = DisposableCgroup::create(parent, true)?;
    if let Err(e) = cancellation_check() {
        let cleanup = group.cleanup();
        return Err(format!("{e}; disposable target cleanup: {cleanup:?}").into());
    }
    let action = Action::MemoryLow {
        bytes: 32 * 1024 * 1024,
    };
    let mut execution_ns = None;
    let report = xynoos_linux_control::transaction::run_during(
        &mut group,
        &snapshot,
        action,
        true,
        |target| {
            cancellation_check()?;
            let mut child = Command::new(std::env::current_exe().map_err(|e| e.to_string())?)
                .arg("gated-memory-worker")
                .stdin(Stdio::piped())
                .stdout(Stdio::null())
                .spawn()
                .map_err(|e| format!("worker spawn: {e}"))?;
            let spawned = Instant::now();
            let result = (|| -> Result<(), String> {
                cancellation_check()?;
                target.place_child(&child)?;
                cancellation_check()?;
                target.verify_child()?;
                available_memory(&system::collect_system())?;
                if spawned.elapsed() >= Duration::from_secs(5) {
                    return Err("worker timeout before release".into());
                }
                xynoos_linux_control::validate_telemetry(&snapshot, Instant::now(), action)?;
                if target.read_low()? != 32 * 1024 * 1024 {
                    return Err("control changed before release".into());
                }
                cancellation_check()?;
                let started = Instant::now();
                child
                    .stdin
                    .take()
                    .ok_or("missing release pipe")?
                    .write_all(&[1])
                    .map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
                loop {
                    cancellation_check()?;
                    if let Some(status) = child.try_wait().map_err(|e| e.to_string())? {
                        execution_ns = Some(started.elapsed().as_nanos());
                        if !status.success() {
                            return Err(format!("worker failed: {status}"));
                        }
                        return Ok(());
                    }
                    if spawned.elapsed() > Duration::from_secs(5) {
                        return Err("worker timeout".into());
                    }
                    std::thread::sleep(Duration::from_millis(5));
                }
            })();
            if let Err(e) = stop_and_reap(&mut child) {
                target.mark_unresolved();
                return Err(format!(
                    "{result:?}; {e}; target preserved and restoration blocked"
                ));
            }
            result
        },
    );
    let cleanup = if matches!(
        report.outcome,
        Outcome::Conflict | Outcome::RollbackFailed | Outcome::Ambiguous
    ) {
        group.preserve();
        Err("target preserved after conflict or rollback failure".to_string())
    } else {
        group.cleanup()
    };
    let success = report.outcome == Outcome::Restored && cleanup.is_ok();
    println!(
        "{}",
        serde_json::json!({"record":"controlled", "execution_ns":execution_ns, "transaction":report, "cleanup":cleanup, "performance_effect":"NOT VERIFIED: no valid comparator"})
    );
    if !success {
        return Err("controlled experiment failed; see transaction and cleanup report".into());
    }
    Ok(())
}

fn validate_mode(mode: &str) -> Result<(), &'static str> {
    match mode {
        "observe" | "dry-run" => Ok(()),
        "controlled" => Err("controlled intervention unavailable: workload membership and transaction lifetime are not safely supported"),
        _ => Err("usage: optimization_experiment [observe|dry-run|controlled]"),
    }
}

fn main() -> Result<(), Box<dyn std::error::Error>> {
    let args: Vec<_> = std::env::args().collect();
    if args.get(1).is_some_and(|x| x == "empty-child-transaction") {
        return disabled_empty_child_request(&args[2..]).map_err(Into::into);
    }
    if args.len() == 3 && args[1] == "manager-preflight" {
        return manager_preflight(&args[2]);
    }
    if args.len() == 2 && args[1] == "preflight" {
        return preflight();
    }
    if args.len() == 2 && args[1] == "gated-memory-worker" {
        return gated_memory_worker(128);
    }
    if args.len() == 2 && args[1] == "gated-memory-competitor" {
        return gated_memory_worker(512);
    }
    if args.len() == 2 && args[1] == "memory-repeatability" {
        return memory_repeatability();
    }
    if args.get(1).is_some_and(|x| x == "controlled") {
        if args.len() != 5 || args[2] != "--authorize" || args[3] != "--parent" {
            return Err("usage: controlled --authorize --parent /absolute/delegated/parent".into());
        }
        return Err("controlled mode disabled: delegated transient unit verification and lifecycle safety gates remain incomplete".into());
    }
    if args.len() == 3 && args[1] == "worker" {
        return worker(&args[2]);
    }
    let mode = args.get(1).map(String::as_str).unwrap_or("observe");
    if args.len() > 2 {
        return Err("usage: optimization_experiment [observe|dry-run|controlled]".into());
    }
    validate_mode(mode)?;
    println!(
        "{}",
        serde_json::json!({"record":"method", "mode":mode, "comparison":"unsampled versus sampled observation; no intervention", "pairs":3, "duration_ms":1200, "sample_period_ms":200, "memory_cap_bytes":33554432, "io_cap_bytes":8388608, "timeout_ms":5000, "ordering":"alternating AB/BA", "pressure_scope":"shared cgroup and visible system; not attributable to child", "performance_effect":"NOT VERIFIED"})
    );
    for kind in ["idle", "cpu", "memory", "io"] {
        for repeat in 0..3 {
            for sampled in if repeat % 2 == 0 {
                [false, true]
            } else {
                [true, false]
            } {
                let start = Instant::now();
                let mut child = Guard(
                    Command::new(std::env::current_exe()?)
                        .args(["worker", kind])
                        .stdout(Stdio::null())
                        .spawn()?,
                );
                let id = workload::identify(child.0.id())?;
                let mut first = None;
                let mut last = None;
                let mut engine = xynoos_decision::Engine::default();
                let mut scoped_engine = xynoos_decision::WorkloadEngine::default();
                let mut decisions = Vec::new();
                let mut collection_ns = 0u128;
                let mut peak_rss = None::<u64>;
                let mut audits = Vec::new();
                let mut cg_path = None;
                let mut next_sample = Instant::now();
                loop {
                    if let Some(status) = child.0.try_wait()? {
                        if !status.success() {
                            return Err(format!("worker failed: {status}").into());
                        }
                        break;
                    }
                    if sampled && Instant::now() >= next_sample {
                        next_sample = Instant::now() + Duration::from_millis(200);
                        let t = Instant::now();
                        let p = workload::collect(&id);
                        let cg = cgroup_pressure::collect(&id);
                        if let Ok(c) = &cg {
                            cg_path = Some(c.cgroup.path.display().to_string());
                        }
                        if let Ok(rss) = p.memory_bytes.result {
                            peak_rss = Some(peak_rss.unwrap_or(0).max(rss));
                        }
                        let scoped = cg.as_ref().map(|c| {
                            scoped_engine.evaluate(
                                c,
                                &xynoos_decision::WorkloadScope::Shared,
                                Instant::now(),
                            )
                        });
                        let s = system::collect_system();
                        let d = engine.evaluate(&s, Instant::now(), None);
                        if mode == "dry-run" {
                            // This is an inspection of the observed shared cgroup, never authorization to write.
                            if let Ok(c) = &cg {
                                audits.push(xynoos_linux_control::prepare(
                                    &s,
                                    Instant::now(),
                                    &c.cgroup.path,
                                    xynoos_linux_control::Action::MemoryLow {
                                        bytes: 32 * 1024 * 1024,
                                    },
                                ));
                            }
                        }
                        collection_ns += t.elapsed().as_nanos();
                        decisions.push(format!(
                            "system={:?}:{:?} cgroup={:?}",
                            d.kind, d.pressure_percent, scoped
                        ));
                        if first.is_none() {
                            first = Some(p.clone());
                        }
                        last = Some(p);
                    }
