                    if let Some(status) = child.0.try_wait()? {
                        if !status.success() {
                            return Err(format!("worker failed: {status}").into());
                        }
                        break;
                    }
                    if start.elapsed() > Duration::from_secs(5) {
                        return Err("worker timeout".into());
                    }
                    std::thread::sleep(Duration::from_millis(10));
                }
                let delta = first
                    .as_ref()
                    .zip(last.as_ref())
                    .map(|(a, b)| workload::delta(a, b));
                let delta_json = match delta {
                    Some(Ok(d)) => {
                        serde_json::json!({"cpu_ticks":d.cpu_ticks,"cpu_interval_ns":d.cpu_interval.as_nanos(),"read_bytes":d.read_bytes,"write_bytes":d.write_bytes,"io_interval_ns":d.io_interval.as_nanos()})
                    }
                    Some(Err(e)) => serde_json::json!({"error":format!("{e:?}")}),
                    None => serde_json::Value::Null,
                };
                println!(
                    "{}",
                    serde_json::json!({"record":"run", "mode":mode, "workload":kind, "repeat":repeat, "sampled":sampled, "wall_ns":start.elapsed().as_nanos(), "collection_ns":collection_ns, "process_pid":id.pid, "process_start_ticks":id.start_ticks, "sampled_peak_rss_bytes":peak_rss, "process_delta":delta_json, "shared_cgroup":cg_path, "decisions":decisions, "dry_run_audits":audits, "kernel_write":false, "cleanup":"worker exited; temporary I/O unlinked"})
                );
            }
        }
    }
    Ok(())
}

// This parser deliberately does not discover a unit, start a service, or obtain
// a controller session. Authorization alone cannot bypass the implementation gate.
fn disabled_empty_child_request(args: &[String]) -> Result<(), &'static str> {
    if args.len() != 3 || args[0] != "--authorize-physical-mutation" || args[1] != "--unit" {
        return Err(
            "usage: empty-child-transaction --authorize-physical-mutation --unit UNIT.service",
        );
    }
    let unit = &args[2];
    if !unit.starts_with("xynoos-018d-")
        || !unit.ends_with(".service")
        || !unit
            .bytes()
            .all(|c| c.is_ascii_alphanumeric() || c == b'-' || c == b'.')
    {
        return Err("invalid transient unit name");
    }
    Err("physical empty-child transaction disabled: safety gates remain incomplete")
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::{
        disabled_empty_child_request, require_available_memory, stop_and_reap, validate_mode,
        validate_parent_state, MIN_AVAILABLE_BYTES,
    };
    use std::process::{Command, Stdio};
    #[test]
    fn stop_reaps_child_and_is_idempotent() {
        let mut child = Command::new("sleep")
            .arg("30")
            .stdin(Stdio::null())
            .spawn()
            .unwrap();
        stop_and_reap(&mut child).unwrap();
        stop_and_reap(&mut child).unwrap();
        assert!(child.try_wait().unwrap().is_some());
    }
    #[test]
    fn parent_preflight_refuses_unsafe_state() {
        assert!(validate_parent_state("", "cpu memory pids", "cpu pids").is_ok());
        assert!(validate_parent_state("123", "memory", "").is_err());
        assert!(validate_parent_state("", "cpu pids", "").is_err());
        assert!(validate_parent_state("", "memory", "io").is_err());
    }
    #[test]
    fn memory_guard_rejects_missing_and_low() {
        assert!(require_available_memory(None).is_err());
        assert!(require_available_memory(Some(MIN_AVAILABLE_BYTES - 1)).is_err());
        assert_eq!(
            require_available_memory(Some(MIN_AVAILABLE_BYTES)),
            Ok(MIN_AVAILABLE_BYTES)
        );
    }
    #[test]
    fn modes_fail_closed() {
        assert!(validate_mode("observe").is_ok());
        assert!(validate_mode("dry-run").is_ok());
        assert!(validate_mode("controlled").is_err());
        assert!(validate_mode("unknown").is_err());
    }
    #[test]
    fn physical_request_requires_separate_authorization_and_stays_disabled() {
        let args = |items: &[&str]| items.iter().map(|s| (*s).to_owned()).collect::<Vec<_>>();
        for input in [
            args(&[]),
            args(&["--unit", "xynoos-018d-1.service"]),
            args(&["--authorize", "--unit", "xynoos-018d-1.service"]),
            args(&["--authorize-physical-mutation", "--unit", "other.service"]),
            args(&[
                "--authorize-physical-mutation",
                "--unit",
                "xynoos-018d-../x.service",
            ]),
            args(&[
                "--authorize-physical-mutation",
                "--unit",
                "xynoos-018d-1.service",
                "extra",
            ]),
        ] {
            assert!(disabled_empty_child_request(&input).is_err());
        }
        let error = disabled_empty_child_request(&args(&[
            "--authorize-physical-mutation",
            "--unit",
            "xynoos-018d-1.service",
        ]))
        .unwrap_err();
        assert!(error.contains("disabled"));
    }
}


FILE crates/xynoos-decision/examples/validation.rs
use std::time::{Duration, Instant};
use xynoos_decision::Engine;
use xynoos_pressureiq::{system::collect_system, Error};
fn main() {
    let s = collect_system();
    let mut engine = Engine::default();
    println!(
        "stale: {}",
        serde_json::to_string(&engine.evaluate(&s, s.monotonic + Duration::from_secs(6), None))
            .unwrap()
    );
    let mut partial = collect_system();
    partial.io_psi = Err(Error::Permission);
    println!(
        "injected partial: {}",
        serde_json::to_string(&engine.evaluate(&partial, Instant::now(), None)).unwrap()
    );
    let mut missing = collect_system();
    missing.memory.mem.result = Err(Error::Missing("injected meminfo".into()));
    println!(
        "injected missing: {}",
        serde_json::to_string(&engine.evaluate(&missing, Instant::now(), None)).unwrap()
    );
    if let Ok(cg) = &s.cgroup {
        let audit = xynoos_linux_control::prepare(
            &s,
            Instant::now(),
            &cg.path,
            xynoos_linux_control::Action::MemoryLow { bytes: 4096 },
        );
        println!(
            "real adapter inspection: {}",
            serde_json::to_string(&audit).unwrap()
        );
    }
    let start = Instant::now();
    for _ in 0..1000 {
        let s = collect_system();
        let target = s.cgroup.as_ref().ok().map(|c| c.path.as_path());
        std::hint::black_box(engine.evaluate(&s, Instant::now(), target));
    }
    println!(
        "complete collect + contract + decision, 1000 iterations: mean {} ns",
        start.elapsed().as_nanos() / 1000
    );
}


FILE crates/xynoos-decision/examples/workload_validation.rs
//! Bounded read-only Linux experiment. Run with cargo run --release -p xynoos-decision --example workload_validation.
use std::{
    fs::{self, OpenOptions},
    io::Write,
    process::{Child, Command, Stdio},
    time::{Duration, Instant},
};
use xynoos_pressureiq::{cgroup_pressure, system, workload};
struct Guard(Child);
impl Drop for Guard {
    fn drop(&mut self) {
        let _ = self.0.kill();
        let _ = self.0.wait();
    }
}
fn worker(kind: &str) -> Result<(), Box<dyn std::error::Error>> {
    let start = Instant::now();
    let mut data = vec![
        0u8;
        if kind == "memory" {
            32 * 1024 * 1024
        } else {
            4096
        }
    ];
    let path = std::env::temp_dir().join(format!("xynoos-017-{}", std::process::id()));
    let mut file = if kind == "io" {
        let f = OpenOptions::new()
            .write(true)
            .create_new(true)
            .open(&path)?;
        fs::remove_file(&path)?;
        Some(f)
    } else {
        None
    };
    let mut n = 1u64;
    let mut written = 0usize;
    while start.elapsed() < Duration::from_millis(1200) {
        match kind {
            "cpu" => {
                for _ in 0..100_000 {
                    n = n.wrapping_mul(1664525).wrapping_add(1013904223);
                }
                std::hint::black_box(n);
            }
            "memory" => {
                for v in data.iter_mut().step_by(4096) {
                    *v = v.wrapping_add(1);
                }
                std::hint::black_box(&data);
            }
            "io" => {
                if written < 8 * 1024 * 1024 {
                    file.as_mut().unwrap().write_all(&data)?;
                    written += data.len();
                } else {
                    std::thread::sleep(Duration::from_millis(10));
                }
            }
            "idle" => std::thread::sleep(Duration::from_millis(10)),
            _ => return Err("unknown workload".into()),
        }
    }
    Ok(())
}
fn main() -> Result<(), Box<dyn std::error::Error>> {
    let args: Vec<_> = std::env::args().collect();
    if args.len() == 3 && args[1] == "worker" {
        return worker(&args[2]);
    }
    for kind in ["idle", "cpu", "memory", "io"] {
        for repeat in 0..3 {
            for sampled in if repeat % 2 == 0 {
                [false, true]
            } else {
                [true, false]
            } {
                let start = Instant::now();
                let mut child = Guard(
                    Command::new(std::env::current_exe()?)
                        .args(["worker", kind])
                        .stdout(Stdio::null())
                        .spawn()?,
                );
                let id = workload::identify(child.0.id())?;
                let mut first = None;
                let mut last = None;
                let mut engine = xynoos_decision::Engine::default();
                let mut scoped_engine = xynoos_decision::WorkloadEngine::default();
                let mut decisions = Vec::new();
                let mut collection_ns = 0u128;
                let mut next_sample = Instant::now();
                loop {
                    if let Some(status) = child.0.try_wait()? {
                        if !status.success() {
                            return Err(format!("worker failed: {status}").into());
                        }
                        break;
                    }
                    if sampled && Instant::now() >= next_sample {
                        next_sample = Instant::now() + Duration::from_millis(200);
                        let t = Instant::now();
                        let p = workload::collect(&id);
                        let cg = cgroup_pressure::collect(&id);
                        let scoped = cg.as_ref().map(|c| {
                            scoped_engine.evaluate(
                                c,
                                &xynoos_decision::WorkloadScope::Shared,
                                Instant::now(),
                            )
                        });
                        let s = system::collect_system();
                        let d = engine.evaluate(&s, Instant::now(), None);
                        collection_ns += t.elapsed().as_nanos();
                        decisions.push(format!(
                            "system={:?}:{:?} cgroup={:?}",
                            d.kind, d.pressure_percent, scoped
                        ));
                        if first.is_none() {
                            first = Some(p.clone());
                        }
                        last = Some(p);
                    }
                    if let Some(status) = child.0.try_wait()? {
                        if !status.success() {
                            return Err(format!("worker failed: {status}").into());
                        }
                        break;
                    }
                    if start.elapsed() > Duration::from_secs(5) {
                        return Err("worker timeout".into());
                    }
                    std::thread::sleep(Duration::from_millis(10));
                }
                let delta = first
                    .as_ref()
                    .zip(last.as_ref())
                    .map(|(a, b)| workload::delta(a, b));
                println!("kind={kind} repeat={repeat} sampled={sampled} wall_ms={} collection_us={} delta={delta:?} rss={:?} decisions={decisions:?}", start.elapsed().as_millis(), collection_ns/1000, last.as_ref().map(|s| &s.memory_bytes.result));
            }
        }
    }
    Ok(())
}


FILE crates/xynoos-decision/src/lib.rs
//! Read-only policy for sequential, visible-namespace PressureIQ observations.
#![forbid(unsafe_code)]
use serde::Serialize;
use std::{
    collections::VecDeque,
    path::Path,
    time::{Duration, Instant},
};
use xynoos_linux_control::Audit;
use xynoos_pressureiq::system::{
    contract::{self, Contract, Status},
    SystemSnapshot,
};

const MAX_AGE: Duration = Duration::from_secs(5);
const HISTORY: usize = 8;
const ENTER: f64 = 10.0;
const EXIT: f64 = 5.0;
const COOLDOWN: Duration = Duration::from_secs(10);

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize)]
#[serde(rename_all = "snake_case")]
pub enum Kind {
    Observe,
    Recommend,
    Decline,
    RequestAction,
}
#[derive(Debug, Serialize)]
pub struct Decision {
    pub kind: Kind,
    /// Hysteresis state for CPU, memory and I/O, respectively. No write authority.
    pub active_pressure: [bool; 3],
    pub explanation: String,
    pub pressure_percent: Option<[f64; 3]>,
    pub cgroup_memory_bytes: Option<u64>,
    pub cgroup_cpu_usage_us: Option<u64>,
    pub cgroup_io_bytes: Option<serde_json::Value>,
    pub cgroup_cpu_delta_us: Option<u64>,
    pub cgroup_io_delta_bytes: Option<[u64; 2]>,
    pub adapter: Option<Audit>,
}
#[derive(Default)]
pub struct Engine {
    history: VecDeque<(Instant, [f64; 3])>,
    active: [bool; 3],
    last_change: Option<Instant>,
    cgroup_previous: Option<(std::path::PathBuf, u64, [u64; 2])>,
}
impl Engine {
    pub fn history_len(&self) -> usize {
        self.history.len()
    }
    /// Rebuilds freshness at invocation. Target is an explicit current-cgroup identity,
    /// never inferred from visible-system pressure. This function does not execute writes.
    pub fn evaluate(
        &mut self,
        s: &SystemSnapshot,
        now: Instant,
        target: Option<&Path>,
    ) -> Decision {
        let c = contract::build(s, None, None, now, MAX_AGE);
        self.evaluate_contract(s, &c, now, target)
    }
    fn evaluate_contract(
        &mut self,
        s: &SystemSnapshot,
        c: &Contract,
        now: Instant,
        target: Option<&Path>,
    ) -> Decision {
        let mut out = Decision {
            kind: Kind::Decline,
            active_pressure: [false; 3],
            explanation: String::new(),
            pressure_percent: None,
            cgroup_memory_bytes: None,
            cgroup_cpu_usage_us: None,
            cgroup_io_bytes: None,
            cgroup_cpu_delta_us: None,
            cgroup_io_delta_bytes: None,
            adapter: None,
        };
        let read = |key: &str,
                    source: &str,
                    scope: &str,
                    unit: &str|
         -> Result<&serde_json::Value, String> {
            let r = c
                .readings
                .get(key)
                .ok_or_else(|| format!("missing {key}"))?;
            if r.status != Status::Available
                || r.error.is_some()
                || r.age_ns > MAX_AGE.as_nanos()
                || r.source != source
                || r.scope != scope
                || r.unit != unit
            {
                return Err(format!("unusable or ambiguous {key}"));
            }
            r.value
                .as_ref()
                .ok_or_else(|| format!("missing value {key}"))
        };
        let result = (|| -> Result<[f64; 3], String> {
            if s.memory.source_root != Path::new("/proc") {
                return Err("invalid memory source root".into());
            }
            if c.schema_version != 1 || !c.sequential || c.freshness_limit_ns > MAX_AGE.as_nanos() {
                return Err("unsupported contract".into());
            }
            if self.history.back().is_some_and(|(t, _)| s.monotonic <= *t)
                || now
                    .checked_duration_since(s.monotonic)
                    .is_none_or(|d| d > MAX_AGE)
            {
                return Err("stale or non-increasing observation".into());
            }
            let mut values = [0.; 3];
            for (i, (key, source)) in [
                ("cpu_psi", "/proc/pressure/cpu"),
                ("memory_psi", "/proc/pressure/memory"),
                ("io_psi", "/proc/pressure/io"),
            ]
            .iter()
            .enumerate()
            {
                let v = read(key, source, "visible_system", "percent avg10")?
                    .as_f64()
                    .ok_or_else(|| format!("invalid {key}"))?;
                if !v.is_finite() || !(0.0..=100.0).contains(&v) {
                    return Err(format!("invalid {key}"));
                }
                values[i] = v;
            }
            let mem = read("meminfo", "/proc/meminfo", "visible_system", "bytes")?;
            let total = mem
                .get("MemTotal")
                .and_then(|v| v.as_u64())
                .filter(|v| *v > 0)
                .ok_or("missing MemTotal")?;
            let available = mem
                .get("MemAvailable")
                .and_then(|v| v.as_u64())
                .ok_or("missing MemAvailable")?;
            if available > total {
                return Err("ambiguous memory availability".into());
            }
            Ok(values)
        })();
        let values = match result {
            Ok(v) => v,
            Err(e) => {
                self.history.clear();
                self.active = [false; 3];
                self.last_change = None;
                self.cgroup_previous = None;
                out.explanation = e;
                return out;
            }
        };
        out.pressure_percent = Some(values);
        // Cgroup context is optional and must retain the source identity of this snapshot.
        if let Ok(cg) = &s.cgroup {
            let p = cg.path.display().to_string();
            out.cgroup_memory_bytes = read(
                "cgroup_memory",
                &format!("{p}/memory.current"),
                "current_cgroup",
                "bytes",
            )
            .ok()
            .and_then(|v| v.as_u64());
            out.cgroup_cpu_usage_us = read(
                "cgroup_cpu",
                &format!("{p}/cpu.stat"),
                "current_cgroup",
                "microseconds",
            )
            .ok()
            .and_then(|v| v.as_u64());
            out.cgroup_io_bytes = read(
                "cgroup_io",
                &format!("{p}/io.stat"),
                "current_cgroup",
                "bytes",
            )
            .ok()
            .cloned();
        }
        if self
            .history
            .back()
            .is_some_and(|(t, _)| s.monotonic.duration_since(*t) > MAX_AGE)
        {
            self.history.clear();
            self.active = [false; 3];
            self.last_change = None;
            self.cgroup_previous = None;
        }
        if let (Ok(cg), Some(cpu), Some(io)) =
            (&s.cgroup, out.cgroup_cpu_usage_us, &out.cgroup_io_bytes)
        {
            if let (Some(r), Some(w)) = (
                io.get("read").and_then(|v| v.as_u64()),
                io.get("write").and_then(|v| v.as_u64()),
            ) {
                if let Some((path, old_cpu, old_io)) = &self.cgroup_previous {
                    if *path == cg.path {
                        out.cgroup_cpu_delta_us = cpu.checked_sub(*old_cpu);
                        out.cgroup_io_delta_bytes = r
                            .checked_sub(old_io[0])
                            .zip(w.checked_sub(old_io[1]))
                            .map(|(a, b)| [a, b]);
                    }
                }
                self.cgroup_previous = Some((cg.path.clone(), cpu, [r, w]));
            }
        } else {
            self.cgroup_previous = None;
        }
        self.history.push_back((s.monotonic, values));
        if self.history.len() > HISTORY {
            self.history.pop_front();
        }
        if self.history.len() < 2 {
            out.kind = Kind::Observe;
            out.explanation = "need two fresh observations".into();
            return out;
        }
        let previous = self.history[self.history.len() - 2].1;
        let cooling = self
            .last_change
            .is_some_and(|t| now.checked_duration_since(t).is_none_or(|d| d < COOLDOWN));
        if !cooling {
            let mut next = self.active;
            for i in 0..3 {
                if !next[i] && values[i] >= ENTER && previous[i] >= ENTER {
                    next[i] = true;
                } else if next[i] && values[i] <= EXIT && previous[i] <= EXIT {
                    next[i] = false;
                }
            }
            if next != self.active {
                self.active = next;
                self.last_change = Some(now);
            }
        }
        let names: Vec<_> = ["cpu", "memory", "io"]
            .into_iter()
            .enumerate()
            .filter_map(|(i, n)| self.active[i].then_some(n))
            .collect();
        out.active_pressure = self.active;
        if names.is_empty() {
            out.kind = Kind::Observe;
            out.explanation = if cooling {
                "cooldown; no active pressure"
            } else {
                "no sustained pressure above entry threshold"
            }
            .into();
            return out;
        }
        out.kind = Kind::Recommend;
        out.explanation = format!(
            "sustained visible-system {} pressure; target impact and benefit unproven{}",
            names.join(", "),
            if cooling { "; cooldown active" } else { "" }
        );
        if target.is_some() {
            out.explanation
                .push_str("; system pressure is not workload evidence and control is disabled");
        }

        out
    }
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;
    use xynoos_pressureiq::{parse_psi, Error};
    fn sample(t: Instant, cpu: f64, mem: f64, io: f64) -> SystemSnapshot {
        let mut s = xynoos_pressureiq::system::collect_system();
        s.monotonic = t;
        let psi = |n| parse_psi(&format!("some avg10={n} avg60=0 avg300=0 total=1")).unwrap();
        s.cpu_psi = Ok(psi(cpu));
        s.memory.psi.result = Ok(psi(mem));
        s.io_psi = Ok(psi(io));
        s.memory.mem.result = Ok(xynoos_pressureiq::parse_meminfo(
            "MemTotal: 100 kB\nMemAvailable: 50 kB\n",
        )
        .unwrap());
        s
    }
    #[test]
    fn boundaries_hysteresis_and_cooldown() {
        let t = Instant::now();
        let mut e = Engine::default();
        let run = |e: &mut Engine, seconds, v| {
            let at = t + Duration::from_secs(seconds);
            e.evaluate(&sample(at, v, 0., 0.), at, None).kind
        };
        assert_eq!(run(&mut e, 0, 10.), Kind::Observe);
        assert_eq!(run(&mut e, 1, 10.), Kind::Recommend);
        assert_eq!(run(&mut e, 2, 5.), Kind::Recommend);
        assert_eq!(run(&mut e, 3, 5.), Kind::Recommend);
        for i in 4..11 {
            assert_eq!(run(&mut e, i, 5.), Kind::Recommend);
        }
        assert_eq!(run(&mut e, 11, 5.), Kind::Observe);
    }
    #[test]
    fn rejects_invalid_and_resets_history() {
        let t = Instant::now();
        let s = sample(t, 0., 0., 0.);
        let mut e = Engine::default();
        assert_eq!(
            e.evaluate(&s, t + Duration::from_secs(6), None).kind,
            Kind::Decline
        );
        let mut c = contract::build(&s, None, None, t, MAX_AGE);
        c.readings.get_mut("cpu_psi").unwrap().scope = "current_cgroup".into();
        assert_eq!(e.evaluate_contract(&s, &c, t, None).kind, Kind::Decline);
        c.readings.remove("io_psi");
        assert_eq!(e.evaluate_contract(&s, &c, t, None).kind, Kind::Decline);
        let mut bad = s.clone();
        bad.io_psi = Err(Error::Permission);
        assert_eq!(e.evaluate(&bad, t, None).kind, Kind::Decline);
        assert_eq!(e.history_len(), 0);
    }
    #[test]
    fn bounded_deterministic_and_no_action_without_capability() {
        let t = Instant::now();
        let mut a = Engine::default();
        let mut b = Engine::default();
        for i in 0..1000 {
            let at = t + Duration::from_millis(i * 100);
            let s = sample(at, 0., 20., 0.);
            let target = s.cgroup.as_ref().ok().map(|c| c.path.as_path());
            let x = a.evaluate(&s, at, target);
            let y = b.evaluate(&s, at, target);
            assert_eq!(x.kind, y.kind);
            assert_ne!(x.kind, Kind::RequestAction);
            assert!(a.history_len() <= HISTORY);
        }
    }
}

/// Caller assertion about ownership, not inferred from process membership. The operator
/// must guarantee exclusive workload ownership of the cgroup and its descendants throughout
/// both observation windows. This API cannot enforce that external guarantee.
#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
pub enum WorkloadScope {
    Shared,
    OperatorAttestedIsolated {
        process: xynoos_pressureiq::workload::Identity,
        cgroup: xynoos_pressureiq::cgroup_pressure::CgroupIdentity,
    },
}
#[derive(Debug, Serialize)]
pub struct WorkloadDecision {
    pub kind: Kind,
    pub scope: &'static str,
    pub explanation: String,
    pub pressure_percent: Option<[f64; 3]>,
}
type PreviousWorkload = (
    xynoos_pressureiq::workload::Identity,
    xynoos_pressureiq::cgroup_pressure::CgroupIdentity,
    [Instant; 3],
    Instant,
    [f64; 3],
);
#[derive(Default)]
pub struct WorkloadEngine {
    previous: Option<PreviousWorkload>,
    active: [bool; 3],
    last_change: Option<Instant>,
}
impl WorkloadEngine {
    /// Only read-only recommendations are emitted. External isolation is an explicit trust boundary.
    pub fn evaluate(
        &mut self,
        s: &xynoos_pressureiq::cgroup_pressure::Snapshot,
        scope: &WorkloadScope,
        now: Instant,
    ) -> WorkloadDecision {
        let mut out = WorkloadDecision {
            kind: Kind::Decline,
            scope: "cgroup",
            explanation: String::new(),
            pressure_percent: None,
        };
        let result = (|| -> Result<[f64; 3], &'static str> {
            if let WorkloadScope::OperatorAttestedIsolated { process, cgroup } = scope {
                if *process != s.process || *cgroup != s.cgroup {
                    return Err("workload or cgroup identity mismatch");
                }
                if [s.cpu.at, s.memory.at, s.io.at]
                    .into_iter()
                    .any(|at| s.isolation_check.at < at)
                {
                    return Err("isolation check predates PSI readings");
                }
                if now
                    .checked_duration_since(s.isolation_check.at)
                    .is_none_or(|d| d > MAX_AGE)
                    || s.isolation_check.result.is_err()
                {
                    return Err("isolation checks unavailable, failed or stale");
                }
            }
            let mut values = [0.; 3];
            for (i, (reading, filename)) in [
                (&s.cpu, "cpu.pressure"),
                (&s.memory, "memory.pressure"),
                (&s.io, "io.pressure"),
            ]
            .iter()
            .enumerate()
            {
                if reading.path != s.cgroup.path.join(filename) {
                    return Err("cgroup PSI source mismatch");
                }
                if now
                    .checked_duration_since(reading.at)
                    .is_none_or(|d| d > MAX_AGE)
                {
                    return Err("stale or future cgroup PSI");
                }
                let psi = reading
                    .result
                    .as_ref()
                    .map_err(|_| "cgroup PSI unavailable or denied")?;
                let v = psi.some.avg10;
                if !v.is_finite() || !(0.0..=100.0).contains(&v) {
                    return Err("invalid cgroup PSI");
                }
                values[i] = v;
            }
            Ok(values)
        })();
        let values = match result {
            Ok(v) => v,
            Err(e) => {
                *self = Self::default();
                out.explanation = e.into();
                return out;
            }
        };
        out.pressure_percent = Some(values);
        if matches!(scope, WorkloadScope::Shared) {
            *self = Self::default();
            out.kind = Kind::Observe;
            out.scope = "shared_cgroup";
            out.explanation = "shared cgroup pressure cannot be attributed to a workload".into();
            return out;
        }
        out.scope = "operator_attested_isolated_cgroup";
        let at = [s.cpu.at, s.memory.at, s.io.at];
        if let Some((p, c, t, isolation_at, _)) = &self.previous {
            if *p != s.process
                || *c != s.cgroup
                || s.isolation_check.at <= *isolation_at
                || (0..3).any(|i| at[i] <= t[i])
            {
                *self = Self::default();
                out.explanation = "changed identity or non-increasing observation".into();
                return out;
            }
            if (0..3).any(|i| at[i].duration_since(t[i]) > MAX_AGE) {
                *self = Self::default();
            }
        }
        let previous = self.previous.replace((
            s.process.clone(),
            s.cgroup.clone(),
            at,
            s.isolation_check.at,
            values,
        ));
        let Some((_, _, _, _, old)) = previous else {
            out.kind = Kind::Observe;
            out.explanation =
                "need two fresh operator-attested isolated cgroup observations".into();
            return out;
        };
        let cooling = self
            .last_change
            .is_some_and(|t| now.checked_duration_since(t).is_none_or(|d| d < COOLDOWN));
        if !cooling {
            let mut next = self.active;
            for i in 0..3 {
                if !next[i] && values[i] >= ENTER && old[i] >= ENTER {
                    next[i] = true;
                } else if next[i] && values[i] <= EXIT && old[i] <= EXIT {
                    next[i] = false;
                }
            }
            if next != self.active {
                self.active = next;
                self.last_change = Some(now);
            }
        }
        let names: Vec<_> = ["cpu", "memory", "io"]
            .into_iter()
            .enumerate()
            .filter_map(|(i, n)| self.active[i].then_some(n))
            .collect();
        if names.is_empty() {
            out.kind = Kind::Observe;
            out.explanation =
                "no sustained operator-attested isolated cgroup pressure above threshold".into();
        } else {
            out.kind = Kind::Recommend;
            out.explanation = format!("sustained operator-attested isolated cgroup {} pressure; benefit unproven, isolation depends on operator guarantee", names.join(", "));
        }
        out
    }
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod scoped_tests {
    use super::*;
    use xynoos_pressureiq::{
        cgroup_pressure::{CgroupIdentity, PressureSource, Snapshot},
        parse_psi,
        workload::Identity,
        Error,
    };
    fn fixture(at: Instant) -> Snapshot {
        let cg = CgroupIdentity {
            path: "/sys/fs/cgroup/test".into(),
            device: 1,
            inode: 2,
        };
        let source = |name| PressureSource {
            path: cg.path.join(name),
            at,
            result: Ok(parse_psi("some avg10=20 avg60=0 avg300=0 total=50").unwrap()),
        };
        Snapshot {
            process: Identity {
                pid: 123,
                start_ticks: 45,
            },
            cpu: source("cpu.pressure"),
            memory: source("memory.pressure"),
            io: source("io.pressure"),
            cgroup: cg,
            isolation_check: xynoos_pressureiq::workload::Reading { at, result: Ok(()) },
        }
    }
    fn isolated(s: &Snapshot) -> WorkloadScope {
        WorkloadScope::OperatorAttestedIsolated {
            process: s.process.clone(),
            cgroup: s.cgroup.clone(),
        }
    }
    #[test]
    fn shared_is_never_workload_recommendation_and_isolated_requires_two() {
        let t = Instant::now();
        let a = fixture(t);
        let mut b = fixture(t + Duration::from_secs(1));
        let mut e = WorkloadEngine::default();
        assert_eq!(
            e.evaluate(&a, &WorkloadScope::Shared, t).kind,
            Kind::Observe
        );
        assert_eq!(
            e.evaluate(&b, &WorkloadScope::Shared, b.cpu.at).kind,
            Kind::Observe
        );
        assert_eq!(e.evaluate(&a, &isolated(&a), t).kind, Kind::Observe);
        assert_eq!(
            e.evaluate(&b, &isolated(&b), b.cpu.at).kind,
            Kind::Recommend
        );
        b.cgroup.inode += 1;
        assert_eq!(e.evaluate(&b, &isolated(&b), b.cpu.at).kind, Kind::Decline);
        assert_eq!(e.evaluate(&b, &isolated(&a), b.cpu.at).kind, Kind::Decline);
    }
    #[test]
    fn refusal_on_stale_missing_denied_and_wrong_source() {
        let t = Instant::now();
        let a = fixture(t);
        let mut e = WorkloadEngine::default();
        assert_eq!(
            e.evaluate(&a, &isolated(&a), t + Duration::from_secs(6))
                .kind,
            Kind::Decline
        );
        let mut b = a.clone();
        b.isolation_check.result = Err(Error::Malformed("shared".into()));
        assert_eq!(e.evaluate(&b, &isolated(&b), t).kind, Kind::Decline);
        b.isolation_check.result = Ok(());
        b.cpu.result = Err(Error::Permission);
        assert_eq!(e.evaluate(&b, &isolated(&b), t).kind, Kind::Decline);
        b.cpu = a.cpu.clone();
        b.io.result = Err(Error::Unsupported);
        assert_eq!(e.evaluate(&b, &isolated(&b), t).kind, Kind::Decline);
        b.io = a.io.clone();
        b.memory.path = "/proc/pressure/memory".into();
        assert_eq!(e.evaluate(&b, &isolated(&b), t).kind, Kind::Decline);
        let mut b = fixture(t + Duration::from_secs(1));
        b.isolation_check.at = t;
        assert_eq!(e.evaluate(&b, &isolated(&b), b.cpu.at).kind, Kind::Decline);
        b.isolation_check.at = b.cpu.at;
        b.io.at = t;
        assert_eq!(e.evaluate(&a, &isolated(&a), t).kind, Kind::Observe);
        assert_eq!(e.evaluate(&b, &isolated(&b), b.cpu.at).kind, Kind::Decline);
    }
}


FILE crates/xynoos-engine/src/bin/xynoos-desktop.rs
//! Disposable source search / word-frequency indexing worker. No process targeting.
#![forbid(unsafe_code)]
use nix::sys::resource::{getrlimit, setrlimit, Resource};
use serde_json::json;
use std::{
    collections::BTreeMap,
    fs::File,
    io::{self, BufRead, Read, Write},
    time::{Duration, Instant},
};

const MAX_CORPUS: u64 = 4 * 1024 * 1024;
const CHUNK: usize = 32768;
const TERMS: [&str; 4] = ["memory", "pressure", "Result", "cgroup"];

fn limits() -> Result<(), Box<dyn std::error::Error>> {
    for (resource, maximum) in [
        (Resource::RLIMIT_AS, 192 * 1024 * 1024),
        (Resource::RLIMIT_CPU, 20),
        (Resource::RLIMIT_CORE, 0),
    ] {
        let (_, hard) = getrlimit(resource)?;
        let value = hard.min(maximum);
        setrlimit(resource, value, value)?;
        if getrlimit(resource)? != (value, value) {
            return Err("limit read-back mismatch".into());
        }
    }
    std::thread::spawn(|| {
        std::thread::sleep(Duration::from_secs(60));
        std::process::exit(124);
    });
    Ok(())
}

fn main() -> Result<(), Box<dyn std::error::Error>> {
    let args: Vec<_> = std::env::args().skip(1).collect();
    if args.len() != 3
        || !matches!(args[0].as_str(), "search" | "index")
        || args[1] != "--corpus-fd"
    {
        return Err("usage: xynoos-desktop search|index --corpus-fd FD".into());
    }
    let fd: u32 = args[2].parse()?;
    if fd < 3 {
        return Err("corpus must be a separate inherited regular file".into());
    }
    limits()?;
    let file = File::open(format!("/proc/self/fd/{fd}"))?;
    if !file.metadata()?.is_file() || file.metadata()?.len() > MAX_CORPUS {
        return Err("corpus must be regular and at most 4 MiB".into());
    }
    let mut bytes = Vec::new();
    file.take(MAX_CORPUS + 1).read_to_end(&mut bytes)?;
    if bytes.is_empty() || bytes.len() as u64 > MAX_CORPUS || !bytes.is_ascii() {
        return Err("corpus must be nonempty bounded ASCII source".into());
    }
    let corpus = String::from_utf8(bytes)?;
    if corpus
        .split(|c: char| !c.is_ascii_alphanumeric())
        .any(|word| word.len() > 4096)
    {
        return Err("source token exceeds bounded chunk extension".into());
    }
    let identity = crc32fast::hash(corpus.as_bytes());
    let stdin = io::stdin();
    let mut input = stdin.lock();
    let mut output = io::BufWriter::new(io::stdout().lock());
    writeln!(
        output,
        "{}",
        json!({"type":"ready", "role":args[0], "corpus_crc32":identity,
        "bytes":corpus.len(), "pid":std::process::id(), "kernel_mutations":0,
        "limits":{"address_space_bytes":getrlimit(Resource::RLIMIT_AS)?.1,
        "cpu_seconds":getrlimit(Resource::RLIMIT_CPU)?.1,"wall_seconds":60}})
    )?;
    output.flush()?;
    let mut offset = 0;
    let mut pass = 0;
    let mut searches = 0;
    let mut index: BTreeMap<String, u64> = BTreeMap::new();
    // Corpus size bounds chunks; command count independently bounds repeated work.
    for sequence in 0..16384 {
        let mut line = String::new();
        let n = std::io::Read::take(&mut input, 32).read_line(&mut line)?;
        if n == 0 || line == "finish\n" || line == "cancel\n" {
            drop(index);
            drop(corpus);
            writeln!(
                output,
                "{}",
                json!({"type":"final","searches":searches,"passes":pass,
                "partial_index_bytes":offset,"released":true,"cancelled":line != "finish\n"})
            )?;
            output.flush()?;
            return Ok(());
        }
        if line != "work\n" {
            return Err("invalid bounded command".into());
        }
        let started = Instant::now();
        let result = if args[0] == "search" {
            if searches >= 1024 {
                return Err("search request bound".into());
            }
            let term = TERMS[searches % TERMS.len()];
            let matches = corpus.lines().filter(|line| line.contains(term)).count();
            searches += 1;
            json!({"term":term,"matching_lines":matches,"searches":searches})
        } else {
            if pass >= 16 {
                return Err("index pass bound".into());
            }
            // Extend to a token boundary so chunked and whole-corpus indexing agree.
            let mut end = (offset + CHUNK).min(corpus.len());
            while end < corpus.len() && corpus.as_bytes()[end].is_ascii_alphanumeric() {
                end += 1;
            }
            for word in corpus[offset..end].split(|c: char| !c.is_ascii_alphanumeric()) {
                if !word.is_empty() {
                    *index.entry(word.to_ascii_lowercase()).or_default() += 1;
                }
            }
            offset = end;
            let done = offset == corpus.len();
            let mut digest = None;
            let mut words = None;
            if done {
                let mut hash = crc32fast::Hasher::new();
                for (word, count) in &index {
                    hash.update(word.as_bytes());
                    hash.update(&[0]);
                    hash.update(&count.to_le_bytes());
                }
                digest = Some(hash.finalize());
                words = Some(index.len());
                index.clear();
                offset = 0;
                pass += 1;
            }
            json!({"pass_complete":done,"passes":pass,"offset":offset,
                "index_crc32":digest,"unique_words":words})
        };
        writeln!(
            output,
            "{}",
            json!({"type":"result","sequence":sequence,
            "role":args[0],"service_ns":started.elapsed().as_nanos(),"result":result})
        )?;
        output.flush()?;
    }
    Err("command bound exceeded".into())
}


FILE crates/xynoos-engine/src/bin/xynoos-owned.rs
//! Independently bounded experimental worker. No system configuration or process targeting.
#![forbid(unsafe_code)]
use nix::sys::resource::{getrlimit, setrlimit, Resource};
use serde_json::json;
use std::{
    io::{self, BufRead, Read, Write},
    time::{Duration, Instant},
};
use xynoos_engine::owned::{Corpus, CorpusKind, MAX_REQUESTS};

fn limits() -> Result<(), Box<dyn std::error::Error>> {
    for (resource, maximum) in [
        (Resource::RLIMIT_AS, 128 * 1024 * 1024),
        (Resource::RLIMIT_CPU, 8),
        (Resource::RLIMIT_CORE, 0),
    ] {
        let (_, hard) = getrlimit(resource)?;
        let value = hard.min(maximum);
        setrlimit(resource, value, value)?;
        if getrlimit(resource)? != (value, value) {
            return Err("limit read-back mismatch".into());
        }
    }
    // Independent of the harness and stdin progress, including if its parent dies.
    std::thread::spawn(|| {
        std::thread::sleep(Duration::from_secs(25));
        std::process::exit(124);
    });
    Ok(())
}
fn self_memory() -> Result<serde_json::Value, Box<dyn std::error::Error>> {
    let mut text = String::new();
    std::fs::File::open("/proc/self/status")?
        .take(16384)
        .read_to_string(&mut text)?;
    let kib = |key: &str| {
        text.lines().find_map(|line| {
            line.strip_prefix(key)?
                .split_whitespace()
                .next()?
                .parse::<u64>()
                .ok()
        })
    };
    Ok(json!({"rss_kib":kib("VmRSS:"),"hwm_kib":kib("VmHWM:")}))
}
fn main() -> Result<(), Box<dyn std::error::Error>> {
    let args: Vec<_> = std::env::args().skip(1).collect();
    let (arm, kind) = match args.iter().map(String::as_str).collect::<Vec<_>>().as_slice() {
        [arm @ ("baseline" | "observe"), "mixed"] => (*arm, CorpusKind::Mixed),
        ["active", "mixed", "--authorize-owned"] => ("active", CorpusKind::Mixed),
        [arm @ ("baseline" | "observe"), "entropy"] => (*arm, CorpusKind::Entropy),
        ["active", "entropy", "--authorize-owned"] => ("active", CorpusKind::Entropy),
        _ => return Err("usage: xynoos-owned baseline|observe mixed|entropy OR active mixed|entropy --authorize-owned".into()),
    };
    limits()?;
    let mut corpus = Corpus::new(kind);
    let stdin = io::stdin();
    let mut input = stdin.lock();
    let mut output = io::BufWriter::new(io::stdout().lock());
    writeln!(
        output,
        "{}",
        json!({"type":"ready", "workload":"owned_lossless_16mib_v1",
        "arm":arm, "corpus":kind, "identity":corpus.identity(), "retained_bytes":corpus.retained(),
        "limits":{"address_space_bytes":getrlimit(Resource::RLIMIT_AS)?.1,
            "cpu_seconds":getrlimit(Resource::RLIMIT_CPU)?.1,"wall_seconds":25,"requests":MAX_REQUESTS},
        "kernel_mutations":0})
    )?;
    output.flush()?;
    let mut line = String::with_capacity(16);
    for _ in 0..=MAX_REQUESTS {
        line.clear();
        let n = std::io::Read::take(&mut input, 16).read_line(&mut line)?;
        if n == 0 || line == "finish\n" || line == "cancel\n" {
            let retained = corpus.retained();
            let t = Instant::now();
            corpus.restore(line != "finish\n")?;
            writeln!(
                output,
                "{}",
                json!({"type":"final", "restored":true, "verified":true,
                "cancelled":line != "finish\n", "retained_before_restore":retained,
                "retained_after_restore":corpus.retained(), "restore_ns":t.elapsed().as_nanos(),
                "self_memory_after_restore":self_memory()?})
            )?;
            output.flush()?;
            return Ok(());
        }
        if line != "work\n" {
            return Err("invalid bounded command".into());
        }
        let t = Instant::now();
        let policy = if arm == "baseline" {
            None
        } else {
            let snapshot = xynoos_pressureiq::system::collect_system();
            Some(corpus.evaluate(&snapshot, Instant::now(), arm == "active")?)
        };
        let service = Instant::now();
        let checksum = corpus.work()?;
        writeln!(
            output,
            "{}",
            json!({"type":"response","verified":true, "checksum":checksum,
            "service_ns":service.elapsed().as_nanos(),"elapsed_ns":t.elapsed().as_nanos(),
            "retained_bytes":corpus.retained(),"policy":policy,"kernel_mutations":0})
        )?;
        output.flush()?;
    }
    Err("request limit".into())
}


FILE crates/xynoos-engine/src/bin/xynoos-perf.rs
//! Fixed owned workload and component profiler. No control interfaces or shell execution.
#![forbid(unsafe_code)]
use serde_json::json;
use std::{
    hint::black_box,
    io::{self, BufRead, Write},
    time::{Duration, Instant},
};
use xynoos_engine::{Engine, Mode};

fn timed(mut f: impl FnMut(), count: usize) -> Vec<u128> {
    (0..count)
        .map(|_| {
            let t = Instant::now();
            f();
            t.elapsed().as_nanos()
        })
        .collect()
}
fn profile() -> Result<(), Box<dyn std::error::Error>> {
    let collection = timed(
        || {
            black_box(xynoos_pressureiq::system::collect_system());
        },
        24,
    );
    let trace = xynoos_engine::replay::default_trace();
    let (mut snapshot, now) = trace.steps[0].snapshot(Instant::now())?;
    let mut engine = Engine::default();
    let mut tick = now;
    let state = timed(
        || {
            tick += Duration::from_millis(100);
            snapshot.monotonic = tick;
            snapshot.memory.monotonic = tick;
            black_box(engine.evaluate(&snapshot, tick, Mode::Observe, false, false));
        },
        4000,
    );
    use xynoos_aura::{Baseline, DecisionPolicy};
    let aura = timed(
        || {
            black_box(Baseline.decide(
                black_box(xynoos_core::MemoryPressure::High),
                black_box(xynoos_core::CostEstimate {
                    latency_ns: 0,
                    bytes: 4096,
                }),
            ));
        },
        4000,
    );
    let page = vec![17u8; 4096];
    let hypermemory = timed(
        || {
            let mut store = xynoos_hypermemory::PageStore::adaptive(4096, 64, 512 * 1024).unwrap();
            let ids: Vec<_> = (0..64)
                .map(|_| store.insert(black_box(&page)).unwrap())
                .collect();
            for id in ids {
                assert_eq!(store.get(id).unwrap(), page);
            }
            black_box(store.stats());
        },
        100,
    );
    let live = xynoos_pressureiq::system::collect_system();
    let plan = engine.evaluate(&live, Instant::now(), Mode::Observe, false, false);
    let contract = xynoos_pressureiq::system::contract::build(
        &live,
        None,
        None,
        Instant::now(),
        Duration::from_secs(5),
    );
    let contract_build = timed(
        || {
            black_box(xynoos_pressureiq::system::contract::build(
                &live,
                None,
                None,
                Instant::now(),
                Duration::from_secs(5),
            ));
        },
        400,
    );
    let report = timed(
        || {
            let mut bytes = Vec::new();
            xynoos_engine::observation::write(&mut bytes, &plan, 100, &contract).unwrap();
            black_box(bytes);
        },
        1000,
    );
    println!(
        "{}",
        json!({"schema_version":1, "evidence":"local_component_timings_not_physical_benefit", "units":"ns", "pressureiq_collection":collection, "state_machine_with_aura":state, "aura_only":aura, "hypermemory_64_page_verified_roundtrip":hypermemory, "contract_build":contract_build, "report_build_serialize":report})
    );
    Ok(())
}
fn workload() -> Result<(), Box<dyn std::error::Error>> {
    // Exactly 2 MiB of primary buffers; no memory pressure proportional to host RAM.
    let data: Vec<u8> = (0..1024 * 1024)
        .map(|i| ((i * 17 + i / 4096) % 251) as u8)
        .collect();
    let mut copy = vec![0; data.len()];
    let expected = crc32fast::hash(&data);
    let stdin = io::stdin();
    let mut input = stdin.lock();
    let stdout = io::stdout();
    let mut output = io::BufWriter::new(stdout.lock());
    writeln!(
        output,
        "{}",
        json!({"ready":true,"workload":"copy_crc_1mib_x16_v1","crc32":expected,"primary_buffer_bytes":2*data.len()})
    )?;
    output.flush()?;
    // A bounded reader rejects oversized input before allocating it in full.
    let mut line = String::with_capacity(8);
    for _ in 0..1001 {
        line.clear();
        let n = std::io::Read::take(&mut input, 16).read_line(&mut line)?;
        if n == 0 {
            return Ok(());
        }
        if line != "work\n" {
            return Err("expected bounded work command".into());
        }
        let t = Instant::now();
        for _ in 0..16 {
            copy.copy_from_slice(black_box(&data));
            if crc32fast::hash(black_box(&copy)) != expected {
                return Err("integrity failure".into());
            }
        }
        let elapsed = t.elapsed().as_nanos();
        writeln!(
            output,
            "{}",
            json!({"elapsed_ns":elapsed,"crc32":expected,"verified":true})
        )?;
        output.flush()?;
    }
    Err("request limit".into())
}
fn main() -> Result<(), Box<dyn std::error::Error>> {
    let args: Vec<_> = std::env::args().skip(1).collect();
    match args.as_slice() {
        [command] if command == "profile" => profile(),
        [command] if command == "workload" => workload(),
        _ => Err("usage: xynoos-perf profile|workload; no active optimization available".into()),
    }
}


FILE crates/xynoos-engine/src/lib.rs
//! Bounded PressureIQ \u2192 decision hysteresis \u2192 AURA \u2192 owned-buffer executor.
//! Live kernel mutation is unconditionally refused, including with authorization.
#![forbid(unsafe_code)]

pub mod observation;
pub mod owned;
pub mod recovery;
pub mod replay;

use serde::Serialize;
use std::time::{Duration, Instant};
use xynoos_aura::{Baseline, DecisionPolicy};
use xynoos_core::{CostEstimate, MemoryPressure, StorageTier};
use xynoos_decision::{Decision, Kind};
use xynoos_pressureiq::system::SystemSnapshot;

pub const MAX_STEPS: usize = 60;
pub const PAGE_SIZE: usize = 4096;
pub const PAGES: usize = 64;
pub const STORE_BUDGET: usize = 512 * 1024;

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize)]
#[serde(rename_all = "snake_case")]
pub enum Mode {
    Baseline,
    Observe,
    Simulate,
    Controlled,
}
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize)]
#[serde(rename_all = "snake_case")]
pub enum Action {
    KeepOwnedRaw,
    CompressOwnedPages,
    BackOff,
}
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize)]
#[serde(rename_all = "snake_case")]
pub enum Reason {
    Baseline,
    NoSustainedMemoryPressure,
    SustainedMemoryPressure,
    CpuOrIoPressure,
    InsufficientAvailableMemory,
    TelemetryUnavailable,
    AuthorizationRequired,
    PhysicalMutationLocked,
    Cancelled,
}
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize)]
#[serde(rename_all = "snake_case")]
pub enum Outcome {
    Observed,
    Simulated,
    Baseline,
    Refused,
    Cancelled,
}
#[derive(Debug, Serialize)]
pub struct Plan {
    pub mode: Mode,
    pub action: Action,
    pub reason: Reason,
    pub outcome: Outcome,
    pub decision: Option<Decision>,
    pub kernel_write_available: bool,
    pub physical_admission: Option<xynoos_linux_control::Decision>,
    pub maximum_store_accounted_bytes: usize,
}
#[derive(Default)]
pub struct Engine {
    pressure: xynoos_decision::Engine,
    previous_swap: Option<(u64, u64)>,
    cancelled: bool,
}
impl Engine {
    /// No caller-supplied plan is accepted by the executor. A plan is diagnostic data.
    /// Simulation authorization permits only this process's own bounded page store.
    pub fn evaluate(
        &mut self,
        snapshot: &SystemSnapshot,
        now: Instant,
        mode: Mode,
        authorized: bool,
        cancelled: bool,
    ) -> Plan {
        let mut p = Plan {
            mode,
            action: Action::KeepOwnedRaw,
            reason: Reason::Baseline,
            outcome: Outcome::Baseline,
            decision: None,
            kernel_write_available: false,
            physical_admission: None,
            maximum_store_accounted_bytes: STORE_BUDGET,
        };
        self.cancelled |= cancelled;
        if self.cancelled {
            p.action = Action::BackOff;
            p.reason = Reason::Cancelled;
            p.outcome = Outcome::Cancelled;
            return p;
        }
        if mode == Mode::Baseline {
            return p;
        }
        let d = self.pressure.evaluate(snapshot, now, None);
        // Swap counters are required diagnostics; absent/reset input never means zero.
        let swap = snapshot.memory.vm.result.as_ref().ok().and_then(|vm| {
            vm.counters
                .get("pswpin")
                .copied()
                .zip(vm.counters.get("pswpout").copied())
        });
        let vm_valid =
            swap.is_some_and(|(i, o)| self.previous_swap.is_none_or(|(pi, po)| i >= pi && o >= po));
        let valid = d.kind != Kind::Decline
            && vm_valid
            && now
                .checked_duration_since(snapshot.memory.monotonic)
                .is_some_and(|age| age <= Duration::from_secs(5));
        p.outcome = Outcome::Observed;
        p.reason = Reason::NoSustainedMemoryPressure;
        if !valid {
            self.pressure = xynoos_decision::Engine::default();
            self.previous_swap = None;
            p.action = Action::BackOff;
            p.reason = Reason::TelemetryUnavailable;
            p.outcome = Outcome::Refused;
        } else if snapshot
            .memory
            .mem
            .result
            .as_ref()
            .ok()
            .and_then(|m| m.bytes.get("MemAvailable"))
            .is_none_or(|n| *n < 200 * 1024 * 1024)
        {
            p.action = Action::BackOff;
            p.reason = Reason::InsufficientAvailableMemory;
        } else if d.active_pressure[0]
            || d.active_pressure[2]
            || d.pressure_percent
                .is_some_and(|v| v[0] >= 10.0 || v[2] >= 10.0)
        {
            p.action = Action::BackOff;
            p.reason = Reason::CpuOrIoPressure;
        } else {
            let pressure = if d.active_pressure[1] {
                if d.pressure_percent.is_some_and(|v| v[1] >= 20.0) {
                    MemoryPressure::High
                } else {
                    MemoryPressure::Moderate
                }
            } else {
                MemoryPressure::Low
            };
            // Existing AURA policy is deliberately simple; no invented cost predictions.
            let tier = Baseline.decide(
                pressure,
                CostEstimate {
                    latency_ns: 0,
                    bytes: PAGE_SIZE,
                },
            );
            if tier == StorageTier::Compressed {
                p.action = Action::CompressOwnedPages;
                p.reason = Reason::SustainedMemoryPressure;
            }
        }
        if valid {
            self.previous_swap = swap;
        }
        p.decision = Some(d);
        if mode == Mode::Controlled {
            // Use the same Linux execution admission boundary as existing callers.
            p.physical_admission = Some(xynoos_linux_control::physical_admission(authorized));
            p.action = Action::BackOff;
            p.outcome = Outcome::Refused;
            p.reason = if authorized {
                Reason::PhysicalMutationLocked
            } else {
                Reason::AuthorizationRequired
            };
        } else if mode == Mode::Simulate && p.outcome != Outcome::Refused {
            if authorized {
                p.outcome = Outcome::Simulated;
            } else {
                p.action = Action::BackOff;
                p.reason = Reason::AuthorizationRequired;
                p.outcome = Outcome::Refused;
            }
        }
        p
    }
}


FILE crates/xynoos-engine/src/observation.rs
//! Serialize the existing observation schema without an intermediate JSON tree.
use crate::Plan;
use serde::Serialize;
use xynoos_pressureiq::system::contract::Contract;

#[derive(Serialize)]
struct Observation<'a> {
    schema_version: u32,
    evidence: &'static str,
    sequential: bool,
    scope: &'static str,
    kernel_mutations: u64,
    owned_buffer_mutations: u64,
    plan: &'a Plan,
    decision_ns: u128,
    telemetry: &'a Contract,
}

/// The borrowed report retains every telemetry error and safety field.
/// Streaming avoids cloning telemetry into `serde_json::Value` and a second String.
pub fn write(
    writer: impl std::io::Write,
    plan: &Plan,
    decision_ns: u128,
    telemetry: &Contract,
) -> serde_json::Result<()> {
    serde_json::to_writer(
        writer,
        &Observation {
            schema_version: 1,
            evidence: "live_read_only",
            sequential: true,
            scope: "visible_namespace",
            kernel_mutations: 0,
            owned_buffer_mutations: 0,
            plan,
            decision_ns,
            telemetry,
        },
    )
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;
    use crate::{Engine, Mode};
    use std::time::{Duration, Instant};
    #[test]
    fn streaming_preserves_schema_and_missing_telemetry() {
        let trace = crate::replay::default_trace();
        for step in &trace.steps {
            let (s, now) = step.snapshot(Instant::now()).unwrap();
            for mode in [Mode::Observe, Mode::Controlled] {
                let plan = Engine::default().evaluate(&s, now, mode, true, false);
                let contract = xynoos_pressureiq::system::contract::build(
                    &s,
                    None,
                    None,
                    now,
                    Duration::from_secs(5),
                );
                let old = serde_json::json!({"schema_version":1,"evidence":"live_read_only", "sequential":true,
                    "scope":"visible_namespace","kernel_mutations":0,"owned_buffer_mutations":0,
                    "plan":plan,"decision_ns":123,"telemetry":contract});
                let mut bytes = Vec::new();
                write(&mut bytes, &plan, 123, &contract).unwrap();
                assert_eq!(
                    serde_json::from_slice::<serde_json::Value>(&bytes).unwrap(),
                    old
                );
            }
        }
    }
}


FILE crates/xynoos-engine/src/owned.rs
//! Experimental cooperative storage. Single owner; no kernel-control executor.
//! Raw blocks are replaced only after exact decode equality. No duplicate corpus is retained.
use crate::{Engine, Mode, Outcome, Reason};
use serde::Serialize;
use std::time::{Duration, Instant};
use xynoos_aura::cooperative::{choose, Choice};
use xynoos_pressureiq::system::SystemSnapshot;

pub const BLOCK: usize = 1024 * 1024;
pub const BLOCKS: usize = 16;
pub const RETENTION_BUDGET: usize = 8 * BLOCK;
pub const MAX_REQUESTS: usize = 256;
pub const MAX_AGE: Duration = Duration::from_secs(1);

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize)]
#[serde(rename_all = "snake_case")]
pub enum CorpusKind {
    Mixed,
    Entropy,
}
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize)]
#[serde(rename_all = "snake_case")]
pub enum Effect {
    Kept,
    Observed,
    RefusedTelemetry,
    RefusedMemory,
    BackOff,
    Compressed,
    PartialFreshnessStop,
    NoBenefit,
    Cancelled,
}
#[derive(Debug, Serialize)]
pub struct Report {
    pub effect: Effect,
    pub aura_choice: &'static str,
    pub retained_before: usize,
    pub retained_after: usize,
    pub sustained_retention: bool,
    pub host_pressure: Option<[bool; 3]>,
    pub host_psi_percent: Option<[f64; 3]>,
    pub host_reason: Option<Reason>,
    pub action_ns: u128,
    pub changed_blocks: usize,
}
#[derive(Default)]
struct Retention {
    previous_high: bool,
    active: bool,
    previous_at: Option<Instant>,
    last_attempt: Option<Instant>,
}
impl Retention {
    fn update(&mut self, bytes: usize, at: Instant) -> bool {
        let continuous = self.previous_at.is_some_and(|p| {
            at.checked_duration_since(p)
                .is_some_and(|d| d > Duration::ZERO && d <= MAX_AGE)
        });
        if !continuous {
            self.previous_high = false;
            self.active = false;
        }
        let high = bytes > RETENTION_BUDGET;
        if continuous && high && self.previous_high {
            self.active = true;
        } else if !high {
            self.active = false;
        }
        self.previous_high = high;
        self.previous_at = Some(at);
        self.active
    }
    fn cooling(&self, now: Instant) -> bool {
        self.last_attempt.is_some_and(|t| {
            now.checked_duration_since(t)
                .is_none_or(|d| d < Duration::from_secs(10))
        })
    }
}
enum BlockData {
    Raw(Box<[u8]>),
    Packed(Box<[u8]>),
}
pub struct Corpus {
    blocks: Vec<BlockData>,
    checksums: Vec<u32>,
    scratch: Vec<u8>,
    kind: CorpusKind,
    engine: Engine,
    retention: Retention,
    cancelled: bool,
    requests: usize,
}
fn generated(index: usize, kind: CorpusKind) -> Vec<u8> {
    let mut seed = 0x12345678u32 ^ (index as u32 + 1);
    (0..BLOCK)
        .map(|i| {
            seed ^= seed << 13;
            seed ^= seed >> 17;
            seed ^= seed << 5;
            if kind == CorpusKind::Mixed && i % 1024 >= 256 {
                let text = b"owned application record: lossless cold corpus; retain all data\n";
                text[i % text.len()]
            } else {
                seed as u8
            }
        })
        .collect()
}
impl Corpus {
    pub fn new(kind: CorpusKind) -> Self {
        let blocks: Vec<_> = (0..BLOCKS)
            .map(|i| BlockData::Raw(generated(i, kind).into_boxed_slice()))
            .collect();
        let checksums = blocks
            .iter()
            .map(|b| match b {
                BlockData::Raw(v) => crc32fast::hash(v),
                _ => unreachable!(),
            })
            .collect();
        Self {
            blocks,
            checksums,
            scratch: vec![0; BLOCK],
            kind,
            engine: Engine::default(),
            retention: Retention::default(),
            cancelled: false,
            requests: 0,
        }
    }
    /// Payload allocation, including the reusable decode/copy scratch. Metadata excluded.
    pub fn retained(&self) -> usize {
        self.scratch.capacity()
            + self
                .blocks
                .iter()
                .map(|b| match b {
                    BlockData::Raw(v) | BlockData::Packed(v) => v.len(),
                })
                .sum::<usize>()
    }
    pub fn identity(&self) -> &[u32] {
        &self.checksums
    }
    fn decode(&mut self, index: usize) -> Result<(), &'static str> {
        match &self.blocks[index] {
            BlockData::Raw(v) => self.scratch.copy_from_slice(v),
            BlockData::Packed(v) => {
                let n = lz4_flex::block::decompress_into(v, &mut self.scratch)
                    .map_err(|_| "decode failure")?;
                if n != BLOCK {
                    return Err("decoded length mismatch");
                }
            }
        }
        if crc32fast::hash(&self.scratch) != self.checksums[index] {
            return Err("checksum mismatch");
        }
        Ok(())
    }
    /// Same four block copies/decodes and CRCs for every arm. All blocks are read cyclically.
    pub fn work(&mut self) -> Result<u32, &'static str> {
        if self.cancelled || self.requests >= MAX_REQUESTS {
            return Err("cancelled or request limit");
        }
        let mut hash = crc32fast::Hasher::new();
        for j in 0..4 {
            let index = (self.requests * 4 + j) % BLOCKS;
            self.decode(index)?;
            hash.update(&self.scratch);
        }
        self.requests += 1;
        Ok(hash.finalize())
    }
    /// Private typed action is selected and admitted here; callers cannot supply executable plans.
    pub fn evaluate(
        &mut self,
        snapshot: &SystemSnapshot,
        now: Instant,
        active_opt_in: bool,
    ) -> Result<Report, &'static str> {
        let before = self.retained();
        let mut out = Report {
            effect: Effect::Observed,
            aura_choice: "not_evaluated",
            retained_before: before,
            retained_after: before,
            sustained_retention: false,
            host_pressure: None,
            host_psi_percent: None,
            host_reason: None,
            action_ns: 0,
            changed_blocks: 0,
        };
        if self.cancelled {
            out.effect = Effect::Cancelled;
            return Ok(out);
        }
        let plan = self
            .engine
            .evaluate(snapshot, now, Mode::Observe, false, false);
        out.host_reason = Some(plan.reason);
        if let Some(d) = &plan.decision {
            out.host_pressure = Some(d.active_pressure);
            out.host_psi_percent = d.pressure_percent;
        }
        if plan.outcome == Outcome::Refused
            || [snapshot.monotonic, snapshot.memory.monotonic]
                .iter()
                .any(|t| now.checked_duration_since(*t).is_none_or(|d| d > MAX_AGE))
        {
            self.retention = Retention {
                last_attempt: self.retention.last_attempt,
                ..Default::default()
            };
            out.effect = Effect::RefusedTelemetry;
            return Ok(out);
        }
        if snapshot
            .memory
            .mem
            .result
            .as_ref()
            .ok()
            .and_then(|m| m.bytes.get("MemAvailable"))
            .is_none_or(|n| *n < 256 * BLOCK as u64)
        {
            self.retention.previous_at = None;
            out.effect = Effect::RefusedMemory;
            return Ok(out);
        }
        out.sustained_retention = self.retention.update(before, snapshot.monotonic);
        let busy = plan.reason == Reason::CpuOrIoPressure;
        let action = choose(out.sustained_retention, busy, self.retention.cooling(now));
        out.aura_choice = match action {
            Choice::Keep => "keep",
            Choice::BackOff => "back_off",
            Choice::Compress => "compress",
        };
        if !active_opt_in {
            return Ok(out);
        }
        out.effect = match action {
            Choice::Keep => Effect::Kept,
            Choice::BackOff => Effect::BackOff,
            Choice::Compress => {
                // Check freshness again at the actual mutation boundary.
                if snapshot.memory.monotonic.elapsed() > MAX_AGE {
                    out.effect = Effect::RefusedTelemetry;
                    return Ok(out);
                }
                self.retention.last_attempt = Some(now);
                let t = Instant::now();
                let mut partial = false;
                for b in &mut self.blocks {
                    if snapshot.memory.monotonic.elapsed() > MAX_AGE {
                        partial = true;
                        break;
                    }
                    let BlockData::Raw(raw) = b else { continue };
                    let packed = lz4_flex::block::compress(raw).into_boxed_slice();
                    // Require >=12.5% saving; bounded expansion never replaces the original.
                    if packed.len() > raw.len() * 7 / 8 {
                        continue;
                    }
                    let n = lz4_flex::block::decompress_into(&packed, &mut self.scratch)
                        .map_err(|_| "compression verification decode")?;
                    if n != BLOCK || self.scratch != **raw {
                        return Err("compression verification equality");
                    }
                    // Exact equality precedes replacement; raw allocation is now freed.
                    *b = BlockData::Packed(packed);
                    out.changed_blocks += 1;
                }
                out.action_ns = t.elapsed().as_nanos();
                if partial {
                    Effect::PartialFreshnessStop
                } else if out.changed_blocks > 0 {
                    Effect::Compressed
                } else {
                    Effect::NoBenefit
                }
            }
        };
        out.retained_after = self.retained();
        Ok(out)
    }
    /// Reverse every completed replacement. Byte equality to independent generation is checked.
    pub fn restore(&mut self, cancelled: bool) -> Result<(), &'static str> {
        self.cancelled |= cancelled;
        for i in 0..BLOCKS {
            self.decode(i)?;
            let expected = generated(i, self.kind);
            if expected != self.scratch {
                return Err("restoration equality failure");
            }
            if matches!(self.blocks[i], BlockData::Packed(_)) {
                self.blocks[i] = BlockData::Raw(self.scratch.clone().into_boxed_slice());
            }
        }
        Ok(())
    }
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;
    fn sample(at: Instant, cpu: f64) -> SystemSnapshot {
        let mut s = crate::replay::default_trace().steps[0]
            .snapshot(at)
            .unwrap()
            .0;
        s.monotonic = at;
        s.memory.monotonic = at;
        s.cpu_psi = Ok(xynoos_pressureiq::parse_psi(&format!(
            "some avg10={cpu} avg60=0 avg300=0 total=1"
        ))
        .unwrap());
        s
    }
    #[test]
    fn state_transitions_and_cooldown() {
        let t = Instant::now();
        let mut s = Retention::default();
        assert!(!s.update(16 * BLOCK, t));
        assert!(s.update(16 * BLOCK, t + Duration::from_millis(1)));
        s.last_attempt = Some(t);
        assert!(s.cooling(t + Duration::from_secs(9)));
        assert!(!s.cooling(t + Duration::from_secs(10)));
        assert!(!s.update(BLOCK, t + Duration::from_millis(2)));
        assert!(!s.update(16 * BLOCK, t + Duration::from_secs(3)));
    }
    #[test]
    fn compression_is_real_equivalent_and_reversible() {
        let mut raw = Corpus::new(CorpusKind::Mixed);
        let mut active = Corpus::new(CorpusKind::Mixed);
        let t = Instant::now();
        assert_eq!(
            active.evaluate(&sample(t, 0.), t, true).unwrap().effect,
            Effect::Kept
        );
        let at = Instant::now();
        let r = active.evaluate(&sample(at, 0.), at, true).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(r.effect, Effect::Compressed);
        assert_eq!(r.changed_blocks, BLOCKS);
        assert!(r.retained_after < r.retained_before / 2);
        for _ in 0..8 {
            assert_eq!(raw.work(), active.work());
        }
        let at = Instant::now();
        assert_eq!(
            active.evaluate(&sample(at, 0.), at, true).unwrap().effect,
            Effect::BackOff
        );
        active.restore(false).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(raw.retained(), active.retained());
        active.restore(true).unwrap();
        assert!(active.work().is_err());
    }
    #[test]
    fn telemetry_authorization_backoff_and_entropy() {
        let mut c = Corpus::new(CorpusKind::Entropy);
        for _ in 0..2 {
            let at = Instant::now();
            assert_eq!(
                c.evaluate(&sample(at, 0.), at, false).unwrap().effect,
                Effect::Observed
            );
        }
        let at = Instant::now();
        let r = c.evaluate(&sample(at, 0.), at, true).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(r.effect, Effect::NoBenefit);
        assert_eq!(r.retained_before, r.retained_after);
        let at = Instant::now();
        let stale = at - Duration::from_secs(2);
        assert_eq!(
            c.evaluate(&sample(stale, 0.), at, true).unwrap().effect,
            Effect::RefusedTelemetry
        );
        let at = Instant::now();
        assert_eq!(
            c.evaluate(&sample(at, 99.), at, true).unwrap().effect,
            Effect::BackOff
        );
        c.restore(false).unwrap();
    }
    #[test]
    fn corruption_and_partial_restoration_failure_are_errors() {
        let mut c = Corpus::new(CorpusKind::Mixed);
        if let BlockData::Raw(v) = &mut c.blocks[3] {
            v[0] ^= 1;
        }
        assert_eq!(c.restore(false), Err("checksum mismatch"));
    }

    #[test]
    fn low_memory_missing_and_reset_telemetry_refuse_without_changes() {
        let mut c = Corpus::new(CorpusKind::Mixed);
        let before = c.retained();
        let at = Instant::now();
        let mut s = sample(at, 0.);
        s.memory.mem.result = Ok(xynoos_pressureiq::parse_meminfo(
            "MemTotal: 4194304 kB\nMemAvailable: 204800 kB\n",
        )
        .unwrap());
        assert_eq!(
            c.evaluate(&s, at, true).unwrap().effect,
            Effect::RefusedMemory
        );
        let at = Instant::now();
        let mut s = sample(at, 0.);
        s.io_psi = Err(xynoos_pressureiq::Error::Permission);
        assert_eq!(
            c.evaluate(&s, at, true).unwrap().effect,
            Effect::RefusedTelemetry
        );
        let at = Instant::now();
        let mut s = sample(at, 0.);
        s.memory
            .vm
            .result
            .as_mut()
            .unwrap()
            .counters
            .insert("pswpin".into(), 10);
        c.evaluate(&s, at, false).unwrap();
        let at = Instant::now();
        assert_eq!(
            c.evaluate(&sample(at, 0.), at, true).unwrap().effect,
            Effect::RefusedTelemetry
        );
        assert_eq!(c.retained(), before);
    }

    #[test]
    fn compressed_corruption_after_partial_reverse_is_not_success() {
        let mut c = Corpus::new(CorpusKind::Mixed);
        for _ in 0..2 {
            let at = Instant::now();
            c.evaluate(&sample(at, 0.), at, true).unwrap();
        }
        assert!(matches!(&c.blocks[0], BlockData::Packed(_)));
        if let BlockData::Packed(v) = &mut c.blocks[3] {
            v.fill(0);
        }
        assert!(c.restore(false).is_err());
        assert!(matches!(&c.blocks[0], BlockData::Raw(_)));
        assert!(matches!(&c.blocks[3], BlockData::Packed(_)));
    }
}


FILE crates/xynoos-engine/src/recovery.rs
//! Durable simulation evidence and a read-only restart diagnostic, never replay.
//! CRC32 detects accidental damage; it is not authentication or ownership evidence.
use crate::replay::Result;
use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};
use std::{
    fs::{File, OpenOptions},
    io::{Read, Write},
    os::unix::fs::OpenOptionsExt,
    path::Path,
};

pub const MAX_BYTES: usize = 256 * 1024;
const MAX_RECORDS: usize = crate::MAX_STEPS * 3 + 1;
const IDENTITY: &str = "simulation-only:owned-pages-v1";
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
#[serde(rename_all = "snake_case")]
pub enum Stage {
    Captured,
    ApplyIntent,
    RestoredVerified,
    RunComplete,
}
#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
#[serde(deny_unknown_fields)]
pub struct Entry {
    pub identity: String,
    pub step: usize,
    pub total_steps: usize,
    pub stage: Stage,
    pub original_bytes: usize,
    pub compress_requested: bool,
}
impl Entry {
    pub(crate) fn owned(
        step: usize,
        total_steps: usize,
        stage: Stage,
        compress_requested: bool,
    ) -> Self {
        Self {
            identity: IDENTITY.into(),
            step,
            total_steps,
            stage,
            original_bytes: crate::PAGES * crate::PAGE_SIZE,
            compress_requested,
        }
    }
}
#[derive(Debug, Serialize, Deserialize)]
#[serde(deny_unknown_fields)]
struct Payload {
    version: u32,
    scope: String,
    sequence: usize,
    previous_crc32: u32,
    entry: Entry,
}
#[derive(Debug, Serialize, Deserialize)]
#[serde(deny_unknown_fields)]
struct Record {
    payload: Payload,
    crc32: u32,
}
/// New regular file only; no overwrites, symlinks, FIFO blocking or deletion on error.
/// Each intent is flushed before private buffer work. Power-loss guarantees depend on
/// the filesystem/storage honoring sync; no physical cgroup intent is ever accepted.
pub struct Journal {
    file: File,
    sequence: usize,
    previous: u32,
    bytes: usize,
    poisoned: bool,
    last: Option<Entry>,
}
impl Journal {
    pub fn create(path: &Path) -> Result<Self> {
        let parent = path
            .parent()
            .filter(|p| !p.as_os_str().is_empty())
            .unwrap_or(Path::new("."));
        let parent = std::fs::canonicalize(parent)?;
        if ["/proc", "/sys", "/dev"]
            .iter()
            .any(|p| parent.starts_with(p))
        {
            return Err("journal must be outside system filesystems".into());
        }
        let name = path.file_name().ok_or("missing journal filename")?;
        let file = OpenOptions::new()
            .write(true)
            .create_new(true)
            .mode(0o600)
            .custom_flags(libc::O_NOFOLLOW | libc::O_NONBLOCK)
            .open(parent.join(name))?;
        if !file.metadata()?.is_file() {
            return Err("journal must be regular".into());
        }
        file.sync_all()?;
        File::open(parent)?.sync_all()?;
        Ok(Self {
            file,
            sequence: 0,
            previous: 0,
            bytes: 0,
            poisoned: false,
            last: None,
        })
    }
    pub fn append(&mut self, entry: Entry) -> Result<()> {
        if self.poisoned || self.sequence >= MAX_RECORDS {
            return Err("journal record bound".into());
        }
        if !ordered(self.last.as_ref(), &entry) {
            return Err("journal stage ordering conflict".into());
        }
        let (line, crc) = encode(self.sequence, self.previous, entry.clone())?;
        if self.bytes + line.len() > MAX_BYTES {
            return Err("journal byte bound".into());
        }
        // Advance only after sync. On error the caller stops and preserves this file.
        self.poisoned = true;
        self.file.write_all(&line)?;
        self.file.sync_all()?;
        self.sequence += 1;
        self.previous = crc;
        self.bytes += line.len();
        self.last = Some(entry);
        self.poisoned = false;
        Ok(())
    }
}
fn ordered(last: Option<&Entry>, e: &Entry) -> bool {
    if e.identity != IDENTITY
        || e.original_bytes != crate::PAGES * crate::PAGE_SIZE
        || !(1..=crate::MAX_STEPS).contains(&e.total_steps)
        || e.step >= e.total_steps
    {
        return false;
    }
    match last {
        None => e.step == 0 && e.stage == Stage::Captured,
        Some(last) if e.total_steps == last.total_steps => match last.stage {
            Stage::RestoredVerified => {
                (e.step == last.step + 1 && e.stage == Stage::Captured)
                    || (e.step == last.step
