                        && e.step + 1 == e.total_steps
                        && e.stage == Stage::RunComplete
                        && e.compress_requested == last.compress_requested)
            }
            Stage::RunComplete => false,
            _ => {
                e.step == last.step
                    && e.compress_requested == last.compress_requested
                    && matches!(
                        (last.stage, e.stage),
                        (Stage::Captured, Stage::ApplyIntent)
                            | (Stage::ApplyIntent, Stage::RestoredVerified)
                    )
            }
        },
        Some(_) => false,
    }
}
fn encode(sequence: usize, previous_crc32: u32, entry: Entry) -> Result<(Vec<u8>, u32)> {
    let payload = Payload {
        version: 1,
        scope: "owned_buffer_simulation".into(),
        sequence,
        previous_crc32,
        entry,
    };
    let crc32 = crc32fast::hash(&serde_json::to_vec(&payload)?);
    let mut bytes = serde_json::to_vec(&Record { payload, crc32 })?;
    bytes.push(b'\n');
    Ok((bytes, crc32))
}
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize)]
#[serde(rename_all = "snake_case")]
pub enum Status {
    SimulationRestored,
    Unresolved,
    Corrupt,
    IdentityMismatch,
}
#[derive(Debug, Serialize)]
pub struct Diagnostic {
    pub schema_version: u32,
    pub status: Status,
    pub valid_prefix_records: usize,
    pub last: Option<Entry>,
    pub physical_mutation_available: bool,
    pub automatic_recovery_available: bool,
    pub manual_intervention_required: bool,
    pub explanation: String,
}
pub fn inspect_file(path: &Path) -> Result<Diagnostic> {
    let file = OpenOptions::new()
        .read(true)
        .custom_flags(libc::O_NOFOLLOW | libc::O_NONBLOCK)
        .open(path)?;
    if !file.metadata()?.is_file() {
        return Err("recovery evidence must be a regular file".into());
    }
    let mut bytes = Vec::new();
    file.take(MAX_BYTES as u64 + 1).read_to_end(&mut bytes)?;
    Ok(inspect(&bytes, None))
}
/// `expected_identity` is useful for fixture/restart comparisons only. It is never
/// fresh kernel evidence and cannot change the unconditional recovery write lock.
pub fn inspect(bytes: &[u8], expected_identity: Option<&str>) -> Diagnostic {
    let mut d = Diagnostic {
        schema_version: 1,
        status: Status::Unresolved,
        valid_prefix_records: 0,
        last: None,
        physical_mutation_available: false,
        automatic_recovery_available: false,
        manual_intervention_required: true,
        explanation:
            "No completed evidence; preserve records and inspect manually. No writes performed."
                .into(),
    };
    let result = (|| -> Result<()> {
        if bytes.len() > MAX_BYTES {
            return Err("journal byte bound".into());
        }
        let mut previous = 0;
        for line in bytes.split_inclusive(|b| *b == b'\n') {
            if d.valid_prefix_records >= MAX_RECORDS || !line.ends_with(b"\n") {
                return Err("truncated or excessive journal; valid prefix retained".into());
            }
            let r: Record = serde_json::from_slice(line)?;
            let p = &r.payload;
            if p.version != 1
                || p.scope != "owned_buffer_simulation"
                || p.sequence != d.valid_prefix_records
                || p.previous_crc32 != previous
                || r.crc32 != crc32fast::hash(&serde_json::to_vec(p)?)
                || p.entry.identity != IDENTITY
                || p.entry.step >= crate::MAX_STEPS
                || p.entry.original_bytes != crate::PAGES * crate::PAGE_SIZE
            {
                return Err("journal integrity, scope or bounds failure".into());
            }
            let e = &p.entry;
            if !ordered(d.last.as_ref(), e) {
                return Err("journal stage ordering conflict".into());
            }
            previous = r.crc32;
            d.last = Some(e.clone());
            d.valid_prefix_records += 1;
        }
        Ok(())
    })();
    if let Err(e) = result {
        d.status = Status::Corrupt;
        d.explanation = format!("{e}; preserve original evidence; no replay.");
    } else if expected_identity
        .is_some_and(|id| d.last.as_ref().is_some_and(|last| last.identity != id))
    {
        d.status = Status::IdentityMismatch;
        d.explanation =
            "Restart identity differs; no restoration or deletion is authorized.".into();
    } else if d
        .last
        .as_ref()
        .is_some_and(|e| e.stage == Stage::RunComplete)
    {
        d.status = Status::SimulationRestored;
        d.manual_intervention_required = false;
        d.explanation = "All declared owned-buffer steps verified and released; terminal record present. Simulation evidence only; no kernel recovery claim.".into();
    } else {
        d.explanation = "Incomplete owned-buffer evidence; effect is uncertain. Preserve file, no replay. Process-private buffers are reclaimed by the OS after process death; this says nothing about kernel controls.".into();
    }
    d
}


FILE crates/xynoos-engine/src/replay.rs
//! Identical bounded workload and telemetry replay for all three benchmark arms.
use crate::{Action, Engine, Mode, Outcome, Plan, PAGES, PAGE_SIZE, STORE_BUDGET};
use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};
use std::{
    error::Error,
    hint::black_box,
    path::PathBuf,
    time::{Duration, Instant, UNIX_EPOCH},
};
use xynoos_pressureiq::{system::SystemSnapshot, Source};

pub type Result<T> = std::result::Result<T, Box<dyn Error>>;
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]
#[serde(deny_unknown_fields)]
pub struct Step {
    pub at_ms: u64,
    pub age_ms: u64,
    pub cpu_psi: Option<f64>,
    pub memory_psi: Option<f64>,
    pub io_psi: Option<f64>,
    pub total_kib: Option<u64>,
    pub available_kib: Option<u64>,
    pub swap_in_pages: Option<u64>,
    pub swap_out_pages: Option<u64>,
}
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]
#[serde(deny_unknown_fields)]
pub struct Trace {
    pub schema_version: u32,
    pub steps: Vec<Step>,
}
impl Trace {
    pub fn parse(bytes: &[u8]) -> Result<Self> {
        if bytes.len() > 65536 {
            return Err("trace exceeds 64 KiB".into());
        }
        let trace: Self = serde_json::from_slice(bytes)?;
        trace.validate()?;
        Ok(trace)
    }
    pub fn validate(&self) -> Result<()> {
        if self.schema_version != 1 || self.steps.is_empty() || self.steps.len() > crate::MAX_STEPS
        {
            return Err("unsupported trace version or step count (1..60)".into());
        }
        let mut previous = None;
        for s in &self.steps {
            if s.at_ms > 300_000
                || s.age_ms > 60_000
                || previous.is_some_and(|p| s.at_ms <= p || s.at_ms - p > 5000)
            {
                return Err("trace clocks must increase with gaps <= 5 s; total <= 300 s".into());
            }
            for n in [s.cpu_psi, s.memory_psi, s.io_psi].into_iter().flatten() {
                if !n.is_finite() || !(0.0..=100.0).contains(&n) {
                    return Err("invalid PSI".into());
                }
            }
            previous = Some(s.at_ms);
        }
        Ok(())
    }
}
pub fn default_trace() -> Trace {
    Trace::parse(include_bytes!("../../../fixtures/mvp-trace.json")).expect("built-in trace")
}
fn source<T>(result: std::result::Result<T, xynoos_pressureiq::Error>) -> Source<T> {
    Source {
        result,
        read_ns: 0,
        parse_ns: 0,
    }
}
impl Step {
    /// Fixture source labels match PressureIQ's contract, but the outer report always
    /// marks replay. No real collection or sleep occurs in this conversion.
    pub fn snapshot(&self, origin: Instant) -> Result<(SystemSnapshot, Instant)> {
        use xynoos_pressureiq::{parse_meminfo, parse_psi, parse_vmstat, Error};
        if self.at_ms > 300_000 || self.age_ms > 60_000 {
            return Err("trace clock bounds".into());
        }
        // Evaluation ticks always increase. Stale samples were collected earlier;
        // their age must not make the simulated clock jump forward and then back.
        let now = origin
            .checked_add(Duration::from_millis(60_000 + self.at_ms))
            .ok_or("trace clock overflow")?;
        let at = now
            .checked_sub(Duration::from_millis(self.age_ms))
            .ok_or("trace clock underflow")?;
        let missing = || Error::Missing("trace field".into());
        let psi = |n: Option<f64>| {
            n.ok_or_else(missing)
                .and_then(|n| parse_psi(&format!("some avg10={n} avg60={n} avg300={n} total=0")))
        };
        let mem = self
            .total_kib
            .zip(self.available_kib)
            .ok_or_else(missing)
            .and_then(|(t, a)| parse_meminfo(&format!("MemTotal: {t} kB\nMemAvailable: {a} kB\n")));
        let vm = self
            .swap_in_pages
            .zip(self.swap_out_pages)
            .ok_or_else(missing)
            .and_then(|(i, o)| parse_vmstat(&format!("pswpin {i}\npswpout {o}\n")));
        Ok((
            SystemSnapshot {
                timestamp: UNIX_EPOCH + Duration::from_millis(60_000 + self.at_ms - self.age_ms),
                monotonic: at,
                elapsed_ns: 0,
                memory: xynoos_pressureiq::Snapshot {
                    source_root: PathBuf::from("/proc"),
                    timestamp: UNIX_EPOCH
                        + Duration::from_millis(60_000 + self.at_ms - self.age_ms),
                    monotonic: at,
                    elapsed_ns: 0,
                    psi: source(psi(self.memory_psi)),
                    mem: source(mem),
                    vm: source(vm),
                },
                cpu: Err(Error::Unsupported),
                cpu_psi: psi(self.cpu_psi),
                io_psi: psi(self.io_psi),
                disks: Err(Error::Unsupported),
                cgroup: Err(Error::Unsupported),
                cpu_frequency_khz: Err(Error::Unsupported),
                load_one: Err(Error::Unsupported),
                gpu: vec![],
                thermal: vec![],
                power: vec![],
            },
            now,
        ))
    }
}
#[derive(Debug, Serialize)]
pub struct Workload {
    pub original_bytes: usize,
    pub stored_payload_bytes: usize,
    pub accounted_store_bytes: usize,
    pub compressed_pages: usize,
    pub raw_pages: usize,
    pub byte_verification_passed: bool,
    pub sampled_process_rss_kib: Option<u64>,
    pub workload_ns: u128,
}
fn pages() -> Vec<Vec<u8>> {
    let mut seed = 0x12345678u32;
    (0..PAGES)
        .map(|p| {
            (0..PAGE_SIZE)
                .map(|i| {
                    seed ^= seed << 13;
                    seed ^= seed >> 17;
                    seed ^= seed << 5;
                    match p % 4 {
                        0 => 0,
                        1 => (i % 13) as u8,
                        2 if i % 64 < 48 => ((i / 64 + p) % 16) as u8,
                        _ => seed as u8,
                    }
                })
                .collect()
        })
        .collect()
}
// Private executor: it never receives a user-constructed Plan or a kernel path.
fn workload(data: &[Vec<u8>], compress: bool) -> Result<Workload> {
    let start = Instant::now();
    let (stored, accounted, compressed_pages, raw_pages, sampled_process_rss_kib) = if compress {
        let mut store = xynoos_hypermemory::PageStore::adaptive(PAGE_SIZE, PAGES, STORE_BUDGET)?;
        let mut ids = Vec::with_capacity(PAGES);
        for page in data {
            ids.push(store.insert(black_box(page))?);
        }
        for (id, page) in ids.into_iter().zip(data) {
            if store.get(black_box(id))? != *page {
                return Err("owned page verification failed".into());
            }
        }
        let s = store.stats();
        (
            s.stored_bytes,
            s.accounted_bytes,
            s.compressed_pages,
            s.raw_pages,
            rss_kib(),
        )
    } else {
        let raw = black_box(data).to_vec();
        if raw != data {
            return Err("raw page verification failed".into());
        }
        black_box(&raw);
        let bytes = raw.iter().map(Vec::len).sum();
        (bytes, bytes, 0, PAGES, rss_kib())
    };
    Ok(Workload {
        original_bytes: PAGES * PAGE_SIZE,
        stored_payload_bytes: stored,
        accounted_store_bytes: accounted,
        compressed_pages,
        raw_pages,
        byte_verification_passed: true,
        sampled_process_rss_kib,
        workload_ns: start.elapsed().as_nanos(),
    })
}
fn rss_kib() -> Option<u64> {
    status_kib("VmRSS:")
}
fn status_kib(key: &str) -> Option<u64> {
    std::fs::read_to_string("/proc/self/status")
        .ok()?
        .lines()
        .find(|line| line.starts_with(key))?
        .split_whitespace()
        .nth(1)?
        .parse()
        .ok()
}
#[derive(Debug, Serialize)]
pub struct Row {
    pub step: usize,
    pub plan: Plan,
    pub decision_ns: u128,
    pub response_ns: u128,
    pub workload: Workload,
}
#[derive(Debug, Serialize)]
pub struct Report {
    pub schema_version: u32,
    pub evidence: &'static str,
    pub telemetry: &'static str,
    pub mode: Mode,
    pub kernel_mutations: u64,
    pub trace_crc32: u32,
    pub workload_crc32: u32,
    pub rows: Vec<Row>,
    pub safety_refusals: usize,
    pub owned_buffer_verifications: usize,
    pub cancelled_at_step: Option<usize>,
    pub process_high_water_rss_kib: Option<u64>,
    pub total_ns: u128,
}
pub fn run(trace: &Trace, mode: Mode, authorized: bool) -> Result<Report> {
    run_with_evidence(trace, mode, authorized, |_| Ok(()))
}
pub fn run_with_evidence(
    trace: &Trace,
    mode: Mode,
    authorized: bool,
    evidence: impl FnMut(crate::recovery::Entry) -> Result<()>,
) -> Result<Report> {
    run_with_control(trace, mode, authorized, evidence, |_, _| false)
}
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub enum CancelPoint {
    BeforeDecision,
    BeforeWork,
}
pub fn run_with_control(
    trace: &Trace,
    mode: Mode,
    authorized: bool,
    mut evidence: impl FnMut(crate::recovery::Entry) -> Result<()>,
    mut cancelled: impl FnMut(usize, CancelPoint) -> bool,
) -> Result<Report> {
    trace.validate()?;
    if mode == Mode::Controlled {
        return Err("physical mode cannot execute replay".into());
    }
    let data = pages(); // Same dataset, generated outside the measured workload interval.
    let workload_crc32 = {
        let mut h = crc32fast::Hasher::new();
        for p in &data {
            h.update(p);
        }
        h.finalize()
    };
    let start = Instant::now();
    let origin = Instant::now();
    let mut engine = Engine::default();
    let mut rows = Vec::with_capacity(trace.steps.len());
    let mut cancelled_at_step = None;
    for (step, s) in trace.steps.iter().enumerate() {
        if cancelled(step, CancelPoint::BeforeDecision) {
            cancelled_at_step = Some(step);
            break;
        }
        let response = Instant::now();
        let (snapshot, now) = s.snapshot(origin)?;
        let decision = Instant::now();
        let plan = engine.evaluate(&snapshot, now, mode, authorized, false);
        let decision_ns = decision.elapsed().as_nanos();
        let compress = mode == Mode::Simulate
            && plan.outcome == Outcome::Simulated
            && plan.action == Action::CompressOwnedPages;
        for stage in [
            crate::recovery::Stage::Captured,
            crate::recovery::Stage::ApplyIntent,
        ] {
            evidence(crate::recovery::Entry::owned(
                step,
                trace.steps.len(),
                stage,
                compress,
            ))?;
        }
        if cancelled(step, CancelPoint::BeforeWork) {
            cancelled_at_step = Some(step);
            break;
        }
        let work = workload(&data, compress)?;
        // Workload verifies every page and releases the private store before return.
        // Only the terminal record certifies that return; a crash before it is unresolved.
        evidence(crate::recovery::Entry::owned(
            step,
            trace.steps.len(),
            crate::recovery::Stage::RestoredVerified,
            compress,
        ))?;
        rows.push(Row {
            step,
            plan,
            decision_ns,
            response_ns: response.elapsed().as_nanos(),
            workload: work,
        });
    }
    if cancelled_at_step.is_none() {
        let last = rows.last().expect("validated nonempty trace");
        evidence(crate::recovery::Entry::owned(
            last.step,
            trace.steps.len(),
            crate::recovery::Stage::RunComplete,
            mode == Mode::Simulate
                && last.plan.outcome == Outcome::Simulated
                && last.plan.action == Action::CompressOwnedPages,
        ))?;
    }
    let safety_refusals = rows
        .iter()
        .filter(|r| r.plan.outcome == Outcome::Refused)
        .count();
    Ok(Report {
        schema_version: 1,
        evidence: "measured_userspace_work_with_simulated_pressure",
        telemetry: "deterministic_replay",
        mode,
        kernel_mutations: 0,
        trace_crc32: crc32fast::hash(&serde_json::to_vec(trace)?),
        workload_crc32,
        owned_buffer_verifications: rows.len(),
        cancelled_at_step,
        process_high_water_rss_kib: status_kib("VmHWM:"),
        safety_refusals,
        rows,
        total_ns: start.elapsed().as_nanos(),
    })
}


FILE crates/xynoos-engine/tests/integrated.rs
use std::{
    fs,
    path::PathBuf,
    sync::atomic::{AtomicU64, Ordering},
    time::{Duration, Instant},
};
use xynoos_engine::{
    recovery::{self, Journal, Stage, Status},
    replay::{self, CancelPoint},
    Action, Engine, Mode, Outcome, Reason,
};

static NEXT: AtomicU64 = AtomicU64::new(0);
struct Temp(PathBuf);
impl Temp {
    fn new() -> Self {
        let p = std::env::temp_dir().join(format!(
            "xynoos-mvp-{}-{}",
            std::process::id(),
            NEXT.fetch_add(1, Ordering::Relaxed)
        ));
        fs::create_dir(&p).unwrap();
        Self(p)
    }
}
impl Drop for Temp {
    fn drop(&mut self) {
        fs::remove_dir_all(&self.0).unwrap();
    }
}

#[test]
fn three_arms_share_trace_and_workload_but_only_simulation_compresses() {
    let t = replay::default_trace();
    let baseline = replay::run(&t, Mode::Baseline, true).unwrap();
    let observe = replay::run(&t, Mode::Observe, true).unwrap();
    let sim = replay::run(&t, Mode::Simulate, true).unwrap();
    assert_eq!(baseline.trace_crc32, sim.trace_crc32);
    assert_eq!(observe.workload_crc32, sim.workload_crc32);
    assert_eq!(sim.safety_refusals, 2);
    assert_eq!(observe.safety_refusals, 2);
    assert_eq!(sim.owned_buffer_verifications, t.steps.len());
    assert_eq!(sim.kernel_mutations, 0);
    assert!(baseline
        .rows
        .iter()
        .all(|r| r.plan.decision.is_none() && r.workload.compressed_pages == 0));
    assert!(observe
        .rows
        .iter()
        .all(|r| r.workload.compressed_pages == 0));
    assert!(sim.rows[2].workload.compressed_pages > 0);
    assert!(sim.rows[2].workload.stored_payload_bytes < sim.rows[2].workload.original_bytes);
    assert_eq!(sim.rows[4].plan.reason, Reason::CpuOrIoPressure);
    assert_eq!(sim.rows[6].plan.reason, Reason::TelemetryUnavailable);
    assert_eq!(sim.rows[7].plan.reason, Reason::TelemetryUnavailable);
    assert_eq!(sim.rows[8].plan.action, Action::KeepOwnedRaw);
    assert_eq!(sim.rows[9].plan.action, Action::CompressOwnedPages);
    assert_eq!(sim.rows[12].plan.action, Action::KeepOwnedRaw);
    assert!(sim.rows.iter().all(|r| r.workload.byte_verification_passed
        && r.workload.accounted_store_bytes <= xynoos_engine::STORE_BUDGET));
}

#[test]
fn authorization_and_physical_lock_are_independent_of_caller_intent() {
    let t = replay::default_trace();
    let denied = replay::run(&t, Mode::Simulate, false).unwrap();
    assert!(denied.rows.iter().all(|r| r.workload.compressed_pages == 0));
    let (s, now) = t.steps[2].snapshot(Instant::now()).unwrap();
    for authorized in [false, true] {
        let p = Engine::default().evaluate(&s, now, Mode::Controlled, authorized, false);
        assert_eq!(p.outcome, Outcome::Refused);
        assert_eq!(p.action, Action::BackOff);
        assert!(!p.kernel_write_available);
        assert_eq!(
            p.reason,
            if authorized {
                Reason::PhysicalMutationLocked
            } else {
                Reason::AuthorizationRequired
            }
        );
        assert!(replay::run(&t, Mode::Controlled, authorized).is_err());
    }
}

#[test]
fn telemetry_refusals_reset_history_and_swap_counter_reset_is_not_activity() {
    let t = replay::default_trace();
    let origin = Instant::now();
    let (s, now) = t.steps[2].snapshot(origin).unwrap();
    let mut engine = Engine::default();
    assert_eq!(
        engine.evaluate(&s, now, Mode::Simulate, true, false).action,
        Action::KeepOwnedRaw
    );
    let mut step = t.steps[3].clone();
    step.swap_out_pages = Some(0);
    let (s, now) = step.snapshot(origin).unwrap();
    assert_eq!(
        engine.evaluate(&s, now, Mode::Simulate, true, false).reason,
        Reason::TelemetryUnavailable
    );
    let mut step = t.steps[3].clone();
    step.at_ms = 4000;
    let (s, now) = step.snapshot(origin).unwrap();
    assert_eq!(
        engine.evaluate(&s, now, Mode::Simulate, true, false).action,
        Action::KeepOwnedRaw
    );
    let mut engine = Engine::default();
    let mut bad = s.clone();
    bad.memory.monotonic = now + Duration::from_secs(1);
    assert_eq!(
        engine
            .evaluate(&bad, now, Mode::Observe, false, false)
            .reason,
        Reason::TelemetryUnavailable
    );
    bad = s.clone();
    bad.memory.vm.result = Err(xynoos_pressureiq::Error::Permission);
    assert_eq!(
        engine
            .evaluate(&bad, now, Mode::Observe, false, false)
            .reason,
        Reason::TelemetryUnavailable
    );
    bad = s.clone();
    bad.memory.source_root = "/tmp/forged".into();
    assert_eq!(
        engine
            .evaluate(&bad, now, Mode::Observe, false, false)
            .reason,
        Reason::TelemetryUnavailable
    );
    assert_eq!(
        engine
            .evaluate(
                &s,
                now + Duration::from_secs(6),
                Mode::Observe,
                false,
                false
            )
            .reason,
        Reason::TelemetryUnavailable
    );
}

#[test]
fn policy_threshold_sweep_uses_real_parsers_and_aura_decisions() {
    let t = replay::default_trace();
    let origin = Instant::now();
    for percent in (0..=100).step_by(5) {
        let mut engine = Engine::default();
        let mut step = t.steps[2].clone();
        step.at_ms = 0;
        step.memory_psi = Some(f64::from(percent));
        let (s, now) = step.snapshot(origin).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(
            engine.evaluate(&s, now, Mode::Simulate, true, false).action,
            Action::KeepOwnedRaw
        );
        step.at_ms = 1000;
        let (s, now) = step.snapshot(origin).unwrap();
        let p = engine.evaluate(&s, now, Mode::Simulate, true, false);
        assert_eq!(
            p.action,
            if percent >= 10 {
                Action::CompressOwnedPages
            } else {
                Action::KeepOwnedRaw
            }
        );
        step.at_ms = 2000;
        step.io_psi = Some(10.0);
        let (s, now) = step.snapshot(origin).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(
            engine.evaluate(&s, now, Mode::Simulate, true, false).reason,
            Reason::CpuOrIoPressure
        );
    }
}

#[test]
fn low_memory_and_cancellation_back_off_without_owned_mutations() {
    let t = replay::default_trace();
    let mut step = t.steps[2].clone();
    step.available_kib = Some(1024);
    let (s, now) = step.snapshot(Instant::now()).unwrap();
    let mut e = Engine::default();
    assert_eq!(
        e.evaluate(&s, now, Mode::Simulate, true, false).reason,
        Reason::InsufficientAvailableMemory
    );
    assert_eq!(
        e.evaluate(&s, now, Mode::Simulate, true, true).outcome,
        Outcome::Cancelled
    );
    assert_eq!(
        e.evaluate(&s, now, Mode::Simulate, true, false).outcome,
        Outcome::Cancelled
    );
    let mut entries = vec![];
    let r = replay::run_with_control(
        &t,
        Mode::Simulate,
        true,
        |entry| {
            entries.push(entry);
            Ok(())
        },
        |step, point| step == 2 && point == CancelPoint::BeforeWork,
    )
    .unwrap();
    assert_eq!(r.cancelled_at_step, Some(2));
    assert_eq!(r.rows.len(), 2);
    assert_eq!(entries.last().unwrap().stage, Stage::ApplyIntent);
    assert!(!entries.iter().any(|e| e.stage == Stage::RunComplete));
}

#[test]
fn durable_evidence_restart_and_every_interrupted_stage_fail_closed() {
    let temp = Temp::new();
    let path = temp.0.join("evidence.jsonl");
    let t = replay::default_trace();
    let mut journal = Journal::create(&path).unwrap();
    replay::run_with_evidence(&t, Mode::Simulate, true, |e| journal.append(e)).unwrap();
    drop(journal);
    let bytes = fs::read(&path).unwrap();
    let d = recovery::inspect_file(&path).unwrap();
    assert_eq!(d.status, Status::SimulationRestored);
    assert_eq!(d.valid_prefix_records, t.steps.len() * 3 + 1);
    assert!(!d.physical_mutation_available && !d.automatic_recovery_available);
    assert_eq!(
        recovery::inspect(&bytes, Some("different-restart-identity")).status,
        Status::IdentityMismatch
    );
    for (i, b) in bytes.iter().enumerate() {
        if *b == b'\n' && i + 1 < bytes.len() {
            let prefix = recovery::inspect(&bytes[..i + 1], None);
            assert_eq!(prefix.status, Status::Unresolved);
            assert!(prefix.manual_intervention_required);
            if i > 0 {
                assert_eq!(recovery::inspect(&bytes[..i], None).status, Status::Corrupt);
            }
        }
    }
    assert_eq!(fs::read(&path).unwrap(), bytes); // Inspection never repairs or deletes.
    assert!(Journal::create(&path).is_err());
}

#[test]
fn corruption_reordering_and_changed_originals_preserve_valid_prefix() {
    let temp = Temp::new();
    let path = temp.0.join("journal");
    let t = replay::default_trace();
    let mut journal = Journal::create(&path).unwrap();
    replay::run_with_evidence(&t, Mode::Simulate, true, |e| journal.append(e)).unwrap();
    drop(journal);
    let bytes = fs::read(&path).unwrap();
    let lines: Vec<_> = bytes.split_inclusive(|b| *b == b'\n').collect();
    let corrupt = [lines[0], b"{broken}\n", lines[2]].concat();
    let d = recovery::inspect(&corrupt, None);
    assert_eq!(d.status, Status::Corrupt);
    assert_eq!(d.valid_prefix_records, 1);
    let reordered = [lines[0], lines[2], lines[1]].concat();
    assert_eq!(recovery::inspect(&reordered, None).status, Status::Corrupt);
    let mut altered = bytes.clone();
    let position = altered.iter().position(|b| *b == b'1').unwrap();
    altered[position] = b'2';
    assert_eq!(recovery::inspect(&altered, None).status, Status::Corrupt);
    assert_eq!(
        recovery::inspect(&vec![b' '; recovery::MAX_BYTES + 1], None).status,
        Status::Corrupt
    );
    assert_eq!(recovery::inspect(&[], None).status, Status::Unresolved);
}

#[test]
fn evidence_io_failure_stops_execution_and_preserves_durable_intent() {
    let temp = Temp::new();
    let path = temp.0.join("journal");
    let mut j = Journal::create(&path).unwrap();
    let r = replay::run_with_evidence(&replay::default_trace(), Mode::Simulate, true, |e| {
        if e.stage == Stage::RestoredVerified {
            return Err("injected evidence sync failure".into());
        }
        j.append(e)
    });
    assert!(r.is_err());
    drop(j);
    let d = recovery::inspect_file(&path).unwrap();
    assert_eq!(d.status, Status::Unresolved);
    assert_eq!(d.last.unwrap().stage, Stage::ApplyIntent);
}

#[test]
fn evidence_paths_refuse_symlinks_and_special_files() {
    use std::os::unix::fs::symlink;
    let temp = Temp::new();
    let target = temp.0.join("target");
    fs::write(&target, b"preserve").unwrap();
    let link = temp.0.join("link");
    symlink(&target, &link).unwrap();
    assert!(Journal::create(&link).is_err());
    assert!(recovery::inspect_file(&link).is_err());
    assert!(recovery::inspect_file(&temp.0).is_err());
    assert!(recovery::inspect_file(std::path::Path::new("/dev/null")).is_err());
    assert_eq!(fs::read(target).unwrap(), b"preserve");
}

#[test]
fn traces_are_bounded_and_malformed_input_never_reaches_executor() {
    let mut step = replay::default_trace().steps[0].clone();
    step.at_ms = u64::MAX;
    assert!(step.snapshot(Instant::now()).is_err());
    let mut t = replay::default_trace();
    t.steps[1].at_ms = 0;
    assert!(t.validate().is_err());
    let mut t = replay::default_trace();
    t.steps[0].memory_psi = Some(f64::NAN);
    assert!(t.validate().is_err());
    let mut t = replay::default_trace();
    t.steps[0].age_ms = 60001;
    assert!(t.validate().is_err());
    let mut t = replay::default_trace();
    t.steps = t.steps.iter().cycle().take(61).cloned().collect();
    assert!(t.validate().is_err());
    assert!(replay::Trace::parse(&vec![b' '; 65537]).is_err());
    assert!(replay::Trace::parse(br#"{"schema_version":1,"steps":[],"unknown":true}"#).is_err());
}


FILE crates/xynoos-hypermemory/src/lib.rs
#![forbid(unsafe_code)]
use std::{collections::HashMap, fmt, mem::size_of};
use xynoos_core::PageId;
#[derive(Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub enum Error {
    InvalidPageSize,
    InvalidCapacity,
    PayloadChecksumMismatch,
    ChecksumMismatch,
    Allocation,
    BudgetExceeded,
    InvalidLength { expected: usize, actual: usize },
    Full,
    NotFound,
    IdExhausted,
    Decompression,
}
impl fmt::Display for Error {
    fn fmt(&self, f: &mut fmt::Formatter<'_>) -> fmt::Result {
        write!(f, "{self:?}")
    }
}
impl std::error::Error for Error {}
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub struct Stats {
    pub page_count: usize,
    pub capacity_pages: usize,
    pub original_bytes: usize,
    pub compressed_bytes: usize,
    pub compressed_allocated_bytes: usize,
    pub metadata_bytes_lower_bound: usize,
    pub hash_map_capacity: usize,
    pub store_struct_bytes: usize,
    pub raw_pages: usize,
    pub compressed_pages: usize,
    pub raw_bytes: usize,
    pub stored_bytes: usize,
    pub buffer_capacity_bytes: usize,
    pub budget_bytes: usize,
    pub accounted_bytes: usize,
}
impl Stats {
    pub fn compression_ratio(&self) -> Option<f64> {
        (self.original_bytes != 0).then(|| self.stored_bytes as f64 / self.original_bytes as f64)
    }
}
/// Batas ukuran simulasi untuk membatasi alokasi setiap pengambilan.
pub const MAX_PAGE_SIZE: usize = 1024 * 1024;
struct Entry {
    payload: Vec<u8>,
    payload_crc: u32,
    original_crc: u32,
    raw: bool,
}
pub struct PageStore {
    page_size: usize,
    capacity: usize,
    next: u64,
    adaptive: bool,
    budget: usize,
    accounted: usize,
    pages: HashMap<PageId, Entry>,
}
impl PageStore {
    pub fn new(page_size: usize, capacity: usize) -> Result<Self, Error> {
        if page_size == 0 || page_size > MAX_PAGE_SIZE {
            return Err(Error::InvalidPageSize);
        }
        if capacity
            .checked_mul(lz4_flex::block::get_maximum_output_size(page_size))
            .is_none()
            || capacity.checked_mul(size_of::<(PageId, Entry)>()).is_none()
        {
            return Err(Error::InvalidCapacity);
        }
        Ok(Self {
            page_size,
            capacity,
            next: 0,
            adaptive: false,
            budget: usize::MAX,
            accounted: 0,
            pages: HashMap::new(),
        })
    }
    /// Membuat store adaptif dengan batas kapasitas buffer dan metadata entri aktif.
    pub fn adaptive(page_size: usize, capacity: usize, budget: usize) -> Result<Self, Error> {
        let mut store = Self::new(page_size, capacity)?;
        store.adaptive = true;
        store.budget = budget;
        Ok(store)
    }
    /// Membuat store 4 KiB; konfigurasi berlebihan ditolak.
    pub fn with_capacity(capacity: usize) -> Result<Self, Error> {
        Self::new(4096, capacity)
    }
    pub fn insert(&mut self, data: &[u8]) -> Result<PageId, Error> {
        if data.len() != self.page_size {
            return Err(Error::InvalidLength {
                expected: self.page_size,
                actual: data.len(),
            });
        }
        if self.pages.len() >= self.capacity {
            return Err(Error::Full);
        }
        let next = self.next.checked_add(1).ok_or(Error::IdExhausted)?;
        let id = PageId(self.next);
        let compressed = lz4_flex::block::compress(data);
        // Vec capacity tidak pernah lebih kecil dari len; lewati alokasi raw jika
        // kompresi sudah pasti lebih kecil dari kandidat raw mana pun.
        let (payload, raw) = if self.adaptive && compressed.capacity() >= data.len() {
            let mut raw = Vec::new();
            raw.try_reserve_exact(data.len())
                .map_err(|_| Error::Allocation)?;
            if raw.capacity() <= compressed.capacity() {
                raw.extend_from_slice(data);
                (raw, true)
            } else {
                (compressed, false)
            }
        } else {
            (compressed, false)
        };
        let cost = payload
            .capacity()
            .checked_add(size_of::<(PageId, Entry)>())
            .ok_or(Error::BudgetExceeded)?;
        let accounted = self
            .accounted
            .checked_add(cost)
            .ok_or(Error::BudgetExceeded)?;
        if accounted > self.budget {
            return Err(Error::BudgetExceeded);
        }
        self.pages.try_reserve(1).map_err(|_| Error::Allocation)?;
        let entry = Entry {
            payload_crc: crc32fast::hash(&payload),
            original_crc: crc32fast::hash(data),
            payload,
            raw,
        };
        self.pages.insert(id, entry);
        self.next = next;
        self.accounted = accounted;
        Ok(id)
    }
    pub fn get(&self, id: PageId) -> Result<Vec<u8>, Error> {
        let data = self.pages.get(&id).ok_or(Error::NotFound)?;
        if crc32fast::hash(&data.payload) != data.payload_crc {
            return Err(Error::PayloadChecksumMismatch);
        }
        let mut output = Vec::new();
        output
            .try_reserve_exact(self.page_size)
            .map_err(|_| Error::Allocation)?;
        output.resize(self.page_size, 0);
        let written = if data.raw {
            if data.payload.len() != self.page_size {
                return Err(Error::Decompression);
            }
            output.copy_from_slice(&data.payload);
            self.page_size
        } else {
            lz4_flex::block::decompress_into(&data.payload, &mut output)
                .map_err(|_| Error::Decompression)?
        };
        if written != self.page_size {
            return Err(Error::Decompression);
        }
        if crc32fast::hash(&output) != data.original_crc {
            return Err(Error::ChecksumMismatch);
        }
        Ok(output)
    }
    pub fn remove(&mut self, id: PageId) -> Result<(), Error> {
        let entry = self.pages.remove(&id).ok_or(Error::NotFound)?;
        self.accounted = self
            .accounted
            .checked_sub(
                entry
                    .payload
                    .capacity()
                    .checked_add(size_of::<(PageId, Entry)>())
                    .expect("validated entry cost"),
            )
            .expect("accounting invariant");
        Ok(())
    }
    pub fn stats(&self) -> Stats {
        Stats {
            raw_pages: self.pages.values().filter(|e| e.raw).count(),
            compressed_pages: self.pages.values().filter(|e| !e.raw).count(),
            raw_bytes: self
                .pages
                .values()
                .filter(|e| e.raw)
                .map(|e| e.payload.len())
                .fold(0usize, |a, b| {
                    a.checked_add(b).expect("accounting invariant")
                }),
            stored_bytes: self
                .pages
                .values()
                .map(|e| e.payload.len())
                .fold(0usize, |a, b| {
                    a.checked_add(b).expect("accounting invariant")
                }),
            buffer_capacity_bytes: self
                .pages
                .values()
                .map(|e| e.payload.capacity())
                .fold(0usize, |a, b| {
                    a.checked_add(b).expect("accounting invariant")
                }),
            budget_bytes: self.budget,
            accounted_bytes: self.accounted,
            hash_map_capacity: self.pages.capacity(),
            store_struct_bytes: size_of::<Self>(),
            page_count: self.pages.len(),
            capacity_pages: self.capacity,
            original_bytes: self
                .pages
                .len()
                .checked_mul(self.page_size)
                .expect("validated capacity"),
            compressed_bytes: self
                .pages
                .values()
                .filter(|e| !e.raw)
                .map(|e| e.payload.len())
                .fold(0usize, |a, b| {
                    a.checked_add(b).expect("accounting invariant")
                }),
            compressed_allocated_bytes: self
                .pages
                .values()
                .filter(|e| !e.raw)
                .map(|e| e.payload.capacity())
                .fold(0usize, |a, b| {
                    a.checked_add(b).expect("accounting invariant")
                }),
            metadata_bytes_lower_bound: self
                .pages
                .len()
                .checked_mul(size_of::<(PageId, Entry)>())
                .expect("validated capacity"),
        }
    }
}
#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;
    #[test]
    fn round_trips_and_statistics() {
        let mut s = PageStore::with_capacity(3).unwrap();
        let mut x = 1u32;
        let random: Vec<u8> = (0..4096)
            .map(|_| {
                x ^= x << 13;
                x ^= x >> 17;
                x ^= x << 5;
                x as u8
            })
            .collect();
        let pages = [
            vec![0; 4096],
            (0..4096).map(|i| (i % 13) as u8).collect(),
            random,
        ];
        let ids: Vec<_> = pages.iter().map(|p| s.insert(p).unwrap()).collect();
        for (id, p) in ids.iter().zip(pages.iter()) {
            assert_eq!(&s.get(*id).unwrap(), p);
        }
        let st = s.stats();
        assert_eq!(st.page_count, 3);
        assert_eq!(st.original_bytes, 12288);
        assert_eq!(
            st.compressed_bytes,
            s.pages.values().map(|e| e.payload.len()).sum()
        );
        assert_eq!(
            st.compression_ratio(),
            Some(st.compressed_bytes as f64 / 12288.0)
        );
        assert_eq!(s.insert(&pages[0]), Err(Error::Full));
        s.remove(ids[0]).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(s.get(ids[0]), Err(Error::NotFound));
        assert_eq!(s.stats().page_count, 2);
    }
    #[test]
    fn invalid() {
        assert!(matches!(PageStore::new(0, 1), Err(Error::InvalidPageSize)));
        let mut s = PageStore::new(8, 1).unwrap();
        assert!(matches!(
            s.insert(&[0; 7]),
            Err(Error::InvalidLength { .. })
        ));
        assert_eq!(s.remove(PageId(99)), Err(Error::NotFound));
        assert_eq!(s.stats().compression_ratio(), None);
    }
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod integrity_tests {
    use super::*;
    #[test]
    fn corruption_and_isolation() {
        let mut s = PageStore::with_capacity(2).unwrap();
        let a = s.insert(&[0; 4096]).unwrap();
        let b = s.insert(&[1; 4096]).unwrap();
        s.pages.get_mut(&a).unwrap().payload[0] ^= 1;
        let before = s.stats();
        assert_eq!(s.get(a), Err(Error::PayloadChecksumMismatch));
        assert_eq!(s.stats(), before);
        assert_eq!(s.get(b).unwrap(), vec![1; 4096]);
    }
    #[test]
    fn truncation_and_decoder_failure() {
        let mut s = PageStore::with_capacity(1).unwrap();
        let a = s.insert(&[0; 4096]).unwrap();
        let e = s.pages.get_mut(&a).unwrap();
        e.payload.truncate(1);
        assert_eq!(s.get(a), Err(Error::PayloadChecksumMismatch));
        let e = s.pages.get_mut(&a).unwrap();
        e.payload_crc = crc32fast::hash(&e.payload);
        assert_eq!(s.get(a), Err(Error::Decompression));
    }
    #[test]
    fn checksum_and_length() {
        let mut s = PageStore::with_capacity(1).unwrap();
        let a = s.insert(&[0; 4096]).unwrap();
        s.pages.get_mut(&a).unwrap().original_crc ^= 1;
        assert_eq!(s.get(a), Err(Error::ChecksumMismatch));
        let e = s.pages.get_mut(&a).unwrap();
        e.payload = lz4_flex::block::compress(&[0; 16]);
        e.payload_crc = crc32fast::hash(&e.payload);
        assert_eq!(s.get(a), Err(Error::Decompression));
    }
    #[test]
    fn failures_preserve_state() {
        let mut s = PageStore::with_capacity(1).unwrap();
        let a = s.insert(&[0; 4096]).unwrap();
        let st = s.stats();
        assert_eq!(s.insert(&[1; 4096]), Err(Error::Full));
        assert!(s.insert(&[]).is_err());
        assert_eq!(s.remove(PageId(99)), Err(Error::NotFound));
        assert_eq!(s.stats(), st);
        s.remove(a).unwrap();
        let b = s.insert(&[0; 4096]).unwrap();
        assert_ne!(a, b);
        assert_eq!(s.get(a), Err(Error::NotFound));
        s.remove(b).unwrap();
        s.next = u64::MAX;
        let st = s.stats();
        assert_eq!(s.insert(&[0; 4096]), Err(Error::IdExhausted));
        assert_eq!(s.stats(), st);
        assert!(matches!(
            PageStore::new(MAX_PAGE_SIZE + 1, 1),
            Err(Error::InvalidPageSize)
        ));
        assert!(matches!(
            PageStore::new(4096, usize::MAX),
            Err(Error::InvalidCapacity)
        ));
    }
    #[test]
    fn generated_operation_sequences() {
        let mut seed = 0x12345678u32;
        for size in [1, 16, 4096, 8192] {
            let mut s = PageStore::new(size, 16).unwrap();
            let mut model = Vec::new();
            for step in 0..512 {
                let data: Vec<_> = (0..size)
                    .map(|i| {
                        seed ^= seed << 13;
                        seed ^= seed >> 17;
                        seed ^= seed << 5;
                        if step % 3 == 0 {
                            0
                        } else if step % 3 == 1 {
                            (i % 7) as u8
                        } else {
                            seed as u8
                        }
                    })
                    .collect();
                if model.len() == 16 {
                    let (id, _) = model.remove(0);
                    s.remove(id).unwrap();
                    assert_eq!(s.get(id), Err(Error::NotFound));
                }
                let id = s.insert(&data).unwrap();
                model.push((id, data));
                for (id, data) in &model {
                    assert_eq!(s.get(*id).unwrap(), *data);
                }
                assert_eq!(s.stats().original_bytes, model.len() * size);
            }
        }
    }
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod malformed_tests {
    use super::*;
    #[test]
    fn bounded_malformed_blocks() {
        let mut s = PageStore::new(64, 1).unwrap();
        let id = s.insert(&[0; 64]).unwrap();
        let mut seed = 42u32;
        for len in 0..256 {
            let e = s.pages.get_mut(&id).unwrap();
            e.payload = (0..len)
                .map(|_| {
                    seed ^= seed << 13;
                    seed ^= seed >> 17;
                    seed ^= seed << 5;
                    seed as u8
                })
                .collect();
            e.payload_crc = crc32fast::hash(&e.payload);
            let before = s.stats();
            let result = s.get(id);
            if let Ok(data) = result {
                assert_eq!(data, vec![0; 64]);
            }
            assert_eq!(s.stats(), before);
        }
    }
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod adaptive_tests {
    use super::*;
    #[test]
    fn mixed_and_budget() {
        let mut seed = 123u32;
        let random: Vec<u8> = (0..4096)
            .map(|_| {
                seed ^= seed << 13;
                seed ^= seed >> 17;
                seed ^= seed << 5;
                seed as u8
            })
            .collect();
        let mut s = PageStore::adaptive(4096, 3, 10000).unwrap();
        let a = s.insert(&random).unwrap();
        let b = s.insert(&[0; 4096]).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(s.get(a).unwrap(), random);
        assert_eq!(s.get(b).unwrap(), vec![0; 4096]);
        let st = s.stats();
        assert_eq!((st.raw_pages, st.compressed_pages), (1, 1));
        assert_eq!(
            st.accounted_bytes,
            st.buffer_capacity_bytes + st.metadata_bytes_lower_bound
        );
        s.remove(a).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(s.stats().raw_pages, 0);
        let mut t = PageStore::adaptive(4096, 1, 1).unwrap();
        let before = t.stats();
        assert_eq!(t.insert(&[0; 4096]), Err(Error::BudgetExceeded));
        assert_eq!(t.stats(), before);
    }
    #[test]
    fn raw_corruption_is_detected() {
        let mut s = PageStore::adaptive(1, 1, 100).unwrap();
        let id = s.insert(&[42]).unwrap();
        assert!(s.pages[&id].raw);
        s.pages.get_mut(&id).unwrap().payload[0] ^= 1;
        assert_eq!(s.get(id), Err(Error::PayloadChecksumMismatch));
    }
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod budget_boundary_tests {
    use super::*;
    #[test]
    fn boundary_and_reuse() {
        let input = [0; 4096];
        let mut probe = PageStore::adaptive(4096, 2, usize::MAX).unwrap();
        let id = probe.insert(&input).unwrap();
        let cost = probe.stats().accounted_bytes;
        for budget in [cost, cost + 1] {
            let mut s = PageStore::adaptive(4096, 1, budget).unwrap();
            let a = s.insert(&input).unwrap();
            assert_eq!(s.stats().accounted_bytes, cost);
            let before = s.stats();
            assert_eq!(s.insert(&input), Err(Error::Full));
            assert_eq!(s.stats(), before);
            s.remove(a).unwrap();
            assert_eq!(s.stats().accounted_bytes, 0);
            assert_ne!(a, s.insert(&input).unwrap());
        }
        let mut s = PageStore::adaptive(4096, 2, cost - 1).unwrap();
        let before = s.stats();
        assert_eq!(s.insert(&input), Err(Error::BudgetExceeded));
        assert_eq!(s.stats(), before);
        assert_eq!(s.next, 0);
        probe.remove(id).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(probe.stats().accounted_bytes, 0);
    }
    #[test]
    fn overflow_and_raw_integrity() {
        assert!(matches!(
            PageStore::adaptive(4096, usize::MAX, usize::MAX),
            Err(Error::InvalidCapacity)
        ));
        let mut s = PageStore::adaptive(1, 1, usize::MAX).unwrap();
        let id = s.insert(&[42]).unwrap();
        assert!(s.pages[&id].raw);
        s.pages.get_mut(&id).unwrap().original_crc ^= 1;
        assert_eq!(s.get(id), Err(Error::ChecksumMismatch));
        let e = s.pages.get_mut(&id).unwrap();
        e.payload.clear();
        e.payload_crc = crc32fast::hash(&e.payload);
        assert_eq!(s.get(id), Err(Error::Decompression));
    }
    #[test]
    fn mixed_sequence() {
        let mut s = PageStore::adaptive(128, 4, 4096).unwrap();
        let mut seed = 12u32;
        let mut live = Vec::new();
        for n in 0..128 {
            let page: Vec<u8> = (0..128)
                .map(|_| {
                    seed ^= seed << 13;
                    seed ^= seed >> 17;
                    seed ^= seed << 5;
                    if n % 2 == 0 {
                        0
                    } else {
                        seed as u8
                    }
                })
                .collect();
            if live.len() == 4 {
                let (id, _) = live.remove(0);
                s.remove(id).unwrap();
                assert_eq!(s.get(id), Err(Error::NotFound));
            }
            let id = s.insert(&page).unwrap();
            live.push((id, page));
            for (id, p) in &live {
                assert_eq!(s.get(*id).unwrap(), *p);
            }
            let st = s.stats();
            assert_eq!(st.raw_pages + st.compressed_pages, live.len());
            assert_eq!(st.stored_bytes, st.raw_bytes + st.compressed_bytes);
            assert_eq!(
                st.accounted_bytes,
                st.buffer_capacity_bytes + st.metadata_bytes_lower_bound
            );
        }
    }
}


FILE crates/xynoos-linux-control/examples/probe.rs
use std::{path::Path, time::Instant};
use xynoos_linux_control::{discover, execute, prepare, Action};
use xynoos_pressureiq::system::collect_system;
fn main() {
    let s = collect_system();
    let target = Path::new("/sys/fs/cgroup/init");
    let action = Action::MemoryLow { bytes: 0 };
    let caps = discover();
    let audit = prepare(&s, Instant::now(), target, action);
    println!("capabilities={}", serde_json::to_string(&caps).unwrap());
    println!("dry_run={}", serde_json::to_string(&audit).unwrap());
    println!("authorized_execution={:?}", execute(&audit, true));
    let n = 1000u32;
    let start = Instant::now();
    for _ in 0..n {
        std::hint::black_box(prepare(&s, Instant::now(), target, action));
    }
    println!(
        "prepare_mean_ns={} iterations={n}",
        start.elapsed().as_nanos() / u128::from(n)
    );
    let start = Instant::now();
    for _ in 0..n {
        std::hint::black_box(execute(&audit, false));
    }
    println!(
        "decline_mean_ns={} iterations={n}",
        start.elapsed().as_nanos() / u128::from(n)
    );
}


FILE crates/xynoos-linux-control/examples/transaction_bench.rs
//! Bounded in-memory transaction microbenchmark; excludes kernel I/O and telemetry collection.
use xynoos_linux_control::{
    transaction::{run, Backend, Identity, Outcome},
    Action,
};
struct Memory(u64);
impl Backend for Memory {
    fn ready_to_restore(&mut self) -> Result<(), String> {
        Ok(()) // This in-memory fixture never starts a worker.
    }
    fn identity(&mut self) -> Result<Identity, String> {
        Ok(Identity {
            device: 1,
            inode: 2,
            control_inode: 3,
        })
    }
    fn read_low(&mut self) -> Result<u64, String> {
        Ok(self.0)
    }
    fn write_low(&mut self, value: u64) -> Result<(), String> {
        self.0 = value;
        Ok(())
    }
}
fn main() {
    let snapshot = xynoos_pressureiq::system::collect_system();
    let mut fixture = Memory(0);
    let n = 1000;
    let start = std::time::Instant::now();
    for _ in 0..n {
        let result = run(
            &mut fixture,
            &snapshot,
            Action::MemoryLow { bytes: 4096 },
            true,
        );
        assert_eq!(result.outcome, Outcome::Restored);
        std::hint::black_box(result);
    }
    println!(
        "in_memory_transaction_mean_ns={} iterations={n}",
        start.elapsed().as_nanos() / n
    );
}


FILE crates/xynoos-linux-control/src/bound_child.rs
//! A session-owned empty child. All authority is derived from the held parent fd.
//! Checks and cgroup operations are sequential, so exclusive delegation is required.
use crate::transaction::{openat, read_at};
use std::{
    ffi::{CStr, CString},
    fs::File,
    io::Read,
    os::{fd::AsRawFd, unix::fs::MetadataExt},
};

#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub(crate) struct FileId {
    pub dev: u64,
    pub ino: u64,
}
fn id(f: &File) -> Result<FileId, String> {
    let m = f.metadata().map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
    Ok(FileId {
        dev: m.dev(),
        ino: m.ino(),
    })
}
fn check_parent(parent: &File, expected: FileId) -> Result<(), String> {
    if id(parent)? != expected {
        return Err("parent descriptor identity changed".into());
    }
    let meta = parent.metadata().map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
    // SAFETY: geteuid has no preconditions.
    let uid = unsafe { libc::geteuid() };
    if uid == 0 || meta.uid() != uid || meta.mode() & 0o022 != 0 {
        return Err("unsafe parent ownership or permissions".into());
    }
    Ok(())
}
fn no_descendants(stat: &str) -> bool {
    let lines: Vec<_> = stat
        .lines()
        .filter(|l| l.split_whitespace().next() == Some("nr_descendants"))
        .collect();
    lines.len() == 1 && lines[0].split_whitespace().collect::<Vec<_>>() == ["nr_descendants", "0"]
}

pub(crate) struct BoundChild {
    name: CString,
    dir: File,
    identity: FileId,
    uid: u32,
}
impl BoundChild {
    #[cfg(test)]
    pub(crate) fn fixture(parent: &File, name: &CStr) -> Self {
        let dir = openat(parent.as_raw_fd(), name, libc::O_RDONLY | libc::O_DIRECTORY).unwrap();
        let identity = id(&dir).unwrap();
        let uid = dir.metadata().unwrap().uid();
        Self {
            name: name.to_owned(),
            dir,
            identity,
            uid,
        }
    }
    pub(crate) fn population(&self, parent: &File, expected: FileId) -> Result<String, String> {
        if !self.identity_matches(parent, expected)? {
            return Err("child identity changed".into());
        }
        let population = read_at(&self.dir, c"cgroup.procs")?;
        if !self.identity_matches(parent, expected)? {
            return Err("child identity changed during read".into());
        }
        Ok(population)
    }
    pub(crate) fn identity(&self) -> crate::child_memory::ChildIdentity {
        crate::child_memory::ChildIdentity {
            device: self.identity.dev,
            inode: self.identity.ino,
        }
    }
    /// Compare the held child with the entry under the held parent. Other validation
    /// remains the responsibility of `verify` before any mutation.
    pub(crate) fn identity_matches(&self, parent: &File, expected: FileId) -> Result<bool, String> {
        check_parent(parent, expected)?;
        let current = openat(
            parent.as_raw_fd(),
            &self.name,
            libc::O_RDONLY | libc::O_DIRECTORY,
        )?;
        Ok(id(&current)? == self.identity && id(&self.dir)? == self.identity)
    }
    pub(crate) fn read_low(&self, parent: &File, expected: FileId) -> Result<u64, String> {
        self.verify(parent, expected)?;
        let control = openat(self.dir.as_raw_fd(), c"memory.low", libc::O_RDONLY)?;
        let meta = control.metadata().map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
        if !meta.is_file() || meta.dev() != self.identity.dev || meta.uid() != self.uid {
            return Err("unsafe memory.low file identity or type".into());
        }
        let mut text = String::new();
        (&control)
            .take(33)
            .read_to_string(&mut text)
            .map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
        if text.len() > 32 {
            return Err("memory.low read too large".into());
        }
        let value = text
            .trim()
            .parse()
            .map_err(|_| "invalid memory.low".to_string())?;
        self.verify(parent, expected)?;
        let current = openat(self.dir.as_raw_fd(), c"memory.low", libc::O_RDONLY)?;
        if id(&current)? != id(&control)? {
            return Err("memory.low identity changed".into());
        }
        Ok(value)
    }
    // Only called after the containing session validates its held binding and hierarchy.
    pub(crate) fn create(parent: &File, expected: FileId) -> Result<Self, String> {
        crate::transaction::physical_mutation_disabled()?;
        let mut random = [0u8; 16];
        File::open("/dev/urandom")
            .and_then(|mut f| f.read_exact(&mut random))
            .map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
        let name = CString::new(format!(
            "xynoos-test-{}",
            random
                .iter()
                .map(|b| format!("{b:02x}"))
                .collect::<String>()
        ))
        .unwrap();
        Self::create_named(parent, expected, name, |_, _| Ok(()))
    }
    fn create_named(
        parent: &File,
        expected: FileId,
        name: CString,
        after_mkdir: impl FnOnce(&File, &CStr) -> Result<(), String>,
    ) -> Result<Self, String> {
        check_parent(parent, expected)?;
        let pm = parent.metadata().map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
        // SAFETY: geteuid has no preconditions.
        let uid = unsafe { libc::geteuid() };
        if uid == 0 || pm.uid() != uid || pm.mode() & 0o022 != 0 {
            return Err("unsafe parent ownership or permissions".into());
        }
        // SAFETY: held directory and single-component name are valid.
        if unsafe { libc::mkdirat(parent.as_raw_fd(), name.as_ptr(), 0o700) } != 0 {
            return Err(format!(
                "child creation failed: {}",
                std::io::Error::last_os_error()
            ));
        }
        // No cleanup on failure: identity/ownership may be unknown or another writer may race us.
        let child_name = name.to_string_lossy().into_owned();
        let result: Result<Self, String> = (|| {
            after_mkdir(parent, &name)?;
            let dir = openat(
                parent.as_raw_fd(),
                &name,
                libc::O_RDONLY | libc::O_DIRECTORY,
            )?;
            let m = dir.metadata().map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
            if m.uid() != uid || m.dev() != expected.dev || m.mode() & 0o077 != 0 {
                return Err("unsafe child ownership, device or permissions".into());
            }
            let child = Self {
                name,
                dir,
                identity: FileId {
                    dev: m.dev(),
                    ino: m.ino(),
                },
                uid,
            };
            child.verify(parent, expected)?;
            Ok(child)
        })();
        result.map_err(|e| format!("child {child_name} may remain; cleanup not attempted: {e}"))
    }
    pub(crate) fn verify(&self, parent: &File, expected: FileId) -> Result<(), String> {
        check_parent(parent, expected)?;
        let current = openat(
            parent.as_raw_fd(),
            &self.name,
            libc::O_RDONLY | libc::O_DIRECTORY,
        )?;
        let m = current.metadata().map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
        let held = self.dir.metadata().map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
        if id(&self.dir)? != self.identity
            || id(&current)? != self.identity
            || m.uid() != self.uid
            || held.uid() != self.uid
            || m.mode() & 0o077 != 0
            || held.mode() & 0o077 != 0
            || m.dev() != expected.dev
        {
            return Err("child identity or ownership changed".into());
        }
        if !read_at(&current, c"cgroup.procs")?.trim().is_empty()
            || !no_descendants(&read_at(&current, c"cgroup.stat")?)
        {
            return Err("child populated or has descendants".into());
        }
        Ok(())
    }
    pub(crate) fn controllers(&self, parent: &File, expected: FileId) -> Result<String, String> {
        self.verify(parent, expected)?;
        let value = read_at(&self.dir, c"cgroup.controllers")?;
        self.verify(parent, expected)?;
        Ok(value)
    }
    pub(crate) fn remove(&self, parent: &File, expected: FileId) -> Result<(), String> {
        crate::transaction::physical_mutation_disabled()?;
        self.remove_with(parent, expected, |parent, name| {
            // SAFETY: checked parent and name; rmdir also refuses populated cgroups.
            if unsafe { libc::unlinkat(parent.as_raw_fd(), name.as_ptr(), libc::AT_REMOVEDIR) } != 0
            {
                return Err(format!(
                    "child removal failed: {}",
                    std::io::Error::last_os_error()
                ));
            }
            Ok(())
        })
    }
    fn remove_with(
        &self,
        parent: &File,
        expected: FileId,
        remove: impl FnOnce(&File, &CStr) -> Result<(), String>,
    ) -> Result<(), String> {
        self.verify(parent, expected)?;
        remove(parent, &self.name)?;
        match openat(
            parent.as_raw_fd(),
            &self.name,
            libc::O_RDONLY | libc::O_DIRECTORY,
        ) {
            Ok(_) => Err("child name present after removal".into()),
            Err(_) => {
                // Distinguish ENOENT from permission errors, symlinks and other failures.
                let mut st = std::mem::MaybeUninit::<libc::stat>::uninit();
                // SAFETY: valid descriptor, name and output buffer.
                let rc = unsafe {
                    libc::fstatat(
                        parent.as_raw_fd(),
                        self.name.as_ptr(),
                        st.as_mut_ptr(),
                        libc::AT_SYMLINK_NOFOLLOW,
                    )
                };
                if rc == -1 && std::io::Error::last_os_error().raw_os_error() == Some(libc::ENOENT)
                {
                    Ok(())
                } else {
                    Err("child removal read-back ambiguous".into())
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;
    use std::{
        fs,
        os::unix::fs::{symlink, PermissionsExt},
        path::PathBuf,
        sync::atomic::{AtomicUsize, Ordering},
    };
    static NEXT: AtomicUsize = AtomicUsize::new(0);
    struct Fixture {
        path: PathBuf,
        parent: File,
        id: FileId,
    }
    impl Fixture {
        fn new() -> Self {
            let path = std::env::temp_dir().join(format!(
                "xynoos-bound-child-{}-{}",
                std::process::id(),
                NEXT.fetch_add(1, Ordering::Relaxed)
            ));
            fs::create_dir(&path).unwrap();
            fs::set_permissions(&path, fs::Permissions::from_mode(0o700)).unwrap();
            let parent = File::open(&path).unwrap();
            let id = id(&parent).unwrap();
            Self { path, parent, id }
        }
        fn create(&self) -> Result<BoundChild, String> {
            let path = self.path.join("child");
            BoundChild::create_named(
                &self.parent,
                self.id,
                CString::new("child").unwrap(),
                move |_, _| {
                    fs::write(path.join("cgroup.procs"), "").map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
                    fs::write(path.join("cgroup.stat"), "nr_descendants 0\n")
                        .map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
                    fs::write(path.join("cgroup.controllers"), "cpu memory pids\n")
                        .map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
                    Ok(())
                },
            )
        }
        fn remove(&self, child: &BoundChild) -> Result<(), String> {
            let path = self.path.join("child");
            child.remove_with(&self.parent, self.id, move |_, _| {
                fs::remove_dir_all(path).map_err(|e| e.to_string())
            })
        }
    }
    impl Drop for Fixture {
        fn drop(&mut self) {
            let _ = fs::remove_dir_all(&self.path);
        }
    }
    #[test]
    fn creation_inspection_and_verified_removal() {
        let f = Fixture::new();
        let child = f.create().unwrap();
        assert!(child
            .controllers(&f.parent, f.id)
            .unwrap()
            .split_whitespace()
            .any(|c| c == "memory"));
        f.remove(&child).unwrap();
        assert!(!f.path.join("child").exists());
    }
    #[test]
    fn memory_low_read_is_descriptor_bound_and_rejects_bad_files() {
        let f = Fixture::new();
        let child = f.create().unwrap();
        let path = f.path.join("child/memory.low");
        assert_eq!(child.identity_matches(&f.parent, f.id), Ok(true));
        assert!(child.read_low(&f.parent, f.id).is_err());
        fs::write(&path, "8192\n").unwrap();
        assert_eq!(child.read_low(&f.parent, f.id).unwrap(), 8192);
        fs::write(&path, "1".repeat(33)).unwrap();
        assert!(child.read_low(&f.parent, f.id).is_err());
        fs::remove_file(&path).unwrap();
        symlink("cgroup.procs", &path).unwrap();
        assert!(child.read_low(&f.parent, f.id).is_err());
        fs::remove_file(&path).unwrap();
        fs::create_dir(&path).unwrap();
        assert!(child.read_low(&f.parent, f.id).is_err());
        fs::remove_dir(&path).unwrap();
        fs::write(&path, "4096").unwrap();
        fs::rename(f.path.join("child"), f.path.join("old")).unwrap();
        fs::create_dir(f.path.join("child")).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(child.identity_matches(&f.parent, f.id), Ok(false));
        assert!(child.read_low(&f.parent, f.id).is_err());
    }
    #[test]
    fn wrong_parent_and_duplicate_refused() {
        let f = Fixture::new();
        let other = Fixture::new();
        assert!(BoundChild::create_named(
            &other.parent,
            f.id,
            CString::new("child").unwrap(),
            |_, _| Ok(())
        )
        .is_err());
        assert!(BoundChild::create_named(
            &f.parent,
            FileId {
                ino: f.id.ino + 1,
                ..f.id
            },
            CString::new("child").unwrap(),
            |_, _| Ok(())
        )
        .is_err());
        let _child = f.create().unwrap();
        assert!(f.create().is_err());
    }
    #[test]
    fn population_descendants_and_identity_changes_refuse_cleanup() {
        let f = Fixture::new();
        let child = f.create().unwrap();
        let path = f.path.join("child");
        fs::write(path.join("cgroup.procs"), "42\n").unwrap();
        assert!(f.remove(&child).is_err());
        fs::write(path.join("cgroup.procs"), "").unwrap();
        fs::write(path.join("cgroup.stat"), "nr_descendants 1\n").unwrap();
        assert!(f.remove(&child).is_err());
        fs::write(path.join("cgroup.stat"), "nr_descendants 0\n").unwrap();
        fs::set_permissions(&path, fs::Permissions::from_mode(0o777)).unwrap();
        assert!(f.remove(&child).is_err());
        fs::set_permissions(&path, fs::Permissions::from_mode(0o700)).unwrap();
        fs::set_permissions(&f.path, fs::Permissions::from_mode(0o777)).unwrap();
        assert!(f.remove(&child).is_err());
        fs::set_permissions(&f.path, fs::Permissions::from_mode(0o700)).unwrap();
        fs::rename(&path, f.path.join("old")).unwrap();
        fs::create_dir(&path).unwrap();
        fs::set_permissions(&path, fs::Permissions::from_mode(0o700)).unwrap();
        assert!(f.remove(&child).is_err());
        fs::remove_dir(&path).unwrap();
        symlink(f.path.join("old"), &path).unwrap();
        assert!(f.remove(&child).is_err());
    }
    #[test]
    fn symlink_duplicate_and_removal_failure_are_preserved() {
        let f = Fixture::new();
        symlink("elsewhere", f.path.join("child")).unwrap();
        assert!(f.create().is_err());
        fs::remove_file(f.path.join("child")).unwrap();
        let child = f.create().unwrap();
        assert!(child
            .remove_with(&f.parent, f.id, |_, _| Err(
                "injected removal failure".into()
            ))
            .is_err());
        assert!(f.path.join("child").is_dir());
        // Real rmdir against fixture files fails; no automatic retry occurs.
        assert!(child.remove(&f.parent, f.id).is_err());
    }
}


FILE crates/xynoos-linux-control/src/child_memory.rs
//! Guarded memory.low access for a session-owned empty child.
//! The production writer is deliberately disabled; the protocol is exercised with fixtures.
use crate::MAX_LOW_BYTES;

/// Identity of the registered child, derived from its held directory descriptor.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub struct ChildIdentity {
    pub device: u64,
    pub inode: u64,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub enum ErrorKind {
    RefusedBeforeWrite,
    WriteOutcomeAmbiguous,
    ReadBackMismatch,
    IdentityConflict,
    CompetingValue,
    RestorationFailure,
}

/// Diagnostic state is retained even when a restoration wraps another failure.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub struct MemoryLowError {
    pub kind: ErrorKind,
    pub message: String,
    /// Last successfully observed value; it may predate an attempted write.
    pub observed: Option<u64>,
    pub cause: Option<Box<MemoryLowError>>,
}
impl MemoryLowError {
    fn new(kind: ErrorKind, message: impl Into<String>, observed: Option<u64>) -> Self {
        Self {
            kind,
            message: message.into(),
            observed,
            cause: None,
        }
    }
    fn restoration(self) -> Self {
        Self {
            kind: ErrorKind::RestorationFailure,
            message: "memory.low restoration failed".into(),
            observed: self.observed,
            cause: Some(Box::new(self)),
        }
    }
}

// Every method validates or uses the same held child; a fixture supplies deterministic I/O.
pub(crate) trait Access {
    fn verify(&mut self, child: ChildIdentity) -> Result<(), String>;
    fn read(&mut self) -> Result<u64, String>;
    fn prepare_write(&mut self) -> Result<(), String>;
    // An error or short write after this call begins is always ambiguous.
    fn write(&mut self, bytes: &[u8]) -> Result<usize, String>;
    fn write_enabled(&self) -> bool;
}

pub(crate) fn read<A: Access>(io: &mut A, child: ChildIdentity) -> Result<u64, MemoryLowError> {
    io.verify(child)
        .map_err(|e| MemoryLowError::new(ErrorKind::IdentityConflict, e, None))?;
    let value = io
        .read()
        .map_err(|e| MemoryLowError::new(ErrorKind::RefusedBeforeWrite, e, None))?;
    io.verify(child)
        .map_err(|e| MemoryLowError::new(ErrorKind::IdentityConflict, e, Some(value)))?;
    Ok(value)
}

pub(crate) fn change<A: Access>(
    io: &mut A,
    unresolved: &mut bool,
    child: ChildIdentity,
    expected: u64,
    value: u64,
    authorized: bool,
    restoring: bool,
) -> Result<u64, MemoryLowError> {
    let result = change_inner(io, unresolved, child, expected, value, authorized);
    if restoring {
        result.map_err(MemoryLowError::restoration)
    } else {
        result
    }
}
fn change_inner<A: Access>(
    io: &mut A,
    unresolved: &mut bool,
    child: ChildIdentity,
    expected: u64,
    value: u64,
    authorized: bool,
) -> Result<u64, MemoryLowError> {
    if *unresolved
        || !authorized
        || !io.write_enabled()
        || value > MAX_LOW_BYTES
        || !value.is_multiple_of(4096)
    {
        return Err(MemoryLowError::new(
            ErrorKind::RefusedBeforeWrite,
            "authorization, bounds, disabled writer, or unresolved previous write",
            None,
        ));
    }
    let observed = read(io, child)?;
    if observed != expected {
        return Err(MemoryLowError::new(
            ErrorKind::CompetingValue,
            "memory.low differs from expected value",
            Some(observed),
        ));
    }
    io.prepare_write()
        .map_err(|e| MemoryLowError::new(ErrorKind::RefusedBeforeWrite, e, Some(observed)))?;
    io.verify(child)
        .map_err(|e| MemoryLowError::new(ErrorKind::IdentityConflict, e, Some(observed)))?;
    let current = io
        .read()
        .map_err(|e| MemoryLowError::new(ErrorKind::RefusedBeforeWrite, e, Some(observed)))?;
    if current != expected {
        return Err(MemoryLowError::new(
            ErrorKind::CompetingValue,
            "memory.low changed before write",
            Some(current),
        ));
    }
    io.verify(child)
        .map_err(|e| MemoryLowError::new(ErrorKind::IdentityConflict, e, Some(current)))?;
    // Latch before entering the syscall: all errors after this point require investigation.
    *unresolved = true;
    let text = value.to_string();
    let n = io
        .write(text.as_bytes())
        .map_err(|e| MemoryLowError::new(ErrorKind::WriteOutcomeAmbiguous, e, Some(observed)))?;
    if n != text.len() {
        return Err(MemoryLowError::new(
            ErrorKind::WriteOutcomeAmbiguous,
            "short memory.low write",
            Some(observed),
        ));
    }
    io.verify(child)
        .map_err(|e| MemoryLowError::new(ErrorKind::IdentityConflict, e, Some(observed)))?;
    let actual = io.read().map_err(|e| {
        MemoryLowError::new(
            ErrorKind::WriteOutcomeAmbiguous,
            format!("read-back failed: {e}"),
            Some(observed),
        )
    })?;
    io.verify(child)
        .map_err(|e| MemoryLowError::new(ErrorKind::IdentityConflict, e, Some(actual)))?;
    if actual != value {
        return Err(MemoryLowError::new(
            ErrorKind::ReadBackMismatch,
            "memory.low read-back differs",
            Some(actual),
        ));
    }
    *unresolved = false;
    Ok(actual)
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;
    const CHILD: ChildIdentity = ChildIdentity {
        device: 1,
        inode: 2,
    };
    struct Fixture {
        parent_ok: bool,
        child: ChildIdentity,
        value: Option<u64>,
        permission: bool,
        mode: &'static str,
        writes: usize,
        reads: usize,
    }
    impl Fixture {
        fn new() -> Self {
            Self {
                parent_ok: true,
                child: CHILD,
                value: Some(4096),
                permission: true,
                mode: "ok",
                writes: 0,
                reads: 0,
            }
        }
    }
    impl Access for Fixture {
        fn verify(&mut self, child: ChildIdentity) -> Result<(), String> {
            if !self.parent_ok || self.child != child {
                Err("parent or child identity changed".into())
            } else {
                Ok(())
            }
        }
        fn read(&mut self) -> Result<u64, String> {
            self.reads += 1;
            if self.mode == "compete" && self.reads == 2 {
                self.value = Some(16384);
            }
            self.value.ok_or("missing memory.low".into())
        }
        fn prepare_write(&mut self) -> Result<(), String> {
            if self.permission {
                Ok(())
            } else {
                Err("permission denied".into())
            }
        }
        fn write(&mut self, bytes: &[u8]) -> Result<usize, String> {
            self.writes += 1;
            self.value = Some(std::str::from_utf8(bytes).unwrap().parse().unwrap());
            match self.mode {
                "error" => Err("injected failure after possible mutation".into()),
                "short" => Ok(bytes.len() - 1),
                "mismatch" => {
                    self.value = Some(12288);
                    Ok(bytes.len())
                }
                _ => Ok(bytes.len()),
            }
        }
        fn write_enabled(&self) -> bool {
            self.mode != "disabled"
        }
    }
    fn apply(f: &mut Fixture, latch: &mut bool) -> Result<u64, MemoryLowError> {
        change(f, latch, CHILD, 4096, 8192, true, false)
    }
    #[test]
    fn successful_read_and_write_readback() {
        let mut f = Fixture::new();
        assert_eq!(read(&mut f, CHILD), Ok(4096));
        let mut latch = false;
        assert_eq!(apply(&mut f, &mut latch), Ok(8192));
        assert!(!latch);
        assert_eq!(f.writes, 1);
    }
    #[test]
    fn refuses_authorization_identity_missing_and_permission() {
        let mut f = Fixture::new();
        let mut latch = false;
        assert_eq!(
            change(&mut f, &mut latch, CHILD, 4096, 8192, false, false)
                .unwrap_err()
                .kind,
            ErrorKind::RefusedBeforeWrite
        );
        assert_eq!(
            read(&mut f, ChildIdentity { inode: 3, ..CHILD })
                .unwrap_err()
                .kind,
            ErrorKind::IdentityConflict
        );
        f.parent_ok = false;
        assert_eq!(
            read(&mut f, CHILD).unwrap_err().kind,
            ErrorKind::IdentityConflict
        );
        f.parent_ok = true;
        f.child.inode = 3;
        assert_eq!(
            read(&mut f, CHILD).unwrap_err().kind,
            ErrorKind::IdentityConflict
        );
        f.child = CHILD;
        f.value = None;
        assert_eq!(
            read(&mut f, CHILD).unwrap_err().kind,
            ErrorKind::RefusedBeforeWrite
        );
        f.value = Some(4096);
        f.permission = false;
        assert_eq!(
            apply(&mut f, &mut latch).unwrap_err().kind,
            ErrorKind::RefusedBeforeWrite
        );
        assert_eq!(f.writes, 0);
        assert!(!latch);
        f.permission = true;
        f.mode = "disabled";
        assert_eq!(
            apply(&mut f, &mut latch).unwrap_err().kind,
            ErrorKind::RefusedBeforeWrite
        );
        assert_eq!(f.writes, 0);
        f.mode = "ok";
        assert_eq!(
            change(
                &mut f,
                &mut latch,
                CHILD,
                4096,
                MAX_LOW_BYTES + 4096,
                true,
                false
            )
            .unwrap_err()
            .kind,
            ErrorKind::RefusedBeforeWrite
        );
        assert_eq!(f.writes, 0);
    }
    #[test]
    fn ambiguous_short_mismatch_and_no_retry() {
        for (mode, kind) in [
            ("error", ErrorKind::WriteOutcomeAmbiguous),
            ("short", ErrorKind::WriteOutcomeAmbiguous),
            ("mismatch", ErrorKind::ReadBackMismatch),
        ] {
            let mut f = Fixture::new();
            f.mode = mode;
            let mut latch = false;
            let error = apply(&mut f, &mut latch).unwrap_err();
            assert_eq!(error.kind, kind, "{mode}");
            assert!(latch);
            assert_eq!(
                apply(&mut f, &mut latch).unwrap_err().kind,
                ErrorKind::RefusedBeforeWrite
            );
            assert_eq!(f.writes, 1);
            if mode == "mismatch" {
                assert_eq!(error.observed, Some(12288));
            }
        }
    }
    #[test]
    fn competing_value_and_restoration_failure_are_reported() {
        let mut f = Fixture::new();
        f.mode = "compete";
        let mut latch = false;
        let e = apply(&mut f, &mut latch).unwrap_err();
        assert_eq!(
            (e.kind, e.observed),
            (ErrorKind::CompetingValue, Some(16384))
        );
        assert_eq!(f.writes, 0);
        let mut f = Fixture::new();
        f.mode = "error";
        let e = change(&mut f, &mut latch, CHILD, 4096, 8192, true, true).unwrap_err();
        assert_eq!(e.kind, ErrorKind::RestorationFailure);
        assert_eq!(e.cause.unwrap().kind, ErrorKind::WriteOutcomeAmbiguous);
        assert!(latch);
    }
}


FILE crates/xynoos-linux-control/src/controller.rs
//! Guarded controller activation and restoration. Checks and writes are not atomic;
//! recorded state is in memory only and cannot provide crash recovery.
use std::collections::BTreeSet;

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub struct ParentIdentity {
    pub device: u64,
    pub inode: u64,
}

/// All reads and writes must be relative to the same verified directory.
/// A backend must reject replacement or loss of the designated unit binding.
trait ControllerBackend {
    fn verify_unit(&mut self) -> Result<ParentIdentity, String>;
    fn processes(&mut self) -> Result<String, String>;
    fn available(&mut self) -> Result<String, String>;
    fn enabled(&mut self) -> Result<String, String>;
