        };
        if !enabled {
            return Err(fault(FaultKind::Before, "live adapter mutations disabled"));
        }
        self.evidence_capacity()?;
        self.safety(stage, recovery_stage(stage)).map_err(|mut e| {
            if recovery_stage(stage) {
                e.kind = FaultKind::Unavailable;
            }
            e
        })?;
        self.session
            .verify_for_mutation()
            .map_err(|e| fault(FaultKind::Unavailable, e))?;
        Ok(MutationPermit { _private: () })
    }
    fn mutation(
        &mut self,
        stage: Stage,
        result: Result<MutationOutcome, MutationOutcome>,
    ) -> Result<(), Fault> {
        let (ok, outcome) = match result {
            Ok(o) => (true, o),
            Err(o) => (false, o),
        };
        let kind = match outcome.status {
            MutationStatus::AppliedAndVerified => FaultKind::Unavailable,
            MutationStatus::RefusedBeforeMutation
            | MutationStatus::CleanupRequired
            | MutationStatus::RestorationFailure => FaultKind::Before,
            MutationStatus::Conflict => FaultKind::Conflict,
            MutationStatus::AmbiguousMutation => FaultKind::Ambiguous,
        };
        let message = outcome.to_string();
        self.mutation_evidence.push((stage, outcome));
        if ok {
            Ok(())
        } else {
            Err(fault(kind, message))
        }
    }
    fn child(&mut self, identity: &str) -> Result<ChildIdentity, Fault> {
        let c = self
            .session
            .registered_child_identity()
            .map_err(|e| fault(FaultKind::Unavailable, e))?;
        if child_key(c) != identity {
            return Err(fault(FaultKind::Conflict, "registered child mismatch"));
        }
        Ok(c)
    }
    fn active_controllers(&mut self) -> Result<(), Fault> {
        let mut expected: BTreeSet<_> = self
            .session
            .original_controllers()
            .iter()
            .cloned()
            .collect();
        expected.insert("memory".into());
        let current = self
            .session
            .backend
            .enabled()
            .map_err(|e| fault(FaultKind::Unavailable, e))?;
        if controller_set(current).map_err(|e| fault(FaultKind::Conflict, e))? != expected {
            return Err(fault(FaultKind::Conflict, "active controller set changed"));
        }
        Ok(())
    }
    fn low_error(&mut self, stage: Stage, error: MemoryLowError) -> Fault {
        // A post-write identity failure is ambiguous even though its typed cause
        // is IdentityConflict. Never let the coordinator recover through a latch.
        let kind = if self.session.low_unresolved {
            FaultKind::Ambiguous
        } else {
            low_kind(&error)
        };
        let message = error.message.clone();
        self.low_errors.push((stage, error));
        fault(kind, message)
    }
}
impl Backend for ControllerSessionAdapter {
    fn inspect(&mut self, stage: Stage) -> Result<Observation, Fault> {
        self.evidence_capacity()?;
        let o = self.session.observe();
        self.session_evidence.push((stage, o.clone()));
        if o.activation_unresolved || o.child_unresolved || o.memory_low_unresolved {
            return Err(fault(FaultKind::Ambiguous, "session mutation unresolved"));
        }
        if o.validity != ObservationValidity::Valid
            || o.completeness != ObservationCompleteness::Complete
        {
            return Err(fault(
                if o.validity == ObservationValidity::Invalid {
                    FaultKind::Conflict
                } else {
                    FaultKind::Unavailable
                },
                "session observation invalid or incomplete",
            ));
        }
        self.session
            .verify_for_mutation()
            .map_err(|e| fault(FaultKind::Unavailable, e))?;
        // Inspect still collects evidence after a refusal; it never clears the latch.
        let safety_satisfied = self.safety(stage, false).is_ok();
        let child = if let Some(c) = o.registered_child {
            let inherited = self
                .session
                .child_controllers()
                .map_err(|e| fault(FaultKind::Unavailable, e))?
                .split_whitespace()
                .any(|c| c == "memory");
            let low = self
                .session
                .read_child_memory_low(c.identity)
                .map_err(|e| self.low_error(stage, e))?;
            Some(CoordinatorChild {
                identity: child_key(c.identity),
                empty: c.population.as_ref().is_ok_and(Vec::is_empty),
                // The mandatory strict verifier above checks zero child descendants.
                descendants: 0,
                memory_inherited: inherited,
                low: Some(low),
            })
        } else {
            None
        };
        let id = o.identity;
        // Include the complete historical binding identity, not just a pathname.
        let binding = format!("{:?}", id);
        Ok(Observation {
            binding,
            parent_empty: o.parent_population.as_ref().is_ok_and(Vec::is_empty),
            safety_satisfied,
            available_controllers: o
                .available_controllers
                .map_err(|e| fault(FaultKind::Unavailable, e))?,
            controllers: o
                .enabled_controllers
                .map_err(|e| fault(FaultKind::Unavailable, e))?,
            child,
        })
    }
    fn activate(&mut self, expected: &BTreeSet<String>) -> Result<(), Fault> {
        let permit = self.guard(Stage::Activate)?;
        let o = self.session.observe();
        if o.enabled_controllers.as_ref().ok() != Some(expected) {
            return Err(fault(FaultKind::Conflict, "controller capture changed"));
        }
        let result = self.session.activate_prepared(permit);
        self.mutation(Stage::Activate, result)
    }
    fn create_child(&mut self) -> Result<String, Fault> {
        let permit = self.guard(Stage::CreateChild)?;
        self.active_controllers()?;
        let result = self.session.create_empty_child(permit);
        self.mutation(Stage::CreateChild, result)?;
        let child = self
            .session
            .registered_child_identity()
            .map_err(|e| fault(FaultKind::Ambiguous, e))?;
        // Capture independently of coordinator progress so direct trait calls cannot
        // remove a child whose low value was modified and never restored.
        let low = self
            .session
            .read_child_memory_low(child)
            .map_err(|e| self.low_error(Stage::CreateChild, e))?;
        self.child_original_low = Some((child, low));
        Ok(child_key(child))
    }
    fn write_low(
        &mut self,
        stage: Stage,
        identity: &str,
        expected: u64,
        value: u64,
    ) -> Result<(), Fault> {
        if !matches!(
            stage,
            Stage::ApplyLow | Stage::RestoreLow | Stage::RecoveryRestoreLow
        ) {
            return Err(fault(FaultKind::Before, "invalid memory.low stage"));
        }
        let permit = self.guard(stage)?;
        let child = self.child(identity)?;
        let restoring = matches!(stage, Stage::RestoreLow | Stage::RecoveryRestoreLow);
        self.session
            .change_child_memory_low(permit, child, expected, value, true, restoring)
            .map(|_| ())
            .map_err(|e| self.low_error(stage, e))
    }
    fn remove_child(&mut self, stage: Stage, identity: &str) -> Result<(), Fault> {
        if !matches!(stage, Stage::RemoveChild | Stage::RecoveryRemoveChild) {
            return Err(fault(FaultKind::Before, "invalid removal stage"));
        }
        let permit = self.guard(stage)?;
        let child = self.child(identity)?;
        self.active_controllers()?;
        let low = self
            .session
            .read_child_memory_low(child)
            .map_err(|e| self.low_error(stage, e))?;
        if self.child_original_low != Some((child, low)) {
            return Err(fault(
                FaultKind::Conflict,
                "original child memory.low not verified before removal",
            ));
        }
        let result = self.session.remove_empty_child(permit);
        self.mutation(stage, result)
    }
    fn restore_controllers(
        &mut self,
        stage: Stage,
        original: &BTreeSet<String>,
    ) -> Result<(), Fault> {
        if !matches!(
            stage,
            Stage::RestoreControllers | Stage::RecoveryRestoreControllers
        ) {
            return Err(fault(
                FaultKind::Before,
                "invalid controller restoration stage",
            ));
        }
        let permit = self.guard(stage)?;
        if self
            .session
            .original_controllers()
            .iter()
            .cloned()
            .collect::<BTreeSet<_>>()
            != *original
        {
            return Err(fault(
                FaultKind::Conflict,
                "original controller record mismatch",
            ));
        }
        let result = self.session.restore(permit, true);
        self.mutation(stage, result)
    }
}


FILE crates/xynoos-linux-control/src/controller/session_io.rs
//! Private cgroup I/O boundary. Production construction requires a live Binding;
//! fixture implementations exist only in unit tests. No caller-supplied paths.
use super::ParentIdentity;
use crate::{bound_child::FileId, child_memory::ChildIdentity, systemd_identity::BoundIdentity};
use std::ffi::CStr;

// Policy, authorization, hierarchy checks and unresolved latches stay in the
// controller/session, above this interface. Child methods retain BoundChild checks.
pub(super) trait SessionIo: Send + Sync {
    fn bound_identity(&self) -> BoundIdentity;
    fn diagnostic(&self) -> serde_json::Value;
    fn revalidate(&self) -> Result<(), String>;
    fn read_parent(&self, name: &CStr) -> Result<String, String>;
    fn read_manager(&self, name: &CStr) -> Result<String, String>;
    fn write_controller(&mut self, command: &[u8]) -> Result<(), String>;
    fn registered_child(&self) -> Option<ChildIdentity>;
    fn child_identity_matches(&self) -> Result<bool, String>;
    fn verify_child(&self, parent: ParentIdentity) -> Result<(), String>;
    fn child_population(&self) -> Result<String, String>;
    fn read_low(&self) -> Result<u64, String>;
    fn child_controllers(&self, parent: ParentIdentity) -> Result<String, String>;
    fn create_child(&mut self, parent: ParentIdentity) -> Result<ChildIdentity, String>;
    fn remove_child(&mut self, parent: ParentIdentity) -> Result<(), String>;

    // These hooks do not exist in production builds; DescriptorIo never opts in.
    #[cfg(test)]
    fn fixture_write_enabled(&self) -> bool {
        false
    }
    #[cfg(test)]
    fn fixture_prepare_low(&mut self) -> Result<(), String> {
        Err("fixture writer disabled".into())
    }
    #[cfg(test)]
    fn fixture_write_low(&mut self, _bytes: &[u8]) -> Result<usize, String> {
        Err("fixture writer disabled".into())
    }
    #[cfg(test)]
    fn as_any_mut(&mut self) -> &mut dyn std::any::Any;
}

pub(super) struct DescriptorIo {
    pub(super) binding: crate::systemd_identity::Binding,
    pub(super) child: Option<crate::bound_child::BoundChild>,
}
impl DescriptorIo {
    pub(super) fn new(binding: crate::systemd_identity::Binding) -> Self {
        Self {
            binding,
            child: None,
        }
    }
    fn parent_id(&self) -> FileId {
        let (dev, ino) = self.binding.parent_identity();
        FileId { dev, ino }
    }
    fn child(&self) -> Result<&crate::bound_child::BoundChild, String> {
        self.child.as_ref().ok_or("no registered child".into())
    }
}
fn file_id(parent: ParentIdentity) -> FileId {
    FileId {
        dev: parent.device,
        ino: parent.inode,
    }
}
impl SessionIo for DescriptorIo {
    fn bound_identity(&self) -> BoundIdentity {
        self.binding.bound_identity()
    }
    fn diagnostic(&self) -> serde_json::Value {
        self.binding.diagnostic()
    }
    fn revalidate(&self) -> Result<(), String> {
        self.binding.revalidate()
    }
    fn read_parent(&self, name: &CStr) -> Result<String, String> {
        crate::transaction::read_at(self.binding.parent(), name)
    }
    fn read_manager(&self, name: &CStr) -> Result<String, String> {
        crate::transaction::read_at(self.binding.manager(), name)
    }
    fn write_controller(&mut self, command: &[u8]) -> Result<(), String> {
        crate::transaction::physical_mutation_disabled()?;
        use std::os::{fd::AsRawFd, unix::fs::FileExt};
        let f = crate::transaction::openat(
            self.binding.parent().as_raw_fd(),
            c"cgroup.subtree_control",
            libc::O_WRONLY,
        )?;
        let n = f.write_at(command, 0).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
        if n != command.len() {
            return Err("short controller write".into());
        }
        Ok(())
    }
    fn registered_child(&self) -> Option<ChildIdentity> {
        self.child.as_ref().map(|child| child.identity())
    }
    fn child_identity_matches(&self) -> Result<bool, String> {
        self.child()?
            .identity_matches(self.binding.parent(), self.parent_id())
    }
    fn verify_child(&self, parent: ParentIdentity) -> Result<(), String> {
        self.child()?.verify(self.binding.parent(), file_id(parent))
    }
    fn child_population(&self) -> Result<String, String> {
        self.child()?
            .population(self.binding.parent(), self.parent_id())
    }
    fn read_low(&self) -> Result<u64, String> {
        self.child()?
            .read_low(self.binding.parent(), self.parent_id())
    }
    fn child_controllers(&self, parent: ParentIdentity) -> Result<String, String> {
        self.child()?
            .controllers(self.binding.parent(), file_id(parent))
    }
    fn create_child(&mut self, parent: ParentIdentity) -> Result<ChildIdentity, String> {
        crate::transaction::physical_mutation_disabled()?;
        let child = crate::bound_child::BoundChild::create(self.binding.parent(), file_id(parent))?;
        let identity = child.identity();
        self.child = Some(child);
        Ok(identity)
    }
    fn remove_child(&mut self, parent: ParentIdentity) -> Result<(), String> {
        crate::transaction::physical_mutation_disabled()?;
        self.child()?
            .remove(self.binding.parent(), file_id(parent))?;
        self.child = None;
        Ok(())
    }
    #[cfg(test)]
    fn as_any_mut(&mut self) -> &mut dyn std::any::Any {
        self
    }
}


FILE crates/xynoos-linux-control/src/controller/session_io_tests.rs
//! These tests inject only I/O: ControllerSession, LinuxController::verify_unit,
//! SessionLow and the typed mutation orchestration are the production code paths.
//! No Binding, filesystem, systemd call, worker or kernel mutation is used here.
use super::*;
use crate::{child_memory::ChildIdentity, systemd_identity::BoundIdentity};
use std::{
    ffi::CStr,
    sync::{Arc, Mutex},
};

const PARENT: ParentIdentity = ParentIdentity {
    device: 7,
    inode: 11,
};
const CHILD: ChildIdentity = ChildIdentity {
    device: 7,
    inode: 13,
};
#[derive(Clone, Copy, Default)]
enum WriteMode {
    #[default]
    Apply,
    ErrorAfterApply,
    ShortWrite,
    Mismatch,
    ReadBackError,
}
struct State {
    enable_low: bool,
    low_writes: usize,
    low_mode: Option<(usize, &'static str)>,
    compete_low: bool,
    trace: Vec<String>,
    parent: ParentIdentity,
    enabled: String,
    available: String,
    parent_procs: String,
    manager_procs: String,
    extra_descendants: bool,
    manager_descendants: bool,
    child: Option<ChildIdentity>,
    child_entry: ChildIdentity,
    child_populated: bool,
    low: Result<u64, String>,
    controller_read_error: bool,
    change_on_reread: bool,
    enabled_reads: usize,
    revalidations: usize,
    identity_failure_after_write: bool,
    write_mode: WriteMode,
    readback_failed: bool,
    fail_create: bool,
    fail_remove: bool,
    writes: Vec<Vec<u8>>,
    creates: usize,
    removes: usize,
}
impl Default for State {
    fn default() -> Self {
        Self {
            enable_low: false,
            low_writes: 0,
            low_mode: None,
            compete_low: false,
            trace: Vec::new(),
            parent: PARENT,
            enabled: "cpu pids".into(),
            available: "cpu memory pids".into(),
            parent_procs: String::new(),
            manager_procs: std::process::id().to_string(),
            extra_descendants: false,
            manager_descendants: false,
            child: None,
            child_entry: CHILD,
            child_populated: false,
            low: Ok(4096),
            controller_read_error: false,
            change_on_reread: false,
            enabled_reads: 0,
            revalidations: 0,
            identity_failure_after_write: false,
            write_mode: WriteMode::Apply,
            readback_failed: false,
            fail_create: false,
            fail_remove: false,
            writes: Vec::new(),
            creates: 0,
            removes: 0,
        }
    }
}
struct FixtureIo(Arc<Mutex<State>>);
impl FixtureIo {
    fn check_child(s: &State) -> Result<(), String> {
        if s.parent != PARENT || s.child != Some(s.child_entry) || s.child_populated {
            return Err("injected child identity, ownership or population failure".into());
        }
        Ok(())
    }
}
impl SessionIo for FixtureIo {
    fn bound_identity(&self) -> BoundIdentity {
        BoundIdentity {
            unit_id: "in-memory-fixture.service".into(),
            invocation_id: vec![1; 16],
            manager_pid: std::process::id(),
            manager_start_ticks: 19,
            parent: (PARENT.device, PARENT.inode),
            manager: (7, 12),
        }
    }
    fn diagnostic(&self) -> serde_json::Value {
        serde_json::json!({"fixture": true})
    }
    fn revalidate(&self) -> Result<(), String> {
        let mut s = self.0.lock().unwrap();
        s.revalidations += 1;
        if s.parent != PARENT || (s.identity_failure_after_write && !s.writes.is_empty()) {
            return Err("injected binding identity failure".into());
        }
        Ok(())
    }
    fn read_parent(&self, name: &CStr) -> Result<String, String> {
        let mut s = self.0.lock().unwrap();
        match name.to_bytes() {
            b"cgroup.procs" => Ok(s.parent_procs.clone()),
            b"cgroup.stat" => Ok(format!(
                "nr_descendants {}",
                1 + usize::from(s.child.is_some()) + usize::from(s.extra_descendants)
            )),
            b"cgroup.controllers" => Ok(s.available.clone()),
            b"cgroup.subtree_control" => {
                s.enabled_reads += 1;
                if s.controller_read_error || s.readback_failed {
                    return Err("injected controller read failure".into());
                }
                if s.change_on_reread && s.enabled_reads == 2 {
                    s.enabled = "cpu".into();
                }
                Ok(s.enabled.clone())
            }
            _ => panic!("unexpected parent read {name:?}"),
        }
    }
    fn read_manager(&self, name: &CStr) -> Result<String, String> {
        let s = self.0.lock().unwrap();
        match name.to_bytes() {
            b"cgroup.procs" => Ok(s.manager_procs.clone()),
            b"cgroup.stat" => Ok(format!(
                "nr_descendants {}",
                usize::from(s.manager_descendants)
            )),
            _ => panic!("unexpected manager read {name:?}"),
        }
    }
    fn write_controller(&mut self, command: &[u8]) -> Result<(), String> {
        let mut s = self.0.lock().unwrap();
        s.trace.push(String::from_utf8(command.to_vec()).unwrap());
        s.writes.push(command.to_vec());
        s.readback_failed = matches!(s.write_mode, WriteMode::ReadBackError);
        if !matches!(s.write_mode, WriteMode::Mismatch) {
            s.enabled = match command {
                b"+memory" => "cpu memory pids".into(),
                b"-memory" => "cpu pids".into(),
                _ => panic!("unexpected controller command"),
            };
        }
        if matches!(
            s.write_mode,
            WriteMode::ErrorAfterApply | WriteMode::ShortWrite
        ) {
            return Err("injected error after possible write".into());
        }
        Ok(())
    }
    fn registered_child(&self) -> Option<ChildIdentity> {
        self.0.lock().unwrap().child
    }
    fn child_identity_matches(&self) -> Result<bool, String> {
        let s = self.0.lock().unwrap();
        Ok(s.child == Some(s.child_entry))
    }
    fn verify_child(&self, parent: ParentIdentity) -> Result<(), String> {
        assert_eq!(parent, PARENT);
        Self::check_child(&self.0.lock().unwrap())
    }
    fn child_population(&self) -> Result<String, String> {
        let s = self.0.lock().unwrap();
        if s.child != Some(s.child_entry) {
            return Err("injected child inode mismatch".into());
        }
        Ok(if s.child_populated {
            "99".into()
        } else {
            String::new()
        })
    }
    fn read_low(&self) -> Result<u64, String> {
        let s = self.0.lock().unwrap();
        Self::check_child(&s)?;
        s.low.clone()
    }
    fn child_controllers(&self, parent: ParentIdentity) -> Result<String, String> {
        self.verify_child(parent)?;
        Ok(self.0.lock().unwrap().available.clone())
    }
    fn create_child(&mut self, parent: ParentIdentity) -> Result<ChildIdentity, String> {
        assert_eq!(parent, PARENT);
        let mut s = self.0.lock().unwrap();
        assert!(s.child.is_none());
        s.trace.push("create".into());
        s.creates += 1;
        s.child = Some(CHILD);
        if s.fail_create {
            return Err("injected child creation failure after possible mkdir".into());
        }
        Ok(CHILD)
    }
    fn remove_child(&mut self, parent: ParentIdentity) -> Result<(), String> {
        self.verify_child(parent)?;
        let mut s = self.0.lock().unwrap();
        s.trace.push("remove".into());
        s.removes += 1;
        if s.fail_remove {
            return Err("injected child cleanup failure".into());
        }
        s.child = None;
        Ok(())
    }
    fn fixture_write_enabled(&self) -> bool {
        self.0.lock().unwrap().enable_low
    }
    fn fixture_prepare_low(&mut self) -> Result<(), String> {
        let mut s = self.0.lock().unwrap();
        if s.compete_low {
            s.low = Ok(12288);
            s.compete_low = false;
        }
        if s.low_mode == Some((s.low_writes + 1, "before")) {
            return Err("injected prepare refusal".into());
        }
        Ok(())
    }
    fn fixture_write_low(&mut self, bytes: &[u8]) -> Result<usize, String> {
        let mut s = self.0.lock().unwrap();
        s.low_writes += 1;
        let value = std::str::from_utf8(bytes).unwrap().parse::<u64>().unwrap();
        s.trace.push(format!("low:{value}"));
        s.low = Ok(value);
        let mode = s
            .low_mode
            .filter(|(n, _)| *n == s.low_writes)
            .map(|(_, m)| m);
        match mode {
            Some("error") => Err("injected low write error after effect".into()),
            Some("short") => Ok(bytes.len() - 1),
            Some("mismatch") => {
                s.low = Ok(12288);
                Ok(bytes.len())
            }
            Some("identity") => {
                s.child_entry.inode += 1;
                Ok(bytes.len())
            }
            Some("readback") => {
                s.low = Err("injected readback error".into());
                Ok(bytes.len())
            }
            _ => Ok(bytes.len()),
        }
    }
    fn as_any_mut(&mut self) -> &mut dyn std::any::Any {
        self
    }
}
struct Fixture {
    session: ControllerSession,
    state: Arc<Mutex<State>>,
}
impl Fixture {
    fn new() -> Self {
        let state = Arc::new(Mutex::new(State::default()));
        Self {
            session: ControllerSession {
                backend: LinuxController {
                    io: Box::new(FixtureIo(state.clone())),
                    // Test-only authority over in-memory state; no live Binding exists.
                    authorized: true,
                    child_unresolved: false,
                },
                activation: None,
                original_before_attempt: None,
                activation_unresolved: false,
                low_unresolved: false,
            },
            state,
        }
    }
    fn activate(&mut self) {
        applied(
            self.session
                .activate_prepared(session_adapter::fixture_permit())
                .unwrap(),
            MutationOperation::Activate,
        );
    }
    fn child(&mut self) {
        self.activate();
        applied(
            self.session
                .create_empty_child(session_adapter::fixture_permit())
                .unwrap(),
            MutationOperation::CreateChild,
        );
        assert_eq!(self.session.registered_child_identity(), Ok(CHILD));
    }
}
fn applied(outcome: MutationOutcome, operation: MutationOperation) {
    assert_eq!(outcome.operation, operation);
    assert_eq!(outcome.status, MutationStatus::AppliedAndVerified);
    assert_eq!(outcome.mutation_attempted, Some(true));
    assert!(outcome.verified);
    assert_eq!(outcome.expected, outcome.observed);
    assert!(outcome.error.is_none());
}
fn failed(
    outcome: &MutationOutcome,
    op: MutationOperation,
    status: MutationStatus,
    attempted: Option<bool>,
) {
    assert_eq!(outcome.operation, op);
    assert_eq!(outcome.status, status);
    assert_eq!(outcome.mutation_attempted, attempted);
    assert!(!outcome.verified);
    assert_eq!(outcome.error.as_ref().unwrap().operation, op);
}
fn observation(
    o: &SessionObservation,
    validity: ObservationValidity,
    completeness: ObservationCompleteness,
) {
    assert_eq!(o.validity, validity);
    assert_eq!(o.completeness, completeness);
}

#[test]
fn valid_session_observation_and_verified_lifecycle() {
    let mut f = Fixture::new();
    let o = f.session.observe();
    observation(
        &o,
        ObservationValidity::Valid,
        ObservationCompleteness::Complete,
    );
    assert_eq!(o.activation, ObservedActivation::NotActive);
    assert_eq!(o.parent_population, Ok(vec![]));
    assert_eq!(o.manager_population, Ok(vec![std::process::id()]));
    assert_eq!(
        o.enabled_controllers,
        Ok(BTreeSet::from(["cpu".into(), "pids".into()]))
    );
    assert!(o.registered_child.is_none() && o.issues.is_empty());
    assert_eq!(f.state.lock().unwrap().revalidations, 2);
    assert!(f.state.lock().unwrap().writes.is_empty());
    assert_eq!(f.session.verify_for_mutation(), Ok(PARENT));
    f.child();
    let o = f.session.observe();
    observation(
        &o,
        ObservationValidity::Valid,
        ObservationCompleteness::Complete,
    );
    assert_eq!(o.activation, ObservedActivation::Active);
    assert_eq!(o.registered_child.unwrap().memory_low, Ok(4096));
    assert_eq!(f.session.child_controllers(), Ok("cpu memory pids".into()));
    assert_eq!(f.session.read_child_memory_low(CHILD), Ok(4096));
    applied(
        f.session
            .remove_empty_child(session_adapter::fixture_permit())
            .unwrap(),
        MutationOperation::RemoveChild,
    );
    applied(
        f.session
            .restore(session_adapter::fixture_permit(), true)
            .unwrap(),
        MutationOperation::RestoreControllers,
    );
    observation(
        &f.session.observe(),
        ObservationValidity::Valid,
        ObservationCompleteness::Complete,
    );
    assert_eq!(
        f.state.lock().unwrap().writes,
        [b"+memory".to_vec(), b"-memory".to_vec()]
    );
}
#[test]
fn controller_read_failure_is_unknown_and_refuses_activation() {
    let mut f = Fixture::new();
    f.state.lock().unwrap().controller_read_error = true;
    let o = f.session.observe();
    observation(
        &o,
        ObservationValidity::Unknown,
        ObservationCompleteness::Incomplete,
    );

        &f.session
            .activate_prepared(session_adapter::fixture_permit())
            .unwrap_err(),

        MutationStatus::RefusedBeforeMutation,
        Some(false),
    );
    assert!(f.state.lock().unwrap().writes.is_empty());
}
#[test]
fn controller_change_between_observations_is_not_cached_authority() {
    let mut f = Fixture::new();
    observation(
        &f.session.observe(),
        ObservationValidity::Valid,
        ObservationCompleteness::Complete,
    );

        &o,
        ObservationValidity::Invalid,
        ObservationCompleteness::Complete,
    );
    assert_eq!(o.activation, ObservedActivation::NotActive);
    assert!(o.enabled_controllers.unwrap().contains("memory"));
    failed(
        &f.session
            .activate_prepared(session_adapter::fixture_permit())
            .unwrap_err(),
        MutationOperation::Activate,
        MutationStatus::RefusedBeforeMutation,
        Some(false),
    );
    assert!(f.state.lock().unwrap().writes.is_empty());
}

#[test]
fn parent_identity_mismatch_invalidates_and_refuses() {
    let mut f = Fixture::new();
    f.state.lock().unwrap().parent.inode += 1;
    let o = f.session.observe();
    observation(
        &o,
        ObservationValidity::Invalid,
        ObservationCompleteness::Incomplete,
    );
    assert!(o.binding_before.is_err() && o.binding_after.is_err());
    assert_eq!(o.identity.parent, (PARENT.device, PARENT.inode));
    assert!(f.session.verify_for_mutation().is_err());
    failed(
        &f.session
            .activate_prepared(session_adapter::fixture_permit())
            .unwrap_err(),
        MutationOperation::Activate,
        MutationStatus::RefusedBeforeMutation,
        Some(false),
    );
    assert!(f.state.lock().unwrap().writes.is_empty());
}
#[test]
fn registered_child_inode_mismatch_preserves_child() {
    let mut f = Fixture::new();
    f.child();
    f.state.lock().unwrap().child_entry.inode += 1;
    let o = f.session.observe();
    observation(
        &o,
        ObservationValidity::Unknown,
        ObservationCompleteness::Incomplete,
    );
    let child = o.registered_child.unwrap();
    assert_eq!(child.identity, CHILD);
    assert!(child.population.is_err() && child.memory_low.is_err());
    assert!(f.session.verify_for_mutation().is_err());
    assert_eq!(
        f.session.read_child_memory_low(CHILD).unwrap_err().kind,
        crate::child_memory::ErrorKind::IdentityConflict
    );
    failed(
        &f.session
            .remove_empty_child(session_adapter::fixture_permit())
            .unwrap_err(),
        MutationOperation::RemoveChild,
        MutationStatus::Conflict,
        Some(false),
    );
    failed(
        &f.session
            .restore(session_adapter::fixture_permit(), true)
            .unwrap_err(),
        MutationOperation::RestoreControllers,
        MutationStatus::CleanupRequired,
        Some(false),
    );
    assert_eq!(f.state.lock().unwrap().removes, 0);
    assert_eq!(f.state.lock().unwrap().child, Some(CHILD));
    assert_eq!(f.state.lock().unwrap().writes.len(), 1);
}
#[test]
fn memory_low_read_failure_is_typed_and_incomplete() {
    let mut f = Fixture::new();
    f.child();
    f.state.lock().unwrap().low = Err("injected memory.low read failure".into());
    let o = f.session.observe();
    observation(
        &o,
        ObservationValidity::Unknown,
        ObservationCompleteness::Incomplete,
    );
    assert!(o.registered_child.unwrap().memory_low.is_err());
    let e = f.session.read_child_memory_low(CHILD).unwrap_err();
    assert_eq!(e.kind, crate::child_memory::ErrorKind::RefusedBeforeWrite);
    assert_eq!(e.observed, None);
    assert!(!f.session.observe().memory_low_unresolved);
    assert_eq!(f.state.lock().unwrap().writes.len(), 1);
}
#[test]
fn pre_write_refusal_uses_real_hierarchy_and_population_checks() {
    for case in 0..4 {
        let mut f = Fixture::new();
        {
            let mut s = f.state.lock().unwrap();
            match case {
                0 => s.parent_procs = "99".into(),
                1 => s.manager_procs = "0".into(),
                2 => s.extra_descendants = true,
                3 => s.manager_descendants = true,
                _ => unreachable!(),
            }
        }
        assert!(f.session.verify_for_mutation().is_err(), "case {case}");
        failed(
            &f.session
                .activate_prepared(session_adapter::fixture_permit())
                .unwrap_err(),
            MutationOperation::Activate,
            MutationStatus::RefusedBeforeMutation,
            Some(false),
        );
        assert!(f.state.lock().unwrap().writes.is_empty());
    }
}
#[test]
fn ambiguous_controller_write_retains_expected_and_observed_certainty() {
    let mut f = Fixture::new();
    f.state.lock().unwrap().write_mode = WriteMode::ErrorAfterApply;
    let e = f
        .session
        .activate_prepared(session_adapter::fixture_permit())
        .unwrap_err();
    failed(
        &e,
        MutationOperation::Activate,
        MutationStatus::AmbiguousMutation,
        Some(true),
    );
    assert_eq!(
        e.last_verified.unwrap().controllers,
        Some(vec!["cpu".into(), "pids".into()])
    );
    assert_eq!(
        e.expected.unwrap().controllers,
        Some(vec!["cpu".into(), "memory".into(), "pids".into()])
    );
    assert_eq!(e.observed, None);
    let o = f.session.observe();
    assert_eq!(o.activation, ObservedActivation::Unresolved);
    assert!(o.activation_unresolved);
    observation(
        &o,
        ObservationValidity::Unknown,
        ObservationCompleteness::Incomplete,
    );
    assert_eq!(f.state.lock().unwrap().writes.len(), 1);
}
#[test]
fn controller_write_readback_mismatch_is_ambiguous_with_observed_state() {
    let mut f = Fixture::new();
    f.state.lock().unwrap().write_mode = WriteMode::Mismatch;
    let e = f
        .session
        .activate_prepared(session_adapter::fixture_permit())
        .unwrap_err();
    failed(
        &e,
        MutationOperation::Activate,
        MutationStatus::AmbiguousMutation,
        Some(true),
    );
    assert_eq!(
        e.observed.as_ref().unwrap().controllers,
        Some(vec!["cpu".into(), "pids".into()])
    );
    assert_ne!(e.observed, e.expected);
    assert!(f.session.observe().activation_unresolved);
}

    let e = f
        .session
        .activate_prepared(session_adapter::fixture_permit())
        .unwrap_err();
    failed(
        &e,
        MutationOperation::Activate,
        MutationStatus::AmbiguousMutation,
        Some(true),
    );
    assert_eq!(e.observed, None);
    assert!(f.session.observe().activation_unresolved);
}
#[test]
fn child_cleanup_failure_latches_and_blocks_controller_restoration() {
    let mut f = Fixture::new();
    f.child();
    f.state.lock().unwrap().fail_remove = true;
    let e = f
        .session
        .remove_empty_child(session_adapter::fixture_permit())
        .unwrap_err();
    failed(
        &e,
        MutationOperation::RemoveChild,
        MutationStatus::AmbiguousMutation,
        None,
    );
    assert_eq!(e.last_verified.unwrap().child, Some(CHILD));
    assert_eq!(e.expected.unwrap().child_present, Some(false));
    assert_eq!(e.observed, None);
    let o = f.session.observe();
    assert!(o.child_unresolved);
    observation(
        &o,
        ObservationValidity::Unknown,
        ObservationCompleteness::Incomplete,
    );
    assert!(f.session.verify_for_mutation().is_err());
    failed(
        &f.session
            .remove_empty_child(session_adapter::fixture_permit())
            .unwrap_err(),
        MutationOperation::RemoveChild,
        MutationStatus::CleanupRequired,
        Some(false),
    );
    failed(
        &f.session
            .restore(session_adapter::fixture_permit(), true)
            .unwrap_err(),
        MutationOperation::RestoreControllers,
        MutationStatus::CleanupRequired,
        Some(false),
    );
    assert_eq!(f.state.lock().unwrap().removes, 1);
    assert_eq!(f.state.lock().unwrap().child, Some(CHILD));
    assert_eq!(f.state.lock().unwrap().writes.len(), 1);
}
#[test]
fn controller_restoration_failure_before_write_is_not_retried() {
    let mut f = Fixture::new();
    f.activate();
    f.state.lock().unwrap().parent.inode += 1;
    let e = f
        .session
        .restore(session_adapter::fixture_permit(), true)
        .unwrap_err();
    failed(
        &e,
        MutationOperation::RestoreControllers,
        MutationStatus::RestorationFailure,
        Some(false),
    );
    assert_eq!(
        e.expected.unwrap().controllers,
        Some(vec!["cpu".into(), "pids".into()])
    );
    f.state.lock().unwrap().parent = PARENT;
    assert!(f.session.observe().activation_unresolved);
    failed(
        &f.session
            .restore(session_adapter::fixture_permit(), true)
            .unwrap_err(),
        MutationOperation::RestoreControllers,
        MutationStatus::CleanupRequired,
        Some(false),
    );
    assert_eq!(f.state.lock().unwrap().writes.len(), 1);
}
#[test]
fn controller_restoration_write_failure_or_mismatch_stays_ambiguous() {
    for mode in [
        WriteMode::ErrorAfterApply,
        WriteMode::Mismatch,
        WriteMode::ReadBackError,
    ] {
        let mut f = Fixture::new();
        f.activate();
        f.state.lock().unwrap().write_mode = mode;
        let e = f
            .session
            .restore(session_adapter::fixture_permit(), true)
            .unwrap_err();
        failed(
            &e,
            MutationOperation::RestoreControllers,
            MutationStatus::AmbiguousMutation,
            Some(true),
        );
        assert!(f.session.observe().activation_unresolved);
        failed(
            &f.session
                .restore(session_adapter::fixture_permit(), true)
                .unwrap_err(),
            MutationOperation::RestoreControllers,
            MutationStatus::CleanupRequired,
            Some(false),
        );
        assert_eq!(f.state.lock().unwrap().writes.len(), 2);
    }
}
#[test]
fn unresolved_mutation_blocks_retry_even_after_valid_io_returns() {
    let mut f = Fixture::new();
    f.state.lock().unwrap().write_mode = WriteMode::ErrorAfterApply;
    failed(
        &f.session
            .activate_prepared(session_adapter::fixture_permit())
            .unwrap_err(),
        MutationOperation::Activate,
        MutationStatus::AmbiguousMutation,
        Some(true),
    );
    f.state.lock().unwrap().write_mode = WriteMode::Apply;
    let o = f.session.observe();
    assert!(o.binding_before.is_ok() && o.binding_after.is_ok());
    assert_eq!(o.activation, ObservedActivation::Unresolved);
    assert!(f.session.verify_for_mutation().is_err());
    failed(
        &f.session
            .activate_prepared(session_adapter::fixture_permit())
            .unwrap_err(),
        MutationOperation::Activate,
        MutationStatus::RefusedBeforeMutation,
        Some(false),
    );
    failed(
        &f.session
            .create_empty_child(session_adapter::fixture_permit())
            .unwrap_err(),
        MutationOperation::CreateChild,
        MutationStatus::RefusedBeforeMutation,
        Some(false),
    );
    failed(
        &f.session
            .restore(session_adapter::fixture_permit(), true)
            .unwrap_err(),
        MutationOperation::RestoreControllers,
        MutationStatus::CleanupRequired,
        Some(false),
    );
    assert_eq!(f.state.lock().unwrap().writes.len(), 1);
    assert_eq!(f.state.lock().unwrap().creates, 0);
}
#[test]

    let mut f = Fixture::new();

        &f.session

    failed(

            .unwrap_err(),
        MutationOperation::CreateChild,
        MutationStatus::RefusedBeforeMutation,
        Some(false),
    );
    failed(
        &f.session
            .restore(session_adapter::fixture_permit(), true)
            .unwrap_err(),
        MutationOperation::RestoreControllers,
        MutationStatus::CleanupRequired,
        Some(false),
    );
    assert_eq!(f.state.lock().unwrap().creates, 1);
}

    let e = f
        .session
        .activate_prepared(session_adapter::fixture_permit())
        .unwrap_err();

        &e,
        MutationOperation::Activate,
        MutationStatus::AmbiguousMutation,

#[test]
fn observation_and_fixture_identity_cannot_enable_writes() {
    let mut f = Fixture::new();
    f.session.backend.authorized = false;
    observation(
        &f.session.observe(),
        ObservationValidity::Valid,
        ObservationCompleteness::Complete,
    );
    assert_eq!(f.session.verify_for_mutation(), Ok(PARENT));
    assert!(f.session.backend.enable_memory().is_err());
    assert!(f.state.lock().unwrap().writes.is_empty());
    f.session.backend.authorized = true;
    f.child();
    failed(
        &f.session
            .restore(session_adapter::fixture_permit(), false)
            .unwrap_err(),
        MutationOperation::RestoreControllers,
        MutationStatus::RefusedBeforeMutation,
        Some(false),
    );
    for authorized in [false, true] {
        let e = f
            .session
            .change_child_memory_low(
                session_adapter::fixture_permit(),
                CHILD,
                4096,
                8192,
                authorized,
                false,
            )
            .unwrap_err();
        assert_eq!(e.kind, crate::child_memory::ErrorKind::RefusedBeforeWrite);
        assert!(!f.session.observe().memory_low_unresolved);
        assert_eq!(f.state.lock().unwrap().low, Ok(4096));
    }
    assert_eq!(f.state.lock().unwrap().writes.len(), 1);
}
#[test]
fn populated_registered_child_refuses_cleanup_without_mutation() {
    let mut f = Fixture::new();
    f.child();
    f.state.lock().unwrap().child_populated = true;
    observation(
        &f.session.observe(),
        ObservationValidity::Invalid,
        ObservationCompleteness::Incomplete,
    );
    assert!(f.session.verify_for_mutation().is_err());
    failed(
        &f.session
            .remove_empty_child(session_adapter::fixture_permit())
            .unwrap_err(),
        MutationOperation::RemoveChild,
        MutationStatus::RefusedBeforeMutation,
        Some(false),
    );
    assert_eq!(f.state.lock().unwrap().removes, 0);
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod adapter_integration {
    use super::*;
    use crate::{
        empty_child_coordinator::{
            self as coordinator, Backend, Certainty, FaultKind, Outcome, Stage,
        },
        safety::{self, SafetyDecision},
    };
    type IntegratedFixture = (
        ControllerSessionAdapter,
        Arc<Mutex<State>>,
        Arc<Mutex<(u64, bool)>>,
    );
    fn adapter() -> IntegratedFixture {
        let f = Fixture::new();
        f.state.lock().unwrap().enable_low = true;
        let telemetry = Arc::new(Mutex::new((u64::MAX, false)));
        let readings = telemetry.clone();
        let a = ControllerSessionAdapter::fixture(f.session, move |thresholds| {
            let (available, stale) = *readings.lock().unwrap();
            safety::fixture_boundary(thresholds, available, stale)
        });
        (a, f.state, telemetry)
    }
    fn run(a: &mut ControllerSessionAdapter) -> coordinator::Report {
        coordinator::run(a, true, 8192, |_| false)
    }
    fn clean(s: &Arc<Mutex<State>>) {
        let s = s.lock().unwrap();
        assert!(s.child.is_none());
        assert_eq!(s.enabled, "cpu pids");
        assert_eq!(s.low, Ok(4096));
    }
    #[test]
    fn integrated_evidence_budget_refuses_without_discarding_history_or_writing() {
        let (mut a, state, _) = adapter();
        for _ in 0..128 {
            a.inspect(Stage::Binding).unwrap();
        }
        assert_eq!(a.session_evidence.len(), 128);
        assert!(a.inspect(Stage::Binding).is_err());
        assert!(a.activate(&["cpu".into(), "pids".into()].into()).is_err());
        assert_eq!(a.session_evidence.len(), 128);
        assert!(a.mutation_evidence.is_empty());
        clean(&state);
    }
    #[test]
    fn transient_telemetry_refusal_recovers_without_reopening_forward_authority() {
        // Refuse at the guard, after activation, after creation and after low apply.
        for refused_read in [3, 4, 6, 10] {
            let f = Fixture::new();
            f.state.lock().unwrap().enable_low = true;
            let state = f.state.clone();
            let mut reads = 0;
            let mut a = ControllerSessionAdapter::fixture(f.session, move |thresholds| {
                reads += 1;
                safety::fixture_boundary(
                    thresholds,
                    if reads == refused_read { 0 } else { u64::MAX },
                    false,
                )
            });
            let r = run(&mut a);
            assert_eq!(
                r.outcome,
                Outcome::RestoredAfterFailure,
                "read {refused_read}: {r:?}"
            );
            clean(&state);
            let expected: &[&str] = match refused_read {
                3 => &[],
                4 => &["+memory", "-memory"],
                6 => &["+memory", "create", "remove", "-memory"],
                _ => &[
                    "+memory", "create", "low:8192", "low:4096", "remove", "-memory",
                ],
            };
            assert_eq!(state.lock().unwrap().trace, expected);
            let before = state.lock().unwrap().trace.clone();
            assert_eq!(
                a.activate(&BTreeSet::from(["cpu".into(), "pids".into()]))
                    .unwrap_err()
                    .kind,
                FaultKind::Before
            );
            assert_eq!(state.lock().unwrap().trace, before);
        }
    }
    #[test]
    fn unavailable_recovery_telemetry_preserves_verified_resources() {
        for failed in [
            Stage::VerifyActivation,
            Stage::VerifyChild,
            Stage::VerifyApplied,
        ] {
            let (mut a, state, telemetry) = adapter();
            let mut before = Vec::new();
            let r = coordinator::run(&mut a, true, 8192, |stage| {
                if stage == failed {
                    *telemetry.lock().unwrap() = (0, true);
                    before = state.lock().unwrap().trace.clone();
                }
                false
            });
            assert_eq!(r.outcome, Outcome::RecoveryUnavailable, "{failed:?}: {r:?}");
            assert!(r.recovery_failed_transition.is_some());
            assert_eq!(state.lock().unwrap().trace, before);
            assert!(!a.session_observation().activation_unresolved);
            assert_eq!(r.controller_restoration_verified, Certainty::Unknown);
        }
    }
    #[test]
    fn second_cancellation_is_deferred_during_bounded_recovery() {
        let (mut a, state, _) = adapter();
        let mut calls = Vec::new();
        let r = coordinator::run(&mut a, true, 8192, |stage| {
            calls.push(stage);
            stage == Stage::VerifyApplied || stage == Stage::RecoveryRestoreLow
        });
        assert_eq!(r.outcome, Outcome::RestoredAfterFailure);
        assert!(!calls.contains(&Stage::RecoveryRestoreLow));
        clean(&state);
        assert_eq!(
            state.lock().unwrap().trace,
            ["+memory", "create", "low:8192", "low:4096", "remove", "-memory"]
        );
    }
    #[test]
    fn integrated_complete_lifecycle() {
        let (mut a, s, _) = adapter();
        let r = run(&mut a);
        assert_eq!(r.outcome, Outcome::Complete);
        assert_eq!(r.original_low, Some(4096));
        assert_eq!(r.low_restoration_verified, Certainty::Yes);
        assert_eq!(r.child_remains, Certainty::No);
        assert_eq!(r.controller_restoration_verified, Certainty::Yes);
        clean(&s);
        assert_eq!(
            s.lock().unwrap().trace,
            ["+memory", "create", "low:8192", "low:4096", "remove", "-memory"]
        );
        assert_eq!(a.mutation_evidence.len(), 4);
        assert!(a
            .mutation_evidence
            .iter()
            .all(|(_, o)| o.status == MutationStatus::AppliedAndVerified));
        for stage in [
            Stage::Activate,
            Stage::CreateChild,
            Stage::ApplyLow,
            Stage::RestoreLow,
            Stage::RemoveChild,
            Stage::RestoreControllers,
        ] {
            assert!(a
                .safety_evidence
                .iter()
                .any(|(at, o)| *at == stage && o.decision == SafetyDecision::Eligible));
        }
        assert!(a
            .session_evidence
            .iter()
            .all(|(_, o)| o.validity == ObservationValidity::Valid));
    }
    #[test]
    fn integrated_authorization_and_live_gate_stay_closed() {
        let (mut a, s, _) = adapter();
        let r = coordinator::run(&mut a, false, 8192, |_| false);
        assert_eq!(r.failed_transition, Some(Stage::Authorization));
        assert!(s.lock().unwrap().trace.is_empty());
        let (mut a, s, _) = adapter();
        a.session_observation();
        // The production constructor cannot use the test-only mutation capability.
        let f = Fixture::new();
        let mut production =
            ControllerSessionAdapter::new(f.session, crate::safety::SafetyThresholds::default());
        assert_eq!(
            production.activate(&BTreeSet::new()).unwrap_err().kind,
            FaultKind::Before
        );
        assert!(f.state.lock().unwrap().trace.is_empty());
        assert!(s.lock().unwrap().trace.is_empty());
    }
    #[test]
    fn integrated_telemetry_refusal_blocks_all_later_mutations() {
        for stale in [false, true] {
            let (mut a, s, t) = adapter();
            *t.lock().unwrap() = (0, stale);
            let r = run(&mut a);
            assert_ne!(r.outcome, Outcome::Complete);
            assert!(s.lock().unwrap().trace.is_empty());
            assert!(a
                .safety_evidence
                .iter()
                .any(|(_, o)| matches!(o.decision, SafetyDecision::Refused(_))));
        }
        for failed in [
            Stage::Activate,
            Stage::CreateChild,
            Stage::ApplyLow,
            Stage::RestoreLow,
            Stage::RemoveChild,
            Stage::RestoreControllers,
        ] {
            let (mut a, s, t) = adapter();
            let mut snapshot = None;
            let r = coordinator::run(&mut a, true, 8192, |stage| {
                if stage == failed {
                    *t.lock().unwrap() = (0, false);
                    snapshot = Some(s.lock().unwrap().trace.clone());
                }
                false
            });
            assert_ne!(r.outcome, Outcome::Complete);
            assert_eq!(s.lock().unwrap().trace, snapshot.unwrap(), "{failed:?}");
        }
    }
    #[test]
    fn integrated_stale_binding_refuses_without_mutation() {
        let (mut a, s, _) = adapter();
        s.lock().unwrap().parent.inode += 1;
        let r = run(&mut a);
        assert_eq!(r.outcome, Outcome::Conflict);
        assert!(s.lock().unwrap().trace.is_empty());
        assert!(a.session_evidence[0].1.binding_before.is_err());
    }
    #[test]
    fn integrated_controller_activation_failure_latches() {
        let (mut a, s, _) = adapter();
        s.lock().unwrap().write_mode = WriteMode::ErrorAfterApply;
        let r = run(&mut a);
        assert_eq!(r.outcome, Outcome::Ambiguous);
        assert_eq!(r.failed_transition, Some(Stage::Activate));
        assert!(a.session_observation().activation_unresolved);
        assert_eq!(s.lock().unwrap().trace, ["+memory"]);
        assert_eq!(
            a.mutation_evidence[0].1.status,
            MutationStatus::AmbiguousMutation
        );
    }
    #[test]
    fn late_competing_changes_are_checked_at_child_operation_boundary() {
        for failed in [Stage::CreateChild, Stage::RemoveChild] {
            let (mut a, state, _) = adapter();
            let r = coordinator::run(&mut a, true, 8192, |stage| {
                if stage == failed {
                    let mut s = state.lock().unwrap();
                    if stage == Stage::CreateChild {
                        s.enabled = "cpu memory".into();
                    } else {
                        s.low = Ok(12288);
                    }
                }
                false
            });
            assert_eq!(r.outcome, Outcome::Conflict);
            assert_eq!(r.failed_transition, Some(failed));
            let s = state.lock().unwrap();
            if failed == Stage::CreateChild {
                assert_eq!(s.creates, 0);
            } else {
                assert_eq!(s.removes, 0);
                assert_eq!(s.low, Ok(12288));
            }
            assert!(!s.trace.iter().any(|s| s == "-memory"));
        }
    }
    #[test]
    fn controller_short_write_is_ambiguous_and_never_retried() {
        let (mut a, state, _) = adapter();
        state.lock().unwrap().write_mode = WriteMode::ShortWrite;
        let r = run(&mut a);
        assert_eq!(r.outcome, Outcome::Ambiguous);
        assert_eq!(state.lock().unwrap().writes.len(), 1);
        assert!(a.session_observation().activation_unresolved);
        assert!(a.activate(&BTreeSet::new()).is_err());
        assert_eq!(state.lock().unwrap().trace, ["+memory"]);
    }
    #[test]
    fn adapter_authorization_covers_every_operation() {
        let mut f = Fixture::new();
        f.session.backend.authorized = false;
        let state = f.state.clone();
        let mut a = ControllerSessionAdapter::fixture(f.session, |thresholds| {
            safety::fixture_boundary(thresholds, u64::MAX, false)
        });
        let original = BTreeSet::from(["cpu".into(), "pids".into()]);
        assert_eq!(a.activate(&original).unwrap_err().kind, FaultKind::Before);
        assert_eq!(a.create_child().unwrap_err().kind, FaultKind::Before);
        for stage in [
            Stage::ApplyLow,
            Stage::RestoreLow,
            Stage::RecoveryRestoreLow,
        ] {
            assert_eq!(
                a.write_low(stage, "7:13", 4096, 8192).unwrap_err().kind,
                FaultKind::Before
            );
        }
        for stage in [Stage::RemoveChild, Stage::RecoveryRemoveChild] {
            assert_eq!(
                a.remove_child(stage, "7:13").unwrap_err().kind,
                FaultKind::Before
            );
        }
        for stage in [Stage::RestoreControllers, Stage::RecoveryRestoreControllers] {
            assert_eq!(
                a.restore_controllers(stage, &original).unwrap_err().kind,
                FaultKind::Before
            );
        }
        assert!(state.lock().unwrap().trace.is_empty());
    }
    #[test]
    fn integrated_child_creation_failure_preserves_ownership() {
        let (mut a, s, _) = adapter();
        s.lock().unwrap().fail_create = true;
        let r = run(&mut a);
        assert_eq!(r.outcome, Outcome::Ambiguous);
        assert_eq!(r.failed_transition, Some(Stage::CreateChild));
        assert!(a.session_observation().child_unresolved);
        assert_eq!(s.lock().unwrap().trace, ["+memory", "create"]);
        assert_eq!(s.lock().unwrap().child, Some(CHILD));
    }
    #[test]
    fn integrated_low_ambiguity_and_readback_mismatch_never_retry() {
        for mode in ["error", "short", "mismatch", "readback", "identity"] {
            let (mut a, s, _) = adapter();
            s.lock().unwrap().low_mode = Some((1, mode));
            let r = run(&mut a);
            assert_eq!(r.outcome, Outcome::Ambiguous, "{mode}");
            assert_eq!(r.failed_transition, Some(Stage::ApplyLow));
            assert!(a.session_observation().memory_low_unresolved);
            assert_eq!(s.lock().unwrap().trace, ["+memory", "create", "low:8192"]);
            assert_eq!(
                a.low_errors[0].1.kind,
                if mode == "mismatch" {
                    crate::child_memory::ErrorKind::ReadBackMismatch
                } else if mode == "identity" {
                    crate::child_memory::ErrorKind::IdentityConflict
                } else {
                    crate::child_memory::ErrorKind::WriteOutcomeAmbiguous
                }
            );
        }
    }
    #[test]
    fn integrated_low_prewrite_refusal_recovers_in_order() {
        let (mut a, s, _) = adapter();
        s.lock().unwrap().low_mode = Some((1, "before"));
        let r = run(&mut a);
        assert_eq!(r.outcome, Outcome::RestoredAfterFailure);
        assert_eq!(
            a.low_errors[0].1.kind,
            crate::child_memory::ErrorKind::RefusedBeforeWrite
        );
        clean(&s);
        assert_eq!(
            s.lock().unwrap().trace,
            ["+memory", "create", "remove", "-memory"]
        );
    }
    #[test]
    fn integrated_competing_low_is_not_overwritten() {
        let (mut a, s, _) = adapter();
        s.lock().unwrap().compete_low = true;
        let r = run(&mut a);
        assert_eq!(r.outcome, Outcome::Conflict);
        assert_eq!(
            a.low_errors[0].1.kind,
            crate::child_memory::ErrorKind::CompetingValue
        );
        assert!(!a.session_observation().memory_low_unresolved);
        assert_eq!(s.lock().unwrap().low, Ok(12288));
        assert_eq!(s.lock().unwrap().trace, ["+memory", "create"]);
    }
    #[test]
    fn integrated_low_restoration_failure_retains_cause_and_child() {
        let (mut a, s, _) = adapter();
        s.lock().unwrap().low_mode = Some((2, "error"));
        let r = run(&mut a);
        assert_eq!(r.outcome, Outcome::Ambiguous);
        assert_eq!(r.failed_transition, Some(Stage::RestoreLow));
        let e = &a.low_errors[0].1;
        assert_eq!(e.kind, crate::child_memory::ErrorKind::RestorationFailure);
        assert_eq!(
            e.cause.as_ref().unwrap().kind,
            crate::child_memory::ErrorKind::WriteOutcomeAmbiguous
        );
        assert!(a.session_observation().memory_low_unresolved);
        assert_eq!(
            s.lock().unwrap().trace,
            ["+memory", "create", "low:8192", "low:4096"]
        );
    }
    #[test]
    fn integrated_child_cleanup_failure_blocks_parent_restoration() {
        let (mut a, s, _) = adapter();
        s.lock().unwrap().fail_remove = true;
        let r = run(&mut a);
        assert_eq!(r.outcome, Outcome::Ambiguous);
        assert_eq!(r.failed_transition, Some(Stage::RemoveChild));
        assert!(a.session_observation().child_unresolved);
        assert_eq!(s.lock().unwrap().child, Some(CHILD));
        assert_eq!(
            s.lock().unwrap().trace,
            ["+memory", "create", "low:8192", "low:4096", "remove"]
        );
    }
    #[test]
    fn integrated_controller_restoration_refuses_with_child() {
        let (mut a, s, _) = adapter();
        let r = coordinator::run(&mut a, true, 8192, |stage| stage == Stage::RemoveChild);
        assert_eq!(r.outcome, Outcome::RestoredAfterFailure);
        clean(&s);
        let (mut a, s, _) = adapter();
        let r = coordinator::run(&mut a, true, 8192, |stage| {
            if stage == Stage::RemoveChild {
                s.lock().unwrap().fail_remove = true;
            }
            false
        });
        assert_eq!(r.outcome, Outcome::Ambiguous);
        let original = BTreeSet::from(["cpu".into(), "pids".into()]);
        assert!(a
            .restore_controllers(Stage::RecoveryRestoreControllers, &original)
            .is_err());
        assert!(!s.lock().unwrap().trace.iter().any(|x| x == "-memory"));
    }
    #[test]
    fn integrated_cancellation_runs_existing_reverse_recovery() {
        for cancelled in [
            Stage::Authorization,
            Stage::Binding,
            Stage::CaptureControllers,
            Stage::Activate,
            Stage::VerifyActivation,
            Stage::CreateChild,
            Stage::VerifyChild,
            Stage::CaptureLow,
            Stage::ApplyLow,
            Stage::VerifyApplied,
            Stage::RestoreLow,
            Stage::VerifyRestored,
            Stage::VerifyEmpty,
            Stage::RemoveChild,
            Stage::RestoreControllers,
            Stage::VerifyFinal,
        ] {
            let (mut a, s, _) = adapter();
            let r = coordinator::run(&mut a, true, 8192, |stage| stage == cancelled);
            assert_eq!(r.failed_transition, Some(cancelled));
            assert_ne!(r.outcome, Outcome::Complete);
            clean(&s);
            if matches!(cancelled, Stage::VerifyApplied | Stage::RestoreLow) {
                assert_eq!(r.outcome, Outcome::RestoredAfterFailure);
                assert_eq!(r.low_restoration_verified, Certainty::Yes);
                assert_eq!(r.child_remains, Certainty::No);
                assert_eq!(
                    s.lock().unwrap().trace,
                    ["+memory", "create", "low:8192", "low:4096", "remove", "-memory"]
                );
                let stages: Vec<_> = a.mutation_evidence.iter().map(|(s, _)| *s).collect();
                assert!(stages.contains(&Stage::RecoveryRemoveChild));
                assert!(stages.contains(&Stage::RecoveryRestoreControllers));
            }
        }
    }
    #[test]
    fn integrated_controller_restoration_write_failure_is_ambiguous() {
        let (mut a, s, _) = adapter();
        let r = coordinator::run(&mut a, true, 8192, |stage| {
            if stage == Stage::RestoreControllers {
                s.lock().unwrap().write_mode = WriteMode::ErrorAfterApply;
            }
            false
        });
        assert_eq!(r.outcome, Outcome::Ambiguous);
        assert_eq!(r.failed_transition, Some(Stage::RestoreControllers));
        assert!(a.session_observation().activation_unresolved);
        assert_eq!(
            a.mutation_evidence.last().unwrap().1.status,
            MutationStatus::AmbiguousMutation
        );
        assert_eq!(
            s.lock().unwrap().trace,
            ["+memory", "create", "low:8192", "low:4096", "remove", "-memory"]
        );
    }
    #[test]
    fn integrated_typed_controller_refusal_and_child_cleanup_guard() {
        let (mut a, s, _) = adapter();
        let original = BTreeSet::from(["cpu".into(), "pids".into()]);
        a.activate(&original).unwrap();
        a.create_child().unwrap();
        assert_eq!(
            a.restore_controllers(Stage::RestoreControllers, &original)
                .unwrap_err()
                .kind,
            FaultKind::Before
        );
        assert_eq!(
            a.mutation_evidence.last().unwrap().1.status,
            MutationStatus::CleanupRequired
        );
        assert_eq!(s.lock().unwrap().trace, ["+memory", "create"]);
        let (mut a, s, _) = adapter();
        let r = coordinator::run(&mut a, true, 8192, |stage| {
            if stage == Stage::RestoreControllers {
                s.lock().unwrap().enabled = "cpu memory".into();
            }
            false
        });
        assert_ne!(r.outcome, Outcome::Complete);
        let outcome = &a.mutation_evidence.last().unwrap().1;
        assert_eq!(outcome.status, MutationStatus::RestorationFailure);
        assert_eq!(outcome.mutation_attempted, Some(false));
        assert!(a.session_observation().activation_unresolved);
        assert_eq!(
            s.lock().unwrap().trace,
            ["+memory", "create", "low:8192", "low:4096", "remove"]
        );
    }
    #[test]
    fn integrated_recovery_failures_stop_reverse_cleanup() {
        for mode in ["low", "child", "controller"] {
            let (mut a, s, _) = adapter();
            let r = coordinator::run(&mut a, true, 8192, |stage| {
                if stage == Stage::VerifyApplied {
                    let mut state = s.lock().unwrap();
                    match mode {
                        "low" => state.low_mode = Some((2, "error")),
                        "child" => state.fail_remove = true,
                        _ => state.write_mode = WriteMode::ErrorAfterApply,
                    };
                    return true;
                }
                false
            });
            assert_eq!(r.outcome, Outcome::Ambiguous);
            assert_eq!(
                r.recovery_failed_transition,
                Some(match mode {
                    "low" => Stage::RecoveryRestoreLow,
                    "child" => Stage::RecoveryRemoveChild,
                    _ => Stage::RecoveryRestoreControllers,
                })
            );
            let state = s.lock().unwrap();
            if mode != "controller" {
                assert!(!state.trace.iter().any(|x| x == "-memory"));
                assert_eq!(state.child, Some(CHILD));
            }
            if mode == "low" {
                assert_eq!(state.trace, ["+memory", "create", "low:8192", "low:4096"]);
            }
        }
    }
}


FILE crates/xynoos-linux-control/src/empty_child_coordinator.rs
//! Transaction orchestration with deterministic fixtures and a session adapter.
//! `controller::ControllerSessionAdapter` keeps live mutation disabled.
//! Backend observations and writes must each independently validate the held binding,
//! identities, population and exclusive delegation; this interface is not authorization.
use serde::Serialize;
use std::collections::BTreeSet;

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize)]
pub enum Stage {
    Authorization,
    Binding,
    CaptureControllers,
    Activate,
    VerifyActivation,
    CreateChild,
    VerifyChild,
    CaptureLow,
    ApplyLow,
    VerifyApplied,
    RestoreLow,
    VerifyRestored,
    VerifyEmpty,
    RemoveChild,
    RestoreControllers,
    VerifyFinal,
    RecoveryInspect,
    RecoveryRestoreLow,
    RecoveryVerifyLow,
    RecoveryRemoveChild,
    RecoveryVerifyRemoval,
    RecoveryRestoreControllers,
    RecoveryVerifyControllers,
}
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize)]
pub enum Outcome {
    Complete,
    RestoredAfterFailure,
    Conflict,
    Ambiguous,
    CleanupRequired,
    RecoveryUnavailable,
}
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize)]
pub enum Certainty {
    Yes,
    No,
    Unknown,
}
#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize)]
pub struct ChildObservation {
    pub identity: String,
    pub empty: bool,
    pub descendants: u64,
    pub memory_inherited: bool,
    pub low: Option<u64>,
}
#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize)]
pub struct Observation {
    /// Opaque identity of the validated unit invocation and held parent/manager descriptors.
    pub binding: String,
    pub parent_empty: bool,
    pub safety_satisfied: bool,
    pub available_controllers: BTreeSet<String>,
    pub controllers: BTreeSet<String>,
    pub child: Option<ChildObservation>,
}
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub enum FaultKind {
    /// The operation is known to have made no mutation.
    Before,
    /// A write or removal may have happened. No automatic retry is safe.
    Ambiguous,
    Conflict,
    Unavailable,
}
#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
pub struct Fault {
    pub kind: FaultKind,
    pub message: String,
}
impl Fault {
    pub fn new(kind: FaultKind, message: impl Into<String>) -> Self {
        Self {
            kind,
            message: message.into(),
        }
    }
}
#[derive(Debug, Serialize)]
pub struct Report {
    pub outcome: Outcome,
    pub last_completed: Option<Stage>,
    pub failed_transition: Option<Stage>,
    pub recovery_failed_transition: Option<Stage>,
    pub original: Option<Observation>,
    pub original_low: Option<u64>,
    pub registered_child_identity: Option<String>,
    pub current: Option<Observation>,
    pub low_restoration_verified: Certainty,
    pub child_remains: Certainty,
    pub controller_restoration_verified: Certainty,
    pub investigation: Vec<String>,
}
/// Contract for a deterministic backend. Mutation methods must fail with Ambiguous
/// if their effect is not known, and must revalidate identity and state themselves.
/// `inspect` must independently revalidate identity and enumerate the entire owned
/// hierarchy. This crate intentionally supplies no live implementation of this trait.
pub trait Backend {
    fn inspect(&mut self, stage: Stage) -> Result<Observation, Fault>;
    fn activate(&mut self, expected: &BTreeSet<String>) -> Result<(), Fault>;
    /// Returns the identity of the specifically created child, never a name prefix.
    fn create_child(&mut self) -> Result<String, Fault>;
    fn write_low(
        &mut self,
        stage: Stage,
        identity: &str,
        expected: u64,
        value: u64,
    ) -> Result<(), Fault>;
    fn remove_child(&mut self, stage: Stage, identity: &str) -> Result<(), Fault>;
    fn restore_controllers(
        &mut self,
        stage: Stage,
        original: &BTreeSet<String>,
    ) -> Result<(), Fault>;
}
#[derive(Default)]
struct Progress {
    original: Option<Observation>,
    original_low: Option<u64>,
    child: Option<String>,
    activated: bool,
    low_applied: bool,
    low_verified_restored: bool,
    child_removed: bool,
    controllers_restored: bool,
    last: Option<Stage>,
    previous: Option<Stage>,
    final_observation: Option<Observation>,
}
fn conflict(message: &str) -> Fault {
    Fault::new(FaultKind::Conflict, message)
}
fn original(p: &Progress) -> &Observation {
    p.original.as_ref().expect("original captured")
}
fn expected_active(p: &Progress) -> BTreeSet<String> {
    let mut v = original(p).controllers.clone();
    v.insert("memory".into());
    v
}
fn validate_base(p: &Progress, o: &Observation, active: bool) -> Result<(), Fault> {
    let initial = original(p);
    if o.binding != initial.binding || !o.parent_empty {
        return Err(conflict("binding changed or parent populated"));
    }
    if o.available_controllers != initial.available_controllers {
        return Err(conflict("available controller set changed"));
    }
    let expected = if active {
        expected_active(p)
    } else {
        initial.controllers.clone()
    };
    if o.controllers != expected {
        return Err(conflict("parent controller state changed"));
    }
    Ok(())
}
fn validate_child<'a>(p: &Progress, o: &'a Observation) -> Result<&'a ChildObservation, Fault> {
    let c = o
        .child
        .as_ref()
        .ok_or_else(|| conflict("registered child missing"))?;
    if Some(&c.identity) != p.child.as_ref()
        || !c.empty
        || c.descendants != 0
        || !c.memory_inherited
    {
        return Err(conflict(
            "child identity, population or descendants changed",
        ));
    }
    Ok(c)
}
fn ensure_low(c: &ChildObservation, value: u64) -> Result<(), Fault> {
    if c.low != Some(value) {
        return Err(conflict("memory.low unavailable or changed"));
    }
    Ok(())
}
fn no_child(o: &Observation) -> Result<(), Fault> {
    if o.child.is_some() {
        Err(conflict("unexpected child"))
    } else {
        Ok(())
    }
}
// A telemetry refusal is known to precede mutation. Keep it distinct from a
// competing-state conflict so already verified resources can enter recovery.
fn forward_inspect<B: Backend>(b: &mut B, stage: Stage) -> Result<Observation, Fault> {
    let o = b.inspect(stage)?;
    if !o.safety_satisfied {
        return Err(Fault::new(
            FaultKind::Before,
            "fresh safety telemetry refused",
        ));
    }
    Ok(o)
}
fn forward<B: Backend>(
    b: &mut B,
    authorized: bool,
    requested: u64,
    cancelled: &mut impl FnMut(Stage) -> bool,
    p: &mut Progress,
) -> Result<(), (Stage, Fault)> {
    macro_rules! step {
        ($stage:expr, $body:expr) => {{
            let stage = $stage;
            if cancelled(stage) {
                return Err((stage, Fault::new(FaultKind::Before, "cancelled")));
            }
            let value = $body.map_err(|e| (stage, e))?;
            p.previous = p.last;
            p.last = Some(stage);
            value
        }};
    }
    step!(
        Stage::Authorization,
        if authorized
            && requested > 0
            && requested <= crate::MAX_LOW_BYTES
            && requested.is_multiple_of(4096)
        {
            Ok(())
        } else {
            Err(Fault::new(
                FaultKind::Before,
                "authorization or bounded value invalid",
            ))
        }
    );
    let o = step!(Stage::Binding, forward_inspect(b, Stage::Binding));
    p.original = Some(o.clone());
    if o.binding.is_empty()
        || !o.parent_empty
        || !o.safety_satisfied
        || o.child.is_some()
        || o.controllers.contains("memory")
        || !o.available_controllers.contains("memory")
        || o.controllers.iter().any(|c| c != "cpu" && c != "pids")
    {
        return Err((
            Stage::Binding,
            conflict("initial binding or hierarchy is unsafe"),
        ));
    }
    let o = step!(
        Stage::CaptureControllers,
        forward_inspect(b, Stage::CaptureControllers)
    );
    validate_base(p, &o, false)
        .and_then(|_| no_child(&o))
        .map_err(|e| (Stage::CaptureControllers, e))?;
    if !o.safety_satisfied {
        return Err((
            Stage::CaptureControllers,
            conflict("memory safety gate failed"),
        ));
    }
    step!(Stage::Activate, b.activate(&original(p).controllers));
    p.activated = true;
    let o = step!(
        Stage::VerifyActivation,
        forward_inspect(b, Stage::VerifyActivation)
    );
    validate_base(p, &o, true)
        .and_then(|_| no_child(&o))
        .map_err(|e| (Stage::VerifyActivation, e))?;
    let child = step!(Stage::CreateChild, b.create_child());
    p.child = Some(child);
    let o = step!(Stage::VerifyChild, forward_inspect(b, Stage::VerifyChild));
    validate_base(p, &o, true).map_err(|e| (Stage::VerifyChild, e))?;
    if !validate_child(p, &o)
        .map_err(|e| (Stage::VerifyChild, e))?
        .memory_inherited
    {
        return Err((
            Stage::VerifyChild,
            conflict("child lacks memory controller"),
        ));
    }
    let o = step!(Stage::CaptureLow, forward_inspect(b, Stage::CaptureLow));
    validate_base(p, &o, true).map_err(|e| (Stage::CaptureLow, e))?;
    let c = validate_child(p, &o).map_err(|e| (Stage::CaptureLow, e))?;
    let low = c.low.ok_or_else(|| {
        (
            Stage::CaptureLow,
            Fault::new(FaultKind::Before, "memory.low unavailable"),
        )
    })?;
    p.original_low = Some(low);
    if low == requested {
        return Err((
            Stage::CaptureLow,
            Fault::new(FaultKind::Before, "test value equals original"),
        ));
    }
    let o = forward_inspect(b, Stage::ApplyLow).map_err(|e| (Stage::ApplyLow, e))?;
    validate_base(p, &o, true)
        .and_then(|_| validate_child(p, &o))
        .and_then(|c| ensure_low(c, low))
        .map_err(|e| (Stage::ApplyLow, e))?;
    if !o.safety_satisfied {
        return Err((Stage::ApplyLow, conflict("memory safety gate failed")));
    }
    step!(
        Stage::ApplyLow,
        b.write_low(Stage::ApplyLow, p.child.as_deref().unwrap(), low, requested)
    );
    p.low_applied = true;
    let o = step!(
        Stage::VerifyApplied,
        forward_inspect(b, Stage::VerifyApplied)
    );
    validate_base(p, &o, true)
        .and_then(|_| validate_child(p, &o))
        .and_then(|c| ensure_low(c, requested))
        .map_err(|e| (Stage::VerifyApplied, e))?;
    let o = forward_inspect(b, Stage::RestoreLow).map_err(|e| (Stage::RestoreLow, e))?;
    validate_base(p, &o, true)
        .and_then(|_| validate_child(p, &o))
        .and_then(|c| ensure_low(c, requested))
        .map_err(|e| (Stage::RestoreLow, e))?;
    step!(
        Stage::RestoreLow,
        b.write_low(
            Stage::RestoreLow,
            p.child.as_deref().unwrap(),
            requested,
            low
        )
    );
    p.low_applied = false;
    let o = step!(
        Stage::VerifyRestored,
        forward_inspect(b, Stage::VerifyRestored)
    );
    validate_base(p, &o, true)
        .and_then(|_| validate_child(p, &o))
        .and_then(|c| ensure_low(c, low))
        .map_err(|e| (Stage::VerifyRestored, e))?;
    p.low_verified_restored = true;
    let o = step!(Stage::VerifyEmpty, forward_inspect(b, Stage::VerifyEmpty));
    validate_base(p, &o, true)
        .and_then(|_| validate_child(p, &o))
        .and_then(|c| ensure_low(c, low))
        .map_err(|e| (Stage::VerifyEmpty, e))?;
    step!(
        Stage::RemoveChild,
        b.remove_child(Stage::RemoveChild, p.child.as_deref().unwrap())
    );
    p.child_removed = true;
    let o = forward_inspect(b, Stage::RemoveChild).map_err(|e| (Stage::RemoveChild, e))?;
    validate_base(p, &o, true)
        .and_then(|_| no_child(&o))
        .map_err(|e| (Stage::RemoveChild, e))?;
    step!(
        Stage::RestoreControllers,
        b.restore_controllers(Stage::RestoreControllers, &original(p).controllers)
    );
    p.controllers_restored = true;
    let o = step!(Stage::VerifyFinal, forward_inspect(b, Stage::VerifyFinal));
    validate_base(p, &o, false)
        .and_then(|_| no_child(&o))
        .map_err(|e| (Stage::VerifyFinal, e))?;
    p.final_observation = Some(o);
    Ok(())
}

/// Run the coordinator using a fixture backend. Cancellation is checked at every
/// forward stage; once recovery begins it runs to a bounded conclusion without
/// accepting further cancellation. There are no retries of ambiguous mutations.
pub fn run<B: Backend>(
    b: &mut B,
    authorized: bool,
    requested: u64,
    mut cancelled: impl FnMut(Stage) -> bool,
) -> Report {
    let mut p = Progress::default();
    let result = forward(b, authorized, requested, &mut cancelled, &mut p);
    let mut report = Report {
        outcome: Outcome::RecoveryUnavailable,
        last_completed: p.last,
        failed_transition: None,
        recovery_failed_transition: None,
        original: p.original.clone(),
        original_low: p.original_low,
        registered_child_identity: p.child.clone(),
        current: None,
        low_restoration_verified: if p.low_verified_restored {
            Certainty::Yes
        } else {
            Certainty::Unknown
        },
        child_remains: Certainty::Unknown,
        controller_restoration_verified: Certainty::Unknown,
        investigation: Vec::new(),
    };
    match result {
        Ok(()) => {
            report.outcome = Outcome::Complete;
            report.low_restoration_verified = Certainty::Yes;
            report.child_remains = Certainty::No;
            report.controller_restoration_verified = Certainty::Yes;
            report.current = p.final_observation;
            return report;
        }
        Err((stage, fault)) => {
            if p.last == Some(stage) {
                p.last = p.previous;
                report.last_completed = p.last;
            }
            report.failed_transition = Some(stage);
            report
                .investigation
                .push(format!("{stage:?}: {}", fault.message));
            if p.original.is_none() {
                report.outcome = match fault.kind {
                    FaultKind::Conflict => Outcome::Conflict,
                    FaultKind::Ambiguous => Outcome::Ambiguous,
                    FaultKind::Before | FaultKind::Unavailable => Outcome::RecoveryUnavailable,
                };
                return report;
            }
            if fault.kind != FaultKind::Before {
                report.outcome = match fault.kind {
                    FaultKind::Conflict => Outcome::Conflict,
                    FaultKind::Ambiguous => Outcome::Ambiguous,
