        if um.dev() != meta.dev() || um.ino() == meta.ino() {
            return Err("cgroup mount root refused".into());
        }
        let controllers = read_at(&parent, c"cgroup.subtree_control")?;
        if !controllers.split_whitespace().any(|v| v == "memory") {
            return Err("memory controller is not delegated to children".into());
        }
        let mut random = [0u8; 16];
        File::open("/dev/urandom")
            .and_then(|mut f| f.read_exact(&mut random))
            .map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
        let name = std::ffi::CString::new(format!(
            "xynoos-test-{}",
            random
                .iter()
                .map(|b| format!("{b:02x}"))
                .collect::<String>()
        ))
        .unwrap();
        // SAFETY: valid directory descriptor and NUL-terminated single component.
        if unsafe { libc::mkdirat(parent.as_raw_fd(), name.as_ptr(), 0o700) } != 0 {
            return Err(io::Error::last_os_error().to_string());
        }
        let dir = openat(
            parent.as_raw_fd(),
            &name,
            libc::O_RDONLY | libc::O_DIRECTORY,
        )
        .map_err(|e| {
            format!(
                "open created child {}: {e}; cleanup not attempted because identity is unknown",
                name.to_string_lossy()
            )
        })?;
        let created = dir.metadata().map_err(|e| {
            format!(
                "stat created child {}: {e}; cleanup not attempted because identity is unknown",
                name.to_string_lossy()
            )
        })?;
        let result = (|| {
            let dm = dir.metadata().map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
            if dm.uid() != uid || dm.dev() != meta.dev() || dm.mode() & 0o022 != 0 {
                return Err("new cgroup ownership mismatch".into());
            }
            if !read_at(&dir, c"cgroup.procs")?.trim().is_empty() {
                return Err("new cgroup is not empty".into());
            }
            let control = openat(dir.as_raw_fd(), c"memory.low", libc::O_RDWR)?;
            let cm = control.metadata().map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
            if cm.dev() != dm.dev() || cm.uid() != uid {
                return Err("control ownership or filesystem mismatch".into());
            }
            let id = Identity {
                device: dm.dev(),
                inode: dm.ino(),
                control_inode: cm.ino(),
            };
            Ok(Self {
                parent: parent.try_clone().map_err(|e| e.to_string())?,
                dir,
                control,
                name: name.clone(),
                id,
                cleanup_on_drop: true,
                member: None,
                unresolved: false,
            })
        })();
        if let Err(error) = result {
            let same = openat(
                parent.as_raw_fd(),
                &name,
                libc::O_RDONLY | libc::O_DIRECTORY,
            )
            .and_then(|f| f.metadata().map_err(|e| e.to_string()))
            .is_ok_and(|m| m.dev() == created.dev() && m.ino() == created.ino());
            if !same {
                return Err(format!(
                    "{error}; cleanup of {} skipped: identity changed or unavailable",
                    name.to_string_lossy()
                ));
            }
            // SAFETY: identity was checked; removal still races another writer.
            if unsafe { libc::unlinkat(parent.as_raw_fd(), name.as_ptr(), libc::AT_REMOVEDIR) } != 0
            {
                return Err(format!(
                    "{error}; cleanup of {} failed: {}",
                    name.to_string_lossy(),
                    io::Error::last_os_error()
                ));
            }
            return Err(error);
        }
        result
    }
}
impl DisposableCgroup {
    /// Place a child owned by this process. The caller must keep it gated until
    /// `verify_child` succeeds and must kill and reap it on every error path.
    pub fn place_child(&mut self, child: &std::process::Child) -> Result<(), String> {
        physical_mutation_disabled()?;
        if self.member.is_some() {
            return Err("target already used".into());
        }
        self.identity()?;
        let id = xynoos_pressureiq::workload::identify(child.id())
            .map_err(|e| format!("child identity: {e:?}"))?;
        let control = openat(self.dir.as_raw_fd(), c"cgroup.procs", libc::O_WRONLY)?;
        use std::os::unix::fs::FileExt;
        let text = id.pid.to_string();
        let written = control
            .write_at(text.as_bytes(), 0)
            .map_err(|e| format!("placement: {e}"))?;
        if written != text.len() {
            return Err("short placement write".into());
        }
        self.member = Some(id);
        self.verify_child()
    }

    /// Verify exact single-process membership, process start time and cgroup identity.
    pub fn verify_child(&mut self) -> Result<(), String> {
        let id = self.member.clone().ok_or("no child placed")?;
        if xynoos_pressureiq::workload::identify(id.pid)
            .map_err(|e| format!("child identity: {e:?}"))?
            != id
        {
            return Err("child identity changed".into());
        }
        let snapshot = xynoos_pressureiq::cgroup_pressure::collect(&id)
            .map_err(|e| format!("membership: {e:?}"))?;
        if snapshot.cgroup.device != self.id.device || snapshot.cgroup.inode != self.id.inode {
            return Err("child cgroup identity mismatch".into());
        }
        self.identity()?;
        if read_at(&self.dir, c"cgroup.procs")?.trim() != id.pid.to_string() {
            return Err("target does not contain exactly the child".into());
        }
        Ok(())
    }

    /// Prevent all further control writes when child termination cannot be verified.
    pub fn mark_unresolved(&mut self) {
        self.unresolved = true;
        self.cleanup_on_drop = false;
    }

    /// Preserve the target for operator inspection after ambiguous state or rollback failure.
    pub fn preserve(mut self) {
        self.cleanup_on_drop = false;
    }

    /// Remove the empty disposable target explicitly and report cleanup errors.
    pub fn cleanup(mut self) -> Result<(), String> {
        physical_mutation_disabled()?;
        // A failed explicit cleanup must not be followed by an unreported retry in Drop.
        self.cleanup_on_drop = false;
        self.ready_to_restore()?;
        // SAFETY: valid descriptor and name; kernel rejects populated groups.
        if unsafe {
            libc::unlinkat(
                self.parent.as_raw_fd(),
                self.name.as_ptr(),
                libc::AT_REMOVEDIR,
            )
        } != 0
        {
            return Err(io::Error::last_os_error().to_string());
        }
        Ok(())
    }
}
impl Backend for DisposableCgroup {
    fn identity(&mut self) -> Result<Identity, String> {
        if self.unresolved {
            return Err("worker termination unresolved; target preserved".into());
        }
        let current = openat(
            self.parent.as_raw_fd(),
            &self.name,
            libc::O_RDONLY | libc::O_DIRECTORY,
        )?;
        let dm = current.metadata().map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
        let control = openat(current.as_raw_fd(), c"memory.low", libc::O_RDONLY)?;
        let cm = control.metadata().map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
        let members = read_at(&current, c"cgroup.procs")?;
        let members = members.trim();
        let allowed = match &self.member {
            None => members.is_empty(),
            Some(id) if members == id.pid.to_string() => {
                xynoos_pressureiq::workload::identify(id.pid).is_ok_and(|current| current == *id)
            }
            Some(id) if members.is_empty() => {
                matches!(
                    xynoos_pressureiq::workload::identify(id.pid),
                    Err(xynoos_pressureiq::Error::Unsupported)
                )
            }
            Some(_) => false,
        };
        if !allowed
            || !read_at(&current, c"cgroup.stat")?
                .lines()
                .any(|l| l.trim() == "nr_descendants 0")
        {
            return Err("unexpected cgroup membership or descendants".into());
        }
        let id = Identity {
            device: dm.dev(),
            inode: dm.ino(),
            control_inode: cm.ino(),
        };
        if id != self.id {
            return Err("cgroup identity changed".into());
        }
        Ok(id)
    }
    fn ready_to_restore(&mut self) -> Result<(), String> {
        self.identity()?;
        if !read_at(&self.dir, c"cgroup.procs")?.trim().is_empty() {
            return Err("controlled target still populated".into());
        }
        Ok(())
    }
    fn read_low(&mut self) -> Result<u64, String> {
        read_at(&self.dir, c"memory.low")?
            .trim()
            .parse()
            .map_err(|_| "invalid memory.low".into())
    }
    fn write_low(&mut self, value: u64) -> Result<(), String> {
        physical_mutation_disabled()?;
        use std::os::unix::fs::FileExt;
        let s = value.to_string();
        let n = self
            .control
            .write_at(s.as_bytes(), 0)
            .map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
        if n != s.len() {
            return Err("short control write".into());
        }
        Ok(())
    }
}
impl Drop for DisposableCgroup {
    fn drop(&mut self) {
        if physical_mutation_disabled().is_err() || !self.cleanup_on_drop {
            return;
        }
        // Do not deliberately remove a replacement target. The final check/removal is not atomic.
        if self.ready_to_restore().is_err() {
            return;
        }
        // SAFETY: removal fails for a populated cgroup.
        unsafe {
            libc::unlinkat(
                self.parent.as_raw_fd(),
                self.name.as_ptr(),
                libc::AT_REMOVEDIR,
            );
        }
    }
}
pub(crate) fn openat(fd: i32, name: &std::ffi::CStr, flags: i32) -> Result<File, String> {
    // SAFETY: valid C string; O_NOFOLLOW prevents final symlink traversal.
    let raw = unsafe {
        libc::openat(
            fd,
            name.as_ptr(),
            flags | libc::O_CLOEXEC | libc::O_NOFOLLOW,
        )
    };
    if raw < 0 {
        Err(io::Error::last_os_error().to_string())
    } else {
        // SAFETY: openat returned a new owned descriptor.
        Ok(unsafe { File::from_raw_fd(raw) })
    }
}
pub(crate) fn open_absolute_dir(path: &Path) -> Result<File, String> {
    use std::os::unix::ffi::OsStrExt;
    if !path.is_absolute() {
        return Err("parent must be absolute".into());
    }
    let mut dir = openat(libc::AT_FDCWD, c"/", libc::O_RDONLY | libc::O_DIRECTORY)?;
    for component in path.components().skip(1) {
        let std::path::Component::Normal(name) = component else {
            return Err("unsafe parent path".into());
        };
        let name = std::ffi::CString::new(name.as_bytes()).map_err(|_| "invalid parent path")?;
        dir = openat(dir.as_raw_fd(), &name, libc::O_RDONLY | libc::O_DIRECTORY)?;
    }
    Ok(dir)
}
pub(crate) fn read_at(dir: &File, name: &std::ffi::CStr) -> Result<String, String> {
    let f = openat(dir.as_raw_fd(), name, libc::O_RDONLY)?;
    let mut s = String::new();
    f.take(4097)
        .read_to_string(&mut s)
        .map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
    if s.len() > 4096 {
        return Err("control read too large".into());
    }
    Ok(s)
}

/// Read-only descriptor-relative manager and parent inspection; this is not
/// authorization to mutate the parent or proof of systemd unit ownership.
#[derive(Debug, Serialize)]
pub struct ManagerParentInspection {
    pub parent_device: u64,
    pub parent_inode: u64,
    pub manager_device: u64,
    pub manager_inode: u64,
    pub controllers: String,
    pub enabled: String,
    pub parent_empty: bool,
}

pub fn inspect_manager_parent(parent_path: &Path) -> Result<ManagerParentInspection, String> {
    let pid = std::process::id();
    let process = xynoos_pressureiq::workload::identify(pid)
        .map_err(|e| format!("manager identity: {e:?}"))?;
    let parent = open_absolute_dir(parent_path)?;
    let meta = parent.metadata().map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
    // SAFETY: geteuid has no preconditions.
    if unsafe { libc::geteuid() } == 0
        || meta.uid() != unsafe { libc::geteuid() }
        || meta.mode() & 0o022 != 0
    {
        return Err("parent ownership or mode invalid".into());
    }
    let mut st = std::mem::MaybeUninit::<libc::statfs>::uninit();
    // SAFETY: parent is an open descriptor and st is valid output storage.
    if unsafe { libc::fstatfs(parent.as_raw_fd(), st.as_mut_ptr()) } != 0 {
        return Err(io::Error::last_os_error().to_string());
    }
    // SAFETY: successful fstatfs initialized st.
    if unsafe { st.assume_init() }.f_type as u64 != 0x6367_7270 {
        return Err("parent is not cgroup v2".into());
    }
    let up = openat(
        parent.as_raw_fd(),
        c"..",
        libc::O_RDONLY | libc::O_DIRECTORY,
    )?;
    let up_meta = up.metadata().map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
    if up_meta.dev() != meta.dev() || up_meta.ino() == meta.ino() {
        return Err("cgroup mount root refused".into());
    }
    let manager = openat(
        parent.as_raw_fd(),
        c"manager",
        libc::O_RDONLY | libc::O_DIRECTORY,
    )?;
    let mm = manager.metadata().map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
    let observed = xynoos_pressureiq::cgroup_pressure::collect(&process)
        .map_err(|e| format!("manager membership: {e:?}"))?;
    if observed.cgroup.device != mm.dev() || observed.cgroup.inode != mm.ino() {
        return Err("manager cgroup identity mismatch".into());
    }
    let procs = read_at(&parent, c"cgroup.procs")?;
    let controllers = read_at(&parent, c"cgroup.controllers")?;
    let enabled = read_at(&parent, c"cgroup.subtree_control")?;
    let check = open_absolute_dir(parent_path)?
        .metadata()
        .map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
    let membership = xynoos_pressureiq::cgroup_pressure::collect(&process)
        .map_err(|e| format!("manager membership changed: {e:?}"))?;
    if (check.dev(), check.ino()) != (meta.dev(), meta.ino())
        || (membership.cgroup.device, membership.cgroup.inode) != (mm.dev(), mm.ino())
    {
        return Err("parent or manager identity changed".into());
    }
    Ok(ManagerParentInspection {
        parent_device: meta.dev(),
        parent_inode: meta.ino(),
        manager_device: mm.dev(),
        manager_inode: mm.ino(),
        controllers,
        enabled,
        parent_empty: procs.trim().is_empty(),
    })
}


FILE crates/xynoos-linux-control/tests/kernel_transaction.rs
#![cfg(target_os = "linux")]
//! Physical opt-in has been removed. This external consumer checks the closed gate.
use std::path::Path;
use xynoos_linux_control::transaction::DisposableCgroup;

#[test]
fn physical_constructor_is_disabled_even_with_authorization() {
    for authorized in [false, true] {
        for path in ["/sys/fs/cgroup", "/nonexistent-xynoos-parent"] {
            let error = DisposableCgroup::create(Path::new(path), authorized)

FILE crates/xynoos-linux-control/tests/linux.rs
#![cfg(target_os = "linux")]
use std::{
    path::Path,
    time::{Duration, Instant},
};
use xynoos_linux_control::{discover, execute, prepare, Action, Decision, MAX_LOW_BYTES};
use xynoos_pressureiq::system::collect_system;

#[test]
fn linux_discovery_and_read_only_decisions() {
    let caps = discover();
    if caps.cgroup2 {
        assert!(caps.controllers.is_some());
        let s = collect_system();
        let invalid = prepare(
            &s,
            Instant::now(),
            Path::new("/sys/fs/cgroup"),
            Action::MemoryLow { bytes: 0 },
        );
        assert!(matches!(invalid.decision, Decision::Declined(_)));
        assert!(matches!(execute(&invalid, false), Decision::Declined(_)));
        assert!(matches!(execute(&invalid, true), Decision::Declined(_)));
    }
}

#[test]
fn stale_bounds_and_unsafe_targets_are_declined() {
    let s = collect_system();
    for (now, target, bytes) in [
        (
            Instant::now() + Duration::from_secs(6),
            "/sys/fs/cgroup/init",
            0,
        ),
        (Instant::now(), "/sys/fs/cgroup/init", MAX_LOW_BYTES + 4096),
        (Instant::now(), "/sys/fs/cgroup/init", 1),
        (Instant::now(), "/sys/fs/cgroup/../init", 0),
        (Instant::now(), "/tmp", 0),
    ] {
        assert!(matches!(
            prepare(&s, now, Path::new(target), Action::MemoryLow { bytes }).decision,
            Decision::Declined(_)
        ));
    }
}

#[test]
fn telemetry_errors_are_not_accepted() {
    let mut s = collect_system();
    s.memory.mem.result = Err(xynoos_pressureiq::Error::Permission);
    let audit = prepare(
        &s,
        Instant::now(),
        Path::new("/sys/fs/cgroup/init"),
        Action::MemoryLow { bytes: 0 },
    );
    assert!(matches!(audit.decision, Decision::Declined(ref reason) if reason.contains("meminfo")));
}


FILE crates/xynoos-linux-control/tests/transactions.rs
#![cfg(target_os = "linux")]
use std::{
    fs,
    os::unix::fs::MetadataExt,
    path::PathBuf,
    sync::atomic::{AtomicU64, Ordering},
    time::Duration,
};
use xynoos_linux_control::{
    transaction::{run, Backend, DisposableCgroup, Identity, Outcome},
    Action, MAX_LOW_BYTES,
};
use xynoos_pressureiq::system::collect_system;
static NEXT: AtomicU64 = AtomicU64::new(0);
struct Fixture {
    dir: PathBuf,
    reads: usize,
    writes: usize,
    fail_write: Option<usize>,
    fail_after_write: Option<usize>,
    change_read: Option<(usize, u64)>,
    identities: usize,
    fail_identity: Option<usize>,
    unresolved: bool,
}
impl Fixture {
    fn new() -> Self {
        let dir = std::env::temp_dir().join(format!(
            "xynoos-control-{}-{}",
            std::process::id(),
            NEXT.fetch_add(1, Ordering::Relaxed)
        ));
        fs::create_dir(&dir).unwrap();
        fs::write(dir.join("memory.low"), "4096").unwrap();
        Self {
            dir,
            reads: 0,
            writes: 0,
            fail_write: None,
            fail_after_write: None,
            change_read: None,
            identities: 0,
            fail_identity: None,
            unresolved: false,
        }
    }
    fn value(&self) -> u64 {
        fs::read_to_string(self.dir.join("memory.low"))
            .unwrap()
            .parse()
            .unwrap()
    }
}
impl Drop for Fixture {
    fn drop(&mut self) {
        fs::remove_dir_all(&self.dir).unwrap();
    }
}
impl Backend for Fixture {
    fn identity(&mut self) -> Result<Identity, String> {
        self.identities += 1;
        if self.fail_identity == Some(self.identities) {
            return Err("injected identity failure".into());
        }
        let d = fs::metadata(&self.dir).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
        let f = fs::metadata(self.dir.join("memory.low")).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
        Ok(Identity {
            device: d.dev(),
            inode: d.ino(),
            control_inode: f.ino(),
        })
    }
    fn ready_to_restore(&mut self) -> Result<(), String> {
        if self.unresolved {
            Err("injected unreaped worker".into())
        } else {
            Ok(())
        }
    }
    fn read_low(&mut self) -> Result<u64, String> {
        self.reads += 1;
        if let Some((n, v)) = self.change_read {
            if self.reads == n {
                fs::write(self.dir.join("memory.low"), v.to_string()).unwrap();
            }
        }
        Ok(self.value())
    }
    fn write_low(&mut self, value: u64) -> Result<(), String> {
        self.writes += 1;
        if self.fail_write == Some(self.writes) {
            return Err("injected write failure".into());
        }
        fs::write(self.dir.join("memory.low"), value.to_string()).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
        if self.fail_after_write == Some(self.writes) {
            return Err("injected error after mutation".into());
        }
        Ok(())
    }
}

#[test]
fn identity_failure_never_triggers_blind_write_or_rollback() {
    for (failure, outcome, writes, value) in [
        (1, Outcome::Declined, 0, 4096),
        (2, Outcome::Conflict, 0, 4096),
        (3, Outcome::Conflict, 1, 8192),
        (4, Outcome::Conflict, 1, 8192),
        (5, Outcome::RollbackFailed, 2, 4096),
    ] {
        let mut f = Fixture::new();
        f.fail_identity = Some(failure);
        let report = run(&mut f, &collect_system(), action(), true);
        assert_eq!(report.outcome, outcome);
        assert_eq!(f.writes, writes);
        assert_eq!(f.value(), value);
    }
}
fn action() -> Action {
    Action::MemoryLow { bytes: 8192 }
}
#[test]
fn success_applies_and_restores() {
    let mut f = Fixture::new();
    let r = run(&mut f, &collect_system(), action(), true);
    assert_eq!(r.outcome, Outcome::Restored);
    assert_eq!(f.value(), 4096);
    assert_eq!(f.writes, 2);
}
#[test]
fn unauthorized_stale_and_bounds_never_write() {
    let mut f = Fixture::new();
    let mut s = collect_system();
    assert_eq!(run(&mut f, &s, action(), false).outcome, Outcome::Declined);
    s.monotonic -= Duration::from_secs(6);
    assert_eq!(run(&mut f, &s, action(), true).outcome, Outcome::Declined);
    assert_eq!(
        run(
            &mut f,
            &collect_system(),
            Action::MemoryLow {
                bytes: MAX_LOW_BYTES + 4096
            },
            true
        )
        .outcome,
        Outcome::Declined
    );
    assert_eq!(f.writes, 0);
}
#[test]
fn apply_failure_and_verification_mismatch() {
    let mut f = Fixture::new();
    f.fail_write = Some(1);
    let r = run(&mut f, &collect_system(), action(), true);
    assert_eq!(r.outcome, Outcome::Ambiguous);
    assert_eq!(f.value(), 4096);
    let mut f = Fixture::new();
    f.change_read = Some((3, 4096));
    let r = run(&mut f, &collect_system(), action(), true);
    assert_eq!(r.outcome, Outcome::VerificationFailed);
    assert_eq!(f.value(), 4096);
}
#[test]
fn concurrent_modification_is_not_overwritten() {
    for read in [2, 3, 4] {
        let mut f = Fixture::new();
        f.change_read = Some((read, 12288));
        let r = run(&mut f, &collect_system(), action(), true);
        assert_eq!(r.outcome, Outcome::Conflict);
        assert_eq!(f.value(), 12288);
    }
}
#[test]
fn rollback_failure_is_explicit() {
    let mut f = Fixture::new();
    f.fail_write = Some(2);
    let r = run(&mut f, &collect_system(), action(), true);
    assert_eq!(r.outcome, Outcome::RollbackFailed);
    assert_eq!(f.value(), 8192);
}
#[test]
fn real_backend_refuses_unauthorized_and_non_cgroup_parent() {
    assert!(DisposableCgroup::create(std::path::Path::new("/sys/fs/cgroup/init"), false).is_err());
    let f = Fixture::new();
    assert!(DisposableCgroup::create(&f.dir, true).is_err());
    if let Ok(meta) = fs::metadata("/sys/fs/cgroup/init") {
        // SAFETY: geteuid has no preconditions.
        if meta.uid() != unsafe { libc::geteuid() } {
            assert!(
                DisposableCgroup::create(std::path::Path::new("/sys/fs/cgroup/init"), true)
                    .is_err()
            );
        }
    }
}

#[test]
fn failed_apply_after_mutation_is_preserved_without_rollback() {
    let mut f = Fixture::new();
    f.fail_after_write = Some(1);
    let report = run(&mut f, &collect_system(), action(), true);
    assert_eq!(report.outcome, Outcome::Ambiguous);
    assert_eq!(f.value(), 8192);
    assert_eq!(f.writes, 1);
    assert!(report.events.iter().any(|e| e.contains("unresolved")));
}

#[test]
fn rollback_verification_failure_is_reported() {
    let mut f = Fixture::new();
    f.change_read = Some((5, 12288));
    let report = run(&mut f, &collect_system(), action(), true);
    assert_eq!(report.outcome, Outcome::RollbackFailed);
    assert_eq!(f.value(), 12288);
}

#[test]
fn operation_runs_only_while_verified_policy_is_active() {
    use xynoos_linux_control::transaction::run_during;
    let mut f = Fixture::new();
    let report = run_during(&mut f, &collect_system(), action(), true, |target| {
        assert_eq!(target.read_low()?, 8192);
        Ok(())
    });
    assert_eq!(report.outcome, Outcome::Restored);
    assert_eq!(f.value(), 4096);
    assert!(report.events.iter().any(|e| e == "operation completed"));
}

#[test]
fn failed_operation_restores_and_reports_failure() {
    use xynoos_linux_control::transaction::run_during;
    let mut f = Fixture::new();
    let report = run_during(&mut f, &collect_system(), action(), true, |_| {
        Err("worker timeout".into())
    });
    assert_eq!(report.outcome, Outcome::OperationFailed);
    assert_eq!(f.value(), 4096);
    assert!(report.events.iter().any(|e| e.contains("worker timeout")));
}

#[test]
fn failed_authorization_and_verification_never_release_worker() {
    use xynoos_linux_control::transaction::run_during;
    for (authorized, fail_write, change_read) in [
        (false, None, None),
        (true, Some(1), None),
        (true, None, Some((3, 4096))),
    ] {
        let mut f = Fixture::new();
        f.fail_write = fail_write;
        f.change_read = change_read;
        let mut called = false;
        let report = run_during(&mut f, &collect_system(), action(), authorized, |_| {
            called = true;
            Ok(())
        });
        assert!(!called);
        assert_ne!(report.outcome, Outcome::Restored);
    }
}

#[test]
fn operation_conflict_is_not_overwritten() {
    use xynoos_linux_control::transaction::run_during;
    let mut f = Fixture::new();
    let report = run_during(&mut f, &collect_system(), action(), true, |target| {
        fs::write(target.dir.join("memory.low"), "12288").map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
        Ok(())
    });
    assert_eq!(report.outcome, Outcome::Conflict);
    assert_eq!(f.value(), 12288);
    assert_eq!(f.writes, 1);
}

#[test]
fn unreaped_worker_blocks_restoration_and_preserves_control() {
    use xynoos_linux_control::transaction::run_during;
    let mut f = Fixture::new();
    let report = run_during(&mut f, &collect_system(), action(), true, |target| {
        target.unresolved = true;
        Err("injected termination failure".into())
    });
    assert_eq!(report.outcome, Outcome::Conflict);
    assert_eq!(f.value(), 8192);
    assert_eq!(f.writes, 1);
    assert!(report
        .events
        .iter()
        .any(|s| s.contains("restoration blocked")));
}


FILE crates/xynoos-pressureiq/examples/pressureiq_research.rs
use std::{hint::black_box, time::Instant};
use xynoos_pressureiq::{parse_meminfo, parse_psi, parse_vmstat};
fn main() {
    let cases=[("psi","some avg10=0.00 avg60=0.00 avg300=0.00 total=123\nfull avg10=0.00 avg60=0.00 avg300=0.00 total=12\n"),("mem","MemTotal: 1234 kB\nMemAvailable: 1200 kB\n"),("vm","pgfault 123\npgscan_direct 5\n")];
    println!("case,iterations,total_ns,ns_per_parse");
    for (name, input) in cases {
        let start = Instant::now();
        for _ in 0..10000 {
            match name {
                "psi" => {
                    black_box(parse_psi(black_box(input)).unwrap());
                }
                "mem" => {
                    black_box(parse_meminfo(black_box(input)).unwrap());
                }
                _ => {
                    black_box(parse_vmstat(black_box(input)).unwrap());
                }
            }
        }
        let ns = start.elapsed().as_nanos();
        println!("{name},10000,{ns},{}", ns / 10000);
    }
}


FILE crates/xynoos-pressureiq/src/cgroup_pressure.rs
//! Read-only cgroup v2 PSI for an explicitly identified process.
//! Membership alone never establishes that a cgroup is exclusive to a workload.
use crate::{parse_psi, read, workload, Error, Psi};
use std::{
    fs,
    os::unix::fs::MetadataExt,
    path::{Component, Path, PathBuf},
    time::{Duration, Instant},
};

#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub struct CgroupIdentity {
    pub path: PathBuf,
    pub device: u64,
    pub inode: u64,
}
#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
pub struct PressureSource {
    pub path: PathBuf,
    pub at: Instant,
    pub result: Result<Psi, Error>,
}
#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
pub struct Snapshot {
    pub process: workload::Identity,
    pub cgroup: CgroupIdentity,
    pub cpu: PressureSource,
    pub memory: PressureSource,
    pub io: PressureSource,
    /// Point-in-time checks, not proof against concurrent membership changes.
    pub isolation_check: workload::Reading<()>,
}

fn io_error(e: std::io::Error) -> Error {
    match e.kind() {
        std::io::ErrorKind::NotFound => Error::Unsupported,
        std::io::ErrorKind::PermissionDenied => Error::Permission,
        _ => Error::Io(e.kind().to_string()),
    }
}
fn checked_identity(path: &Path) -> Result<CgroupIdentity, Error> {
    let root = Path::new("/sys/fs/cgroup");
    let relative = path
        .strip_prefix(root)
        .map_err(|_| Error::Malformed("outside cgroup mount".into()))?;
    let mut cursor = root.to_path_buf();
    for component in relative.components() {
        let Component::Normal(part) = component else {
            return Err(Error::Malformed("cgroup path component".into()));
        };
        cursor.push(part);
        let m = fs::symlink_metadata(&cursor).map_err(io_error)?;
        if m.file_type().is_symlink() || !m.is_dir() {
            return Err(Error::Malformed("cgroup path is not a directory".into()));
        }
    }
    let m = fs::symlink_metadata(&cursor).map_err(io_error)?;
    if m.file_type().is_symlink() || !m.is_dir() {
        return Err(Error::Malformed("cgroup root".into()));
    }
    Ok(CgroupIdentity {
        path: cursor,
        device: m.dev(),
        inode: m.ino(),
    })
}
fn membership(id: &workload::Identity) -> Result<CgroupIdentity, Error> {
    if workload::identify(id.pid)? != *id {
        return Err(Error::Malformed("process identity changed".into()));
    }
    let own_ns = fs::metadata("/proc/self/ns/cgroup").map_err(io_error)?;
    let target_ns = fs::metadata(format!("/proc/{}/ns/cgroup", id.pid)).map_err(io_error)?;
    if (own_ns.dev(), own_ns.ino()) != (target_ns.dev(), target_ns.ino()) {
        return Err(Error::Malformed("different cgroup namespace".into()));
    }
    let member = read(&PathBuf::from(format!("/proc/{}/cgroup", id.pid)))?;
    if member.lines().filter(|l| l.starts_with("0::")).count() != 1 {
        return Err(Error::Malformed("ambiguous cgroup membership".into()));
    }
    let mounts = read(Path::new("/proc/self/mountinfo"))?;
    if mounts
        .lines()
        .filter(|l| {
            l.split_once(" - ")
                .is_some_and(|(_, r)| r.starts_with("cgroup2 "))
        })
        .count()
        != 1
    {
        return Err(Error::Malformed("ambiguous cgroup2 mount".into()));
    }
    let path = crate::system::parse_cgroup_path(&member, &mounts)?;
    let cg = checked_identity(&path)?;
    if workload::identify(id.pid)? != *id {
        return Err(Error::Malformed("process identity changed".into()));
    }
    Ok(cg)
}
fn source(id: &workload::Identity, cg: &CgroupIdentity, name: &str) -> PressureSource {
    let path = cg.path.join(name);
    let at = Instant::now();
    let result = (|| {
        if membership(id)? != *cg {
            return Err(Error::Malformed("cgroup identity changed".into()));
        }
        let result = read(&path).and_then(|v| parse_psi(&v));
        if membership(id)? != *cg {
            return Err(Error::Malformed("cgroup identity changed".into()));
        }
        result
    })();
    PressureSource { path, at, result }
}
fn isolation_check(id: &workload::Identity, cg: &CgroupIdentity) -> workload::Reading<()> {
    let at = Instant::now();
    let result = (|| {
        if membership(id)? != *cg {
            return Err(Error::Malformed("cgroup identity changed".into()));
        }
        let procs = read(&cg.path.join("cgroup.procs"))?;
        let pids: Vec<_> = procs.lines().collect();
        if pids.len() != 1 || pids[0].parse::<u32>().ok() != Some(id.pid) {
            return Err(Error::Malformed(
                "cgroup not exclusively populated by target process".into(),
            ));
        }
        for (i, entry) in fs::read_dir(&cg.path).map_err(io_error)?.enumerate() {
            if i >= 4096 {
                return Err(Error::Bounds);
            }
            let kind = entry.map_err(io_error)?.file_type().map_err(io_error)?;
            if kind.is_dir() || kind.is_symlink() {
                return Err(Error::Malformed("cgroup descendants or symlink".into()));
            }
        }
        if membership(id)? != *cg {
            return Err(Error::Malformed("cgroup identity changed".into()));
        }
        Ok(())
    })();
    workload::Reading { at, result }
}
/// Source timestamps are taken separately; PSI values are percent avg10 and total microseconds.
pub fn collect(id: &workload::Identity) -> Result<Snapshot, Error> {
    let cgroup = membership(id)?;
    Ok(Snapshot {
        process: id.clone(),
        cpu: source(id, &cgroup, "cpu.pressure"),
        memory: source(id, &cgroup, "memory.pressure"),
        io: source(id, &cgroup, "io.pressure"),
        isolation_check: isolation_check(id, &cgroup),
        cgroup,
    })
}
#[derive(Debug)]
pub struct PressureDelta {
    pub interval: Duration,
    pub some_us: u64,
    pub full_us: Option<u64>,
}
/// Deltas are independent per PSI source; reset and missing data are errors.
pub fn delta(a: &Snapshot, b: &Snapshot, source_name: &str) -> Result<PressureDelta, Error> {
    if a.process != b.process || a.cgroup != b.cgroup {
        return Err(Error::Malformed("identity mismatch".into()));
    }
    let expected = a.cgroup.path.join(format!("{source_name}.pressure"));
    let (a, b) = match source_name {
        "cpu" => (&a.cpu, &b.cpu),
        "memory" => (&a.memory, &b.memory),
        "io" => (&a.io, &b.io),
        _ => return Err(Error::Malformed("source".into())),
    };
    if a.path != b.path || a.path != expected {
        return Err(Error::Malformed("source mismatch".into()));
    }
    let interval =
        b.at.checked_duration_since(a.at)
            .filter(|v| !v.is_zero())
            .ok_or_else(|| Error::Malformed("monotonic interval".into()))?;
    let x = a.result.as_ref().map_err(Clone::clone)?;
    let y = b.result.as_ref().map_err(Clone::clone)?;
    let sub = |a: u64, b: u64| {
        b.checked_sub(a)
            .ok_or_else(|| Error::CounterReset(source_name.into()))
    };
    Ok(PressureDelta {
        interval,
        some_us: sub(x.some.total_us, y.some.total_us)?,
        full_us: match (&x.full, &y.full) {
            (Some(x), Some(y)) => Some(sub(x.total_us, y.total_us)?),
            _ => None,
        },
    })
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;
    #[test]
    fn paths_and_independent_deltas() {
        assert!(checked_identity(Path::new("/sys/fs/cgroup/../etc")).is_err());
        assert!(checked_identity(Path::new("/tmp")).is_err());
        let t = Instant::now();
        let cg = CgroupIdentity {
            path: "/sys/fs/cgroup/test".into(),
            device: 1,
            inode: 2,
        };
        let make = |n| {
            let source = |name| PressureSource {
                path: cg.path.join(name),
                at: t + Duration::from_secs(n),
                result: Ok(
                    parse_psi(&format!("some avg10=0 avg60=0 avg300=0 total={}", n * 10)).unwrap(),
                ),
            };
            Snapshot {
                process: workload::Identity {
                    pid: 1,
                    start_ticks: 1,
                },
                cpu: source("cpu.pressure"),
                memory: source("memory.pressure"),
                io: source("io.pressure"),
                isolation_check: workload::Reading {
                    at: t,
                    result: Ok(()),
                },
                cgroup: cg.clone(),
            }
        };
        let a = make(1);
        let mut b = make(2);
        b.memory.result = Err(Error::Permission);
        assert_eq!(delta(&a, &b, "cpu").unwrap().some_us, 10);
        assert!(matches!(delta(&a, &b, "memory"), Err(Error::Permission)));
        b.cpu.result = a.cpu.result.clone();
        let mut old = a.clone();
        old.cpu.result = make(2).cpu.result;
        assert!(matches!(
            delta(&old, &b, "cpu"),
            Err(Error::CounterReset(_))
        ));
        b.cgroup.inode += 1;
        assert!(delta(&a, &b, "io").is_err());
        b.cgroup.inode -= 1;
        let mut wrong = a.clone();
        wrong.io.path = wrong.cgroup.path.join("cpu.pressure");
        b.io.path = wrong.io.path.clone();
        assert!(delta(&wrong, &b, "io").is_err());
    }
}


FILE crates/xynoos-pressureiq/src/lib.rs
#![forbid(unsafe_code)]
use std::{
    collections::BTreeMap,
    fmt,
    fs::File,
    io::{self, Read},
    path::Path,
    time::{Duration, Instant, SystemTime, UNIX_EPOCH},
};
#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub enum Error {
    Unsupported,
    Permission,
    Io(String),
    Malformed(String),
    Missing(String),
    Duplicate(String),
    Overflow(String),
    Bounds,
    CounterReset(String),
}
impl fmt::Display for Error {
    fn fmt(&self, f: &mut fmt::Formatter<'_>) -> fmt::Result {
        write!(f, "{self:?}")
    }
}
impl std::error::Error for Error {}
fn num(s: &str, key: &str) -> Result<u64, Error> {
    s.parse::<u64>().map_err(|e| match e.kind() {
        std::num::IntErrorKind::PosOverflow => Error::Overflow(key.into()),
        _ => Error::Malformed(key.into()),
    })
}
#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq)]
pub struct PressureLine {
    pub avg10: f64,
    pub avg60: f64,
    pub avg300: f64,
    pub total_us: u64,
}
#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq)]
pub struct Psi {
    pub some: PressureLine,
    pub full: Option<PressureLine>,
}
pub fn parse_psi(input: &str) -> Result<Psi, Error> {
    let mut some = None;
    let mut full = None;
    for line in input.lines() {
        let mut words = line.split_whitespace();
        let Some(kind) = words.next() else { continue };
        if kind != "some" && kind != "full" {
            continue;
        }
        let mut fields = BTreeMap::new();
        for item in words {
            let (k, v) = item
                .split_once('=')
                .ok_or_else(|| Error::Malformed(item.into()))?;
            if matches!(k, "avg10" | "avg60" | "avg300" | "total") && fields.insert(k, v).is_some()
            {
                return Err(Error::Duplicate(k.into()));
            }
        }
        let get = |k: &str| {
            fields
                .get(k)
                .copied()
                .ok_or_else(|| Error::Missing(k.into()))
        };
        let avg = |k: &str| -> Result<f64, Error> {
            let v = get(k)?
                .parse::<f64>()
                .map_err(|_| Error::Malformed(k.into()))?;
            if !v.is_finite() || v.is_sign_negative() || !(0.0..=100.0).contains(&v) {
                return Err(Error::Malformed(k.into()));
            }
            Ok(v)
        };
        let value = PressureLine {
            avg10: avg("avg10")?,
            avg60: avg("avg60")?,
            avg300: avg("avg300")?,
            total_us: num(get("total")?, "total")?,
        };
        let slot = if kind == "some" { &mut some } else { &mut full };
        if slot.replace(value).is_some() {
            return Err(Error::Duplicate(kind.into()));
        }
    }
    Ok(Psi {
        some: some.ok_or_else(|| Error::Missing("some".into()))?,
        full,
    })
}
const MEM_FIELDS: [&str; 12] = [
    "MemTotal",
    "MemFree",
    "MemAvailable",
    "Buffers",
    "Cached",
    "SwapTotal",
    "SwapFree",
    "Active",
    "Inactive",
    "Dirty",
    "Writeback",
    "SReclaimable",
];
#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub struct MemInfo {
    pub bytes: BTreeMap<String, u64>,
}
pub fn parse_meminfo(input: &str) -> Result<MemInfo, Error> {
    let mut bytes = BTreeMap::new();
    for line in input.lines() {
        let Some((k, v)) = line.split_once(':') else {
            continue;
        };
        if !MEM_FIELDS.contains(&k) {
            continue;
        }
        let mut fields = v.split_whitespace();
        let raw = fields.next().ok_or_else(|| Error::Missing(k.into()))?;
        if fields.next() != Some("kB") || fields.next().is_some() {
            return Err(Error::Malformed(k.into()));
        }
        let value = num(raw, k)?
            .checked_mul(1024)
            .ok_or_else(|| Error::Overflow(k.into()))?;
        if bytes.insert(k.into(), value).is_some() {
            return Err(Error::Duplicate(k.into()));
        }
    }
    if !bytes.contains_key("MemTotal") {
        return Err(Error::Missing("MemTotal".into()));
    }
    Ok(MemInfo { bytes })
}
#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub struct VmStat {
    pub counters: BTreeMap<String, u64>,
}
pub fn parse_vmstat(input: &str) -> Result<VmStat, Error> {
    let mut counters = BTreeMap::new();
    for line in input.lines() {
        let mut f = line.split_whitespace();
        let Some(k) = f.next() else { continue };
        if !(matches!(k, "pgfault" | "pgmajfault" | "pswpin" | "pswpout")
            || k.starts_with("pgscan_")
            || k.starts_with("pgsteal_")
            || k.starts_with("workingset_refault_"))
        {
            continue;
        }
        let v = num(f.next().ok_or_else(|| Error::Missing(k.into()))?, k)?;
        if f.next().is_some() {
            return Err(Error::Malformed(k.into()));
        }
        if counters.insert(k.into(), v).is_some() {
            return Err(Error::Duplicate(k.into()));
        }
    }
    if counters.is_empty() {
        return Err(Error::Missing("vmstat counters".into()));
    }
    Ok(VmStat { counters })
}
pub fn delta(old: &VmStat, new: &VmStat) -> Result<VmStat, Error> {
    let mut counters = BTreeMap::new();
    for (k, v) in &new.counters {
        if let Some(previous) = old.counters.get(k) {
            counters.insert(
                k.clone(),
                v.checked_sub(*previous)
                    .ok_or_else(|| Error::CounterReset(k.clone()))?,
            );
        }
    }
    Ok(VmStat { counters })
}
#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
pub struct Source<T> {
    pub result: Result<T, Error>,
    pub read_ns: u128,
    pub parse_ns: u128,
}
#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
pub struct Snapshot {
    pub source_root: std::path::PathBuf,
    pub timestamp: SystemTime,
    pub monotonic: Instant,
    pub psi: Source<Psi>,
    pub mem: Source<MemInfo>,
    pub vm: Source<VmStat>,
    pub elapsed_ns: u128,
}
impl Snapshot {
    pub fn partial(&self) -> bool {
        self.psi.result.is_err() || self.mem.result.is_err() || self.vm.result.is_err()
    }
}
fn read(path: &Path) -> Result<String, Error> {
    let mut f = File::open(path).map_err(|e| match e.kind() {
        io::ErrorKind::NotFound => Error::Unsupported,
        io::ErrorKind::PermissionDenied => Error::Permission,
        _ => Error::Io(e.kind().to_string()),
    })?;
    let mut text = String::new();
    f.by_ref()
        .take(1024 * 1024 + 1)
        .read_to_string(&mut text)
        .map_err(|e| Error::Io(e.kind().to_string()))?;
    if text.len() > 1024 * 1024 {
        return Err(Error::Bounds);
    }
    Ok(text)
}
fn source<T>(path: &Path, parser: fn(&str) -> Result<T, Error>) -> Source<T> {
    let now = Instant::now();
    let data = read(path);
    let read_ns = now.elapsed().as_nanos();
    let now = Instant::now();
    let result = data.and_then(|v| parser(&v));
    Source {
        result,
        read_ns,
        parse_ns: now.elapsed().as_nanos(),
    }
}
pub fn collect_from(root: &Path) -> Snapshot {
    let start = Instant::now();
    let timestamp = SystemTime::now();
    let monotonic = Instant::now();
    let psi = source(&root.join("pressure/memory"), parse_psi);
    let mem = source(&root.join("meminfo"), parse_meminfo);
    let vm = source(&root.join("vmstat"), parse_vmstat);
    Snapshot {
        source_root: root.to_path_buf(),
        timestamp,
        monotonic,
        psi,
        mem,
        vm,
        elapsed_ns: start.elapsed().as_nanos(),
    }
}
pub fn collect() -> Snapshot {
    collect_from(Path::new("/proc"))
}
pub fn validate_sampling(count: u32, interval: Duration) -> Result<(), Error> {
    if !(1..=60).contains(&count)
        || interval < Duration::from_millis(100)
        || interval > Duration::from_secs(60)
    {
        Err(Error::Bounds)
    } else {
        Ok(())
    }
}
pub fn sample<F: FnMut(&Snapshot) -> Result<(), Error>>(
    count: u32,
    interval: Duration,
    mut emit: F,
) -> Result<(), Error> {
    validate_sampling(count, interval)?;
    for i in 0..count {
        if i > 0 {
            std::thread::sleep(interval)
        }
        emit(&collect())?;
    }
    Ok(())
}
pub const CSV_HEADER:&str="timestamp_unix_ms,partial,psi_status,psi_some_avg10_pct,psi_full_avg10_pct,psi_some_total_us,mem_status,mem_total_bytes,mem_available_bytes,vm_status,pgfault,pgmajfault,pswpin,pswpout,read_psi_ns,parse_psi_ns,read_mem_ns,parse_mem_ns,read_vm_ns,parse_vm_ns,snapshot_ns";
fn status<T>(s: &Source<T>) -> &'static str {
    match &s.result {
        Ok(_) => "ok",
        Err(Error::Unsupported) => "unsupported",
        Err(Error::Permission) => "permission",
        Err(_) => "error",
    }
}
fn opt<T: fmt::Display>(v: Option<T>) -> String {
    v.map(|x| x.to_string()).unwrap_or_default()
}
pub fn csv_row(s: &Snapshot) -> String {
    let psi = s.psi.result.as_ref().ok();
    let mem = s.mem.result.as_ref().ok();
    let vm = s.vm.result.as_ref().ok();
    let mb = |k| mem.and_then(|m| m.bytes.get(k)).copied();
    let vc = |k| vm.and_then(|v| v.counters.get(k)).copied();
    format!(
        "{},{},{},{},{},{},{},{},{},{},{},{},{},{},{},{},{},{},{},{},{}",
        s.timestamp
            .duration_since(UNIX_EPOCH)
            .unwrap_or_default()
            .as_millis(),
        s.partial(),
        status(&s.psi),
        opt(psi.map(|p| p.some.avg10)),
        opt(psi.and_then(|p| p.full.as_ref().map(|v| v.avg10))),
        opt(psi.map(|p| p.some.total_us)),
        status(&s.mem),
        opt(mb("MemTotal")),
        opt(mb("MemAvailable")),
        status(&s.vm),
        opt(vc("pgfault")),
        opt(vc("pgmajfault")),
        opt(vc("pswpin")),
        opt(vc("pswpout")),
        s.psi.read_ns,
        s.psi.parse_ns,
        s.mem.read_ns,
        s.mem.parse_ns,
        s.vm.read_ns,
        s.vm.parse_ns,
        s.elapsed_ns
    )
}
#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;
    const PSI:&str="full total=6 avg300=0.03 avg10=2.50 avg60=1.00 extra=8\nsome avg60=2 avg10=4 avg300=1 total=10  \n";
    #[test]
    fn psi_ok_and_optional() {
        let p = parse_psi(PSI).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(p.some.total_us, 10);
        assert_eq!(p.full.unwrap().avg10, 2.5);
        assert!(parse_psi("some avg10=0 avg60=0 avg300=0 total=0")
            .unwrap()
            .full
            .is_none());
    }
    #[test]
    fn psi_rejects_invalid() {
        for s in [
            "",
            "full avg10=0 avg60=0 avg300=0 total=0",
            "some avg10=NaN avg60=0 avg300=0 total=0",
            "some avg10=inf avg60=0 avg300=0 total=0",
            "some avg10=-1 avg60=0 avg300=0 total=0",
            "some avg10=-0.00 avg60=0 avg300=0 total=0",
            "some avg10=101 avg60=0 avg300=0 total=0",
            "some avg10=0 avg10=0 avg60=0 avg300=0 total=0",
            "some avg10=0 avg60=0 avg300=0 total=18446744073709551616",
            "some avg10=0 avg60=0 total=0",
        ] {
            assert!(parse_psi(s).is_err(), "{s}");
        }
        assert!(parse_psi(
            "some avg10=0 avg60=0 avg300=0 total=0\nsome avg10=0 avg60=0 avg300=0 total=0"
        )
        .is_err());
    }
    #[test]
    fn meminfo_units_and_optional() {
        let m = parse_meminfo("Cached: 2 kB\nMemTotal: 4 kB\nunknown: 9 kB\n").unwrap();
        assert_eq!(m.bytes["MemTotal"], 4096);
        assert!(!m.bytes.contains_key("MemAvailable"));
        for s in [
            "",
            "MemTotal: 4 MB",
            "MemTotal: 18014398509481984 kB",
            "MemTotal: 1 kB\nMemTotal: 2 kB",
        ] {
            assert!(parse_meminfo(s).is_err());
        }
    }
    #[test]
    fn vm_delta_and_reset() {
        let a = parse_vmstat("unknown 2\npgfault 9\npgscan_direct 1\n").unwrap();
        let b = parse_vmstat("pgfault 12\npgscan_direct 4\npgsteal_new 8\n").unwrap();
        let d = delta(&a, &b).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(d.counters["pgfault"], 3);
        assert!(!d.counters.contains_key("pgsteal_new"));
        assert!(matches!(delta(&b, &a), Err(Error::CounterReset(_))));
        assert!(parse_vmstat("pgfault -1").is_err());
        assert!(parse_vmstat("pgfault 1\npgfault 2").is_err());
        assert!(parse_vmstat("pgfault 18446744073709551616").is_err());
    }
    #[test]
    fn partial_and_csv() {
        let s = collect_from(Path::new("/nonexistent-pressureiq-fixture"));
        assert!(s.partial());
        assert_eq!(s.psi.result, Err(Error::Unsupported));
        assert_eq!(
            csv_row(&s).split(',').count(),
            CSV_HEADER.split(',').count()
        );
    }
    #[test]
    fn bounds() {
        assert!(validate_sampling(0, Duration::from_secs(1)).is_err());
        assert!(validate_sampling(61, Duration::from_secs(1)).is_err());
        assert!(validate_sampling(1, Duration::from_millis(99)).is_err());
        assert!(validate_sampling(1, Duration::from_secs(61)).is_err());
        assert!(validate_sampling(60, Duration::from_secs(1)).is_ok());
    }
}
#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
pub struct SnapshotDelta {
    pub interval: Duration,
    pub vm: Option<VmStat>,
    pub psi_some_us: Option<u64>,
    pub psi_full_us: Option<u64>,
}
pub fn snapshot_delta(old: &Snapshot, new: &Snapshot) -> Result<SnapshotDelta, Error> {
    if old.source_root != new.source_root {
        return Err(Error::Malformed("source root mismatch".into()));
    }
    let interval = new
        .monotonic
        .checked_duration_since(old.monotonic)
        .filter(|v| !v.is_zero())
        .ok_or_else(|| Error::Malformed("monotonic interval".into()))?;
    let vm = match (&old.vm.result, &new.vm.result) {
        (Ok(a), Ok(b)) => Some(delta(a, b)?),
        _ => None,
    };
    let (some, full) = match (&old.psi.result, &new.psi.result) {
        (Ok(a), Ok(b)) => {
            let some = b
                .some
                .total_us
                .checked_sub(a.some.total_us)
                .ok_or_else(|| Error::CounterReset("psi some".into()))?;
            let full = match (&a.full, &b.full) {
                (Some(x), Some(y)) => Some(
                    y.total_us
                        .checked_sub(x.total_us)
                        .ok_or_else(|| Error::CounterReset("psi full".into()))?,
                ),
                _ => None,
            };
            (Some(some), full)
        }
        _ => (None, None),
    };
    Ok(SnapshotDelta {
        interval,
        vm,
        psi_some_us: some,
        psi_full_us: full,
    })
}
#[cfg(test)]
mod delta_tests {
    use super::*;
    #[test]
    fn monotonic_and_reset() {
        let a = collect_from(Path::new("/missing-pressureiq"));
        let mut b = a.clone();
        assert!(snapshot_delta(&a, &b).is_err());
        b.monotonic = a.monotonic + Duration::from_millis(250);
        let d = snapshot_delta(&a, &b).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(d.interval, Duration::from_millis(250));
        assert!(d.vm.is_none());
        let mk = |n| Source {
            result: Ok(Psi {
                some: PressureLine {
                    avg10: 0.,
                    avg60: 0.,
                    avg300: 0.,
                    total_us: n,
                },
                full: None,
            }),
            read_ns: 0,
            parse_ns: 0,
        };
        let mut c = a.clone();
        c.psi = mk(20);
        b.psi = mk(10);
        assert!(matches!(
            snapshot_delta(&c, &b),
            Err(Error::CounterReset(_))
        ));
    }
}

FILE crates/xynoos-pressureiq/src/system.rs
//! Pembacaan userspace; angka bukan observasi atomik dan bukan kontrol sistem.
use crate::{collect, parse_psi, Error, Psi, Snapshot};
use std::{
    collections::BTreeMap,
    fs::File,
    io::Read,
    path::{Component, Path, PathBuf},
    time::{Duration, Instant, SystemTime},
};
static MONOTONIC_ORIGIN: std::sync::OnceLock<Instant> = std::sync::OnceLock::new();
#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub struct Cpu {
    pub ticks: [u64; 8],
    pub logical_cpus: usize,
}
#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub struct Disk {
    pub major: u32,
    pub minor: u32,
    pub name: String,
    pub read_sectors: u64,
    pub write_sectors: u64,
}
#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
pub struct Cgroup {
    pub path: PathBuf,
    pub cpu_usage_us: Result<u64, Error>,
    pub memory_current_bytes: Result<u64, Error>,
    pub io_bytes: Result<(u64, u64), Error>,
}
#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
pub struct Capability {
    pub source: String,
    pub value: Result<String, Error>,
    /// Unit nilai numerik bila atribut dapat diukur.
    pub unit: Option<&'static str>,
    pub numeric: Option<Result<i64, Error>>,
}
#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
pub struct SystemSnapshot {
    pub timestamp: SystemTime,
    pub monotonic: Instant,
    /// Durasi pengumpulan berurutan, dalam nanodetik.
    pub elapsed_ns: u128,
    pub memory: Snapshot,
    pub cpu: Result<Cpu, Error>,
    pub cpu_psi: Result<Psi, Error>,
    pub io_psi: Result<Psi, Error>,
    pub disks: Result<Vec<Disk>, Error>,
    pub cgroup: Result<Cgroup, Error>,
    pub cpu_frequency_khz: Result<u64, Error>,
    pub load_one: Result<f64, Error>,
    pub gpu: Vec<Capability>,
    pub thermal: Vec<Capability>,
    pub power: Vec<Capability>,
}
fn u64v(s: &str) -> Result<u64, Error> {
    s.parse::<u64>().map_err(|e| {
        if matches!(e.kind(), std::num::IntErrorKind::PosOverflow) {
            Error::Overflow("counter".into())
        } else {
            Error::Malformed("counter".into())
        }
    })
}
fn file(p: &Path) -> Result<String, Error> {
    let mut f = File::open(p).map_err(|e| match e.kind() {
        std::io::ErrorKind::NotFound => Error::Unsupported,
        std::io::ErrorKind::PermissionDenied => Error::Permission,
        _ => Error::Io(e.kind().to_string()),
    })?;
    let mut s = String::new();
    f.by_ref()
        .take(1_048_577)
        .read_to_string(&mut s)
        .map_err(|e| {
            if e.kind() == std::io::ErrorKind::PermissionDenied {
                Error::Permission
            } else {
                Error::Io(e.kind().to_string())
            }
        })?;
    if s.len() > 1_048_576 {
        return Err(Error::Bounds);
    }
    Ok(s)
}
pub fn parse_cpu(s: &str) -> Result<Cpu, Error> {
    let mut found = None;
    let mut cpus = 0usize;
    for line in s.lines() {
        let mut f = line.split_whitespace();
        let Some(name) = f.next() else { continue };
        if name == "cpu" {
            if found.is_some() {
                return Err(Error::Duplicate("cpu".into()));
            }
            let vals: Vec<_> = f.take(8).map(u64v).collect::<Result<_, _>>()?;
            if vals.len() < 4 {
                return Err(Error::Missing("cpu ticks".into()));
            }
            let mut ticks = [0; 8];
            ticks[..vals.len()].copy_from_slice(&vals);
            found = Some(ticks);
        } else if name
            .strip_prefix("cpu")
            .is_some_and(|x| !x.is_empty() && x.bytes().all(|c| c.is_ascii_digit()))
        {
            cpus = cpus
                .checked_add(1)
                .ok_or_else(|| Error::Overflow("cpu count".into()))?;
        }
    }
    Ok(Cpu {
        ticks: found.ok_or_else(|| Error::Missing("cpu".into()))?,
        logical_cpus: cpus,
    })
}
pub fn cpu_utilization(old: &Cpu, new: &Cpu, interval: Duration) -> Result<f64, Error> {
    if interval.is_zero() {
        return Err(Error::Malformed("interval nol".into()));
    }
    let mut total = 0u64;
    let mut idle = 0u64;
    for i in 0..8 {
        let d = new.ticks[i]
            .checked_sub(old.ticks[i])
            .ok_or_else(|| Error::CounterReset("cpu".into()))?;
        total = total
            .checked_add(d)
            .ok_or_else(|| Error::Overflow("cpu total".into()))?;
        if i == 3 || i == 4 {
            idle = idle
                .checked_add(d)
                .ok_or_else(|| Error::Overflow("cpu idle".into()))?;
        }
    }
    if total == 0 {
        return Err(Error::Malformed("cpu delta nol".into()));
    }
    Ok((total - idle) as f64 * 100.0 / total as f64)
}
pub fn parse_diskstats(s: &str) -> Result<Vec<Disk>, Error> {
    let mut disks = Vec::new();
    for line in s.lines() {
        let f: Vec<_> = line.split_whitespace().collect();
        if f.is_empty() {
            continue;
        }
        if f.len() < 14 {
            return Err(Error::Malformed("diskstats".into()));
        }
        if disks.len() >= 4096 {
            return Err(Error::Bounds);
        }
        let major = f[0].parse().map_err(|_| Error::Malformed("major".into()))?;
        let minor = f[1].parse().map_err(|_| Error::Malformed("minor".into()))?;
        disks.push(Disk {
            major,
            minor,
            name: f[2].into(),
            read_sectors: u64v(f[5])?,
            write_sectors: u64v(f[9])?,
        });
    }
    if disks.is_empty() {
        return Err(Error::Missing("diskstats".into()));
    }
    Ok(disks)
}
pub fn disk_delta(a: &Disk, b: &Disk) -> Result<(u64, u64), Error> {
    if (a.major, a.minor, &a.name) != (b.major, b.minor, &b.name) {
        return Err(Error::Malformed("disk identity".into()));
    }
    let r = b
        .read_sectors
        .checked_sub(a.read_sectors)
        .ok_or_else(|| Error::CounterReset("disk read".into()))?;
    let w = b
        .write_sectors
        .checked_sub(a.write_sectors)
        .ok_or_else(|| Error::CounterReset("disk write".into()))?;
    Ok((
        r.checked_mul(512)
            .ok_or_else(|| Error::Overflow("disk read bytes".into()))?,
        w.checked_mul(512)
            .ok_or_else(|| Error::Overflow("disk write bytes".into()))?,
    ))
}
pub fn parse_key_values(s: &str) -> Result<BTreeMap<String, u64>, Error> {
    let mut map = BTreeMap::new();
    for line in s.lines() {
        let mut f = line.split_whitespace();
        let Some(k) = f.next() else { continue };
        let v = u64v(f.next().ok_or_else(|| Error::Missing(k.into()))?)?;
        if f.next().is_some() {
            return Err(Error::Malformed(k.into()));
        }
        if map.insert(k.into(), v).is_some() {
            return Err(Error::Duplicate(k.into()));
        }
    }
    Ok(map)
}
pub fn parse_io_stat(s: &str) -> Result<(u64, u64), Error> {
    let (mut read, mut write) = (0u64, 0u64);
    let (mut saw_read, mut saw_write) = (false, false);
    for line in s.lines() {
        let mut f = line.split_whitespace();
        if f.next().is_none() {
            continue;
        }
        for token in f {
            let (k, v) = token
                .split_once('=')
                .ok_or_else(|| Error::Malformed("io.stat".into()))?;
            if k == "rbytes" {
                saw_read = true;
                read = read
                    .checked_add(u64v(v)?)
                    .ok_or_else(|| Error::Overflow("io rbytes".into()))?
            }
            if k == "wbytes" {
                saw_write = true;
                write = write
                    .checked_add(u64v(v)?)
                    .ok_or_else(|| Error::Overflow("io wbytes".into()))?
            }
        }
    }
    if !saw_read || !saw_write {
        return Err(Error::Missing("io.stat rbytes/wbytes".into()));
    }
    Ok((read, write))
}
fn cgroup_path() -> Result<PathBuf, Error> {
    let membership = file(Path::new("/proc/self/cgroup"))?;
    let mountinfo = file(Path::new("/proc/self/mountinfo"))?;
    parse_cgroup_path(&membership, &mountinfo)
}
pub fn parse_cgroup_path(membership: &str, mountinfo: &str) -> Result<PathBuf, Error> {
    let m = membership
        .lines()
        .find_map(|l| l.strip_prefix("0::"))
        .ok_or(Error::Unsupported)?;
    for line in mountinfo.lines() {
        let Some((left, right)) = line.split_once(" - ") else {
            continue;
        };
        if !right.starts_with("cgroup2 ") {
            continue;
        }
        let fields: Vec<_> = left.split_whitespace().collect();
        if fields.len() < 5 {
            continue;
        }
        let unescape = |s: &str| {
            s.replace("\\040", " ")
                .replace("\\011", "\t")
                .replace("\\012", "\n")
                .replace("\\134", "\\")
        };
        let mountroot = PathBuf::from(unescape(fields[3]));
        let mount = PathBuf::from(unescape(fields[4]));
        let member = Path::new(m);
        let Ok(relative) = member.strip_prefix(&mountroot) else {
            continue;
        };
        if relative
            .components()
            .any(|c| !matches!(c, Component::Normal(_)))
        {
            return Err(Error::Malformed("cgroup path".into()));
        }
        return Ok(mount.join(relative));
    }
    Err(Error::Unsupported)
}
fn cgroup() -> Result<Cgroup, Error> {
    let p = cgroup_path()?;
    let cpu_usage_us = file(&p.join("cpu.stat"))
        .and_then(|s| parse_key_values(&s))
        .and_then(|m| {
            m.get("usage_usec")
                .copied()
                .ok_or_else(|| Error::Missing("usage_usec".into()))
        });
    let memory_current_bytes = file(&p.join("memory.current")).and_then(|s| u64v(s.trim()));
    let io_bytes = file(&p.join("io.stat")).and_then(|s| parse_io_stat(&s));
    Ok(Cgroup {
        path: p,
        cpu_usage_us,
        memory_current_bytes,
        io_bytes,
    })
}
fn numeric_attribute(
    attribute: &str,
    value: &Result<String, Error>,
) -> (Option<&'static str>, Option<Result<i64, Error>>) {
    let unit = match attribute {
        "temp" => "millicelsius",
        "capacity" => "percent",
        "power_now" => "microwatt",
        "energy_now" => "microwatt_hour",
        _ => return (None, None),
    };
    let number = value.as_ref().map_err(Clone::clone).and_then(|s| {
        let n = s.parse::<i64>().map_err(|e| {
            if matches!(
                e.kind(),
                std::num::IntErrorKind::PosOverflow | std::num::IntErrorKind::NegOverflow
            ) {
                Error::Overflow(attribute.into())
            } else {
                Error::Malformed(attribute.into())
            }
        })?;
        if (attribute != "temp" && n < 0) || (attribute == "capacity" && n > 100) {
            return Err(Error::Malformed(attribute.into()));
        }
        Ok(n)
    });
    (Some(unit), Some(number))
}
fn discover(root: &str, prefix: &str, attribute: &str) -> Vec<Capability> {
    let mut output = Vec::new();
    match std::fs::read_dir(root) {
        Ok(entries) => {
            for (index, e) in entries.take(257).enumerate() {
                if index == 256 {
                    output.push(Capability {
                        source: root.into(),
                        value: Err(Error::Bounds),
                        unit: None,
                        numeric: None,
                    });
                    break;
                }
                match e {
                    Ok(entry) => {
                        let name = entry.file_name().to_string_lossy().into_owned();
                        if name.starts_with(prefix) && (prefix != "card" || !name.contains('-')) {
                            let path = entry.path().join(attribute);
                            let value = file(&path).and_then(|s| {
                                if s.len() > 4096 {
                                    Err(Error::Bounds)
                                } else {
                                    Ok(s.trim().to_owned())
                                }
                            });
                            let (unit, numeric) = numeric_attribute(attribute, &value);
                            output.push(Capability {
                                source: format!("{root}/{name}/{attribute}"),
                                value,
                                unit,
                                numeric,
                            });
                        }
                    }
                    Err(e) => output.push(Capability {
                        source: root.into(),
                        unit: None,
                        numeric: None,
                        value: Err(Error::Io(e.kind().to_string())),
                    }),
                }
            }
        }
        Err(e) => output.push(Capability {
            source: root.into(),
            unit: None,
            numeric: None,
            value: Err(match e.kind() {
                std::io::ErrorKind::PermissionDenied => Error::Permission,
                std::io::ErrorKind::NotFound => Error::Unsupported,
                _ => Error::Io(e.kind().to_string()),
            }),
        }),
    }
    output.sort_by(|a, b| a.source.cmp(&b.source));
    output
}
pub fn collect_system() -> SystemSnapshot {
    MONOTONIC_ORIGIN.get_or_init(Instant::now);
    let timestamp = SystemTime::now();
    let monotonic = Instant::now();
    let memory = collect();
    let cpu = file(Path::new("/proc/stat")).and_then(|s| parse_cpu(&s));
    let cpu_psi = file(Path::new("/proc/pressure/cpu")).and_then(|s| parse_psi(&s));
    let io_psi = file(Path::new("/proc/pressure/io")).and_then(|s| parse_psi(&s));
    let disks = file(Path::new("/proc/diskstats")).and_then(|s| parse_diskstats(&s));
    let cgroup = cgroup();
    let cpu_frequency_khz = file(Path::new(
        "/sys/devices/system/cpu/cpu0/cpufreq/scaling_cur_freq",
    ))
    .and_then(|s| u64v(s.trim()));
    let load_one = file(Path::new("/proc/loadavg")).and_then(|s| {
        let x = s
            .split_whitespace()
            .next()
            .ok_or_else(|| Error::Missing("loadavg".into()))?
            .parse::<f64>()
            .map_err(|_| Error::Malformed("loadavg".into()))?;
        if !x.is_finite() || x < 0.0 {
            return Err(Error::Malformed("loadavg".into()));
        }
        Ok(x)
    });
    let gpu = discover("/sys/class/drm", "card", "device/vendor");
    let mut thermal = discover("/sys/class/thermal", "thermal_zone", "type");
    thermal.extend(discover("/sys/class/thermal", "thermal_zone", "temp"));
    let mut power = Vec::new();
    for attr in ["type", "status", "capacity", "power_now", "energy_now"] {
        power.extend(discover("/sys/class/power_supply", "", attr));
    }
    SystemSnapshot {
        timestamp,
        monotonic,
        elapsed_ns: monotonic.elapsed().as_nanos(),
        memory,
        cpu,
        cpu_psi,
        io_psi,
        disks,
        cgroup,
        cpu_frequency_khz,
        load_one,
        gpu,
        thermal,
        power,
    }
}
#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;
    #[test]
    fn cpu_delta_and_resets() {
        let a = parse_cpu("cpu 100 5 25 200 10 1 2 3 400 0\ncpu0 1 2 3 4\ncpu1 1 2 3 4\n").unwrap();
        let b = parse_cpu("cpu 110 5 30 205 10 1 2 3 500 0\ncpu0 1 2 3 4\ncpu1 1 2 3 4").unwrap();
        assert_eq!(a.logical_cpus, 2);
        assert_eq!(
            cpu_utilization(&a, &b, Duration::from_secs(1)).unwrap(),
            75.0
        );
        assert!(cpu_utilization(&a, &a, Duration::ZERO).is_err());
        assert!(matches!(
            cpu_utilization(&b, &a, Duration::from_secs(1)),
            Err(Error::CounterReset(_))
        ));
        assert!(parse_cpu("cpu 1 2 xyz 4").is_err());
        assert!(parse_cpu("cpu 1 2 3 4\ncpu 1 2 3 4").is_err());
    }
    #[test]
    fn disk_units_identity_and_reset() {
        let a = parse_diskstats("8 0 sda 10 0 100 0 10 0 200 0 0 0 0")
            .unwrap()
            .remove(0);
        let mut b = a.clone();
        b.read_sectors += 5;
        b.write_sectors += 2;
        assert_eq!(disk_delta(&a, &b).unwrap(), (2560, 1024));
        assert!(disk_delta(&b, &a).is_err());
        b.name = "other".into();
        assert!(disk_delta(&a, &b).is_err());
        assert!(parse_diskstats("8 0 sda 1").is_err());
    }
    #[test]
    fn cgroup_values() {
        assert_eq!(
            numeric_attribute("temp", &Ok("-500".into()))
                .1
                .unwrap()
                .unwrap(),
            -500
        );
        assert!(numeric_attribute("capacity", &Ok("101".into()))
            .1
            .unwrap()
            .is_err());
        assert!(numeric_attribute("power_now", &Ok("x".into()))
            .1
            .unwrap()
            .is_err());
        assert_eq!(
            parse_cgroup_path(
                "0::/init\n",
                "1 2 0:1 / /sys/fs/cgroup rw - cgroup2 cgroup rw"
            )
            .unwrap(),
            PathBuf::from("/sys/fs/cgroup/init")
        );
        assert!(parse_cgroup_path(
            "0::/../escape",
            "1 2 0:1 / /sys/fs/cgroup rw - cgroup2 cgroup rw"
        )
        .is_err());
        assert!(parse_cgroup_path("1:cpu:/", "").is_err());
        assert_eq!(
            parse_key_values("usage_usec 123\nuser_usec 22\n").unwrap()["usage_usec"],
            123
        );
        assert!(parse_key_values("x 1\nx 2").is_err());
        assert_eq!(
            parse_io_stat("8:0 rbytes=12 wbytes=9\n8:16 rbytes=1 wbytes=2\n").unwrap(),
            (13, 11)
        );
        assert!(parse_io_stat("").is_err());
        assert!(
            parse_io_stat("8:0 rbytes=18446744073709551615 wbytes=1\n8:1 rbytes=1 wbytes=0")
                .is_err()
        );
    }
}

/// Version 1 is additive: consumers must ignore unknown fields and statuses.
/// All observations are sequential and their age is conservatively measured
/// from the beginning of collection, not from an atomic kernel snapshot.
pub mod contract {
    use super::*;
    use serde::Serialize;
    use serde_json::{json, Value};

    #[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize)]
    #[serde(rename_all = "snake_case")]
    pub enum Status {
        Available,
        Unsupported,
        PermissionDenied,
        ReadError,
        ParseError,
        Stale,
        NotYetSampled,
        Unavailable,
        Truncated,
    }

    #[derive(Debug, Serialize)]
    pub struct Reading {
        pub source: String,
        pub scope: String,
        pub unit: String,
        pub status: Status,
        pub value: Option<Value>,
        pub error: Option<String>,
        pub age_ns: u128,
    }
    #[derive(Debug, Serialize)]
    pub struct Contract {
        pub schema_version: u32,
        pub timestamp_unix_ms: Option<u128>,
        pub collection_duration_ns: u128,
        /// Process-local monotonic offset. It cannot be compared across processes.
        pub monotonic_start_ns: Option<u128>,
        pub sequential: bool,
        pub freshness_limit_ns: u128,
        pub readings: BTreeMap<String, Reading>,
    }
    pub fn status(error: &Error) -> Status {
        match error {
            Error::Unsupported => Status::Unsupported,
            Error::Permission => Status::PermissionDenied,
            Error::Io(_) => Status::ReadError,
            _ => Status::ParseError,
        }
    }
    fn reading<T>(
        source: &str,
        scope: &str,
        unit: &str,
        result: Result<T, Error>,
        age: Duration,
        limit: Duration,
        convert: impl FnOnce(T) -> Value,
    ) -> Reading {
        let (status, value, error) = match result {
            Ok(v) if age > limit => (Status::Stale, Some(convert(v)), None),
            Ok(v) => (Status::Available, Some(convert(v)), None),
            Err(e) => (status(&e), None, Some(e.to_string())),
        };
        Reading {
            source: source.into(),
            scope: scope.into(),
            unit: unit.into(),
            status,
            value,
            error,
            age_ns: age.as_nanos(),
        }
    }
    /// Uses the caller's monotonic clock and threshold; backwards clocks are stale.
    pub fn build(
        s: &SystemSnapshot,
        previous: Option<&SystemSnapshot>,
        disk_name: Option<&str>,
        now: Instant,
        limit: Duration,
    ) -> Contract {
        let age = now
            .checked_duration_since(s.monotonic)
            .unwrap_or(Duration::MAX);
        let mut readings = BTreeMap::new();
        macro_rules! add {
            ($key:expr,$source:expr,$scope:expr,$unit:expr,$result:expr,$convert:expr) => {
                readings.insert(
                    $key.into(),
                    reading($source, $scope, $unit, $result, age, limit, $convert),
                );
            };
        }
        add!(
            "cpu_ticks",
            "/proc/stat",
            "visible_system",
            "USER_HZ ticks",
            s.cpu.clone(),
            |v: Cpu| json!({"ticks":v.ticks,"logical_cpus":v.logical_cpus})
        );
        add!(
            "cpu_psi",
            "/proc/pressure/cpu",
            "visible_system",
            "percent avg10",
            s.cpu_psi.clone(),
            |v: Psi| json!(v.some.avg10)
        );
        add!(
            "io_psi",
            "/proc/pressure/io",
            "visible_system",
            "percent avg10",
            s.io_psi.clone(),
            |v: Psi| json!(v.some.avg10)
        );
        add!(
            "memory_psi",
            "/proc/pressure/memory",
            "visible_system",
            "percent avg10",
            s.memory.psi.result.clone(),
            |v: Psi| json!(v.some.avg10)
        );
        add!(
            "meminfo",
            "/proc/meminfo",
            "visible_system",
            "bytes",
            s.memory.mem.result.clone(),
            |v: crate::MemInfo| json!(v.bytes)
        );
        add!(
            "vmstat",
            "/proc/vmstat",
            "visible_system",
            "kernel counters",
            s.memory.vm.result.clone(),
            |v: crate::VmStat| json!(v.counters)
        );
        let cgpath = s
            .cgroup
            .as_ref()
            .map(|c| c.path.display().to_string())
            .unwrap_or_else(|_| "cgroup2 (unresolved)".into());
        let cg = |f: fn(&Cgroup) -> Result<u64, Error>| {
            s.cgroup.as_ref().map_err(Clone::clone).and_then(f)
        };
        add!(
            "cgroup_cpu",
            &format!("{cgpath}/cpu.stat"),
            "current_cgroup",
            "microseconds",
            cg(|c| c.cpu_usage_us.clone()),
            |v| json!(v)
        );
        add!(
            "cgroup_memory",
            &format!("{cgpath}/memory.current"),
            "current_cgroup",
            "bytes",
            cg(|c| c.memory_current_bytes.clone()),
            |v| json!(v)
        );
        add!(
            "cgroup_io",
            &format!("{cgpath}/io.stat"),
            "current_cgroup",
            "bytes",
            s.cgroup
                .as_ref()
                .map_err(Clone::clone)
                .and_then(|c| c.io_bytes.clone()),
            |v: (u64, u64)| json!({"read":v.0,"write":v.1})
        );
        let selected = || -> Result<Disk, Error> {
            let name = disk_name
                .ok_or_else(|| Error::Missing("explicit disk selection required".into()))?;
            s.disks
                .as_ref()
                .map_err(Clone::clone)?
                .iter()
                .find(|d| d.name == name)
                .cloned()
                .ok_or_else(|| Error::Missing(format!("disk {name}")))
        };
        let disk = selected();
        let kind = disk
            .as_ref()
            .ok()
            .map(|d| {
                let path = PathBuf::from(format!("/sys/dev/block/{}:{}", d.major, d.minor));
                match std::fs::canonicalize(path) {
                    Ok(p) if p.starts_with("/sys/devices/virtual") => "virtual",
                    Ok(p) if p.starts_with("/sys/devices/") => "device_backed",
                    _ => "unknown",
                }
            })
            .unwrap_or("unknown");
        add!(
            "disk",
            "/proc/diskstats",
            "container_visible_devices",
            "512-byte sectors",
            disk.clone(),
            |v: Disk| json!({"name":v.name,"major":v.major,"minor":v.minor,"read_sectors":v.read_sectors,"write_sectors":v.write_sectors,"sysfs_class":kind})
        );
        if disk_name.is_some() && matches!(disk, Err(Error::Missing(_))) {
            readings.get_mut("disk").unwrap().status = Status::Unavailable;
        }
        let mut delta = reading(
            "/proc/diskstats",
            "container_visible_devices",
            "bytes",
            disk.clone().and_then(|d| {
                let p = previous.ok_or_else(|| Error::Missing("previous sample".into()))?;
                let elapsed = s
                    .monotonic
                    .checked_duration_since(p.monotonic)
                    .filter(|d| !d.is_zero())
                    .ok_or_else(|| Error::Malformed("sample interval".into()))?;
                if now
                    .checked_duration_since(p.monotonic)
                    .is_none_or(|a| a > limit)
                    || elapsed > limit
                {
                    return Err(Error::Malformed("previous sample stale".into()));
                }
                let old = p
                    .disks
                    .as_ref()
                    .map_err(Clone::clone)?
                    .iter()
                    .find(|x| x.name == d.name)
                    .ok_or_else(|| Error::Missing("previous disk".into()))?;
                disk_delta(old, &d)
            }),
            age,
            limit,
            |v: (u64, u64)| json!({"read":v.0,"write":v.1}),
        );
        if previous.is_none() && disk.is_ok() {
            delta.status = Status::NotYetSampled;
            delta.error = None;
        }
        if previous.is_some_and(|p| {
            now.checked_duration_since(p.monotonic)
                .is_none_or(|a| a > limit)
        }) && disk.is_ok()
        {
            delta.status = Status::Stale;
            delta.value = None;
            delta.error = Some("previous sample stale".into());
        }
        readings.insert("disk_delta".into(), delta);
        if disk_name.is_some() && matches!(disk, Err(Error::Missing(_))) {
            readings.get_mut("disk_delta").unwrap().status = Status::Unavailable;
        }
        if disk_name.is_none() {
            for name in ["disk", "disk_delta"] {
                let r = readings.get_mut(name).unwrap();
                r.status = Status::Unavailable;
            }
        }
        let cpu_delta = previous.map(|p| {
            let elapsed = s
                .monotonic
                .checked_duration_since(p.monotonic)
                .ok_or_else(|| Error::Malformed("sample interval".into()))?;
            cpu_utilization(
                p.cpu.as_ref().map_err(Clone::clone)?,
                s.cpu.as_ref().map_err(Clone::clone)?,
                elapsed,
            )
        });
        let mut cpu_rate = reading(
            "/proc/stat",
            "visible_system",
            "percent",
            cpu_delta.unwrap_or_else(|| {
                s.cpu
                    .as_ref()
                    .map_err(Clone::clone)
