                    .and_then(|_| Err(Error::Missing("previous sample".into())))
            }),
            age,
            limit,
            |v| json!(v),
        );
        if previous.is_none() && s.cpu.is_ok() {
            cpu_rate.status = Status::NotYetSampled;
            cpu_rate.error = None;
        }
        if s.cpu.is_ok()
            && previous.is_some_and(|p| {
                now.checked_duration_since(p.monotonic)
                    .is_none_or(|a| a > limit)
            })
        {
            cpu_rate.status = Status::Stale;
            cpu_rate.value = None;
            cpu_rate.error = Some("previous sample stale".into());
        }
        readings.insert("cpu_utilization".into(), cpu_rate);
        for (group, caps) in [
            ("gpu", &s.gpu),
            ("thermal", &s.thermal),
            ("power", &s.power),
        ] {
            let root = match group {
                "gpu" => "/sys/class/drm",
                "thermal" => "/sys/class/thermal",
                _ => "/sys/class/power_supply",
            };
            let mut summary = reading(
                root,
                "visible_devices",
                "attributes",
                Ok(caps.len()),
                age,
                limit,
                |v| json!(v),
            );
            if let Some(failure) = caps.iter().find(|c| c.source == root && c.value.is_err()) {
                let error = failure.value.as_ref().unwrap_err();
                summary.status = if matches!(error, Error::Bounds) {
                    Status::Truncated
                } else {
                    status(error)
                };
                summary.error = Some(error.to_string());
            }
            readings.insert(format!("{group}_discovery"), summary);
            for (i, cap) in caps.iter().enumerate() {
                let result = match &cap.numeric {
                    Some(number) => number.clone().map(|n| json!(n)),
                    None => cap.value.clone().map(|s| json!(s)),
                };
                add!(
                    &format!("{group}_{i}"),
                    &cap.source,
                    "visible_devices",
                    cap.unit.unwrap_or("text"),
                    result,
                    |v| v
                );
                if matches!(cap.value, Err(Error::Bounds))
                    && [
                        "/sys/class/drm",
                        "/sys/class/thermal",
                        "/sys/class/power_supply",
                    ]
                    .contains(&cap.source.as_str())
                {
                    readings.get_mut(&format!("{group}_{i}")).unwrap().status = Status::Truncated;
                }
            }
        }
        Contract {
            schema_version: 1,
            timestamp_unix_ms: s
                .timestamp
                .duration_since(SystemTime::UNIX_EPOCH)
                .ok()
                .map(|d| d.as_millis()),
            collection_duration_ns: s.elapsed_ns,
            monotonic_start_ns: MONOTONIC_ORIGIN
                .get()
                .and_then(|origin| s.monotonic.checked_duration_since(*origin))
                .map(|d| d.as_nanos()),
            sequential: true,
            freshness_limit_ns: limit.as_nanos(),
            readings,
        }
    }
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod contract_tests {
    use super::*;
    use contract::{build, Status};
    #[test]
    fn freshness_and_readiness_preserve_errors_and_identity() {
        let mut a = collect_system();
        a.cpu = Ok(parse_cpu("cpu 10 0 0 10\ncpu0 1 1 1 1").unwrap());
        a.disks = Ok(vec![parse_diskstats("8 0 sda 1 0 10 0 1 0 20 0 0 0 0")
            .unwrap()
            .remove(0)]);
        let first = build(&a, None, Some("sda"), a.monotonic, Duration::from_secs(2));
        assert_eq!(first.readings["disk_delta"].status, Status::NotYetSampled);
        assert_eq!(
            first.readings["cpu_utilization"].status,
            Status::NotYetSampled
        );
        assert_eq!(
            build(&a, None, None, a.monotonic, Duration::from_secs(1)).readings["disk"].status,
            Status::Unavailable
        );
        assert_eq!(
            build(
                &a,
                None,
                Some("sda"),
                a.monotonic + Duration::from_secs(3),
                Duration::from_secs(2)
            )
            .readings["disk"]
                .status,
            Status::Stale
        );
        let mut b = a.clone();
        b.monotonic += Duration::from_secs(1);
        b.disks.as_mut().unwrap()[0].minor = 1;
        assert_eq!(
            build(
                &b,
                Some(&a),
                Some("sda"),
                b.monotonic,
                Duration::from_secs(2)
            )
            .readings["disk_delta"]
                .status,
            Status::ParseError
        );
        b.cpu = Err(Error::Permission);
        assert_eq!(
            build(&b, None, None, b.monotonic, Duration::from_secs(2)).readings["cpu_utilization"]
                .status,
            Status::PermissionDenied
        );
        b.cpu = a.cpu.clone();
        b.monotonic = a.monotonic;
        assert_eq!(
            build(
                &b,
                Some(&a),
                Some("sda"),
                b.monotonic,
                Duration::from_secs(2)
            )
            .readings["cpu_utilization"]
                .status,
            Status::ParseError
        );
    }
    #[test]
    fn discovery_preserves_partial_and_marks_truncation() {
        let root = std::env::temp_dir().join(format!("xynoos-discovery-{}", std::process::id()));
        std::fs::create_dir_all(&root).unwrap();
        for i in 0..257 {
            std::fs::create_dir(root.join(format!("item{i}"))).unwrap();
        }
        let found = discover(root.to_str().unwrap(), "item", "type");
        assert_eq!(found.len(), 257);
        assert!(found.iter().any(|c| matches!(c.value, Err(Error::Bounds))));
        std::fs::remove_dir_all(root).unwrap();
        assert!(matches!(
            discover("/definitely/missing/xynoos", "", "type")[0].value,
            Err(Error::Unsupported)
        ));
    }
    #[cfg(unix)]
    #[test]
    fn filesystem_permission_and_missing_are_distinct() {
        use std::os::unix::fs::PermissionsExt;
        let path = std::env::temp_dir().join(format!("xynoos-private-{}", std::process::id()));
        std::fs::write(&path, "private").unwrap();
        std::fs::set_permissions(&path, std::fs::Permissions::from_mode(0o0)).unwrap();
        let result = file(&path);
        std::fs::set_permissions(&path, std::fs::Permissions::from_mode(0o600)).unwrap();
        std::fs::remove_file(&path).unwrap();
        if std::env::var("USER").ok().as_deref() != Some("root") {
            assert!(matches!(result, Err(Error::Permission)));
        }
        assert!(matches!(file(&path), Err(Error::Unsupported)));
    }
}


FILE crates/xynoos-pressureiq/src/workload.rs
//! Read-only Linux process observations. A PID and its kernel start time form the identity.
//! Process counters exclude children; PSI is not exposed per process by Linux.
use crate::{read, Error};
use std::{path::PathBuf, time::Instant};

#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub struct Identity {
    pub pid: u32,
    pub start_ticks: u64,
}
#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
pub struct Reading<T> {
    pub at: Instant,
    pub result: Result<T, Error>,
}
#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
pub struct Cpu {
    pub user_ticks: u64,
    pub system_ticks: u64,
}
#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
pub struct Io {
    pub read_bytes: u64,
    pub write_bytes: u64,
}
#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
pub struct ProcessSnapshot {
    pub identity: Identity,
    pub cpu: Reading<Cpu>,
    pub memory_bytes: Reading<u64>,
    pub io: Reading<Io>,
    /// Linux has no per-process PSI; cgroup PSI must not be attributed to this PID.
    pub pressure: Reading<()>,
}
fn stat(pid: u32) -> Result<(Identity, Cpu), Error> {
    let text = read(&PathBuf::from(format!("/proc/{pid}/stat")))?;
    let (_, tail) = text
        .rsplit_once(") ")
        .ok_or_else(|| Error::Malformed("stat comm".into()))?;
    let f: Vec<_> = tail.split_whitespace().collect();
    let get = |i: usize| -> Result<u64, Error> {
        f.get(i)
            .ok_or_else(|| Error::Missing("stat field".into()))?
            .parse()
            .map_err(|_| Error::Malformed("stat field".into()))
    };
    Ok((
        Identity {
            pid,
            start_ticks: get(19)?,
        },
        Cpu {
            user_ticks: get(11)?,
            system_ticks: get(12)?,
        },
    ))
}
fn keyed(text: &str, key: &str) -> Result<u64, Error> {
    let line = text
        .lines()
        .find_map(|l| l.strip_prefix(key))
        .ok_or_else(|| Error::Missing(key.into()))?;
    line.split_whitespace()
        .next()
        .ok_or_else(|| Error::Missing(key.into()))?
        .parse()
        .map_err(|_| Error::Malformed(key.into()))
}
fn guarded<T>(id: &Identity, f: impl FnOnce() -> Result<T, Error>) -> Reading<T> {
    let at = Instant::now();
    let result = (|| {
        if stat(id.pid)?.0 != *id {
            return Err(Error::Malformed("process identity changed".into()));
        }
        let value = f()?;
        if stat(id.pid)?.0 != *id {
            return Err(Error::Malformed("process identity changed".into()));
        }
        Ok(value)
    })();
    Reading { at, result }
}
pub fn identify(pid: u32) -> Result<Identity, Error> {
    if pid == 0 {
        return Err(Error::Bounds);
    }
    Ok(stat(pid)?.0)
}
pub fn collect(id: &Identity) -> ProcessSnapshot {
    let cpu = guarded(id, || Ok(stat(id.pid)?.1));
    let memory_bytes = guarded(id, || {
        let s = read(&PathBuf::from(format!("/proc/{}/status", id.pid)))?;
        keyed(&s, "VmRSS:")?
            .checked_mul(1024)
            .ok_or_else(|| Error::Overflow("VmRSS".into()))
    });
    let io = guarded(id, || {
        let s = read(&PathBuf::from(format!("/proc/{}/io", id.pid)))?;
        Ok(Io {
            read_bytes: keyed(&s, "read_bytes:")?,
            write_bytes: keyed(&s, "write_bytes:")?,
        })
    });
    ProcessSnapshot {
        identity: id.clone(),
        cpu,
        memory_bytes,
        io,
        pressure: Reading {
            at: Instant::now(),
            result: Err(Error::Unsupported),
        },
    }
}
#[derive(Debug)]
pub struct Delta {
    pub cpu_interval: std::time::Duration,
    pub cpu_ticks: u64,
    pub io_interval: std::time::Duration,
    pub read_bytes: u64,
    pub write_bytes: u64,
}
pub fn delta(a: &ProcessSnapshot, b: &ProcessSnapshot) -> Result<Delta, Error> {
    if a.identity != b.identity {
        return Err(Error::Malformed("process identity mismatch".into()));
    }
    let interval = |x: Instant, y: Instant| {
        y.checked_duration_since(x)
            .filter(|d| !d.is_zero())
            .ok_or_else(|| Error::Malformed("monotonic interval".into()))
    };
    let (ac, bc) = (
        a.cpu.result.as_ref().map_err(Clone::clone)?,
        b.cpu.result.as_ref().map_err(Clone::clone)?,
    );
    let (ai, bi) = (
        a.io.result.as_ref().map_err(Clone::clone)?,
        b.io.result.as_ref().map_err(Clone::clone)?,
    );
    let sub = |x: u64, y: u64| {
        y.checked_sub(x)
            .ok_or_else(|| Error::CounterReset("process counter".into()))
    };
    Ok(Delta {
        cpu_interval: interval(a.cpu.at, b.cpu.at)?,
        cpu_ticks: sub(ac.user_ticks, bc.user_ticks)?
            .checked_add(sub(ac.system_ticks, bc.system_ticks)?)
            .ok_or_else(|| Error::Overflow("cpu ticks".into()))?,
        io_interval: interval(a.io.at, b.io.at)?,
        read_bytes: sub(ai.read_bytes, bi.read_bytes)?,
        write_bytes: sub(ai.write_bytes, bi.write_bytes)?,
    })
}
#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;
    #[test]
    fn identity_and_intervals() {
        let id = identify(std::process::id()).unwrap();
        let a = collect(&id);
        assert!(a.cpu.result.is_ok());
        assert!(a.pressure.result.is_err());
        assert!(delta(&a, &a).is_err());
        let mut wrong = id.clone();
        wrong.start_ticks += 1;
        assert!(collect(&wrong).cpu.result.is_err());
        let mut b = a.clone();
        b.cpu.at += std::time::Duration::from_secs(1);
        b.io.at += std::time::Duration::from_secs(1);
        b.cpu.result.as_mut().unwrap().user_ticks = 0;
        let mut previous = a.clone();
        previous.cpu.result.as_mut().unwrap().user_ticks = 1;
        assert!(matches!(delta(&previous, &b), Err(Error::CounterReset(_))));
        b.io.result = Err(Error::Permission);
        assert!(matches!(delta(&a, &b), Err(Error::Permission)));
    }
}


FILE scripts/benchmark_desktop.py
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Matched source-search/indexing experiment. No cgroup, scheduling or application writes."""
import argparse
import hashlib
import itertools
import json
import os
from pathlib import Path
import selectors
import signal
import stat
import subprocess
import sys
import time

import benchmark_physical as common
import laptop_diagnostic as diag

ARMS = ('baseline', 'observe', 'active')
STOP = None


def stop(sig, _frame):
    global STOP
    STOP = signal.Signals(sig).name


def identity(pid):
    status = common.numbers(common.read(f'/proc/{pid}/status'))
    raw = common.read(f'/proc/{pid}/stat')
    fields = raw.rsplit(')', 1)[-1].split() if raw else []
    exe = os.stat(f'/proc/{pid}/exe')
    return {'pid': pid, 'uid': status.get('Uid'), 'ppid': status.get('PPid'),
            'start_ticks': int(fields[19]), 'exe_device_inode': [exe.st_dev, exe.st_ino],
            'cgroup': common.read(f'/proc/{pid}/cgroup'),
            'children': common.read(f'/proc/{pid}/task/{pid}/children')}


class OwnedChild(common.Child):
    """Single reaper, pinned executable, pidfd signals; never signal a saved PID."""
    def __init__(self, binary, role, corpus_fd, selector):
        self.name = role
        self.usage = None
        self.pidfd = None
        self.buffers = {'stdout': b'', 'stderr': b''}
        self.bytes = 0
        binary_fd = os.open(binary, os.O_RDONLY | os.O_NOFOLLOW | os.O_CLOEXEC)
        try:
            info = os.fstat(binary_fd)
            if not stat.S_ISREG(info.st_mode) or info.st_uid != os.geteuid() or info.st_mode & 0o6022:
                raise RuntimeError('worker must be a caller-owned regular file without group/other write access')
            self.expected_exe = [info.st_dev, info.st_ino]
            command = ([str(binary), 'observe', '--count', '60', '--interval-ms', '1000'] if role == 'observer'
                       else [str(binary), role, '--corpus-fd', str(corpus_fd)])
            self.proc = subprocess.Popen(command, executable=f'/proc/self/fd/{binary_fd}',
                pass_fds=(binary_fd,) if role == 'observer' else (binary_fd, corpus_fd),
                stdin=subprocess.PIPE, stdout=subprocess.PIPE, stderr=subprocess.PIPE, bufsize=0)
        finally:
            os.close(binary_fd)
        try:
            self.pidfd = os.pidfd_open(self.proc.pid)
            self.initial_identity = identity(self.proc.pid)
            self.check_identity()
            for channel in self.buffers:
                stream = getattr(self.proc, channel)
                os.set_blocking(stream.fileno(), False)
                selector.register(stream, selectors.EVENT_READ, (self, channel))
        except BaseException:
            self.stop()
            raise
        self.ready = role == 'observer'
        self.pending = None
        self.count = 0
        self.final = None
        self.finish_sent = False

    def check_identity(self):
        if self.reap() is not None:
            raise RuntimeError(self.name + ' exited unexpectedly')
        actual = identity(self.proc.pid)
        if (actual != self.initial_identity or actual['uid'] != os.geteuid() or
            actual['ppid'] != os.getpid() or actual['exe_device_inode'] != self.expected_exe or
            not actual['cgroup'] or actual['cgroup'] != common.read('/proc/self/cgroup') or
            actual['children'] is None or actual['children'].strip()):
            raise RuntimeError(self.name + ' identity/ownership/cgroup/descendant mismatch')

    def send(self, command):
        self.check_identity()
        if self.finish_sent:
            raise RuntimeError('command after terminal command')
        self.proc.stdin.write(command.encode() + b'\n')
        self.proc.stdin.flush()
        if command in ('finish', 'cancel'):
            self.finish_sent = True

    def stop(self):
        escalation = False
        if self.proc.stdin and not self.proc.stdin.closed:
            self.proc.stdin.close()
        if self.reap() is None:
            # pidfd is authoritative; unreaped direct-child PID is startup fallback only.
            if self.pidfd is not None:
                signal.pidfd_send_signal(self.pidfd, signal.SIGTERM)
            else:
                os.kill(self.proc.pid, signal.SIGTERM)
        deadline = time.monotonic() + 1
        while self.reap() is None and time.monotonic() < deadline:
            time.sleep(.005)
        if self.reap() is None:
            escalation = True
            signal.pidfd_send_signal(self.pidfd, signal.SIGKILL) if self.pidfd is not None else os.kill(self.proc.pid, signal.SIGKILL)
            deadline = time.monotonic() + 1
            while self.reap() is None and time.monotonic() < deadline:
                time.sleep(.005)
        for channel in ('stdout', 'stderr'):
            getattr(self.proc, channel).close()
        if self.pidfd is not None:
            os.close(self.pidfd)
            self.pidfd = None
        return {'name': self.name, 'identity': getattr(self, 'initial_identity', None),
                'reaped': self.proc.returncode is not None, 'exit_code': self.proc.returncode,
                'kill_escalation': escalation, 'usage': self.usage,
                'final': getattr(self, 'final', None),
                'stderr_tail': self.buffers['stderr'][-4096:].decode(errors='replace')}


def make_corpus(root, destination):
    """Curated tracked source, no user projects, symlinks or archives; immutable memfd per run."""
    paths = subprocess.check_output(['git', 'ls-files', '-z', 'crates', 'scripts'], cwd=root).split(b'\0')
    files, chunks = {}, []
    for raw in sorted(p for p in paths if p):
        path = Path(os.fsdecode(raw))
        if path.suffix not in ('.rs', '.py'):
            continue
        source = root / path
        with source.open('rb') as stream:
            if source.is_symlink() or not stat.S_ISREG(os.fstat(stream.fileno()).st_mode):
                raise RuntimeError('source corpus symlink/nonregular file')
            data = stream.read(512 * 1024 + 1)
        if len(data) > 512 * 1024:
            raise RuntimeError('source file too large')
        files[str(path)] = hashlib.sha256(data).hexdigest()
        # Explicit byte-preserving ASCII escape of non-ASCII source bytes.
        chunks.append(b'\nFILE ' + raw + b'\n' + data.decode('utf-8').encode('ascii', 'backslashreplace') + b'\n')
    corpus = b''.join(chunks)
    if not 1 <= len(corpus) <= 4 * 1024 * 1024:
        raise RuntimeError('corpus outside 1 byte..4 MiB')
    destination.write_bytes(corpus)
    destination.with_suffix('.manifest.json').write_text(json.dumps({
        'files_sha256': files, 'corpus_sha256': hashlib.sha256(corpus).hexdigest(),
        'bytes': len(corpus), 'encoding': 'UTF-8 source to ASCII backslashreplace; file markers included'}, indent=2)+'\n')


def sealed_corpus(path):
    import fcntl
    with path.open('rb') as source:
        if not stat.S_ISREG(os.fstat(source.fileno()).st_mode):
            raise RuntimeError('corpus must be regular')
        data = source.read(4 * 1024 * 1024 + 1)
    if not 1 <= len(data) <= 4 * 1024 * 1024 or not data.isascii():
        raise RuntimeError('invalid bounded ASCII corpus')
    fd = os.memfd_create('xynoos-disposable-source', os.MFD_CLOEXEC | os.MFD_ALLOW_SEALING)
    try:
        with os.fdopen(os.dup(fd), 'wb') as stream:
            stream.write(data)
        os.lseek(fd, 0, os.SEEK_SET)
        fcntl.fcntl(fd, fcntl.F_ADD_SEALS, fcntl.F_SEAL_WRITE | fcntl.F_SEAL_GROW | fcntl.F_SEAL_SHRINK | fcntl.F_SEAL_SEAL)
        return fd, data
    except BaseException:
        os.close(fd)
        raise


def run_arm(arm, args, fd, corpus, emit, repeat):
    started, cpu = time.monotonic_ns(), time.process_time_ns()
    children, samples, searches, indexes = [], [], [], []
    error = None
    observations = 0
    foreground_with_index = 0
    background_with_search = 0
    result = {'type': 'run', 'arm': arm, 'repeat': repeat, 'status': 'incomplete'}
    emit({'type': 'run_start', 'arm': arm, 'repeat': repeat})
    previous = current = None
    observed = arm == 'baseline'
    next_request = started
    next_sample = started
    last_decision = None
    foreground_done = background_done = workload_started = None
    finish_deadline = None
    preflight = 'refused_cancelled' if STOP else diag.admission(diag.snapshot())
    if preflight.startswith('refused'):
        result.update(error=preflight, children=[], search_results=[], index_results=[])
        emit(result)
        return result
    import zlib
    expected_crc = zlib.crc32(corpus)
    oracle = [sum(term in line for line in corpus.splitlines()) for term in (b'memory', b'pressure', b'Result', b'cgroup')]
    import collections
    import re
    words = collections.Counter(word.lower() for word in re.findall(rb'[a-zA-Z0-9]+', corpus))
    oracle_index_crc = 0
    for word, count in sorted(words.items()):
        oracle_index_crc = zlib.crc32(word + b'\0' + count.to_bytes(8, 'little'), oracle_index_crc)
    oracle_words = len(words)
    del words
    deadline_started = time.monotonic_ns()
    with selectors.DefaultSelector() as selector:
        try:
            current = diag.snapshot()
            decision = diag.admission(current)
            if decision.startswith('refused'):
                raise RuntimeError(decision)
            for role in ('search', 'index'):
                if STOP or diag.admission(current).startswith('refused'):
                    raise RuntimeError('cancelled or telemetry expired before launch')
                child = OwnedChild(args.worker, role, fd, selector)
                children.append(child)
                emit({'type': 'child_started', 'arm': arm, 'repeat': repeat, 'role': role, 'identity': child.initial_identity})
            search, index = children
            if arm != 'baseline':
                if STOP or diag.admission(current).startswith('refused'):
                    raise RuntimeError('cancelled or telemetry expired before observer launch')
                child = OwnedChild(args.observer, 'observer', fd, selector)
                children.append(child)
                emit({'type': 'child_started', 'arm': arm, 'repeat': repeat, 'role': 'observer', 'identity': child.initial_identity})
            while True:
                now = time.monotonic_ns()
                if STOP:
                    raise RuntimeError('cancelled: ' + STOP)
                if now - deadline_started > args.timeout * 1e9:
                    raise RuntimeError('arm timeout; no further work admitted')
                if now >= next_sample:
                    previous, current = current, diag.snapshot()
                    decision = diag.admission(current, previous)
                    if decision.startswith('refused'):
                        raise RuntimeError(decision)
                    sample = {'host': current, 'processes': {c.name: common.proc_sample(c.proc.pid)
                              for c in children if c.proc.returncode is None}, 'harness': common.proc_sample(os.getpid())}
                    samples.append(sample)
                    emit({'type': 'sample', 'arm': arm, 'repeat': repeat, **sample})
                    for child in children:
                        if not child.finish_sent:
                            child.check_identity()
                    next_sample = now + 100_000_000
                both_ready = all(c.ready for c in children)
                if both_ready and observed:
                    if workload_started is None:
                        workload_started = next_request = now
                    # Foreground arrivals use a fixed absolute schedule, so a stall cannot disappear as think time.
                    if search.pending is None and search.count < args.requests and now >= next_request:
                        due = next_request
                        foreground_with_index += int(index.pending is not None)
                        search.send('work')
                        search.pending = (time.monotonic_ns(), due)
                    decision = diag.admission(current, previous)
                    if decision.startswith('refused'):
                        raise RuntimeError(decision)
                    allow = arm != 'active' or (search.pending is None and search.count < args.requests
                            and next_request - now >= 5_000_000 and decision == 'admitted') or (
                            search.count == args.requests and decision == 'admitted')
                    if index.pending is None and len(indexes) < args.index_passes:
                        policy = diag.Admission.ADMITTED if allow else (diag.Admission.FOREGROUND if search.pending else
                                 (diag.Admission.ARRIVAL if decision == 'admitted' else decision))
                        if policy != last_decision:
                            emit({'type': 'admission', 'arm': arm, 'repeat': repeat, 'outcome': policy,
                                  'telemetry_ns': current['monotonic_ns'], 'index_chunks_completed': index.count})
                            last_decision = policy
                        if allow:
                            background_with_search += int(search.pending is not None)
                            index.send('work')
                            index.pending = (time.monotonic_ns(), now)
                for key, _ in selector.select(.001):
                    child, channel = key.data
                    data = os.read(key.fileobj.fileno(), 65536)
                    if not data:
                        selector.unregister(key.fileobj)
                        continue
                    child.bytes += len(data)
                    if child.bytes > 4 * 1024 * 1024:
                        raise RuntimeError('child output bound')
                    child.buffers[channel] += data
                    if len(child.buffers[channel]) > 65536:
                        raise RuntimeError('child line bound')
                    if channel == 'stderr':
                        continue
                    while b'\n' in child.buffers[channel]:
                        line, child.buffers[channel] = child.buffers[channel].split(b'\n', 1)
                        report = json.loads(line)
                        if child.name == 'observer':
                            if (report.get('evidence') != 'live_read_only' or report.get('kernel_mutations') != 0 or
                                report.get('owned_buffer_mutations') != 0 or report['plan']['kernel_write_available']):
                                raise RuntimeError('observer safety contract')
                            observed = True
                            observations += 1
                            emit({'type': 'observation', 'arm': arm, 'repeat': repeat, 'report': report})
                        elif report.get('type') == 'ready' and not child.ready:
                            if (report.get('pid') != child.proc.pid or report.get('role') != child.name or
                                report.get('corpus_crc32') != expected_crc or report.get('bytes') != len(corpus) or
                                report.get('kernel_mutations') != 0 or report['limits']['address_space_bytes'] > 192 * 1024 * 1024 or
                                report['limits']['cpu_seconds'] > 20 or report['limits']['wall_seconds'] != 60):
                                raise RuntimeError('worker readiness/limits/corpus mismatch')
                            child.ready = True
                        elif report.get('type') == 'result' and child.pending is not None:
                            if report.get('sequence') != child.count or report.get('role') != child.name:
                                raise RuntimeError('worker sequence mismatch')
                            sent, due = child.pending
                            completed = time.monotonic_ns()
                            row = {'type': 'task', 'arm': arm, 'repeat': repeat, 'role': child.name,
                                   'response_ns': completed - due, 'roundtrip_ns': completed - sent, **report}
                            row['type'] = 'task'
                            value = report['result']
                            if child.name == 'search':
                                if value != {'term': ('memory', 'pressure', 'Result', 'cgroup')[child.count % 4],
                                             'matching_lines': oracle[child.count % 4], 'searches': child.count + 1}:
                                    raise RuntimeError('source search oracle mismatch')
                                searches.append(row)
                                next_request += args.interval_ms * 1_000_000
                            elif value['pass_complete']:
                                if (value['passes'] != len(indexes) + 1 or value['offset'] != 0 or
                                    value['index_crc32'] != oracle_index_crc or value['unique_words'] != oracle_words):
                                    raise RuntimeError('index completion mismatch')
                                indexes.append(value)
                                if len(indexes) > 1 and value['index_crc32'] != indexes[0]['index_crc32']:
                                    raise RuntimeError('index mismatch between refreshes')
                            emit(row)
                            child.count += 1
                            child.pending = None
                        elif report.get('type') == 'final' and child.finish_sent and child.final is None:
                            if report.get('released') is not True or report.get('cancelled') is not False:
                                raise RuntimeError('worker final/release mismatch')
                            if report.get('searches') != (args.requests if child.name == 'search' else 0) or report.get('passes') != (args.index_passes if child.name == 'index' else 0) or report.get('partial_index_bytes') != 0:
                                raise RuntimeError('incomplete work at release')
                            child.final = report
                        else:
                            raise RuntimeError('unexpected child report; ambiguous effect')
                now = time.monotonic_ns()
                if len(searches) == args.requests and not search.finish_sent:
                    foreground_done = now - workload_started
                    search.send('finish')
                if len(indexes) == args.index_passes and not index.finish_sent:
                    background_done = now - workload_started
                    index.send('finish')
                if search.finish_sent and index.finish_sent:
                    finish_deadline = finish_deadline or now + 1_000_000_000
                    if now > finish_deadline:
                        raise RuntimeError('release acknowledgement timeout')
                    if search.final and index.final and search.reap() == 0 and index.reap() == 0:
                        result['status'] = 'complete'
                        break
                for child in children:
                    if child.reap() is not None and (not child.finish_sent or child.proc.returncode != 0):
                        raise RuntimeError(child.name + ' premature exit')
        except (Exception, KeyboardInterrupt) as exc:
            error = f'{type(exc).__name__}: {exc}'
        finally:
            cleanup = []
            for child in reversed(children):
                try:
                    if child.name != 'observer' and child.proc.returncode is None and not child.finish_sent:
                        # Cancel has no external rollback: private index is discarded on exit.
                        child.send('cancel')
                        end = time.monotonic() + .2
                        while child.reap() is None and time.monotonic() < end:
                            time.sleep(.005)
                    cleanup.append(child.stop())
                except Exception as exc:
                    try:
                        outcome = child.stop()
                        outcome['cleanup_error'] = str(exc)
                        cleanup.append(outcome)
                    except Exception as final_exc:
                        cleanup.append({'name': child.name, 'reaped': False, 'error': str(final_exc)})
    if not all(c['reaped'] and not c.get('kill_escalation') and not c.get('cleanup_error') for c in cleanup):
        result['status'] = 'incomplete'
        error = (error or '') + '; termination uncertain or escalated'
    result.update(error=error, observations=observations,
                  foreground_requests_with_index_inflight=foreground_with_index,
                  background_chunks_dispatched_during_search=background_with_search, search_latency_ns=common.quantiles([r['response_ns'] for r in searches]),
                  latency_above_1s=sum(r['response_ns'] >= 1_000_000_000 for r in searches),
                  roundtrip_ns=common.quantiles([r['roundtrip_ns'] for r in searches]),
                  search_results=[r['result'] for r in searches], index_results=indexes,
                  foreground_completion_ns=foreground_done, background_completion_ns=background_done,
                  arm_wall_ns=time.monotonic_ns() - started, harness_cpu_ns=time.process_time_ns() - cpu,
                  samples=samples, children=cleanup,
                  host_window=diag.window(samples[0]['host'], samples[-1]['host']) if len(samples) > 1 else None)
    emit(result)
    return result


def summarize(runs):
    pairs = []
    for repeat in sorted({r['repeat'] for r in runs}):
        group = {r['arm']: r for r in runs if r['repeat'] == repeat and r['status'] == 'complete'}
        if {'baseline', 'observe'} <= set(group):
            pairs.append({'repeat': repeat, **{arm: {
                'p95_over_baseline': group[arm]['search_latency_ns']['p95'] / group['baseline']['search_latency_ns']['p95'],
                'foreground_completion_over_baseline': group[arm]['foreground_completion_ns'] / group['baseline']['foreground_completion_ns'],
                'background_completion_over_baseline': group[arm]['background_completion_ns'] / group['baseline']['background_completion_ns']}
                for arm in ('observe', 'active') if arm in group}})
    return {'complete_runs': {arm: sum(r['arm'] == arm and r['status'] == 'complete' for r in runs) for arm in ARMS},
            'paired': pairs, 'failed_runs': sum(r['status'] != 'complete' for r in runs),
            'desktop_improvement_established': False,
            'interpretation': 'source-search task latency only; GUI/browser evidence is separate; '
                              'report background completion cost and session variability; no pooled significance claim'}


def main():
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
    root = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
    parser.add_argument('--worker', type=Path, default=root / 'target/release/xynoos-desktop')
    parser.add_argument('--observer', type=Path, default=root / 'target/release/xynoos-mvp')
    parser.add_argument('--corpus', type=Path, required=True)
    parser.add_argument('--environment', choices=('Codespaces', 'physical'), required=True)
    parser.add_argument('--label', required=True)
    parser.add_argument('--output', type=Path, required=True)
    parser.add_argument('--authorize-disposable-index-admission', action='store_true')
    parser.add_argument('--repeats', type=int, default=6)
    parser.add_argument('--requests', type=int, default=120)
    parser.add_argument('--interval-ms', type=int, default=20)
    parser.add_argument('--index-passes', type=int, default=16)
    parser.add_argument('--timeout', type=int, default=45)
    parser.add_argument('--arms', choices=('baseline-observe', 'all'), default='baseline-observe',
                        help='all adds separately authorized cooperative active arm')
    parser.add_argument('--only-arm', choices=ARMS, help='optional long manual browser-matched sessions; balance order externally')
    parser.add_argument('--prepare-corpus', action='store_true', help='prepare curated repository source and exit')
    args = parser.parse_args()
    if args.prepare_corpus:
        if args.corpus.exists() or args.corpus.with_suffix('.manifest.json').exists():
            parser.error('corpus destination exists')
        make_corpus(root, args.corpus)
        return 0
    if not (args.repeats in (6, 12) and 16 <= args.requests <= 1024 and 1 <= args.index_passes <= 16 and
            5 <= args.interval_ms <= 100 and 5 <= args.timeout <= 45):
        parser.error('repeats 6|12, requests 16..1024, passes 1..16, interval 5..100, timeout 5..45')
    if (args.only_arm == 'active' or (args.only_arm is None and args.arms == 'all')) and not args.authorize_disposable_index_admission:
        parser.error('active requires --authorize-disposable-index-admission; only owned indexing admission is authorized')
    if os.geteuid() == 0:
        parser.error('run as a normal desktop user, without sudo')
    if os.environ.get('CODESPACES') == 'true' and args.environment != 'Codespaces':
        parser.error('Codespaces must be labelled Codespaces')
    args.worker, args.observer = args.worker.resolve(), args.observer.resolve()
    args.output.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=False)
    for sig in (signal.SIGINT, signal.SIGTERM, signal.SIGHUP):
        signal.signal(sig, stop)
    fd, corpus = sealed_corpus(args.corpus)
    runs = []
    warmups = []
    status = 'incomplete'
    try:
        with (args.output / 'events.jsonl').open('x', buffering=1) as log:
            def emit(row):
                log.write(json.dumps(row, separators=(',', ':'), allow_nan=False) + '\n')
                if row['type'] in ('manifest', 'run_start', 'child_started', 'run', 'end'):
                    log.flush()
                    os.fsync(log.fileno())
            emit({'type': 'manifest', 'schema': 'xynoos-desktop-v1', **diag.context(args.label, args.environment),
                  'configuration': {k: str(v) if isinstance(v, Path) else v for k, v in vars(args).items()},
                  'corpus_sha256': hashlib.sha256(corpus).hexdigest(), 'binary_sha256': {
                      str(p): hashlib.sha256(p.read_bytes()).hexdigest() for p in (args.worker, args.observer)},
                  'source_commit': common.read(Path(__file__).parent / 'SOURCE_COMMIT'),
                  'order': 'one selected-arm warmup; measured AB/BA or six ABC permutations balanced; single-arm mode requires external balancing',
                  'authorization_scope': 'only defer new disposable indexing chunks; physical kernel mutation disabled'})
            try:
                selected = ARMS if args.arms == 'all' else ARMS[:2]
                orders = list(itertools.permutations(selected))
                for repeat in range(-1, args.repeats):
                    for arm in ((args.only_arm,) if args.only_arm else (selected if repeat == -1 else orders[repeat % len(orders)])):
                        result = run_arm(arm, args, fd, corpus, emit, repeat)
                        if repeat >= 0:
                            runs.append(result)
                        else:
                            warmups.append({'arm': arm, 'status': result['status'], 'error': result['error']})
                        if result['status'] != 'complete':
                            raise RuntimeError(result['error'])
                        print(f'{repeat} {arm}: complete', flush=True)
                if len({json.dumps((r['search_results'], r['index_results']), sort_keys=True) for r in runs}) != 1:
                    raise RuntimeError('cross-arm output mismatch')
                status = 'complete'
            except (Exception, KeyboardInterrupt) as exc:
                emit({'type': 'failure', 'error': f'{type(exc).__name__}: {exc}'})
            emit({'type': 'end', 'status': status})
            summary = {'status': status, 'environment': args.environment, 'label': args.label,
                       'expected_runs': args.repeats * (1 if args.only_arm else len(selected)), 'warmup': warmups, **summarize(runs)}
            (args.output / 'summary.json').write_text(json.dumps(summary, indent=2) + '\n')
        print(json.dumps(summary, indent=2))
    finally:
        os.close(fd)
    return 0 if status == 'complete' else 1


if __name__ == '__main__':
    raise SystemExit(main())


FILE scripts/benchmark_mvp.py
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Small userspace comparison; never launches services or changes kernel controls."""
import argparse
import hashlib
import itertools
import json
import math
import os
from pathlib import Path
import platform
import subprocess
import tempfile
import time

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
MODES = ("baseline", "observe", "simulate")


def read(path):
    try:
        with open(path, "rb") as file:
            return file.read(1048577).decode()[:1048576]
    except (OSError, UnicodeError):
        return None


def context():
    vm = {}
    for line in (read("/proc/vmstat") or "").splitlines():
        fields = line.split()
        if len(fields) == 2 and fields[0] in ("pswpin", "pswpout"):
            vm[fields[0]] = int(fields[1])
    psi = {}
    for line in (read("/proc/pressure/memory") or "").splitlines():
        name, *fields = line.split()
        psi[name] = {key: float(value) if key != "total" else int(value)
                     for key, value in (field.split("=", 1) for field in fields)}
    return {"memory_psi": psi or None, "swap_page_counters": vm or None,
            "meminfo": read("/proc/meminfo")}


def run(command):
    before = context()
    with tempfile.TemporaryFile() as stdout, tempfile.TemporaryFile() as stderr:
        started = time.perf_counter_ns()
        proc = subprocess.Popen(command, stdout=stdout, stderr=stderr, cwd=ROOT)
        deadline = time.monotonic() + 15
        while True:
            pid, status, usage = os.wait4(proc.pid, os.WNOHANG)
            if pid:
                break
            if time.monotonic() > deadline:
                proc.kill()
                _, status, usage = os.wait4(proc.pid, 0)
                proc.returncode = os.waitstatus_to_exitcode(status)
                raise RuntimeError("owned benchmark process timed out and was reaped")
            time.sleep(0.001)
        process_wall_ns = time.perf_counter_ns() - started
        proc.returncode = os.waitstatus_to_exitcode(status)
        stdout.seek(0)
        stderr.seek(0)
        if proc.returncode:
            raise RuntimeError(stderr.read(65536).decode(errors="replace"))
        output = stdout.read(1048577)
        if len(output) > 1048576:
            raise RuntimeError("child report exceeds 1 MiB")
    return {"command": list(map(str, command)), "process_wall_ns": process_wall_ns,
            "process_user_cpu_ns": round(usage.ru_utime * 1e9),
            "process_system_cpu_ns": round(usage.ru_stime * 1e9),
            "spawn_inclusive_peak_rss_kib": usage.ru_maxrss,
            "host_before": before, "host_after": context(),
            "report": json.loads(output)}


def quantiles(values):
    values = sorted(values)
    if not values:
        return None
    return {"count": len(values), **{
        f"p{percent}": values[max(0, math.ceil(len(values) * percent / 100) - 1)]
        for percent in (50, 95, 99)}}


def summarize(runs):
    result = {}
    for mode in MODES:
        group = [run for run in runs if run["mode"] == mode]
        rows = [row for run in group for row in run["report"]["rows"]]
        result[mode] = {
            "process_wall_ns": quantiles([run["process_wall_ns"] for run in group]),
            "engine_workload_total_ns": quantiles([run["report"]["total_ns"] for run in group]),
            "process_cpu_ns": quantiles([run["process_user_cpu_ns"] + run["process_system_cpu_ns"] for run in group]),
            "process_high_water_rss_kib": quantiles([run["report"]["process_high_water_rss_kib"] for run in group if run["report"]["process_high_water_rss_kib"] is not None]),
            "sampled_loaded_rss_kib": quantiles([row["workload"]["sampled_process_rss_kib"] for row in rows if row["workload"]["sampled_process_rss_kib"] is not None]),
            "decision_ns": quantiles([row["decision_ns"] for row in rows]),
            "response_ns": quantiles([row["response_ns"] for row in rows]),
            "workload_ns": quantiles([row["workload"]["workload_ns"] for row in rows]),
            "stored_payload_bytes": quantiles([row["workload"]["stored_payload_bytes"] for row in rows]),
            "safety_refusals": sum(run["report"]["safety_refusals"] for run in group),
            "private_buffer_verifications": sum(run["report"]["owned_buffer_verifications"] for run in group),
            "compression_steps": sum(row["workload"]["compressed_pages"] > 0 for row in rows),
        }
    return result


def main():
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
    parser.add_argument("--repeats", type=int, default=12)
    parser.add_argument("--output", type=Path, required=True)
    args = parser.parse_args()
    if not 6 <= args.repeats <= 60 or args.repeats % 6:
        parser.error("repeats must be a multiple of 6 within 6..60")
    output = args.output.resolve()
    summary_path = output.with_suffix(".summary.json")
    if output.exists() or summary_path.exists():
        parser.error("refusing to replace existing evidence")
    binary = ROOT / "target/release/xynoos-bench"
    cli = ROOT / "target/release/xynoos-cli"
    # Warm each arm once, discard these measurements, then balance all permutations.
    for mode in MODES:
        run([binary, "--mvp", mode])
    run([cli, "mvp", "observe"])
    runs = []
    orders = list(itertools.permutations(MODES))
    for repeat in range(args.repeats):
        for mode in orders[repeat % len(orders)]:
            measured = run([binary, "--mvp", mode])
            measured.update(mode=mode, repeat=repeat)
            report = measured["report"]
            if report["kernel_mutations"] or not all(row["workload"]["byte_verification_passed"] for row in report["rows"]):
                raise RuntimeError("unexpected mutation or failed workload integrity")
            runs.append(measured)
    if len({(r["report"]["trace_crc32"], r["report"]["workload_crc32"]) for r in runs}) != 1:
        raise RuntimeError("comparison trace/workload mismatch")
    live = [run([cli, "mvp", "observe"]) for _ in range(args.repeats)]
    summary = {"schema_version": 1,
               "evidence": "Codespaces userspace measurements; pressure replay is simulation; no physical A/B",
               "repeats_per_arm": args.repeats, "arms": summarize(runs),
               "live_observe_separate": {
                   "collection_ns": quantiles([r["report"]["telemetry"]["collection_duration_ns"] for r in live]),
                   "decision_ns": quantiles([r["report"]["decision_ns"] for r in live]),
                   "process_cpu_ns": quantiles([r["process_user_cpu_ns"] + r["process_system_cpu_ns"] for r in live]),
               }}
    evidence = {"schema_version": 1, "summary": summary,
                "environment": {"platform": platform.platform(), "machine": platform.machine(),
                    "cpu_model": next((line.split(":",1)[1].strip() for line in (read("/proc/cpuinfo") or "").splitlines() if line.startswith("model name")), None),
                    "source_base_commit": subprocess.check_output(["git", "rev-parse", "HEAD"], cwd=ROOT, text=True).strip(),
                    "binary_sha256": {str(p.relative_to(ROOT)): hashlib.sha256(p.read_bytes()).hexdigest() for p in (binary,cli)},
                    "trace_sha256": hashlib.sha256((ROOT / "fixtures/mvp-trace.json").read_bytes()).hexdigest()},
                "runs": runs, "live_observe": live}
    source_paths = sorted([p for p in (ROOT / "crates").rglob("*") if p.suffix in (".rs", ".toml")] + [ROOT / "Cargo.toml", ROOT / "Cargo.lock"])
    evidence["environment"]["source_code_sha256"] = {str(p.relative_to(ROOT)): hashlib.sha256(p.read_bytes()).hexdigest() for p in source_paths}
    with output.open("x") as file:
        json.dump(evidence, file, separators=(",", ":")); file.write("\n")
    with summary_path.open("x") as file:
        json.dump(summary, file, indent=2); file.write("\n")
    print(json.dumps(summary, indent=2))


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()


FILE scripts/benchmark_owned.py
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Three-arm owned lossless corpus experiment. No kernel control; stdlib only."""
import argparse
import datetime
import hashlib
import itertools
import json
import math
import os
from pathlib import Path
import platform
import selectors
import signal
import subprocess
import sys
import time
import benchmark_physical as base

ARMS = ('baseline', 'observe', 'active')
ORDERS = tuple(itertools.permutations(ARMS))
STOP = None


def provenance():
    directory = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
    packaged = base.read(directory/'SOURCE_MANIFEST.json')
    if packaged:
        return json.loads(packaged)
    root = directory.parent
    try:
        commit = subprocess.check_output(['git', 'rev-parse', 'HEAD'], cwd=root, text=True, timeout=2).strip()
    except (OSError, subprocess.SubprocessError):
        commit = None
    paths = [root/'Cargo.toml', root/'Cargo.lock', *sorted((root/'crates').rglob('*.rs')),
             *sorted((root/'crates').glob('*/Cargo.toml')),
             directory/'benchmark_owned.py', directory/'benchmark_physical.py', directory/'inspect_owned.py']
    return {'base_commit': commit, 'source_sha256': {str(p.relative_to(root)): hashlib.sha256(p.read_bytes()).hexdigest()
                                                  for p in paths if p.is_file()}}


def interrupt(signum, _frame):
    global STOP
    STOP = signal.Signals(signum).name


def run_arm(arm, args, emit, repeat):
    started = time.monotonic_ns()
    cpu = time.process_time_ns()
    deadline = time.monotonic() + args.timeout
    responses, samples, children = [], [], []
    result = {'type': 'run', 'arm': arm, 'repeat': repeat, 'status': 'incomplete'}
    ready = final = None
    pending = None
    next_request = 0
    next_sample = 0
    finishing = False
    finish_deadline = None
    work_start = work_end = None
    error = None
    emit({'type': 'run_start', 'arm': arm, 'repeat': repeat})
    with selectors.DefaultSelector() as selector:
        try:
            base.safe(base.host())
            command = [str(args.worker), arm, args.corpus]
            if arm == 'active':
                if not args.authorize_owned:
                    raise RuntimeError('active requires --authorize-owned')
                command.append('--authorize-owned')
            child = base.Child(command, selector, 'workload')
            children.append(child)
            while True:
                now = time.monotonic()
                if now >= deadline:
                    raise RuntimeError('arm timeout')
                if STOP and not finishing:
                    child.proc.stdin.write(b'cancel\n')
                    child.proc.stdin.flush()
                    finishing = True
                    finish_deadline = now + 1
                if finish_deadline and now >= finish_deadline:
                    raise RuntimeError('owned final/restore timeout')
                if now >= next_sample:
                    h = base.host()
                    samples.append({'host': h, 'processes': {'workload': base.proc_sample(child.proc.pid),
                                    'harness': base.proc_sample(os.getpid())}})
                    emit({'type': 'sample', 'arm': arm, 'repeat': repeat, **samples[-1]})
                    base.safe(h)
                    next_sample = now + .1
                if ready and not finishing and pending is None and now >= next_request:
                    pending = time.monotonic_ns()
                    if work_start is None:
                        work_start = pending
                    child.proc.stdin.write(b'work\n')
                    child.proc.stdin.flush()
                for key, _ in selector.select(.005):
                    _, channel = key.data
                    data = os.read(key.fileobj.fileno(), 65536)
                    if not data:
                        selector.unregister(key.fileobj)
                        continue
                    child.bytes += len(data)
                    if child.bytes > 2 * 1024 * 1024:
                        raise RuntimeError('child output exceeds 2 MiB')
                    child.buffers[channel] += data
                    if len(child.buffers[channel]) > 65536:
                        raise RuntimeError('child line exceeds 64 KiB')
                    if channel == 'stderr':
                        continue
                    while b'\n' in child.buffers[channel]:
                        line, child.buffers[channel] = child.buffers[channel].split(b'\n', 1)
                        row = json.loads(line)
                        if row['type'] == 'ready' and ready is None:
                            if (row.get('arm') != arm or row.get('corpus') != args.corpus
                                or row.get('workload') != 'owned_lossless_16mib_v1'
                                or row.get('retained_bytes') != 17 * 1024 * 1024
                                or not 0 < row['limits']['address_space_bytes'] <= 128 * 1024 * 1024
                                or not 0 < row['limits']['cpu_seconds'] <= 8
                                or row['limits']['wall_seconds'] != 25
                                or row.get('kernel_mutations') != 0):
                                raise RuntimeError('worker readiness/limits mismatch')
                            ready = row
                            emit({'type': 'ready', 'arm': arm, 'repeat': repeat, 'report': row})
                        elif row['type'] == 'response' and pending is not None:
                            if row.get('verified') is not True or row.get('kernel_mutations') != 0:
                                raise RuntimeError('worker integrity/safety failure')
                            row.update(response_ns=time.monotonic_ns()-pending,
                                       process=base.proc_sample(child.proc.pid))
                            responses.append(row)
                            emit({'type': 'response', 'arm': arm, 'repeat': repeat,
                                  'request': len(responses), **row})
                            pending = None
                            next_request = time.monotonic() + args.interval_ms / 1000
                            if len(responses) == args.requests and not finishing:
                                work_end = time.monotonic_ns()
                                child.proc.stdin.write(b'finish\n')
                                child.proc.stdin.flush()
                                finishing = True
                                finish_deadline = time.monotonic() + 1
                        elif row['type'] == 'final' and finishing and final is None:
                            if row.get('restored') is not True or row.get('verified') is not True:
                                raise RuntimeError('restoration failure')
                            if row['retained_after_restore'] != 17 * 1024 * 1024:
                                raise RuntimeError('restored allocation mismatch')
                            final = row
                            # Capture restoration HWM before the worker may disappear.
                            final['process'] = base.proc_sample(child.proc.pid)
                            emit({'type': 'restoration', 'arm': arm, 'repeat': repeat, 'report': row})
                        else:
                            raise RuntimeError('unexpected protocol row')
                code = child.reap()
                if code is not None:
                    # Drain pipe EOF before deciding that a final report is missing.
                    if selector.get_map():
                        continue
                    if code != 0 or final is None or STOP or len(responses) != args.requests or final['cancelled']:
                        raise RuntimeError('cancelled: '+STOP if STOP else 'worker exit/protocol failure')
                    result['status'] = 'complete'
                    break
        except (Exception, KeyboardInterrupt) as exc:
            error = f'{type(exc).__name__}: {exc}'
        finally:
            cleanup = []
            for child in reversed(children):
                try:
                    cleanup.append(child.stop())
                except Exception as exc:
                    cleanup.append({'reaped': False, 'error': str(exc)})
    if not all(c['reaped'] and not c.get('kill_escalation') and c.get('exit_code') == 0 for c in cleanup):
        result['status'] = 'incomplete'
    result.update(error=error, responses=responses, samples=samples, children=cleanup, ready=ready, final=final,
                  completion_ns=work_end-work_start if work_end else None,
                  arm_wall_ns=time.monotonic_ns()-started, harness_cpu_ns=time.process_time_ns()-cpu)
    emit(result)
    return result


def peak_hwm(run):
    values = ([s['processes']['workload']['hwm_kib'] for s in run['samples']]
              + [row['process']['hwm_kib'] for row in run['responses']]
              + [run['final']['self_memory_after_restore']['hwm_kib']])
    return max((v for v in values if v is not None), default=None)


def summary(runs):
    result = {}
    for arm in ARMS:
        group = [r for r in runs if r['arm'] == arm and r['status'] == 'complete']
        rows = [row for r in group for row in r['responses']]
        steady = [row for r in group for row in r['responses'][8:]]
        effects = {}
        for row in rows:
            if row['policy']:
                name = row['policy']['effect']
                effects[name] = effects.get(name, 0) + 1
        cpu = [c['usage']['user_cpu_ns']+c['usage']['system_cpu_ns'] for r in group for c in r['children'] if c.get('usage')]
        peaks = [peak_hwm(r) for r in group]
        result[arm] = {'complete_runs': len(group),
            **{key: base.quantiles([row[key] for row in rows]) for key in ('response_ns', 'service_ns', 'elapsed_ns')},
            'completion_ns': base.quantiles([r['completion_ns'] for r in group]),
            'arm_wall_ns': base.quantiles([r['arm_wall_ns'] for r in group]),
            'child_cpu_ns': base.quantiles(cpu),
            'harness_cpu_ns': base.quantiles([r['harness_cpu_ns'] for r in group]),
            'steady_payload_bytes': base.quantiles([row['retained_bytes'] for row in steady]),
            'steady_rss_kib': base.quantiles([row['process']['rss_kib'] for row in steady if row['process']['rss_kib'] is not None]),
            'sampled_peak_hwm_kib': base.quantiles([v for v in peaks if v is not None]),
            'harness_rss_kib': base.quantiles([s['processes']['harness']['rss_kib'] for r in group for s in r['samples']
                                            if s['processes']['harness']['rss_kib'] is not None]),
            'policy_effects': effects,
            'actions': sum(row['policy']['changed_blocks'] > 0 for row in rows if row['policy']),
            'blocks_replaced': sum(row['policy']['changed_blocks'] for row in rows if row['policy']),
            'action_ns': base.quantiles([row['policy']['action_ns'] for row in rows if row['policy'] and row['policy']['action_ns']]),
            'restoration_ns': base.quantiles([r['final']['restore_ns'] for r in group]),
            'context_per_run': [base.context_delta(r['samples']) for r in group]}
    paired = []
    for i in sorted({r['repeat'] for r in runs}):
        triple = {r['arm']: r for r in runs if r['repeat'] == i and r['status'] == 'complete'}
        if len(triple) == 3:
            reference = triple['baseline']
            equivalent = all(r['ready']['identity'] == reference['ready']['identity'] and
                [x['checksum'] for x in r['responses']] == [x['checksum'] for x in reference['responses']] for r in triple.values())
            paired.append({'repeat': i, 'correctness_equivalent': equivalent,
                **{arm+'_completion_ratio': triple[arm]['completion_ns']/reference['completion_ns'] for arm in ('observe', 'active')},
                **{arm+'_wall_ratio': triple[arm]['arm_wall_ns']/reference['arm_wall_ns'] for arm in ('observe', 'active')}})
    return {'arms': result, 'paired': paired, 'failed_runs': sum(r['status'] != 'complete' for r in runs),
            'refused_policy_samples': sum(row['policy']['effect'].startswith('refused') for r in runs for row in r['responses'] if row['policy']),
            'interpretation': 'Private cooperative corpus only. Steady memory excludes first 8 requests; CPU/wall include generation, policy, verification, restoration. Host counters are shared context. No desktop benefit claim.'}


def main():
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
    parser.add_argument('--worker', type=Path, default=Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]/'target/release/xynoos-owned')
    parser.add_argument('--environment', required=True, choices=('Codespaces', 'physical'))
    parser.add_argument('--label', required=True)
    parser.add_argument('--output', type=Path, required=True)
    parser.add_argument('--authorize-owned', action='store_true')
    parser.add_argument('--corpus', choices=('mixed', 'entropy'), default='mixed')
    parser.add_argument('--repeats', type=int, default=6)
    parser.add_argument('--requests', type=int, default=128)
    parser.add_argument('--interval-ms', type=int, default=10)
    parser.add_argument('--timeout', type=float, default=24)
    args = parser.parse_args()
    if not args.authorize_owned:
        parser.error('explicit --authorize-owned required for the active arm')
    if not (args.repeats in (6, 12, 18, 24) and 16 <= args.requests <= 256 and 0 <= args.interval_ms <= 20
            and math.isfinite(args.timeout) and 1 <= args.timeout <= 24):
        parser.error('repeats 6/12/18/24, requests 16..256, interval 0..20, timeout 1..24')
    if os.environ.get('CODESPACES') == 'true' and args.environment != 'Codespaces':
        parser.error('Codespaces must be labelled Codespaces')
    args.worker = args.worker.resolve()
    if not args.worker.is_file() or not os.access(args.worker, os.X_OK):
        parser.error('missing worker executable')
    args.output.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=False)
    for sig in (signal.SIGINT, signal.SIGTERM, signal.SIGHUP):
        signal.signal(sig, interrupt)
    runs = []
    status = 'incomplete'
    with (args.output/'events.jsonl').open('x', buffering=1) as log:
        def emit(row):
            log.write(json.dumps(row, separators=(',', ':'), allow_nan=False)+'\n')
            if row['type'] in ('manifest', 'run_start', 'run', 'end', 'failure'):
                log.flush()
                os.fsync(log.fileno())
        emit({'type': 'manifest', 'schema_version': 1, 'experiment': 'owned_lossless_16mib_v1',
            'environment': args.environment, 'label': args.label,
            'utc': datetime.datetime.now(datetime.timezone.utc).isoformat(), 'platform': platform.platform(),
            'cpuinfo': base.read('/proc/cpuinfo', 1048576), 'os_release': base.read('/etc/os-release'),
            'source_commit': base.read(Path(__file__).parent/'SOURCE_COMMIT'),
            'source_manifest': provenance(),
            'sha256': {str(p): hashlib.sha256(p.read_bytes()).hexdigest() for p in
                       (args.worker, Path(__file__), Path(base.__file__))},
            'configuration': {k: str(v) if isinstance(v, Path) else v for k, v in vars(args).items()},
            'order': 'one excluded ABC warmup; all six permutations in each measured block of six',
            'initial_host': base.host(), 'physical_mutation_available': False})
        try:
            for repeat in range(-1, args.repeats):
                order = ARMS if repeat == -1 else ORDERS[repeat % 6]
                for arm in order:
                    run = run_arm(arm, args, emit, repeat)
                    if repeat >= 0:
                        runs.append(run)
                    if run['status'] != 'complete':
                        raise RuntimeError(run['error'] or 'cleanup incomplete')
                print(f'triple {repeat} complete', flush=True)
            if not all(p['correctness_equivalent'] for p in summary(runs)['paired']):
                raise RuntimeError('cross-arm correctness mismatch')
            status = 'complete'
        except (Exception, KeyboardInterrupt) as exc:
            emit({'type': 'failure', 'error': str(exc)})
        report = {'status': status, 'environment': args.environment, 'expected_runs': args.repeats*3,
                  'recorded_runs': len(runs), **summary(runs)}
        emit({'type': 'end', 'status': status})
        with (args.output/'summary.json').open('x') as f:
            json.dump(report, f, indent=2, allow_nan=False)
            f.write('\n')
            f.flush()
            os.fsync(f.fileno())
    print(json.dumps(report, indent=2))
    return 0 if status == 'complete' else 1


if __name__ == '__main__':
    sys.exit(main())


FILE scripts/benchmark_physical.py
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Bounded Linux workload A/B. Stdlib only. No root, control writes or simulation arms."""
import argparse
import datetime
import hashlib
import json
import math
import os
from pathlib import Path
import platform
import selectors
import signal
import statistics
import subprocess
import sys
import time

ARMS = ('baseline', 'observe')
STOP = None


def interrupt(signum, _frame):
    global STOP
    STOP = signal.Signals(signum).name


def read(path, limit=65536):
    try:
        with open(path, 'rb') as f:
            data = f.read(limit + 1)
        return data.decode() if len(data) <= limit else None
    except (OSError, UnicodeError):
        return None


def numbers(text):
    result = {}
    for line in (text or '').splitlines():
        words = line.replace(':', '').split()
        if len(words) >= 2 and words[1].isdigit():
            result[words[0]] = int(words[1])
    return result


def psi(text):
    try:
        return {line.split()[0]: {k: float(v) if k != 'total' else int(v)
                for k, v in (field.split('=') for field in line.split()[1:])}
                for line in text.splitlines()} or None
    except (AttributeError, ValueError, IndexError):
        return None


def host():
    mem = numbers(read('/proc/meminfo'))
    vm = numbers(read('/proc/vmstat'))
    cpu = (read('/proc/stat') or '').splitlines()
    # Device discovery and every read are bounded; absence stays null.
    devices = []
    try:
        for p in sorted(Path('/sys/block').glob('zram*'))[:16]:
            devices.append({'device': p.name, 'mm_stat': read(p / 'mm_stat'),
                            'io_stat': read(p / 'io_stat'), 'stat': read(p / 'stat')})
    except OSError:
        devices = None
    return {'monotonic_ns': time.monotonic_ns(), 'mem_available_kib': mem.get('MemAvailable'),
            'swap_free_kib': mem.get('SwapFree'), 'swap_total_kib': mem.get('SwapTotal'),
            'psi': {k: psi(read('/proc/pressure/' + k)) for k in ('cpu', 'memory', 'io')},
            'swap_pages': {k: vm.get(k) for k in ('pswpin', 'pswpout')},
            'cpu_ticks': [int(x) for x in cpu[0].split()[1:9]] if cpu and cpu[0].startswith('cpu ') else None,
            'loadavg': read('/proc/loadavg'), 'zram': devices,
            'mounted_cgroup_root_context': {k: read('/sys/fs/cgroup/' + k) for k in
                ('memory.current', 'memory.max', 'memory.events', 'memory.pressure')},
            'scope': 'visible_namespace; cgroup fields describe mounted root, not assumed workload scope'}


def safe(snapshot):
    available = snapshot.get('mem_available_kib')
    if available is None:
        raise RuntimeError('MemAvailable unavailable; workload admission refused')
    if available < 256 * 1024:
        raise RuntimeError('MemAvailable below 256 MiB; stop')
    cg = snapshot.get('mounted_cgroup_root_context', {})
    current, maximum = cg.get('memory.current'), cg.get('memory.max')
    if current and maximum and current.strip().isdigit() and maximum.strip().isdigit():
        if int(maximum) - int(current) < 32 * 1024 * 1024:
            raise RuntimeError('mounted cgroup root headroom below 32 MiB; stop')


def proc_sample(pid):
    status = numbers(read(f'/proc/{pid}/status'))
    stat = read(f'/proc/{pid}/stat')
    fields = stat.rsplit(')', 1)[-1].split() if stat and ')' in stat else []
    return {'rss_kib': status.get('VmRSS'), 'hwm_kib': status.get('VmHWM'),
            'user_ticks': int(fields[11]) if len(fields) > 12 else None,
            'system_ticks': int(fields[12]) if len(fields) > 12 else None}


class Child:
    def __init__(self, command, selector, name):
        self.name = name
        self.proc = subprocess.Popen(command, stdin=subprocess.PIPE, stdout=subprocess.PIPE,
                                     stderr=subprocess.PIPE, bufsize=0)
        self.buffers = {'stdout': b'', 'stderr': b''}
        self.bytes = 0
        self.usage = None
        try:
            for channel in self.buffers:
                stream = getattr(self.proc, channel)
                os.set_blocking(stream.fileno(), False)
                selector.register(stream, selectors.EVENT_READ, (self, channel))
        except BaseException:
            self.stop()
            raise

    def reap(self):
        if self.proc.returncode is None:
            pid, status, usage = os.wait4(self.proc.pid, os.WNOHANG)
            if pid:
                self.proc.returncode = os.waitstatus_to_exitcode(status)
                self.usage = {'user_cpu_ns': round(usage.ru_utime * 1e9),
                              'system_cpu_ns': round(usage.ru_stime * 1e9),
                              'wait4_peak_rss_kib': usage.ru_maxrss,
                              'minor_faults': usage.ru_minflt, 'major_faults': usage.ru_majflt,
                              'voluntary_switches': usage.ru_nvcsw, 'involuntary_switches': usage.ru_nivcsw}
        return self.proc.returncode

    def stop(self):
        escalation = False
        if self.proc.stdin and not self.proc.stdin.closed:
            self.proc.stdin.close()
        if self.reap() is None:
            os.kill(self.proc.pid, signal.SIGTERM)
        deadline = time.monotonic() + 1
        while self.reap() is None and time.monotonic() < deadline:
            time.sleep(.005)
        if self.reap() is None:
            escalation = True
            os.kill(self.proc.pid, signal.SIGKILL)
            deadline = time.monotonic() + 1
            while self.reap() is None and time.monotonic() < deadline:
                time.sleep(.005)
        reaped = self.proc.returncode is not None
        for channel in ('stdout', 'stderr'):
            getattr(self.proc, channel).close()
        return {'name': self.name, 'pid': self.proc.pid, 'reaped': reaped, 'exit_code': self.proc.returncode,
                'kill_escalation': escalation, 'usage': self.usage,
                'stderr_tail': self.buffers['stderr'][-4096:].decode(errors='replace')}


def quantiles(values):
    values = sorted(values)
    if not values:
        return None
    return {'count': len(values), **{f'p{p}': values[max(0, math.ceil(len(values)*p/100)-1)] for p in (50, 95, 99)}}


def run_arm(arm, args, emit, repeat):
    start_cpu = time.process_time_ns()
    started = time.monotonic_ns()
    deadline = started + int(args.timeout * 1e9)
    children = []
    response = []
    service = []
    observations = []
    samples = []
    lateness = []
    requests = 0
    pending = None
    ready = False
    error = None
    expected_crc = None
    workload_started = None
    workload_finished = None
    telemetry_ready = arm == 'baseline'
    next_request = 0
    next_sample = started
    next_tick = started + 10_000_000
    result = {'type': 'run', 'arm': arm, 'repeat': repeat, 'status': 'incomplete'}
    emit({'type': 'run_start', 'arm': arm, 'repeat': repeat, 'monotonic_ns': started})
    with selectors.DefaultSelector() as selector:
        try:
            initial = host()
            safe(initial)
            # Start the worker only after safety checks. Observation must be ready before measured work.
            worker = Child([str(args.worker), 'workload'], selector, 'workload')
            children.append(worker)
            if arm == 'observe':
                observer = Child([str(args.observer), 'observe', '--count', '60', '--interval-ms', '500'], selector, 'observer')
                children.append(observer)
            while True:
                if STOP:
                    raise RuntimeError('cancelled: ' + STOP)
                now = time.monotonic_ns()
                if now >= deadline:
                    raise RuntimeError('arm timeout')
                if now >= next_tick:
                    lateness.append(max(0, now - next_tick))
                    # Do not manufacture many observations after a long scheduling pause.
                    next_tick = now + 10_000_000
                if now >= next_sample:
                    snapshot = host()
                    sample = {'elapsed_ns': now - started, 'host': snapshot,
                              'processes': {**{c.name: proc_sample(c.proc.pid) for c in children}, 'harness': proc_sample(os.getpid())}}
                    samples.append(sample)
                    emit({'type': 'sample', 'arm': arm, 'repeat': repeat, **sample})
                    safe(snapshot)
                    next_sample = now + 100_000_000
                if ready and telemetry_ready and pending is None and requests < args.requests and now >= next_request:
                    if workload_started is None:
                        workload_started = time.monotonic_ns()
                    pending = time.monotonic_ns()
                    worker.proc.stdin.write(b'work\n')
                    worker.proc.stdin.flush()
                for key, _ in selector.select(.005):
                    child, channel = key.data
                    data = os.read(key.fileobj.fileno(), 65536)
                    if not data:
                        selector.unregister(key.fileobj)
                        continue
                    child.bytes += len(data)
                    if child.bytes > 2 * 1024 * 1024:
                        raise RuntimeError('child output exceeds 2 MiB')
                    child.buffers[channel] += data
                    if len(child.buffers[channel]) > 65536:
                        raise RuntimeError('child line exceeds 64 KiB')
                    if channel == 'stderr':
                        continue
                    while b'\n' in child.buffers[channel]:
                        line, child.buffers[channel] = child.buffers[channel].split(b'\n', 1)
                        report = json.loads(line)
                        if child.name == 'observer':
                            if (report.get('evidence') != 'live_read_only' or report.get('kernel_mutations') != 0
                                or report.get('owned_buffer_mutations') != 0 or report['plan']['kernel_write_available']):
                                raise RuntimeError('observer contract/safety failure')
                            observations.append(report)
                            telemetry_ready = True
                            # A declined policy may mean optional/required decision telemetry is absent.
                            # Keep that report explicit; independent MemAvailable admission still applies.
                            emit({'type': 'observation', 'arm': arm, 'repeat': repeat, 'report': report})
                        elif not ready:
                            if report.get('ready') is not True or report.get('workload') != 'copy_crc_1mib_x16_v1' or report.get('primary_buffer_bytes') != 2097152:
                                raise RuntimeError('worker readiness mismatch')
                            ready = True
                            expected_crc = report['crc32']
                        else:
                            if pending is None or report.get('verified') is not True or report.get('crc32') != expected_crc:
                                raise RuntimeError('workload integrity/protocol failure')
                            completed = time.monotonic_ns()
                            latency = completed - pending
                            response.append(latency)
                            service.append(report['elapsed_ns'])
                            requests += 1
                            emit({'type': 'response', 'arm': arm, 'repeat': repeat, 'request': requests,
                                  'response_ns': latency, 'service_ns': report['elapsed_ns'], 'crc32': expected_crc})
                            pending = None
                            next_request = completed + int(args.interval_ms * 1e6)
                if requests == args.requests:
                    workload_finished = time.monotonic_ns()
                    if any(c.reap() is not None for c in children):
                        raise RuntimeError('child exited before owned cleanup')
                    result['status'] = 'complete'
                    break
                for child in children:
                    if child.reap() is not None:
                        raise RuntimeError(child.name + ' terminated during workload')
        except (Exception, KeyboardInterrupt) as exc:
            error = f'{type(exc).__name__}: {exc}'
        finally:
            cleanup = []
            for child in reversed(children):
                try:
                    cleanup.append(child.stop())
                except Exception as exc:
                    cleanup.append({'name': child.name, 'pid': child.proc.pid, 'reaped': False,
                                    'kill_escalation': False, 'usage': None, 'error': str(exc)})
    if not all(c['reaped'] and not c['kill_escalation'] for c in cleanup):
        result['status'] = 'incomplete'
        error = (error or '') + '; cleanup ambiguous or kill escalation required'
    result.update(error=error, response_ns=response, service_ns=service,
                  crc32=expected_crc, observations=len(observations), samples=samples,
                  timer_lateness_ns=lateness, children=cleanup,
                  workload_completion_ns=(workload_finished-workload_started) if workload_finished else None,
                  arm_wall_ns=time.monotonic_ns()-started, harness_cpu_ns=time.process_time_ns()-start_cpu)
    emit(result)
    return result


def delta(before, after):
    if before is None or after is None:
        return {'status': 'unavailable', 'delta': None}
    if after < before:
