        return {'status': 'counter_reset', 'delta': None}
    return {'status': 'available', 'delta': after-before}


def context_delta(samples):
    if not samples:
        return None
    first, last = samples[0]['host'], samples[-1]['host']
    a, b = first['cpu_ticks'], last['cpu_ticks']
    busy = None
    if a and b and all(y >= x for x, y in zip(a, b)):
        total = sum(b)-sum(a)
        busy = (total-(b[3]-a[3])-(b[4]-a[4]))/total if total else None
    return {'sample_window_ns': last['monotonic_ns']-first['monotonic_ns'],
            'cpu_busy_fraction': busy,
            'minimum_mem_available_kib': min((s['host']['mem_available_kib'] for s in samples
                                            if s['host']['mem_available_kib'] is not None), default=None),
            'swap_pages': {key: delta(first['swap_pages'][key], last['swap_pages'][key]) for key in ('pswpin', 'pswpout')},
            'psi_total_us': {kind: {level: delta((first['psi'][kind] or {}).get(level, {}).get('total'),
                                                   (last['psi'][kind] or {}).get(level, {}).get('total'))
                                    for level in ('some', 'full')} for kind in ('cpu', 'memory', 'io')},
            'zram_first': first['zram'], 'zram_last': last['zram']}


def summarize(runs):
    summaries = {}
    for arm in ARMS:
        group = [r for r in runs if r['arm'] == arm and r['status'] == 'complete']
        rss = {name: [s['processes'][name]['rss_kib'] for r in group for s in r['samples']
                      if name in s['processes'] and s['processes'][name]['rss_kib'] is not None]
               for name in ('workload', 'observer', 'harness')}
        completion = [r['workload_completion_ns'] for r in group]
        summaries[arm] = {'complete_runs': len(group),
            'response_ns': quantiles([v for r in group for v in r['response_ns']]),
            'service_ns': quantiles([v for r in group for v in r['service_ns']]),
            'per_run_response_ns': [quantiles(r['response_ns']) for r in group],
            'completion_ns': quantiles(completion),
            'completion_cv': statistics.stdev(completion)/statistics.mean(completion) if len(completion)>1 else None,
            'host_context_per_run': [context_delta(r['samples']) for r in group],
            'process_rss_kib': {k: quantiles(v) for k, v in rss.items()},
            'child_cpu_ns': {name: quantiles([c['usage']['user_cpu_ns']+c['usage']['system_cpu_ns'] for r in group
                              for c in r['children'] if c['usage'] and c['name'] == name])
                              for name in ('workload', 'observer')},
            'harness_cpu_ns': quantiles([r['harness_cpu_ns'] for r in group]),
            'timer_lateness_ns': quantiles([v for r in group for v in r['timer_lateness_ns']])}
    paired = []
    for repeat in sorted({r['repeat'] for r in runs}):
        pair = {r['arm']: r for r in runs if r['repeat'] == repeat and r['status'] == 'complete'}
        if len(pair) == 2:
            a, b = pair['baseline'], pair['observe']
            paired.append({'repeat': repeat,
                'completion_observe_over_baseline': b['workload_completion_ns']/a['workload_completion_ns'],
                'p50_response_observe_over_baseline': statistics.median(b['response_ns'])/statistics.median(a['response_ns'])})
    return {'arms': summaries, 'paired': paired, 'failed_runs': sum(r['status'] != 'complete' for r in runs),
            'interpretation': 'timer lateness is a harness scheduling proxy, not GUI/frame/input latency; shared telemetry is context, not attributed benefit',
            'active_optimization': {'status': 'NO-GO', 'available': False, 'reason': 'no demonstrated admissible active action; simulation is excluded'}}


def main():
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
    root = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
    parser.add_argument('--worker', type=Path, default=root/'target/release/xynoos-perf')
    parser.add_argument('--observer', type=Path, default=root/'target/release/xynoos-mvp')
    parser.add_argument('--environment', required=True, choices=('Codespaces', 'physical'))
    parser.add_argument('--label', required=True, help='machine/session description; do not claim another host')
    parser.add_argument('--output', type=Path, required=True, help='new directory; never overwritten')
    parser.add_argument('--repeats', type=int, default=8)
    parser.add_argument('--requests', type=int, default=200)
    parser.add_argument('--interval-ms', type=int, default=10)
    parser.add_argument('--timeout', type=float, default=25)
    args = parser.parse_args()
    if not (2 <= args.repeats <= 30 and args.repeats % 2 == 0 and 10 <= args.requests <= 1000
            and 0 <= args.interval_ms <= 100 and math.isfinite(args.timeout) and 1 <= args.timeout <= 25):
        parser.error('require even repeats 2..30, requests 10..1000, interval 0..100, timeout 1..25 seconds')
    args.worker, args.observer = args.worker.resolve(), args.observer.resolve()
    for binary in (args.worker, args.observer):
        if not binary.is_file() or not os.access(binary, os.X_OK):
            parser.error('missing executable: ' + str(binary))
    if os.environ.get('CODESPACES') == 'true' and args.environment != 'Codespaces':
        parser.error('Codespaces must be labelled Codespaces')
    args.output.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=False)
    for signum in (signal.SIGINT, signal.SIGTERM, signal.SIGHUP):
        signal.signal(signum, interrupt)
    runs = []
    status = 'incomplete'
    with (args.output/'events.jsonl').open('x', buffering=1) as log:
        def emit(event):
            log.write(json.dumps(event, separators=(',', ':'), allow_nan=False)+'\n')
            if event['type'] in ('manifest', 'run_start', 'run', 'end'):
                log.flush()
                os.fsync(log.fileno())
        manifest = {'type': 'manifest', 'schema_version': 1, 'environment': args.environment, 'label': args.label,
                    'utc': datetime.datetime.now(datetime.timezone.utc).isoformat(), 'platform': platform.platform(),
                    'cpuinfo': read('/proc/cpuinfo', 1048576), 'os_release': read('/etc/os-release'),
                    'clock_ticks_per_second': os.sysconf('SC_CLK_TCK'),
                    'configuration': {k: str(v) if isinstance(v, Path) else v for k, v in vars(args).items()},
                    'binary_sha256': {str(p): hashlib.sha256(p.read_bytes()).hexdigest() for p in (args.worker, args.observer)},
                    'harness_sha256': hashlib.sha256(Path(__file__).read_bytes()).hexdigest(),
                    'source_commit': read(Path(__file__).resolve().parent/'SOURCE_COMMIT'),
                    'source_manifest': read(Path(__file__).resolve().parent/'SOURCE_MANIFEST.json'),
                    'initial_host': host(), 'warmup': 'first arm pair recorded as warmup and excluded; one work request in flight; interval after completion',
                    'order': 'AB/BA alternating; even number of measured pairs', 'physical_mutation_available': False}
        emit(manifest)
        try:
            for repeat in range(-1, args.repeats):
                order = ARMS if repeat % 2 == 0 else tuple(reversed(ARMS))
                for arm in order:
                    r = run_arm(arm, args, emit, repeat)
                    if repeat >= 0:
                        runs.append(r)
                    if r['status'] != 'complete':
                        raise RuntimeError(r['error'])
                    if STOP:
                        raise RuntimeError('cancelled: '+STOP)
                print(f'pair {repeat}: complete', flush=True)
            if len({r['crc32'] for r in runs}) != 1:
                raise RuntimeError('cross-arm corpus mismatch')
            status = 'complete'
        except (Exception, KeyboardInterrupt) as exc:
            emit({'type': 'failure', 'error': f'{type(exc).__name__}: {exc}'})
        summary = {'schema_version': 1, 'status': status, 'environment': args.environment,
                   'label': args.label, 'expected_measured_runs': args.repeats*2,
                   'recorded_measured_runs': len(runs), **summarize(runs)}
        emit({'type': 'end', 'status': status})
        with (args.output/'summary.json').open('x') as f:
            json.dump(summary, f, indent=2, allow_nan=False)
            f.write('\n')
            f.flush()
            os.fsync(f.fileno())
    print(json.dumps(summary, indent=2))
    return 0 if status == 'complete' else 1


if __name__ == '__main__':
    sys.exit(main())


FILE scripts/benchmark_task007.py
"""Jalankan dua subprocess release dan validasi ekuivalensi non-timing TASK007."""
import csv,io,subprocess,statistics,resource
from pathlib import Path
root=Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent
rows=[]
for process in range(2):
    result=subprocess.run([str(root/'target/release/examples/fastpath')],check=True,text=True,capture_output=True)
    current=list(csv.DictReader(io.StringIO(result.stdout)))
    assert len(current)==972
    rows.extend(dict(process=process,**r) for r in current)
keys=list(rows[0])
with (root/'docs/benchmarks/task-007-raw.csv').open('w',newline='') as f:
    w=csv.DictWriter(f,fieldnames=keys,lineterminator="\n");w.writeheader();w.writerows(rows)
by={(r['process'],r['trace'],r['seed'],r['policy'],r['selector'],r['sample']):r for r in rows}
for r in rows:
    oracle=by[(r['process'],r['trace'],r['seed'],r['policy'],'bounded',r['sample'])]
    if r['selector'] in ['fast','journal']:
        assert all(r[k]==oracle[k] for k in r if k not in ['selector','event_ns','inspection_ns'])
    other=by[(1-r['process'],r['trace'],r['seed'],r['policy'],r['selector'],r['sample'])]
    assert all(r[k]==other[k] for k in r if k not in ['process','event_ns','inspection_ns'])
groups={}
for r in rows:groups.setdefault((r['trace'],r['policy'],r['selector']),[]).append(r)
fields=['trace','policy','mode','median_event_ns','p95_event_ns','median_ns_per_access','refaults_mean','migrations_mean','rss_note']
with (root/'docs/benchmarks/task-007-summary.csv').open('w',newline='') as f:
    w=csv.DictWriter(f,fieldnames=fields,lineterminator="\n");w.writeheader()
    for (trace,policy,mode),v in groups.items():
        times=sorted(int(r['event_ns']) for r in v)
        w.writerow(dict(trace=trace,policy=policy,mode=mode,median_event_ns=statistics.median(times),p95_event_ns=times[(95*len(times)+99)//100-1],median_ns_per_access=round(statistics.median(times)/512,2),refaults_mean=round(statistics.mean(int(r['refaults']) for r in v),2),migrations_mean=round(statistics.mean(int(r['migrations']) for r in v),2),rss_note='tidak diukur per mode'))
(root/'docs/benchmarks/task-007-reproducibility.txt').write_text('1944 record valid; dua proses identik non-timing; journal/FastPath identik dengan oracle pada setiap sample dan konfigurasi. Warm-up1 dan3 sample tiap mode, seed1/42/2026.\n')

(root/"docs/benchmarks/task-007-process-memory.txt").write_text("Peak RSS maksimum subprocess benchmark (KiB): "+str(resource.getrusage(resource.RUSAGE_CHILDREN).ru_maxrss)+"\nPengukuran seluruh proses, seluruh mode, bukan footprint per-mode. /usr/bin/time tidak tersedia; resource.getrusage dipakai.\n")


FILE scripts/benchmark_task008.py
"""Benchmark Rust allocator dan verifikasi metrik simulator antar mode."""
import csv,io,statistics,subprocess,resource
from pathlib import Path
root=Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent
rows=[]
for process in range(2):
    result=subprocess.run([str(root/'target/release/examples/scalability')],text=True,capture_output=True,check=True)
    data=list(csv.DictReader(io.StringIO(result.stdout)))
    assert len(data)==162
    rows.extend(dict(process=process,**row) for row in data)
by={(r['process'],r['groups'],r['seed'],r['mode'],r['sample']):r for r in rows}
assert len(by)==324
excluded={'mode','event_ns','inspection_ns','alloc_count','alloc_bytes','dealloc_count','estimated_vec_clones','estimated_access_replays'}
for r in rows:
    other=by[(r['process'],r['groups'],r['seed'],'clone',r['sample'])]
    assert all(r[k]==other[k] for k in r if k not in excluded)
    repeat=by[(1-r['process'],r['groups'],r['seed'],r['mode'],r['sample'])]
    assert all(r[k]==repeat[k] for k in r if k not in excluded|{'process'})
root.joinpath('docs/benchmarks/task-008-reproducibility.txt').write_text('324 record: metrik non-timing/non-allocator identik antar mode dan dua subprocess; alokasi dicatat sebagai pengukuran, bukan diuji determinismenya.\n')
with root.joinpath('docs/benchmarks/task-008-raw.csv').open('w',newline='') as f:
    w=csv.DictWriter(f,fieldnames=list(rows[0]),lineterminator='\n');w.writeheader();w.writerows(rows)
fields=['groups','mode','samples','event_ns_median','event_ns_p95','inspection_ns_median','alloc_count_median','alloc_bytes_median','dealloc_count_median','peak_live_bytes','rss_scope']
with root.joinpath('docs/benchmarks/task-008-summary.csv').open('w',newline='') as f:
    w=csv.DictWriter(f,fieldnames=fields,lineterminator='\n');w.writeheader()
    for n in [1,4,8,16,32,64]:
        for mode in ['clone','journal','fast']:
            group=[r for r in rows if int(r['groups'])==n and r['mode']==mode]
            def med(k):return statistics.median(int(r[k]) for r in group)
            times=sorted(int(r['event_ns']) for r in group)
            w.writerow(dict(groups=n,mode=mode,samples=len(group),event_ns_median=med('event_ns'),event_ns_p95=times[(95*len(times)+99)//100-1],inspection_ns_median=med('inspection_ns'),alloc_count_median=med('alloc_count'),alloc_bytes_median=med('alloc_bytes'),dealloc_count_median=med('dealloc_count'),peak_live_bytes='tidak_diukur',rss_scope='subprocess_keseluruhan'))
root.joinpath('docs/benchmarks/task-008-memory.txt').write_text(f'Peak RSS maksimum seluruh subprocess: {resource.getrusage(resource.RUSAGE_CHILDREN).ru_maxrss} KiB; termasuk inisialisasi dan seluruh ukuran/mode.\n')


FILE scripts/benchmark_task009.py
"""Atribusi heap dan ekuivalensi sembilan workload, tiga ukuran, dua subprocess."""
import csv,io,subprocess,statistics,resource
from pathlib import Path
root=Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent
rows=[]
for process in range(2):
 result=subprocess.run([str(root/'target/release/examples/reliability')],text=True,capture_output=True,check=True)
 data=list(csv.DictReader(io.StringIO(result.stdout)));assert len(data)==729
 rows.extend(dict(process=process,**r) for r in data)
by={(r['process'],r['trace'],r['groups'],r['seed'],r['mode'],r['sample']):r for r in rows};assert len(by)==1458
exclude={'mode','event_ns','inspection_ns','alloc_count','alloc_bytes','dealloc_count','estimated_vec_clones','estimated_access_replays','live_count','live_bytes','peak_live_bytes'}
for r in rows:
 other=by[(r['process'],r['trace'],r['groups'],r['seed'],'clone',r['sample'])]
 assert all(r[k]==other[k] for k in r if k not in exclude)
 other=by[(1-r['process'],r['trace'],r['groups'],r['seed'],r['mode'],r['sample'])]
 assert all(r[k]==other[k] for k in r if k not in {'process','event_ns','inspection_ns'})
 assert int(r['peak_live_bytes'])>=int(r['live_bytes'])
with root.joinpath('docs/benchmarks/task-009-raw.csv').open('w',newline='') as f:
 w=csv.DictWriter(f,fieldnames=list(rows[0]),lineterminator='\n');w.writeheader();w.writerows(rows)
fields=['groups','mode','samples','event_ns_median','inspection_ns_median','alloc_count_median','alloc_bytes_median','dealloc_count_median','live_bytes_median','peak_live_bytes_median']
with root.joinpath('docs/benchmarks/task-009-summary.csv').open('w',newline='') as f:
 w=csv.DictWriter(f,fieldnames=fields,lineterminator='\n');w.writeheader()
 for n in [1,16,64]:
  for mode in ['clone','journal','fast']:
   v=[r for r in rows if int(r['groups'])==n and r['mode']==mode]
   d=dict(groups=n,mode=mode,samples=len(v))
   for k in fields[3:]:d[k]=statistics.median(int(r[k.removesuffix('_median')]) for r in v)
   w.writerow(d)
root.joinpath('docs/benchmarks/task-009-reproducibility.txt').write_text('1458 record valid; mode ekuivalen pada metrik simulasi; dua subprocess identik pada semua kolom selain runtime. Semua scope selesai dengan live count nol setelah drop; register tidak penuh.\n')
root.joinpath('docs/benchmarks/task-009-memory.txt').write_text(f'Peak RSS maksimum subprocess keseluruhan: {resource.getrusage(resource.RUSAGE_CHILDREN).ru_maxrss} KiB.\n')

FILE scripts/benchmark_task012.py
"""Eksperimen paired TASK010/TASK012 dengan urutan proses ABBA."""
import csv, io, os, subprocess, tempfile, statistics
from pathlib import Path
root=Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent
bench=root/'docs'/'benchmarks'
with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(prefix='xynoos-012-') as temporary:
    base=Path(temporary)/'base';base.mkdir()
    archive=subprocess.Popen(['git','archive','d082f60ade87b30697fa03384ace1a8089f036d3'],cwd=root,stdout=subprocess.PIPE)
    subprocess.run(['tar','-x','-C',str(base)],stdin=archive.stdout,check=True)
    archive.stdout.close()
    assert archive.wait()==0
    (base/'crates/xynoos-aura/examples/typed_research.rs').write_bytes((root/'crates/xynoos-aura/examples/typed_research.rs').read_bytes())
    env=os.environ.copy();env['CARGO_TARGET_DIR']=str(Path(temporary)/'target')
    subprocess.run(['cargo','build','--offline','--manifest-path',str(base/'Cargo.toml'),'-p','xynoos-aura','--example','typed_research','--release'],env=env,check=True,stdout=subprocess.DEVNULL)
    binaries={'baseline':Path(temporary)/'target/release/examples/typed_research','current':root/'target/release/examples/typed_research'}
    rows=[]
    for process,version in enumerate(['baseline','current','current','baseline']):
        with tempfile.TemporaryFile(mode='w+b') as output:
            child=subprocess.Popen([str(binaries[version])],stdout=output)
            _,status,usage=os.wait4(child.pid,0);child.returncode=os.waitstatus_to_exitcode(status)
            if child.returncode!=0:raise RuntimeError(f'{version}: {child.returncode}')
            output.seek(0);data=list(csv.DictReader(io.TextIOWrapper(output,encoding='utf8')))
        assert len(data)==324
        rows.extend(dict(process=process,version=version,rss_peak_kib=usage.ru_maxrss,**r) for r in data)
assert len(rows)==1296
by={(r['version'],r['process']//2 if r['version']=='current' else (0 if r['process']==0 else 1),r['trace'],r['seed'],r['groups'],r['mode'],r['sample']):r for r in rows}
# Kesesuaian simulasi diperiksa terhadap baseline berurutan untuk setiap process.
base={(r['trace'],r['seed'],r['groups'],r['mode'],r['sample']):r for r in rows if r['version']=='baseline' and r['process']==0}
ignore={'version','process','rss_peak_kib','event_ns','inspection_ns','alloc_count','alloc_bytes','dealloc_count','live_count','live_bytes','peak_live_bytes'}
for r in rows:
    b=base[(r['trace'],r['seed'],r['groups'],r['mode'],r['sample'])]
    assert all(str(r[k])==str(b[k]) for k in r if k not in ignore)
with (bench/'task-012-raw.csv').open('w',newline='') as f:
    w=csv.DictWriter(f,fieldnames=list(rows[0]),lineterminator='\n');w.writeheader();w.writerows(rows)
fields=['groups','mode','version','samples','event_ns_median','event_ns_min','event_ns_max','inspection_ns_median','alloc_count_median','alloc_bytes_median','peak_live_bytes_median','rss_peak_kib_max']
with (bench/'task-012-summary.csv').open('w',newline='') as f:
    w=csv.DictWriter(f,fieldnames=fields,lineterminator='\n');w.writeheader()
    for n in ['1','16','64']:
        for mode in ['clone','journal','fast']:
            for version in ['baseline','current']:
                group=[r for r in rows if r['groups']==n and r['mode']==mode and r['version']==version]
                def med(k):return statistics.median(int(x[k]) for x in group)
                w.writerow(dict(groups=n,mode=mode,version=version,samples=len(group),event_ns_median=med('event_ns'),event_ns_min=min(int(x['event_ns']) for x in group),event_ns_max=max(int(x['event_ns']) for x in group),inspection_ns_median=med('inspection_ns'),alloc_count_median=med('alloc_count'),alloc_bytes_median=med('alloc_bytes'),peak_live_bytes_median=med('peak_live_bytes'),rss_peak_kib_max=max(int(x['rss_peak_kib']) for x in group)))
(bench/'task-012-reproducibility.txt').write_text('1296 record valid; seluruh metrik simulasi identik dengan TASK010 untuk setiap workload/seed/ukuran/mode/sample. Dua subprocess per versi, urutan ABBA. Timing, allocator dan RSS tidak disyaratkan identik.\n')


FILE scripts/benchmark_task013.py
"""Pengamatan live pendek dan benchmark parser fixture; tidak memodifikasi procfs."""
import csv,io,os,statistics,subprocess,tempfile,time
from pathlib import Path
root=Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent
bench=root/'docs/benchmarks'
def run(args):
 with tempfile.TemporaryFile() as out:
  start=time.monotonic_ns();p=subprocess.Popen(args,stdout=out);_,status,usage=os.wait4(p.pid,0);p.returncode=os.waitstatus_to_exitcode(status);wall=time.monotonic_ns()-start
  if p.returncode:raise RuntimeError(f'proses gagal: {p.returncode}')
  out.seek(0);data=out.read().decode()
 return data,usage,wall
live,usage,wall=run([str(root/'target/release/xynoos-cli'),'pressure','sample','--count','5','--interval-ms','200'])
rows=list(csv.DictReader(io.StringIO(live)));assert len(rows)==5 and all(len(r)==21 and None not in r for r in rows)
assert all(r['psi_status']=='ok' and r['mem_status']=='ok' and r['vm_status']=='ok' for r in rows)
(bench/'task-013-raw.csv').write_text(live)
a,_,_=run([str(root/'target/release/examples/pressureiq_research')]);b,_,_=run([str(root/'target/release/examples/pressureiq_research')]);fixtures=list(csv.DictReader(io.StringIO(a)));second=list(csv.DictReader(io.StringIO(b)))
assert [(r['case'],r['iterations']) for r in fixtures]==[(r['case'],r['iterations']) for r in second] and len(fixtures)==3
(bench/'task-013-fixture.csv').write_text(a)
summary=[]
for key in ['read_psi_ns','parse_psi_ns','read_mem_ns','parse_mem_ns','read_vm_ns','parse_vm_ns','snapshot_ns']:
 x=[int(r[key]) for r in rows];summary.append((key,statistics.median(x),'ns',len(x),'live'))
for r in fixtures:summary.append(('fixture_'+r['case'],r['ns_per_parse'],'ns_per_parse',r['iterations'],'syntetis'))
summary.extend([('cpu_total',(usage.ru_utime+usage.ru_stime)*1e9,'ns',5,'seluruh_proses'),('peak_rss',usage.ru_maxrss,'KiB',5,'seluruh_proses'),('wall_total',wall,'ns',5,'seluruh_proses'),('csv_size',len(live.encode()),'byte',5,'live')])
with (bench/'task-013-summary.csv').open('w',newline='') as f:
 w=csv.writer(f,lineterminator='\n');w.writerow(['metric','value','unit','samples_or_iterations','scope']);w.writerows(summary)
(bench/'task-013-reproducibility.txt').write_text('Dua eksekusi parser fixture memiliki kasus dan jumlah iterasi identik. Waktu dan telemetry live berubah; output live 5 baris tervalidasi dengan 21 kolom. Seluruh sumber tersedia pada pengamatan ini.\n')

FILE scripts/inspect_desktop.py
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Inspect partial desktop evidence without signalling, replay or cleanup."""
import argparse
import json
from pathlib import Path
from benchmark_desktop import ARMS, summarize


def inspect(path):
    manifest = None
    starts, runs = {}, {}
    end = None
    truncated = False
    terminal = False
    with path.open('rb') as stream:
        while True:
            line = stream.readline(8 * 1024 * 1024 + 1)
            if not line:
                break
            if len(line) > 8 * 1024 * 1024:
                raise ValueError('evidence line bound')
            try:
                row = json.loads(line)
            except (ValueError, UnicodeError):
                if line.endswith(b'\n') or stream.read(1):
                    raise ValueError('corrupt complete/nonfinal row') from None
                truncated = True
                break
            if terminal:
                raise ValueError('evidence after end')
            kind = row['type']
            if kind == 'manifest':
                if manifest is not None or starts:
                    raise ValueError('duplicate/late manifest')
                manifest = row
            elif kind == 'run_start':
                key = row['repeat'], row['arm']
                if manifest is None or key in starts:
                    raise ValueError('missing manifest or duplicate run start')
                starts[key] = row
            elif kind == 'run':
                key = row['repeat'], row['arm']
                if key not in starts or key in runs:
                    raise ValueError('unstarted/duplicate run')
                runs[key] = row
            elif kind == 'end':
                terminal = True
                end = row['status']
    unresolved = sorted(set(starts) - set(runs))
    config = manifest.get('configuration', {}) if manifest else {}
    arms = (config['only_arm'],) if config.get('only_arm') else (ARMS if config.get('arms', 'all') == 'all' else ARMS[:2])
    expected = {(i, arm) for i in range(config.get('repeats', 0)) for arm in arms}
    expected |= {(-1, arm) for arm in arms}
    complete = bool(manifest and end == 'complete' and not truncated and not unresolved and set(runs) == expected)
    for row in runs.values():
        children = {c['name']: c for c in row['children']}
        wanted = {'search', 'index'} | ({'observer'} if row['arm'] != 'baseline' else set())
        complete &= (row['status'] == 'complete' and set(children) == wanted and all(
            c['reaped'] and not c.get('kill_escalation') and not c.get('cleanup_error') for c in children.values()))
        for role in ('search', 'index'):
            child = children.get(role, {})
            final = child.get('final') or {}
            complete &= (child.get('exit_code') == 0 and final.get('released') is True and final.get('cancelled') is False
                         and final.get('searches') == (config.get('requests') if role == 'search' else 0)
                         and final.get('passes') == (config.get('index_passes') if role == 'index' else 0))
        complete &= (len(row.get('search_results', [])) == config.get('requests') and
                     len(row.get('index_results', [])) == config.get('index_passes'))
    good = [r for r in runs.values() if r['status'] == 'complete']
    if good:
        complete &= len({json.dumps((r['search_results'], r['index_results']), sort_keys=True) for r in good}) == 1
    return {'status': 'complete' if complete else 'partial', 'read_only': True,
            'environment': manifest.get('environment') if manifest else None,
            'truncated_final_row': truncated, 'unresolved_cleanup_unknown': unresolved,
            'recorded_runs': len(runs), **summarize([r for r in runs.values() if r['repeat'] >= 0])}


if __name__ == '__main__':
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
    parser.add_argument('events', type=Path)
    print(json.dumps(inspect(parser.parse_args().events), indent=2))


FILE scripts/inspect_owned.py
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Read owned-experiment evidence without signalling historical PIDs or replaying actions."""
import argparse
import json
from pathlib import Path
from benchmark_owned import ARMS, summary


def inspect(path):
    manifest = terminal = None
    runs, starts = {}, set()
    truncated = False
    with path.open('rb') as f:
        while True:
            line = f.readline(8 * 1024 * 1024 + 1)
            if not line:
                break
            if len(line) > 8 * 1024 * 1024:
                raise ValueError('evidence line exceeds 8 MiB')
            try:
                row = json.loads(line)
            except (ValueError, UnicodeError):
                if line.endswith(b'\n') or f.read(1):
                    raise ValueError('corrupt complete/non-final row') from None
                truncated = True
                break
            if terminal is not None:
                raise ValueError('records after terminal marker')
            kind = row['type']
            if kind == 'manifest':
                if manifest is not None or starts or runs:
                    raise ValueError('duplicate or misplaced manifest')
                manifest = row
            elif kind in ('run_start', 'run'):
                key = row['repeat'], row['arm']
                if key[1] not in ARMS:
                    raise ValueError('unknown arm')
                if kind == 'run_start':
                    if key in starts:
                        raise ValueError('duplicate run start')
                    starts.add(key)
                else:
                    if key in runs or key not in starts:
                        raise ValueError('duplicate or unstarted run')
                    runs[key] = row
            elif kind == 'end':
                terminal = row['status']
    expected = {(i, arm) for i in range(-1, manifest['configuration']['repeats']) for arm in ARMS} if manifest else set()
    measured = [row for (i, _), row in runs.items() if i >= 0]
    valid = bool(manifest) and starts == expected and set(runs) == expected
    for run in runs.values():
        valid &= (run['status'] == 'complete' and run.get('error') is None
            and bool(run.get('children')) and all(c['reaped'] and not c.get('kill_escalation') and c.get('exit_code') == 0 for c in run['children'])
            and bool(run.get('final')) and run['final']['restored'] and run['final']['verified'] and not run['final']['cancelled']
            and len(run['responses']) == manifest['configuration']['requests'])
    report = summary(measured)
    valid &= all(p['correctness_equivalent'] for p in report['paired'])
    return {'status': 'complete' if valid and terminal == 'complete' and not truncated else 'partial',
            'read_only': True, 'environment': manifest.get('environment') if manifest else None,
            'truncated_final_row': truncated,
            'unresolved_cleanup_unknown': sorted(starts-set(runs)),
            'expected_runs_including_warmup': len(expected), 'recorded_runs_including_warmup': len(runs), **report}


if __name__ == '__main__':
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
    parser.add_argument('events', type=Path)
    args = parser.parse_args()
    print(json.dumps(inspect(args.events), indent=2))


FILE scripts/inspect_physical.py
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Read partial physical benchmark JSONL without replaying, signalling or deleting anything."""
import argparse
import json
from pathlib import Path
from benchmark_physical import summarize


def inspect(path):
    runs = []
    started = []
    ended = None
    manifest = None
    truncated = False
    with path.open('rb') as f:
        while True:
            line = f.readline(8 * 1024 * 1024 + 1)
            if not line:
                break
            if len(line) > 8 * 1024 * 1024:
                raise ValueError('evidence line exceeds 8 MiB')
            try:
                event = json.loads(line)
            except (ValueError, UnicodeError):
                if f.read(1) or line.endswith(b'\n'):
                    raise ValueError('corrupt complete/non-final event') from None
                truncated = True
                break
            if event['type'] == 'manifest':
                if manifest is not None:
                    raise ValueError('duplicate manifest')
                manifest = event
            elif event['type'] == 'run_start':
                started.append((event['repeat'], event['arm']))
            elif event['type'] == 'run':
                if any((r['repeat'], r['arm']) == (event['repeat'], event['arm']) for r in runs):
                    raise ValueError('duplicate completed run evidence')
                runs.append(event)
            elif event['type'] == 'end':
                ended = event['status']
    finished = {(r['repeat'], r['arm']) for r in runs}
    unresolved = [key for key in started if key not in finished]
    cleanup = all(r['children'] and all(c['reaped'] and not c.get('kill_escalation', False) for c in r['children']) for r in runs)
    expected = manifest['configuration']['repeats'] * 2 if manifest else None
    measured = [r for r in runs if r['repeat'] >= 0]
    expected_keys = {(i, arm) for i in range(manifest['configuration']['repeats']) for arm in ('baseline', 'observe')} if manifest else set()
    complete = (ended == 'complete' and not truncated and not unresolved and cleanup
                and len(measured) == expected and all(r['status'] == 'complete' for r in runs)
                and {(r['repeat'], r['arm']) for r in measured} == expected_keys)
    return {'status': 'complete' if complete else 'partial', 'read_only': True,
            'environment': manifest.get('environment') if manifest else None,
            'truncated_final_event': truncated, 'unresolved_runs_cleanup_unknown': unresolved,
            'expected_measured_runs': expected, 'recorded_measured_runs': len(measured),
            'cleanup': 'unknown_for_unresolved_runs' if unresolved else 'see_recorded_child_results',
            **summarize(measured)}


if __name__ == '__main__':
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
    parser.add_argument('events', type=Path)
    args = parser.parse_args()
    print(json.dumps(inspect(args.events), indent=2))


FILE scripts/laptop_diagnostic.py
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Read-only laptop bottleneck evidence. No workload, service calls or control writes."""
import argparse
import enum
import json
import os
from pathlib import Path
import platform
import signal
import time

from benchmark_physical import read, numbers, host, delta, quantiles, proc_sample

VM_KEYS = ('pgpgin', 'pgpgout', 'pgmajfault', 'pgfault', 'pswpin', 'pswpout',
           'pgscan_direct', 'pgscan_kswapd', 'pgsteal_direct', 'pgsteal_kswapd',
           'allocstall_dma', 'allocstall_dma32', 'allocstall_normal', 'oom_kill')
CG_KEYS = ('memory.current', 'memory.max', 'memory.high', 'memory.low', 'memory.events',
           'memory.stat', 'memory.pressure', 'cpu.stat', 'cpu.max', 'cpu.pressure',
           'io.stat', 'io.pressure', 'cgroup.events', 'cgroup.procs')
STOP = False


class Admission(str, enum.Enum):
    ADMITTED = 'admitted'
    PRESSURE = 'deferred_pressure'
    FOREGROUND = 'deferred_foreground'
    ARRIVAL = 'deferred_arrival_guard'
    STALE = 'refused_stale_telemetry'
    MEMORY = 'refused_memory_floor'
    CGROUP = 'refused_cgroup_unresolved'
    CGROUP_TELEMETRY = 'refused_cgroup_telemetry'
    HEADROOM = 'refused_cgroup_headroom'
    PSI = 'refused_psi_unavailable'
    RESET = 'refused_counter_reset'
    VM = 'refused_vm_unavailable'
    OOM = 'refused_oom_observed'
    IDENTITY = 'refused_cgroup_changed'
    CLOCK = 'refused_clock'


def cgroup_context():
    membership = read('/proc/self/cgroup')
    mountinfo = read('/proc/self/mountinfo', 1024 * 1024)
    result = {'membership': membership, 'status': 'unresolved', 'ancestors': []}
    try:
        group = next(line[3:] for line in membership.splitlines() if line.startswith('0::'))
        for line in mountinfo.splitlines():
            left, right = line.split(' - ', 1)
            if right.split()[0] != 'cgroup2':
                continue
            fields = left.split()
            root, mount = fields[3:5]
            # Do not guess escaped mount names or paths outside the visible mount.
            if '\\' in root + mount or '..' in Path(group).parts:
                continue
            relative = Path(group).relative_to(root)
            current = Path(mount) / relative
            if not current.is_dir():
                continue
            result.update(status='resolved', path=str(current), mount=mount,
                          device_inode=list((current.stat().st_dev, current.stat().st_ino)))
            while True:
                result['ancestors'].append({'path': str(current),
                    'values': {key: read(current / key) for key in CG_KEYS}})
                if current == Path(mount):
                    return result
                current = current.parent
    except (OSError, ValueError, AttributeError, StopIteration):
        pass
    return result


def snapshot():
    started = time.monotonic_ns()
    sample = host()
    vm = numbers(read('/proc/vmstat'))
    sample.update(vm={k: vm.get(k) for k in VM_KEYS},
                  meminfo=numbers(read('/proc/meminfo')), swaps=read('/proc/swaps'),
                  diskstats=read('/proc/diskstats'),
                  effective_cgroup=cgroup_context(), self_process=proc_sample(os.getpid()))
    sample['read_start_ns'] = started
    sample['monotonic_ns'] = time.monotonic_ns()
    return sample


def background():
    # No cmdlines, open files or user content. At most 256 visible processes.
    rows = []
    try:
        paths = sorted((p for p in Path('/proc').iterdir() if p.name.isdigit()), key=lambda p: int(p.name))
        for p in paths[:256]:
            status = numbers(read(p / 'status'))
            rows.append({'pid': int(p.name), 'comm': read(p / 'comm', 256),
                         'uid': status.get('Uid'), **proc_sample(int(p.name))})
        return {'processes': rows, 'visible_count': len(paths), 'truncated': len(paths) > 256}
    except OSError:
        return None


def context(label, environment):
    return {'schema': 'xynoos-laptop-v1', 'label': label, 'environment': environment,
            'utc_unix_ns': time.time_ns(), 'platform': platform.platform(),
            'os_release': read('/etc/os-release'), 'cpuinfo': read('/proc/cpuinfo', 1024 * 1024),
            'page_size': os.sysconf('SC_PAGE_SIZE'), 'clock_ticks': os.sysconf('SC_CLK_TCK'),
            'cpu_affinity': sorted(os.sched_getaffinity(0)), 'boot_id': read('/proc/sys/kernel/random/boot_id'),
            'session_type': os.environ.get('XDG_SESSION_TYPE'),
            'invocation_id': os.environ.get('INVOCATION_ID'), 'background': background(),
            'block_devices': {p.name: {k: read(p / k) for k in
                ('device/model', 'queue/rotational', 'size', 'stat')} for p in sorted(Path('/sys/block').iterdir())[:32]},
            'power': {p.name: {k: read(p / k) for k in ('type', 'online', 'status', 'capacity')}
                      for p in sorted(Path('/sys/class/power_supply').glob('*'))[:16]},
            'thermal_millidegrees': {p.name: read(p / 'temp') for p in sorted(Path('/sys/class/thermal').glob('thermal_zone*'))[:32]},
            'scope': 'namespace-visible, sequential reads; process names/RSS may be sensitive; no desktop application automation',
            'kernel_mutation_available': False}


def window(first, last):
    elapsed = last['monotonic_ns'] - first['monotonic_ns']
    psi = {kind: {level: delta((first['psi'][kind] or {}).get(level, {}).get('total'),
                               (last['psi'][kind] or {}).get(level, {}).get('total'))
                  for level in ('some', 'full')} for kind in ('cpu', 'memory', 'io')}
    vm = {k: delta(first['vm'].get(k), last['vm'].get(k)) for k in VM_KEYS}
    ticks = None
    a, b = first['cpu_ticks'], last['cpu_ticks']
    if a and b and all(y >= x for x, y in zip(a, b)) and sum(b) > sum(a):
        total = sum(b) - sum(a)
        ticks = {'busy_fraction': 1 - ((b[3] - a[3]) + (b[4] - a[4])) / total,
                 'iowait_fraction': (b[4] - a[4]) / total}
    evidence = []
    for kind, name in (('cpu', 'cpu_contention'), ('memory', 'memory_stalls'), ('io', 'io_stalls')):
        value = psi[kind]['some']['delta']
        if value is not None and elapsed > 0 and value * 1000 / elapsed >= .05:
            evidence.append(name)
    if any(vm[k]['delta'] for k in ('pgscan_direct', 'allocstall_normal')):
        evidence.append('direct_reclaim_activity')
    if any(vm[k]['delta'] for k in ('pswpin', 'pswpout')):
        evidence.append('swap_activity; correlate zram/disk and CPU before attribution')
    if not evidence:
        evidence.append('no_large_PSI_signal_in_this_window; cause_unresolved')
    def disks(text):
        result = {}
        for line in (text or '').splitlines():
            fields = line.split()
            if len(fields) >= 14:
                result[fields[2]] = {key: int(fields[i]) for key, i in
                    (('read_sectors', 5), ('write_sectors', 9), ('read_ms', 6), ('write_ms', 10),
                     ('io_ms', 12), ('weighted_io_ms', 13))}
        return result
    disk_first, disk_last = disks(first['diskstats']), disks(last['diskstats'])
    devices = {name: {key: delta(disk_first[name][key], values[key]) for key in values}
               for name, values in disk_last.items() if name in disk_first}
    before_groups = {a['path']: a['values'] for a in first['effective_cgroup']['ancestors']}
    cgroups = {}
    for ancestor in last['effective_cgroup']['ancestors']:
        name, values = ancestor['path'], ancestor['values']
        old = before_groups.get(name, {})
        cgroups[name] = {kind: {key: delta(numbers(old.get(kind)).get(key), numbers(values.get(kind)).get(key))
            for key in keys} for kind, keys in
            (('cpu.stat', ('usage_usec', 'nr_throttled', 'throttled_usec')),
             ('memory.events', ('high', 'max', 'oom', 'oom_kill')))}
    return {'elapsed_ns': elapsed, 'psi_total_us': psi, 'disk_delta': devices, 'cgroup_delta': cgroups, 'vm_delta': vm, 'cpu': ticks,
            'evidence': evidence,
            'attribution': 'coexisting signals, not exclusive causes; pgpgin/out KiB, pswpin/out pages; '
                           'pgmajfault is a count, not time; PSI full memory supplies stall time; '
                           'CPU full is undefined system-wide; diskstats retain per-device counters; '
                           'zram activity plus CPU PSI suggests overhead but cannot prove compression CPU cost'}


def admission(sample, previous=None, now=None):
    """Fail closed for telemetry ambiguity; pressure defers only future cooperative work."""
    now = time.monotonic_ns() if now is None else now
    if not 0 <= now - sample['read_start_ns'] <= 300_000_000:
        return Admission.STALE
    if sample['mem_available_kib'] is None or sample['mem_available_kib'] < 384 * 1024:
        return Admission.MEMORY
    cg = sample['effective_cgroup']
    if cg['status'] != 'resolved':
        return Admission.CGROUP
    for ancestor in cg['ancestors']:
        values = ancestor['values']
        current, maximum = values.get('memory.current'), values.get('memory.max')
        if current is None or maximum is None:
            return Admission.CGROUP_TELEMETRY
        if maximum.strip() != 'max':
            try:
                if int(maximum) - int(current) < 256 * 1024 * 1024:
                    return Admission.HEADROOM
            except ValueError:
                return Admission.CGROUP_TELEMETRY
    for kind in ('cpu', 'memory', 'io'):
        try:
            levels = ('some',) if kind == 'cpu' else ('some', 'full')
            for level in levels:
                full = sample['psi'][kind][level]
                if (not isinstance(full['total'], int) or full['total'] < 0 or
                    any(not isinstance(full[k], (int, float)) or not 0 <= full[k] <= 100
                        for k in ('avg10', 'avg60', 'avg300'))):
                    return Admission.PSI
                if previous and full['total'] < previous['psi'][kind][level]['total']:
                    return Admission.RESET
            fields = sample['psi'][kind]['some']
            if set(fields) != {'avg10', 'avg60', 'avg300', 'total'} or any(
                    not isinstance(fields[k], (int, float)) or not 0 <= fields[k] <= 100
                    for k in ('avg10', 'avg60', 'avg300')) or not isinstance(fields['total'], int) or fields['total'] < 0:
                return Admission.PSI
            if previous:
                old = previous['psi'][kind]['some']['total']
                if fields['total'] < old:
                    return Admission.RESET
        except (TypeError, KeyError):
            return Admission.PSI
    for key in ('pswpin', 'pswpout', 'oom_kill'):
        value = sample['vm'].get(key)
        if value is None:
            return Admission.VM
        if previous and (previous['vm'].get(key) is None or value < previous['vm'][key]):
            return Admission.RESET
        if previous and key == 'oom_kill' and value > previous['vm'][key]:
            return Admission.OOM
    if previous and (sample['effective_cgroup']['membership'] != previous['effective_cgroup']['membership'] or
                     sample['effective_cgroup']['device_inode'] != previous['effective_cgroup']['device_inode']):
        return Admission.IDENTITY
    if any(sample['psi'][k]['some']['avg10'] >= threshold for k, threshold in
           (('cpu', 15), ('memory', 2), ('io', 5))):
        return Admission.PRESSURE
    if previous:
        elapsed = sample['monotonic_ns'] - previous['monotonic_ns']
        if elapsed <= 0:
            return Admission.CLOCK
        if any((sample['psi'][k]['some']['total'] - previous['psi'][k]['some']['total']) * 1000 / elapsed >= threshold
               for k, threshold in (('cpu', .15), ('memory', .02), ('io', .05))):
            return Admission.PRESSURE
    return Admission.ADMITTED


def main():
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
    parser.add_argument('--environment', choices=('Codespaces', 'physical'), required=True)
    parser.add_argument('--label', required=True)
    parser.add_argument('--seconds', type=int, default=60)
    parser.add_argument('--output', type=Path, required=True)
    args = parser.parse_args()
    if not 1 <= args.seconds <= 600:
        parser.error('seconds must be 1..600')
    if os.environ.get('CODESPACES') == 'true' and args.environment != 'Codespaces':
        parser.error('Codespaces must be labelled Codespaces')
    global STOP
    def stop(_sig, _frame):
        global STOP
        STOP = True
    for sig in (signal.SIGINT, signal.SIGTERM, signal.SIGHUP):
        signal.signal(sig, stop)
    cpu = time.process_time_ns()
    first = last = None
    with args.output.open('x', buffering=1) as log:
        def emit(row):
            log.write(json.dumps(row, allow_nan=False) + '\n')
        emit({'type': 'context', **context(args.label, args.environment)})
        for _ in range(args.seconds * 4 + 1):
            if STOP:
                break
            last = snapshot()
            first = first or last
            emit({'type': 'sample', 'host': last})
            time.sleep(.25)
        emit({'type': 'end', 'status': 'cancelled' if STOP else 'complete',
              'diagnostic_cpu_ns': time.process_time_ns() - cpu,
              'window': window(first, last) if first and last else None})
        log.flush()
        os.fsync(log.fileno())
    return 1 if STOP else 0


if __name__ == '__main__':
    raise SystemExit(main())


FILE scripts/package-018e-diagnostic.py
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Build an auditable package from a clean tracked revision; no live service."""
import hashlib
from pathlib import Path
import shutil
import subprocess
import tarfile
import tempfile

root = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
subprocess.run(["git", "diff", "--exit-code", "HEAD"], cwd=root, check=True)
subprocess.run(["sh", "scripts/tests/test-018e-launcher.sh"], cwd=root, check=True)
subprocess.run(["cargo", "build", "--release", "--target", "x86_64-unknown-linux-musl",
                "-p", "xynoos-decision", "--example", "optimization_experiment"], cwd=root, check=True)
commit = subprocess.check_output(["git", "rev-parse", "HEAD"], cwd=root, text=True).strip()
name = "xynoos-018e-cleanup-mx-linux-x86_64"
archive = root / (name + ".tar.gz")
if archive.exists():
    raise SystemExit("Refusing to replace existing archive: " + str(archive))
with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(prefix="xynoos-018e-package-") as temp:
    package = Path(temp) / name
    package.mkdir()
    files = {
        "target/x86_64-unknown-linux-musl/release/examples/optimization_experiment": "optimization_experiment",
        "scripts/xynoos-018d-launcher.sh": "xynoos-018d-launcher.sh",
        "scripts/xynoos-unit-preflight.py": "xynoos-unit-preflight.py",
        "docs/reports/TASK-018E-CLEANUP-MX-README.md": "README",
        "docs/reports/TASK-018E-CLEANUP.md": "PROTOCOL.md",
        "docs/reports/TASK-018E-CLEANUP-GATES.txt": "QUALITY-GATES.txt",
        "LICENSE": "LICENSE",
    }
    for source, dest in files.items():
        shutil.copy2(root / source, package / dest)
    (package / "SOURCE_COMMIT").write_text(commit + "\n")
    subprocess.run(["git", "archive", "--format=tar.gz", "--output=" + str(package / "SOURCE.tar.gz"), commit], cwd=root, check=True)
    sums = []
    for path in sorted(package.iterdir()):
        sums.append(hashlib.sha256(path.read_bytes()).hexdigest() + "  " + path.name + "\n")
    (package / "SHA256SUMS").write_text("".join(sums))
    subprocess.run(["sha256sum", "-c", "SHA256SUMS"], cwd=package, check=True)
    with tarfile.open(archive, "w:gz") as tar:
        tar.add(package, arcname=name)
checksum = hashlib.sha256(archive.read_bytes()).hexdigest()
(root / (name + ".tar.gz.sha256")).write_text(checksum + "  " + archive.name + "\n")
print("SOURCE_COMMIT=" + commit)
print(checksum + "  " + archive.name)


FILE scripts/package-laptop.py
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Package committed static safe binaries, diagnostic and source admission experiment."""
import hashlib
import json
import os
from pathlib import Path
import shutil
import subprocess
import tarfile
import tempfile

from benchmark_desktop import make_corpus

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]


def sha(path):
    return hashlib.sha256(path.read_bytes()).hexdigest()


def main():
    subprocess.run(['git', 'diff', '--exit-code', 'HEAD'], cwd=ROOT, check=True)
    commit = subprocess.check_output(['git', 'rev-parse', 'HEAD'], cwd=ROOT, text=True).strip()
    tracked = subprocess.check_output(['git', 'ls-files'], cwd=ROOT, text=True).splitlines()
    sources = {
        'scripts/benchmark_desktop.py': 'benchmark_desktop.py',
        'scripts/benchmark_physical.py': 'benchmark_physical.py',
        'scripts/laptop_diagnostic.py': 'laptop_diagnostic.py',
        'scripts/inspect_desktop.py': 'inspect_desktop.py',
        'benchmarks/desktop-probe.html': 'desktop-probe.html',
        'docs/PHASE4-LAPTOP-N5000.md': 'README.md',
        'docs/PHASE4-LAPTOP-SAFETY.md': 'SAFETY.md',
        'docs/PHASE4-LAPTOP-RESEARCH.md': 'RESEARCH.md',
        'docs/reports/PHASE4-LAPTOP-RESULTS.md': 'RESULTS.md',
        'artifacts/phase4-laptop/quality-gates.txt': 'QUALITY-GATES.txt',
        'LICENSE': 'LICENSE',
    }
    required = set(sources) | {'scripts/package-laptop.py', 'crates/xynoos-engine/src/bin/xynoos-desktop.rs',
                               'scripts/tests/test_desktop.py'}
    if not required <= set(tracked):
        raise SystemExit('commit package source, documentation and quality evidence first')
    subprocess.run(['cargo', 'build', '--release', '--target', 'x86_64-unknown-linux-musl',
                    '-p', 'xynoos-engine', '--bin', 'xynoos-desktop', '-p', 'xynoos-cli', '--bin', 'xynoos-mvp'],
                   cwd=ROOT, check=True)
    names = ('xynoos-mvp', 'xynoos-desktop')
    for name in names:
        binary = ROOT / 'target/x86_64-unknown-linux-musl/release' / name
        program = subprocess.check_output(['readelf', '-l', str(binary)], text=True)
        dynamic = subprocess.check_output(['readelf', '-d', str(binary)], text=True)
        description = subprocess.check_output(['file', str(binary)], text=True)
        if 'INTERP' in program or 'NEEDED' in dynamic or not ('static' in description and 'x86-64' in description):
            raise SystemExit('expected static Linux x86_64 executable: ' + description)
        sources[str(binary.relative_to(ROOT))] = name
    destination = ROOT / 'artifacts/phase4-laptop' / commit[:12]
    destination.mkdir(exist_ok=False)
    name = 'xynoos-laptop-' + commit[:12] + '-linux-x86_64'
    archive = destination / (name + '.tar.gz')
    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(prefix='xynoos-laptop-package-') as temp:
        stage = Path(temp) / name
        stage.mkdir(mode=0o700)
        for source, target in sources.items():
            shutil.copy2(ROOT / source, stage / target)
            (stage / target).chmod(0o755 if target in names else 0o644)
        make_corpus(ROOT, stage / 'source-corpus.txt')
        (stage / 'SOURCE_COMMIT').write_text(commit + '\n')
        manifest = {'commit': commit, 'target': 'x86_64-unknown-linux-musl',
                    'rustc': subprocess.check_output(['rustc', '-Vv'], text=True),
                    'cargo': subprocess.check_output(['cargo', '-V'], text=True).strip(),
                    'rustflags': os.environ.get('RUSTFLAGS'),
                    'source_sha256': {p: sha(ROOT / p) for p in tracked if
                        p.startswith(('crates/', 'scripts/', 'benchmarks/')) or p in ('Cargo.toml', 'Cargo.lock')},
                    'build_commands': ['cargo build --release --target x86_64-unknown-linux-musl -p xynoos-engine --bin xynoos-desktop -p xynoos-cli --bin xynoos-mvp']}
        (stage / 'SOURCE_MANIFEST.json').write_text(json.dumps(manifest, indent=2) + '\n')
        subprocess.run(['git', 'archive', '--format=tar.gz', '--output=' + str(stage / 'SOURCE.tar.gz'), commit], cwd=ROOT, check=True)
        (stage / 'SHA256SUMS').write_text(''.join(sha(p) + '  ' + p.name + '\n' for p in sorted(stage.iterdir())))
        subprocess.run(['sha256sum', '-c', 'SHA256SUMS'], cwd=stage, check=True)
        for mode in ('observe', 'recovery'):
            subprocess.run([str(stage / 'xynoos-mvp'), mode], stdout=subprocess.DEVNULL, check=True, timeout=10)
        for argv in (['controlled'], ['controlled', '--authorize']):
            if subprocess.run([str(stage / 'xynoos-mvp'), *argv], capture_output=True, timeout=5).returncode == 0:
                raise SystemExit('unsafe MVP entrypoint admitted')
        subprocess.run(['python3', '-B', str(stage / 'laptop_diagnostic.py'), '--environment', 'Codespaces',
                        '--label', 'staged package diagnostic smoke; Codespaces only', '--seconds', '1',
                        '--output', str(destination / 'diagnostic-smoke.jsonl')], check=True, timeout=10)
        subprocess.run(['python3', '-B', str(stage / 'benchmark_desktop.py'), '--worker', str(stage / 'xynoos-desktop'),
                        '--observer', str(stage / 'xynoos-mvp'), '--corpus', str(stage / 'source-corpus.txt'),
                        '--environment', 'Codespaces', '--label', 'staged static package smoke; Codespaces only',
                        '--arms', 'all', '--authorize-disposable-index-admission', '--requests', '16',
                        '--interval-ms', '5', '--index-passes', '1', '--output', str(destination / 'package-smoke')],
                       stdout=subprocess.DEVNULL, check=True, timeout=45)
        inspected = subprocess.check_output(['python3', '-B', str(stage / 'inspect_desktop.py'),
                                            str(destination / 'package-smoke/events.jsonl')], timeout=5)
        report = json.loads(inspected)
        if report['status'] != 'complete':
            raise SystemExit('staged package smoke incomplete')
        (destination / 'package-inspection.json').write_bytes(inspected)
        with tarfile.open(archive, 'x:gz') as tar:
            tar.add(stage, arcname=name)
        shutil.copy2(stage / 'SOURCE_MANIFEST.json', destination / 'PACKAGE-SOURCE-MANIFEST.json')
    digest = sha(archive)
    archive.with_suffix('.gz.sha256').write_text(digest + '  ' + archive.name + '\n')
    metadata = {'source_commit': commit, 'archive': str(archive.relative_to(ROOT)), 'sha256': digest,
                'physical_kernel_mutation_available': False,
                'active_scope': 'explicitly authorized admission of new chunks to owned disposable source indexer only',
                'requirements': 'Linux x86_64, Python 3.9+, procfs/PSI/cgroup v2, pidfd, memfd seals; no Rust or sudo',
                'measurements': 'Codespaces only; no N5000 desktop benefit established'}
    (destination / 'PACKAGE.json').write_text(json.dumps(metadata, indent=2) + '\n')
    print(json.dumps(metadata, indent=2))


if __name__ == '__main__':
    main()


FILE scripts/package-mvp.py
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Package one committed revision with only the safe static MVP entry point."""
import hashlib
from pathlib import Path
import shutil
import subprocess
import tarfile
import tempfile

root = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
subprocess.run(["git", "diff", "--exit-code", "HEAD"], cwd=root, check=True)
subprocess.run(["cargo", "build", "-p", "xynoos-cli", "--bin", "xynoos-mvp", "--release",
                "--target", "x86_64-unknown-linux-musl"], cwd=root, check=True)
commit = subprocess.check_output(["git", "rev-parse", "HEAD"], cwd=root, text=True).strip()
tracked = subprocess.check_output(["git", "ls-files"], cwd=root, text=True).splitlines()
required = ["crates/xynoos-engine/src/lib.rs", "crates/xynoos-cli/src/bin/xynoos-mvp.rs",
            "docs/MVP-SAFETY-RECOVERY.md", "docs/MVP-N5000-VALIDATION.md"]
if not all(path in tracked for path in required):
    raise SystemExit("MVP sources/docs must be committed before packaging")
name = "xynoos-mvp-" + commit[:12] + "-linux-x86_64"
archive = root / "artifacts/mvp" / (name + ".tar.gz")
if archive.exists():
    raise SystemExit("Refusing to replace package: " + str(archive))
with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(prefix="xynoos-mvp-package-") as temp:
    package = Path(temp) / name
    package.mkdir()
    files = {
        "target/x86_64-unknown-linux-musl/release/xynoos-mvp": "xynoos-mvp",
        "docs/MVP-QUICKSTART.md": "README.md",
        "docs/MVP-ARCHITECTURE.md": "ARCHITECTURE.md",
        "docs/MVP-SAFETY-RECOVERY.md": "SAFETY-RECOVERY.md",
        "docs/MVP-BENCHMARK.md": "BENCHMARK.md",
        "docs/MVP-N5000-VALIDATION.md": "N5000-VALIDATION.md",
        "docs/reports/MVP-RESULTS.md": "RESULTS.md",
        "docs/reports/MVP-QUALITY-GATES.txt": "QUALITY-GATES.txt",
        "artifacts/mvp/benchmark-final.summary.json": "BENCHMARK-SUMMARY.json",
        "fixtures/mvp-trace.json": "TRACE.json",
        "LICENSE": "LICENSE",
    }
    for source, destination in files.items():
        shutil.copy2(root / source, package / destination)
    (package / "SOURCE_COMMIT").write_text(commit + "\n")
    subprocess.run(["git", "archive", "--format=tar.gz", "--output=" + str(package / "SOURCE.tar.gz"), commit], cwd=root, check=True)
    sums = [hashlib.sha256(path.read_bytes()).hexdigest() + "  " + path.name + "\n" for path in sorted(package.iterdir())]
    (package / "SHA256SUMS").write_text("".join(sums))
    subprocess.run(["sha256sum", "-c", "SHA256SUMS"], cwd=package, check=True)
    with tarfile.open(archive, "x:gz") as tar:
        tar.add(package, arcname=name)
checksum = hashlib.sha256(archive.read_bytes()).hexdigest()
archive.with_suffix(archive.suffix + ".sha256").write_text(checksum + "  " + archive.name + "\n")
print("SOURCE_COMMIT=" + commit)
print(checksum + "  " + str(archive.relative_to(root)))


FILE scripts/package-owned.py
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Package committed static safe entry points and reviewed cooperative experiment."""
import hashlib
import json
import os
from pathlib import Path
import shutil
import subprocess
import tarfile
import tempfile

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]


def sha256(path):
    return hashlib.sha256(path.read_bytes()).hexdigest()


def main():
    subprocess.run(['git', 'diff', '--exit-code', 'HEAD'], cwd=ROOT, check=True)
    commit = subprocess.check_output(['git', 'rev-parse', 'HEAD'], cwd=ROOT, text=True).strip()
    files = {
        'target/x86_64-unknown-linux-musl/release/xynoos-mvp': 'xynoos-mvp',
        'target/x86_64-unknown-linux-musl/release/xynoos-perf': 'xynoos-perf',
        'scripts/benchmark_physical.py': 'benchmark_physical.py',
        'target/x86_64-unknown-linux-musl/release/xynoos-owned': 'xynoos-owned',
        'scripts/benchmark_owned.py': 'benchmark_owned.py',
        'scripts/inspect_owned.py': 'inspect_owned.py',
        'scripts/inspect_physical.py': 'inspect_physical.py',
        'docs/PHASE4-N5000.md': 'BASELINE-N5000.md',
        'docs/PHASE4-OWNED-N5000.md': 'README.md',
        'docs/reports/PHASE4-OWNED-PERFORMANCE.md': 'PERFORMANCE.md',
        'docs/reports/PHASE4-OWNED-SAFETY-REVIEW.md': 'SAFETY-REVIEW.md',
        'docs/MVP-SAFETY-RECOVERY.md': 'SAFETY-RECOVERY.md',
        'docs/reports/PHASE4-OWNED-QUALITY-GATES.txt': 'QUALITY-GATES.txt',
        'LICENSE': 'LICENSE',
    }
    tracked = set(subprocess.check_output(['git', 'ls-files'], cwd=ROOT, text=True).splitlines())
    required = {p for p in files if not p.startswith('target/')} | {
        'crates/xynoos-engine/src/bin/xynoos-perf.rs', 'crates/xynoos-engine/src/observation.rs',
        'scripts/package-owned.py', 'crates/xynoos-engine/src/owned.rs',
        'crates/xynoos-engine/src/bin/xynoos-owned.rs', 'crates/xynoos-aura/src/cooperative.rs'}
    if not required <= tracked:
        raise SystemExit('commit all package sources and documents first')
    subprocess.run(['cargo', 'build', '--locked', '--release', '--target', 'x86_64-unknown-linux-musl',
                    '-p', 'xynoos-cli', '--bin', 'xynoos-mvp', '-p', 'xynoos-engine', '--bin', 'xynoos-perf', '--bin', 'xynoos-owned'], cwd=ROOT, check=True)
    for name in ('xynoos-mvp', 'xynoos-perf', 'xynoos-owned'):
        binary = ROOT/'target/x86_64-unknown-linux-musl/release'/name
        headers = subprocess.check_output(['readelf', '-l', str(binary)], text=True)
        description = subprocess.check_output(['file', str(binary)], text=True)
        if 'INTERP' in headers or not ('static' in description and 'x86-64' in description):
            raise SystemExit('expected static Linux x86_64 executable: '+description)
    name = 'xynoos-owned-'+commit[:12]+'-linux-x86_64'
    archive = ROOT/'artifacts/phase4-owned'/(name+'.tar.gz')
    archive.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    if archive.exists():
        raise SystemExit('refusing to overwrite existing package')
    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(prefix='xynoos-phase4-package-') as temp:
        package = Path(temp)/name
        package.mkdir()
        for source, destination in files.items():
            shutil.copy2(ROOT/source, package/destination)
        (package/'SOURCE_COMMIT').write_text(commit+'\n')
        source_paths = sorted(p for p in tracked if p.startswith(('crates/', 'scripts/')) or p in ('Cargo.toml', 'Cargo.lock'))
        (package/'SOURCE_MANIFEST.json').write_text(json.dumps({'commit': commit,
            'rustc': subprocess.check_output(['rustc', '-Vv'], text=True),
            'cargo': subprocess.check_output(['cargo', '-V'], text=True).strip(),
            'build_target': 'x86_64-unknown-linux-musl',
            'rustflags': os.environ.get('RUSTFLAGS'),
            'source_sha256': {p: sha256(ROOT/p) for p in source_paths}}, indent=2)+'\n')
        subprocess.run(['git', 'archive', '--format=tar.gz', '--output='+str(package/'SOURCE.tar.gz'), commit], cwd=ROOT, check=True)
        (package/'SHA256SUMS').write_text(''.join(sha256(p)+'  '+p.name+'\n' for p in sorted(package.iterdir())))
        subprocess.run(['sha256sum', '-c', 'SHA256SUMS'], cwd=package, check=True)
        # Validate the actual distribution before archiving it.
        for command in ([str(package/'xynoos-mvp'), 'observe'], [str(package/'xynoos-mvp'), 'recovery'],
                        [str(package/'xynoos-perf'), 'profile']):
            subprocess.run(command, stdout=subprocess.DEVNULL, check=True, timeout=15)
        refused = subprocess.run([str(package/'xynoos-mvp'), 'controlled', '--authorize'], capture_output=True)
        if refused.returncode == 0:
            raise SystemExit('distribution reached controlled mode')
        subprocess.run(['python3', '-B', str(package/'benchmark_owned.py'), '--worker', str(package/'xynoos-owned'),
                        '--environment', 'Codespaces', '--label', 'staged static package smoke, Codespaces only',
                        '--authorize-owned', '--repeats', '6', '--requests', '16', '--interval-ms', '0',
                        '--output', str(Path(temp)/'smoke')], stdout=subprocess.DEVNULL, check=True, timeout=30)
        inspection = json.loads(subprocess.check_output(
            ['python3', '-B', str(package/'inspect_owned.py'), str(Path(temp)/'smoke/events.jsonl')], timeout=5))
        if inspection['status'] != 'complete':
            raise SystemExit('package smoke evidence incomplete')
        (Path(temp)/'smoke/inspection.json').write_text(json.dumps(inspection, indent=2)+'\n')
        shutil.copytree(Path(temp)/'smoke', ROOT/'artifacts/phase4-owned'/('package-smoke-'+commit[:12]))
        with tarfile.open(archive, 'x:gz') as tar:
            tar.add(package, arcname=name)
    digest = sha256(archive)
    archive.with_suffix(archive.suffix+'.sha256').write_text(digest+'  '+archive.name+'\n')
    metadata = {'source_commit': commit, 'archive': str(archive.relative_to(ROOT)), 'sha256': digest,
                'safe_entrypoints': ['xynoos-mvp observe|simulate|recovery', 'xynoos-perf profile|workload', 'xynoos-owned baseline|observe mixed|entropy', 'xynoos-owned active mixed|entropy --authorize-owned'],
                'physical_mutation_available': False, 'active_optimization_available': True, 'active_scope': 'owned disposable 16 MiB corpus only; no kernel-control path',
                'measurements': 'Codespaces only; N5000 results pending'}
    with (ROOT/'artifacts/phase4-owned/PACKAGE.json').open('x') as f:
        json.dump(metadata, f, indent=2)
        f.write('\n')
    print(json.dumps(metadata, indent=2))


if __name__ == '__main__':
    main()


FILE scripts/package-phase4.py
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Package committed safe static MVP + fixed owned workload + stdlib A/B tools."""
import hashlib
import json
from pathlib import Path
import shutil
import subprocess
import tarfile
import tempfile

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]


def sha256(path):
    return hashlib.sha256(path.read_bytes()).hexdigest()

        raise SystemExit('commit all package sources and documents first')

        (package/'SOURCE_MANIFEST.json').write_text(json.dumps({'commit': commit,
            'source_sha256': {p: sha256(ROOT/p) for p in source_paths}}, indent=2)+'\n')
        subprocess.run(['git', 'archive', '--format=tar.gz', '--output='+str(package/'SOURCE.tar.gz'), commit], cwd=ROOT, check=True)
        (package/'SHA256SUMS').write_text(''.join(sha256(p)+'  '+p.name+'\n' for p in sorted(package.iterdir())))
        subprocess.run(['sha256sum', '-c', 'SHA256SUMS'], cwd=package, check=True)
        # Validate the actual distribution before archiving it.
        for command in ([str(package/'xynoos-mvp'), 'observe'], [str(package/'xynoos-mvp'), 'recovery'],

            subprocess.run(command, stdout=subprocess.DEVNULL, check=True, timeout=15)
        refused = subprocess.run([str(package/'xynoos-mvp'), 'controlled', '--authorize'], capture_output=True)
        if refused.returncode == 0:
            raise SystemExit('distribution reached controlled mode')
        with tarfile.open(archive, 'x:gz') as tar:
            tar.add(package, arcname=name)
    digest = sha256(archive)
    archive.with_suffix(archive.suffix+'.sha256').write_text(digest+'  '+archive.name+'\n')
    metadata = {'source_commit': commit, 'archive': str(archive.relative_to(ROOT)), 'sha256': digest,
                'safe_entrypoints': ['xynoos-mvp observe|simulate|recovery', 'xynoos-perf profile|workload'],
                'physical_mutation_available': False, 'active_optimization_available': False,
                'measurements': 'Codespaces only; N5000 results pending'}
    with (ROOT/'artifacts/phase4/PACKAGE.json').open('x') as f:
        json.dump(metadata, f, indent=2)
        f.write('\n')
    print(json.dumps(metadata, indent=2))


if __name__ == '__main__':
    main()


FILE scripts/quality_mvp.py
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Run the MVP gates sequentially and preserve complete command output."""
import datetime
import argparse
import os
from pathlib import Path
import subprocess
import sys

root = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
parser.add_argument("--output", type=Path, default=root / "docs/reports/MVP-QUALITY-GATES.txt")
args = parser.parse_args()
log_path = args.output
commands = [
    ["cargo", "fmt", "--all", "--", "--check"],
    ["cargo", "clippy", "--workspace", "--all-targets", "--", "-D", "warnings"],
    ["cargo", "check", "--workspace", "--all-targets"],
    ["cargo", "test", "--workspace", "--all-targets"],
    ["cargo", "test", "--workspace", "--doc"],
    ["cargo", "build", "--release", "-p", "xynoos-engine", "--bin", "xynoos-perf", "--bin", "xynoos-owned", "--bin", "xynoos-desktop", "-p", "xynoos-cli", "--bin", "xynoos-mvp"],
    ["chmod", "go-w", "target/release/xynoos-desktop", "target/release/xynoos-mvp"],
    ["python3", "-m", "unittest", "discover", "-s", "scripts/tests", "-p", "test_*.py", "-v"],
    ["cargo", "test", "-p", "xynoos-decision", "--example", "optimization_experiment"],
    ["cargo", "build", "--workspace", "--release"],
    ["cargo", "doc", "--workspace", "--no-deps"],
    ["sh", "scripts/tests/test-018e-launcher.sh"],
    ["cargo", "build", "-p", "xynoos-cli", "--bin", "xynoos-mvp", "--release", "--target", "x86_64-unknown-linux-musl"],
    ["cargo", "build", "-p", "xynoos-engine", "--bin", "xynoos-perf", "--bin", "xynoos-owned", "--bin", "xynoos-desktop", "--release", "--target", "x86_64-unknown-linux-musl"],
    ["file", "target/x86_64-unknown-linux-musl/release/xynoos-mvp", "target/x86_64-unknown-linux-musl/release/xynoos-perf", "target/x86_64-unknown-linux-musl/release/xynoos-owned", "target/x86_64-unknown-linux-musl/release/xynoos-desktop"],
    ["git", "diff", "--check"],
]
with log_path.open("x") as log:
    log.write("MVP gates " + datetime.datetime.now(datetime.timezone.utc).isoformat() + "\n")
    for command in commands:
        env = dict(os.environ)
        if command[:2] == ["cargo", "doc"]:
            env["RUSTDOCFLAGS"] = "-D warnings"
        log.write("\n$ " + ("RUSTDOCFLAGS='-D warnings' " if command[:2] == ["cargo", "doc"] else "") + " ".join(command) + "\n")
        log.flush()
        result = subprocess.run(command, cwd=root, env=env, stdout=log, stderr=subprocess.STDOUT)
        log.write("exit=" + str(result.returncode) + "\n")
        log.flush()
        print("exit=" + str(result.returncode) + " " + " ".join(command), flush=True)
        if result.returncode:
            print("Failure preserved in " + str(log_path), file=sys.stderr)
            sys.exit(result.returncode)


FILE scripts/summarize_task003.py
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Ringkas sampel batch TASK 003 yang dihasilkan xynoos-bench."""
import csv
import statistics
from collections import defaultdict
from pathlib import Path

root = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent / "docs" / "benchmarks"
groups = defaultdict(list)
with (root / "task-003-raw.csv").open(newline="") as source:
    for row in csv.DictReader(source):
        groups[(row["dataset"], row["backend"])].append(row)
fields = ["dataset", "backend", "insert_median_ns", "insert_p95_batch_ns", "get_median_ns", "get_p95_batch_ns", "compress_mib_s", "decompress_mib_s", "ratio", "payload_bytes", "capacity_bytes", "metadata_bytes", "raw_pages", "compressed_pages", "rss_before_kib", "rss_loaded_kib", "peak_kib", "cpu_ms_total"]
with (root / "task-003-summary.csv").open("w", newline="") as output:
    writer = csv.DictWriter(output, fieldnames=fields)
    writer.writeheader()
    for (dataset, backend), rows in groups.items():
        def median(key):
            return round(statistics.median(float(row[key]) for row in rows), 2)
        def p95(key):
            values = sorted(float(row[key]) for row in rows)
            return values[(95 * len(values) + 99) // 100 - 1]
        last = rows[-1]
        writer.writerow(dict(dataset=dataset, backend=backend, insert_median_ns=median("insert_batch_ns_per_page"), insert_p95_batch_ns=p95("insert_batch_ns_per_page"), get_median_ns=median("get_batch_ns_per_page"), get_p95_batch_ns=p95("get_batch_ns_per_page"), compress_mib_s=median("codec_compress_mib_s"), decompress_mib_s=median("codec_decompress_mib_s"), ratio=round(int(last["payload_bytes"]) / 1048576, 6), payload_bytes=last["payload_bytes"], capacity_bytes=last["capacity_bytes"], metadata_bytes=last["metadata_bytes"], raw_pages=last["raw_pages"], compressed_pages=last["compressed_pages"], rss_before_kib=last["rss_before_kib"], rss_loaded_kib=last["rss_loaded_kib"], peak_kib=last["peak_kib"], cpu_ms_total=round(int(last["cpu_ns"]) / 1e6, 2)))


FILE scripts/summarize_task004.py
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Memvalidasi CSV dan merangkum metrik simulasi; waktu nyata dipisahkan."""
import csv
from collections import defaultdict
from pathlib import Path
root = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent / 'docs' / 'benchmarks'
rows = list(csv.DictReader((root / 'task-004-raw.csv').open()))
assert len(rows) == 81
assert len({(r['trace'], r['seed'], r['policy']) for r in rows}) == 81
keys = [k for k in rows[0] if k not in ['trace', 'seed', 'policy', 'profile_bytes']]
groups = defaultdict(list)
for row in rows:
    assert int(row['migrations']) <= 512
    assert int(row['peak_ram_bytes']) <= 65536
    assert int(row['peak_compressed_bytes']) <= 16384
    assert int(row['peak_backing_bytes']) <= 131072
    groups[(row['trace'], row['policy'])].append(row)
with (root / 'task-004-summary.csv').open('w', newline='') as out:
    writer = csv.DictWriter(out, fieldnames=['trace', 'policy'] + keys)
    writer.writeheader()
    for (trace, policy), samples in groups.items():
        writer.writerow({'trace': trace, 'policy': policy, **{k: round(sum(int(r[k]) for r in samples) / len(samples), 2) for k in keys}})

from pathlib import Path
from collections import defaultdict
root=Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent/'docs'/'benchmarks'
rows=list(csv.DictReader((root/'task-006-final-raw.csv').open()))
assert len(rows)==567

assert len(by)==567
for r in rows:
    assert int(r['physical_bytes'])==int(r['metadata'])+int(r['resident_raw'])+int(r['resident_compressed'])
    assert int(r['peak_total'])>=int(r['physical_bytes'])

        other=by[(r['trace'],r['seed'],r['policy'],'bounded')]
        assert all(r[k]==other[k] for k in r if k not in ['selector','event_ns'])
