with (root/'task-006-final-summary.csv').open('w',newline='') as f:
    writer=csv.DictWriter(f,fieldnames=['trace','policy','selector']+keys);writer.writeheader()
    for (trace,policy,selector),v in groups.items():writer.writerow(dict(trace=trace,policy=policy,selector=selector,**{k:round(sum(int(x[k]) for x in v)/3,2) for k in keys}))


FILE scripts/task-014-measure.py
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Eksperimen berbatas; CSV hanya berisi metrik agregat sistem."""
import csv, os, statistics, subprocess, tempfile, time, threading
from pathlib import Path
root = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent
binary = root / 'target/release/xynoos-cli'
out = root / 'docs/benchmarks'
cmd = [str(binary), 'system', 'sample', '--count', '5', '--interval-ms', '200']
for _ in range(2):
    subprocess.run([str(binary), 'system', 'snapshot'], stdout=subprocess.DEVNULL, check=True, timeout=10)
rows = []
runs = []
for run in range(5):
    start = time.monotonic_ns()
    with tempfile.TemporaryFile(mode='w+') as output, tempfile.TemporaryFile(mode='w+') as errors:
        proc = subprocess.Popen(cmd, stdout=output, stderr=errors)
        watchdog = threading.Timer(10, proc.kill)
        watchdog.start()
        if run == 4:
            time.sleep(.15)
            # Workload 150 ms CPU, 4 MiB RAM, 1 MiB I/O; file selalu dibersihkan.
            worker = '''import time,tempfile,os\nfrom pathlib import Path\np=Path('.')\nt=time.monotonic()+.15\nwhile time.monotonic()<t: pass\nb=bytearray(4*1024*1024)\nwith tempfile.NamedTemporaryFile(dir=p) as f:\n f.write(b[:1024*1024]);f.flush();os.fsync(f.fileno())\n'''
            subprocess.run(['python3', '-c', worker], cwd=root, check=True, timeout=5)
        _, status, usage = os.wait4(proc.pid, 0)
        watchdog.cancel()
        proc.returncode = os.waitstatus_to_exitcode(status)
        if proc.returncode:
            errors.seek(0)
            raise RuntimeError(errors.read())
        output.seek(0)
        data = list(csv.DictReader(output))
    elapsed = time.monotonic_ns() - start
    for sample in data:
        sample['run'] = str(run)
        sample['workload'] = 'bounded' if run == 4 else 'idle'
        rows.append(sample)
    runs.append((run,elapsed, usage.ru_utime + usage.ru_stime, usage.ru_maxrss))
with (out/'task-014-raw.csv').open('w', newline='') as f:
    w=csv.DictWriter(f,fieldnames=['run','workload']+list(rows[0].keys())[:-2],lineterminator='\n');w.writeheader();w.writerows(rows)
with (out/'task-014-summary.csv').open('w',newline='') as f:
    w=csv.writer(f,lineterminator="\n");w.writerow(['run','workload','wall_ns','collector_cpu_seconds','collector_peak_rss_kib','median_snapshot_ns','max_snapshot_ns','samples'])
    for run,wall,cpu,rss in runs:
        values=[int(x['snapshot_ns']) for x in rows if int(x['run'])==run]
        w.writerow([run,'bounded' if run==4 else 'idle',wall,f'{cpu:.6f}',rss,int(statistics.median(values)),max(values),len(values)])
print('rows',len(rows),'median_snapshot_ns',statistics.median(int(x['snapshot_ns']) for x in rows),'idle_collector_cpu_s',sum(x[2] for x in runs[:4]),'collector_peak_rss_kib',max(x[3] for x in runs),'csv_bytes',(out/'task-014-raw.csv').stat().st_size)


FILE scripts/tests/test_desktop.py
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Adversarial admission, identity, lifecycle and independent real-task oracles."""
import argparse
import collections
import json
import os
from pathlib import Path
import re
import selectors
import signal
import subprocess
import sys
import tempfile
import time
import unittest
from unittest.mock import patch
import zlib

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2]
sys.path.insert(0, str(ROOT / 'scripts'))
import benchmark_desktop as b
import laptop_diagnostic as d
from inspect_desktop import inspect


class DesktopTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def setUp(self):
        b.STOP = None
        self.temp = tempfile.TemporaryDirectory()
        self.path = Path(self.temp.name)
        self.corpus = self.path / 'source.txt'
        self.corpus.write_bytes((b'memory cgroup pressure Result Some memory\n' * 2000) + b'end')
        self.fd, self.data = b.sealed_corpus(self.corpus)
        self.args = argparse.Namespace(worker=ROOT / 'target/release/xynoos-desktop',
            observer=ROOT / 'target/release/xynoos-mvp', requests=16, interval_ms=5,
            index_passes=2, timeout=5)
        self.rows = []

    def tearDown(self):
        os.close(self.fd)
        self.temp.cleanup()
        b.STOP = None

    def run_arm(self, arm='active'):
        row = b.run_arm(arm, self.args, self.fd, self.data, self.rows.append, 0)
        self.assertTrue(all(c['reaped'] for c in row['children']), row)
        return row

    def test_real_tasks_match_independent_whole_corpus_index(self):
        row = self.run_arm()
        self.assertEqual(row['status'], 'complete', row['error'])
        words = collections.Counter(w.lower() for w in re.findall(rb'[a-zA-Z0-9]+', self.data))
        crc = 0
        for word, count in sorted(words.items()):
            crc = zlib.crc32(word + b'\0' + count.to_bytes(8, 'little'), crc)
        self.assertEqual(row['index_results'][0]['index_crc32'], crc)
        self.assertEqual(row['search_latency_ns']['count'], 16)
        self.assertTrue(any(r['type'] == 'admission' and r['outcome'] == 'deferred_foreground' for r in self.rows))
        self.assertTrue(all(c['final']['released'] for c in row['children'] if c['name'] != 'observer'))

    def test_matched_three_arms_equal_work_and_observer_contract(self):
        rows = [self.run_arm(arm) for arm in b.ARMS]
        self.assertTrue(all(r['status'] == 'complete' for r in rows), rows)
        self.assertEqual(len({json.dumps((r['search_results'], r['index_results'])) for r in rows}), 1)
        self.assertEqual(rows[0]['observations'], 0)
        self.assertGreater(rows[1]['observations'], 0)
        self.assertFalse(b.summarize(rows)['desktop_improvement_established'])

    def test_sealed_corpus_cannot_be_modified(self):
        with self.assertRaises(PermissionError):
            os.write(self.fd, b'x')
        self.assertEqual(os.pread(self.fd, len(self.data), 0), self.data)

    def test_missing_memory_refuses_before_children(self):
        sample = d.snapshot()
        sample['mem_available_kib'] = None
        with patch.object(d, 'snapshot', return_value=sample):
            row = self.run_arm()
        self.assertEqual(row['status'], 'incomplete')
        self.assertEqual(row['children'], [])

    def test_low_memory_after_launch_stops_all_children(self):
        sample = d.snapshot()
        low = dict(sample, mem_available_kib=0)
        with patch.object(d, 'snapshot', side_effect=[sample, sample, low]):
            row = self.run_arm()
        self.assertEqual(row['status'], 'incomplete')
        self.assertEqual(len(row['children']), 3)

    def test_admission_missing_reset_stale_pressure_and_oom(self):
        for kind in ('cpu', 'memory', 'io'):
            sample = d.snapshot()
            sample['psi'][kind] = None
            self.assertEqual(d.admission(sample), 'refused_psi_unavailable')
        sample = d.snapshot()
        self.assertEqual(d.admission(sample, now=sample['read_start_ns'] + 300_000_001), 'refused_stale_telemetry')
        before = d.snapshot()
        after = d.snapshot()
        after['psi']['cpu']['some']['total'] = before['psi']['cpu']['some']['total'] - 1
        self.assertEqual(d.admission(after, before), 'refused_counter_reset')
        after = d.snapshot()
        after['psi']['memory']['some']['avg10'] = 3
        self.assertEqual(d.admission(after), 'deferred_pressure')
        after = d.snapshot()
        after['vm']['oom_kill'] = before['vm']['oom_kill'] + 1
        self.assertEqual(d.admission(after, before), 'refused_oom_observed')
        after = d.snapshot()
        after['effective_cgroup']['ancestors'][0]['values']['memory.max'] = '1'
        self.assertEqual(d.admission(after), 'refused_cgroup_headroom')

    def test_nan_psi_and_cgroup_change_refuse(self):
        sample = d.snapshot()
        sample['psi']['cpu']['some']['avg10'] = float('nan')
        self.assertEqual(d.admission(sample), 'refused_psi_unavailable')
        before, after = d.snapshot(), d.snapshot()
        after['effective_cgroup']['device_inode'] = [0, 0]
        self.assertEqual(d.admission(after, before), 'refused_cgroup_changed')

    def test_identity_mismatch_stops_admission_and_reaps_exact_child(self):
        with selectors.DefaultSelector() as selector:
            child = b.OwnedChild(self.args.worker, 'search', self.fd, selector)
            try:
                child.initial_identity = dict(child.initial_identity, start_ticks=0)
                with self.assertRaisesRegex(RuntimeError, 'identity'):
                    child.send('work')
            finally:
                result = child.stop()
            self.assertTrue(result['reaped'])

    def test_group_writable_binary_rejected(self):
        binary = self.path / 'worker'
        binary.write_bytes(self.args.worker.read_bytes())
        binary.chmod(0o775)
        with selectors.DefaultSelector() as selector:
            with self.assertRaisesRegex(RuntimeError, 'caller-owned'):
                b.OwnedChild(binary, 'search', self.fd, selector)

    def test_cancel_before_launch_and_ambiguous_protocol_stop(self):
        b.STOP = 'SIGINT'
        row = self.run_arm()
        self.assertEqual(row['status'], 'incomplete')
        self.assertTrue(all(c['reaped'] for c in row['children']))
        b.STOP = None
        self.args.worker = ROOT / 'target/release/xynoos-perf'
        row = self.run_arm()
        self.assertEqual(row['status'], 'incomplete')

    def test_worker_input_bound_and_parent_eof(self):
        for payload in (b'not-an-operation\n', b'x' * 1000):
            result = subprocess.run([self.args.worker, 'index', '--corpus-fd', str(self.fd)],
                pass_fds=(self.fd,), input=payload, capture_output=True, timeout=2)
            self.assertNotEqual(result.returncode, 0)
        result = subprocess.run([self.args.worker, 'index', '--corpus-fd', str(self.fd)],
            pass_fds=(self.fd,), input=b'', capture_output=True, timeout=2)
        self.assertEqual(result.returncode, 0)
        self.assertTrue(json.loads(result.stdout.splitlines()[-1])['released'])

    def test_truncated_logs_do_not_certify_cleanup(self):
        path = self.path / 'partial.jsonl'
        content = (json.dumps({'type': 'manifest', 'configuration': {'repeats': 6}, 'environment': 'physical'}) + '\n' +
            json.dumps({'type': 'run_start', 'repeat': 0, 'arm': 'active'}) + '\n{"type":')
        path.write_text(content)
        row = inspect(path)
        self.assertEqual(row['status'], 'partial')
        self.assertTrue(row['truncated_final_row'])
        self.assertEqual(row['unresolved_cleanup_unknown'], [(0, 'active')])
        self.assertEqual(path.read_text(), content)
        path.write_text('{broken}\n')
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError):
            inspect(path)

    def test_signal_preserves_incomplete_evidence_and_reaps(self):
        command = [sys.executable, str(ROOT / 'scripts/benchmark_desktop.py'), '--worker', str(self.args.worker),
            '--observer', str(self.args.observer), '--corpus', str(self.corpus), '--environment', 'Codespaces',
            '--label', 'signal fixture', '--output', str(self.path / 'run'), '--requests', '1024',
            '--interval-ms', '20', '--authorize-disposable-index-admission']
        with subprocess.Popen(command, stdout=subprocess.DEVNULL, stderr=subprocess.PIPE) as proc:
            events = self.path / 'run/events.jsonl'
            deadline = time.monotonic() + 5
            while time.monotonic() < deadline:
                if events.exists() and '"type":"task"' in events.read_text():
                    break
                if proc.poll() is not None:
                    self.fail(proc.stderr.read().decode())
                time.sleep(.01)
            else:
                proc.kill()
                self.fail('no tasks before signal deadline')
            proc.send_signal(signal.SIGTERM)
            proc.communicate(timeout=5)
            self.assertEqual(proc.returncode, 1)
        report = inspect(events)
        self.assertEqual(report['status'], 'partial')
        rows = [json.loads(line) for line in events.read_text().splitlines()]
        self.assertTrue(all(c['reaped'] for r in rows if r['type'] == 'run' for c in r['children']))

    def test_parent_sigkill_leaves_unknown_log_but_no_live_owned_workers(self):
        command = [sys.executable, str(ROOT / 'scripts/benchmark_desktop.py'), '--worker', str(self.args.worker),
            '--observer', str(self.args.observer), '--corpus', str(self.corpus), '--environment', 'Codespaces',
            '--label', 'parent crash fixture', '--output', str(self.path / 'crash'), '--requests', '1024',
            '--interval-ms', '20', '--authorize-disposable-index-admission']
        children = []
        with subprocess.Popen(command, stdout=subprocess.DEVNULL, stderr=subprocess.PIPE) as proc:
            events = self.path / 'crash/events.jsonl'
            deadline = time.monotonic() + 5
            while time.monotonic() < deadline:
                if events.exists():
                    raw = events.read_text()
                    if '"type":"task"' in raw:
                        children = [json.loads(line)['identity'] for line in raw.splitlines()
                                    if '"type":"child_started"' in line]
                        break
                if proc.poll() is not None:
                    self.fail(proc.stderr.read().decode())
                time.sleep(.01)
            proc.kill()
            proc.communicate(timeout=3)
        self.assertEqual(len(children), 2)
        # First arm is baseline. Unreaped or reparented zombies are terminated, but the
        # crashed harness cannot certify wait4/reclamation; inspector must stay partial.
        deadline = time.monotonic() + 3
        while time.monotonic() < deadline:
            live = []
            for child in children:
                raw = b.common.read('/proc/' + str(child['pid']) + '/stat')
                if raw:
                    fields = raw.rsplit(')', 1)[-1].split()
                    if int(fields[19]) == child['start_ticks'] and fields[0] != 'Z':
                        live.append(child['pid'])
            if not live:
                break
            time.sleep(.01)
        self.assertEqual(live, [])
        self.assertEqual(inspect(events)['status'], 'partial')
        self.assertTrue(inspect(events)['unresolved_cleanup_unknown'])

    def test_active_without_authorization_refused(self):
        result = subprocess.run([sys.executable, str(ROOT / 'scripts/benchmark_desktop.py'),
            '--corpus', str(self.corpus), '--environment', 'Codespaces', '--label', 'refusal',
            '--output', str(self.path / 'unauthorized'), '--arms', 'all'], capture_output=True)
        self.assertNotEqual(result.returncode, 0)
        self.assertFalse((self.path / 'unauthorized').exists())


if __name__ == '__main__':
    unittest.main()


FILE scripts/tests/test_owned.py
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Owned lossless experiment: correctness, hard bounds, cancellation and failure evidence."""
import argparse
import copy
import json
import os
from pathlib import Path
import signal
import subprocess
import sys
import tempfile
import time
import unittest
from unittest.mock import patch

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2]
sys.path.insert(0, str(ROOT/'scripts'))
import benchmark_owned as bench
from inspect_owned import inspect
WORKER = ROOT/'target/release/xynoos-owned'


class OwnedTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def setUp(self):
        bench.STOP = None
        self.temp = tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(prefix='xynoos-owned-test-')
        self.directory = Path(self.temp.name)
        self.args = argparse.Namespace(worker=WORKER, corpus='mixed', authorize_owned=True,
                                       requests=16, interval_ms=1, timeout=4)
        self.events = []

    def tearDown(self):
        self.temp.cleanup()
        bench.STOP = None

    def arm(self, arm='active'):
        result = bench.run_arm(arm, self.args, self.events.append, 0)
        self.assertTrue(all(c['reaped'] for c in result['children']), result)
        return result

    def fixture(self, body):
        p = self.directory/'fixture'
        p.write_text('#!'+sys.executable+'\n'+body)
        p.chmod(0o755)
        return p

    def test_matched_real_intervention_output_and_restoration(self):
        runs = [self.arm(arm) for arm in bench.ARMS]
        self.assertTrue(all(r['status'] == 'complete' for r in runs), [(r['arm'], r['error']) for r in runs])
        report = bench.summary(runs)
        self.assertTrue(report['paired'][0]['correctness_equivalent'])
        active = report['arms']['active']
        # Host CPU/I/O backoff is an admissible outcome on a busy test host.
        if active['actions']:
            self.assertEqual(active['blocks_replaced'], 16)
            self.assertLess(active['steady_payload_bytes']['p50'], 8*1024*1024)
        else:
            self.assertTrue(any(row['policy']['effect'] in ('back_off', 'refused_telemetry', 'refused_memory') for row in runs[-1]['responses']))
        self.assertTrue(all(r['final']['restored'] and not r['final']['cancelled'] for r in runs))
        evidence = [{'type':'manifest', 'configuration':{'repeats':6, 'requests':16}}]
        for repeat in range(-1, 6):
            for r in runs:
                evidence.append({'type':'run_start', 'repeat':repeat, 'arm':r['arm']})
                clone = copy.deepcopy(r)
                clone['repeat'] = repeat
                evidence.append(clone)
        evidence.append({'type':'end', 'status':'complete'})
        path = self.directory/'complete.jsonl'
        def write():
            path.write_text(''.join(json.dumps(row)+'\n' for row in evidence))
        write()
        self.assertEqual(inspect(path)['status'], 'complete')
        evidence[2]['status'] = 'incomplete'
        write()
        self.assertEqual(inspect(path)['status'], 'partial')
        evidence[2]['status'] = 'complete'
        evidence[2]['children'][0]['reaped'] = False
        write()
        self.assertEqual(inspect(path)['status'], 'partial')
        evidence[2]['children'][0]['reaped'] = True
        evidence[-2]['responses'][0]['checksum'] ^= 1
        write()
        self.assertEqual(inspect(path)['status'], 'partial')

    def test_entropy_never_claims_savings(self):
        self.args.corpus = 'entropy'
        run = self.arm()
        self.assertEqual(run['status'], 'complete', run['error'])
        self.assertTrue(all(row['retained_bytes'] == 17*1024*1024 for row in run['responses']))
        self.assertEqual(bench.summary([run])['arms']['active']['actions'], 0)

    def test_no_authorization_no_child(self):
        self.args.authorize_owned = False
        run = self.arm()
        self.assertEqual(run['children'], [])
        self.assertIn('authorize', run['error'])
        p = subprocess.run([WORKER, 'active', 'mixed'], capture_output=True)
        self.assertNotEqual(p.returncode, 0)
        self.assertEqual(p.stdout, b'')

    def test_hard_resource_limits_and_eof_reverse(self):
        with subprocess.Popen([WORKER, 'active', 'mixed', '--authorize-owned'], stdin=subprocess.PIPE,
                              stdout=subprocess.PIPE, stderr=subprocess.PIPE) as p:
            ready = json.loads(p.stdout.readline())
            limits = (Path('/proc')/str(p.pid)/'limits').read_text()
            self.assertIn('134217728', limits)
            self.assertIn('Max cpu time              8', limits)
            self.assertEqual(ready['limits']['wall_seconds'], 25)
            p.stdin.write(b'work\nwork\n')
            p.stdin.flush()
            self.assertEqual(json.loads(p.stdout.readline())['type'], 'response')
            self.assertEqual(json.loads(p.stdout.readline())['type'], 'response')
            p.stdin.close()
            final = json.loads(p.stdout.readline())
            self.assertTrue(final['restored'] and final['cancelled'])
            self.assertEqual(p.wait(timeout=2), 0)

    def test_oversized_input_and_request_bound(self):
        p = subprocess.run([WORKER, 'baseline', 'mixed'], input=b'x'*100000, capture_output=True, timeout=4)
        self.assertNotEqual(p.returncode, 0)
        p = subprocess.run([WORKER, 'baseline', 'mixed'], input=b'work\n'*257, capture_output=True, timeout=4)
        self.assertNotEqual(p.returncode, 0)
        self.assertIn(b'request limit', p.stderr)

    def test_worker_death_timeout_and_output_bound(self):
        for body, error in [('raise SystemExit(7)\n', 'worker exit'),
                            ('import time\ntime.sleep(5)\n', 'timeout'),
                            ('import sys,time\nsys.stdout.write("x"*70000)\nsys.stdout.flush()\ntime.sleep(5)\n', '64 KiB')]:
            self.args.worker = self.fixture(body)
            self.args.timeout = .15
            run = self.arm()
            self.assertEqual(run['status'], 'incomplete')
            self.assertIn(error, run['error'])

    def test_missing_memavailable_and_low_headroom_refuse(self):
        for h in [dict(bench.base.host(), mem_available_kib=None),
                  dict(bench.base.host(), mounted_cgroup_root_context={'memory.current':'1','memory.max':'2'})]:
            with patch.object(bench.base, 'host', return_value=h):
                run = self.arm()
            self.assertEqual(run['children'], [])
            self.assertEqual(run['status'], 'incomplete')

    def test_signals_restore_and_record_incomplete(self):
        for sig in (signal.SIGINT, signal.SIGTERM, signal.SIGHUP):
            bench.STOP = None
            self.events = []
            def emit(row):
                self.events.append(row)
                if row['type'] == 'response' and row.get('request') == 3:
                    bench.interrupt(sig, None)
            run = bench.run_arm('active', self.args, emit, 0)
            self.assertEqual(run['status'], 'incomplete')
            self.assertTrue(run['final']['restored'] and run['final']['cancelled'])
            self.assertTrue(all(c['reaped'] and c['exit_code'] == 0 for c in run['children']))

    def test_crash_tail_inspection_is_read_only(self):
        p = self.directory/'partial.jsonl'
        p.write_text(json.dumps({'type':'manifest', 'configuration':{'repeats':6,'requests':16}})+'\n'+
                     json.dumps({'type':'run_start','arm':'active','repeat':0})+'\n'+ '{"type":')
        before = p.read_bytes()
        report = inspect(p)
        self.assertEqual(report['status'], 'partial')
        self.assertEqual(report['unresolved_cleanup_unknown'], [(0, 'active')])
        self.assertEqual(p.read_bytes(), before)

    def test_missing_peak_memory_is_unknown_not_zero(self):
        run = {'samples': [], 'responses': [],
               'final': {'self_memory_after_restore': {'hwm_kib': None}}}
        self.assertIsNone(bench.peak_hwm(run))
        run['responses'] = [{'process': {'hwm_kib': 123}}]
        self.assertEqual(bench.peak_hwm(run), 123)

    def test_independent_watchdog_with_open_idle_stdin(self):
        # Independent worker deadline; harness makes no cancellation call.
        started = time.monotonic()
        with subprocess.Popen([WORKER, 'baseline', 'mixed'], stdin=subprocess.PIPE,
                              stdout=subprocess.PIPE, stderr=subprocess.PIPE) as p:
            self.assertEqual(json.loads(p.stdout.readline())['type'], 'ready')
            self.assertEqual(p.wait(timeout=28), 124)
        self.assertLess(time.monotonic()-started, 28)


if __name__ == '__main__':
    unittest.main()


FILE scripts/tests/test_physical_benchmark.py
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Lifecycle/adversarial tests exercise only owned subprocesses and read-only telemetry."""
import argparse
import json
import os
from pathlib import Path
import signal
import subprocess
import sys
import tempfile
import time
import unittest
from unittest.mock import patch

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2]
sys.path.insert(0, str(ROOT/'scripts'))
import benchmark_physical as bench
from inspect_physical import inspect

WORKER = ROOT/'target/release/xynoos-perf'
OBSERVER = ROOT/'target/release/xynoos-mvp'


class BenchmarkTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def setUp(self):
        bench.STOP = None
        self.temp = tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(prefix='xynoos-bench-test-')
        self.directory = Path(self.temp.name)
        self.args = argparse.Namespace(worker=WORKER, observer=OBSERVER, requests=10, interval_ms=0, timeout=1)
        self.events = []

    def tearDown(self):
        self.temp.cleanup()
        bench.STOP = None

    def fixture(self, body):
        p = self.directory/'fixture'
        p.write_text('#!'+sys.executable+'\n'+body)
        p.chmod(0o755)
        return p

    def arm(self, arm='baseline'):
        result = bench.run_arm(arm, self.args, self.events.append, 0)
        self.assertTrue(all(c['reaped'] for c in result['children']), result)
        return result

    def test_real_workload_integrity_and_proc_resources(self):
        result = self.arm()
        self.assertEqual(result['status'], 'complete', result['error'])
        self.assertEqual(len(result['response_ns']), 10)
        self.assertTrue(result['children'][0]['usage']['user_cpu_ns'] > 0)
        self.assertIsNotNone(bench.summarize([result])['arms']['baseline']['response_ns'])

    def test_real_observation_overlaps_identical_work(self):
        result = self.arm('observe')
        self.assertEqual(result['status'], 'complete', result['error'])
        self.assertGreater(result['observations'], 0)
        self.assertEqual({c['name'] for c in result['children']}, {'observer', 'workload'})

    def test_high_pressure_stops_and_reaps_launched_worker(self):
        initial = bench.host()
        low = dict(initial, mem_available_kib=1)
        with patch.object(bench, 'host', side_effect=[initial, low]):
            result = self.arm()
        self.assertEqual(result['status'], 'incomplete')
        self.assertIn('256 MiB', result['error'])
        self.assertEqual(len(result['children']), 1)

    def test_lost_memavailable_stops_owned_children(self):
        initial = bench.host()
        with patch.object(bench, 'host', side_effect=[initial, dict(initial, mem_available_kib=None)]):
            result = self.arm()
        self.assertIn('unavailable', result['error'])

    def test_missing_psi_stays_unknown(self):
        self.assertIsNone(bench.psi(None))
        self.assertEqual(bench.delta(None, 10)['status'], 'unavailable')
        self.assertEqual(bench.delta(10, 1)['status'], 'counter_reset')
        self.assertEqual(bench.quantiles([1, 2, 3])['p99'], 3)

    def test_mounted_cgroup_low_headroom_refuses(self):
        with self.assertRaisesRegex(RuntimeError, 'headroom'):
            bench.safe({'mem_available_kib': 1024*1024,
                        'mounted_cgroup_root_context': {'memory.current': '100', 'memory.max': '200'}})

    def test_workload_termination_is_not_a_complete_run(self):
        self.args.worker = self.fixture('import sys\nsys.exit(7)\n')
        result = self.arm()
        self.assertEqual(result['status'], 'incomplete')
        self.assertIn('terminated', result['error'])
        self.assertEqual(result['children'][0]['exit_code'], 7)

    def test_observer_termination_is_not_zero_overhead(self):
        self.args.observer = self.fixture('raise SystemExit(4)\n')
        result = self.arm('observe')
        self.assertEqual(result['status'], 'incomplete')
        self.assertIn('observer', result['error'])

    def test_stalled_worker_times_out_and_is_reaped(self):
        self.args.worker = self.fixture('import time\ntime.sleep(30)\n')
        result = self.arm()
        self.assertIn('timeout', result['error'])

    def test_kill_escalation_is_explicit_and_incomplete(self):
        self.args.worker = self.fixture('import signal,time\nsignal.signal(signal.SIGTERM, signal.SIG_IGN)\ntime.sleep(30)\n')
        result = self.arm()
        self.assertEqual(result['status'], 'incomplete')
        self.assertTrue(result['children'][0]['kill_escalation'])
        self.assertEqual(result['children'][0]['exit_code'], -signal.SIGKILL)

    def test_output_bound_stops_child(self):
        self.args.worker = self.fixture('import sys,time\nsys.stdout.write("x"*70000)\nsys.stdout.flush()\ntime.sleep(30)\n')
        result = self.arm()
        self.assertIn('64 KiB', result['error'])

    def test_refusal_and_missing_decision_telemetry_are_explicit(self):
        result = self.arm('observe')
        self.assertEqual(result['status'], 'complete', result['error'])
        reports = [e['report'] for e in self.events if e['type'] == 'observation']
        self.assertTrue(all(r['kernel_mutations'] == 0 for r in reports))
        self.assertTrue(all('reason' in r['plan'] for r in reports))

    def test_worker_rejects_long_input_and_unsupported_modes(self):
        result = subprocess.run([WORKER, 'workload'], input=b'x'*100000, capture_output=True)
        self.assertNotEqual(result.returncode, 0)
        self.assertLess(len(result.stdout), 65536)
        self.assertNotEqual(subprocess.run([WORKER, 'optimize'], capture_output=True).returncode, 0)

    def test_signals_preserve_partial_evidence_and_cleanup(self):
        for sig in (signal.SIGTERM, signal.SIGINT, signal.SIGHUP):
            output = self.directory/str(int(sig))
            command = [sys.executable, str(ROOT/'scripts/benchmark_physical.py'), '--environment', 'Codespaces',
                       '--label', 'lifecycle fixture', '--requests', '1000', '--repeats', '2',
                       '--output', str(output)]
            with subprocess.Popen(command, stdout=subprocess.DEVNULL, stderr=subprocess.PIPE) as proc:
                deadline = time.monotonic()+5
                events = output/'events.jsonl'
                while time.monotonic() < deadline:
                    if events.exists() and '"type":"response"' in events.read_text():
                        break
                    if proc.poll() is not None:
                        self.fail(proc.stderr.read().decode())
                    time.sleep(.01)
                else:
                    proc.kill()
                    self.fail('no response before signal test deadline')
                proc.send_signal(sig)
                proc.communicate(timeout=5)
                self.assertEqual(proc.returncode, 1)
            rows = [json.loads(line) for line in events.read_text().splitlines()]
            run = next(e for e in rows if e['type'] == 'run')
            self.assertEqual(run['status'], 'incomplete')
            self.assertTrue(all(c['reaped'] for c in run['children']))
            self.assertEqual(json.loads((output/'summary.json').read_text())['status'], 'incomplete')
            self.assertEqual(inspect(events)['status'], 'partial')

    def test_crash_tail_is_read_only_and_cleanup_unknown(self):
        path = self.directory/'partial.jsonl'
        content = (json.dumps({'type':'manifest','environment':'physical','configuration':{'repeats':2}})+'\n'+
                   json.dumps({'type':'run_start','repeat':0,'arm':'observe'})+'\n'+ '{"type":')
        path.write_text(content)
        before = path.read_bytes()
        report = inspect(path)
        self.assertEqual(report['status'], 'partial')
        self.assertEqual(report['unresolved_runs_cleanup_unknown'], [(0,'observe')])
        self.assertTrue(report['truncated_final_event'])
        self.assertEqual(before, path.read_bytes())

    def test_end_marker_cannot_promote_failed_runs(self):
        path = self.directory/'contradictory.jsonl'
        rows = [{'type':'manifest','environment':'physical','configuration':{'repeats':2}}]
        for repeat in range(2):
            for arm in bench.ARMS:
                rows.append({'type':'run', 'repeat':repeat, 'arm':arm, 'status':'incomplete',
                             'children':[], 'response_ns':[], 'service_ns':[]})
        rows.append({'type':'end','status':'complete'})
        path.write_text(''.join(json.dumps(r)+'\n' for r in rows))
        self.assertEqual(inspect(path)['status'], 'partial')

    def test_corrupt_complete_event_is_rejected(self):
        path = self.directory/'corrupt.jsonl'
        path.write_text('{broken}\n')
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError):
            inspect(path)

    def test_existing_output_and_incorrect_environment_are_refused(self):
        command = [sys.executable, str(ROOT/'scripts/benchmark_physical.py'), '--environment', 'Codespaces',
                   '--label', 'fixture', '--output', str(self.directory)]
        self.assertNotEqual(subprocess.run(command, capture_output=True).returncode, 0)
        env = dict(os.environ, CODESPACES='true')
        command[command.index('Codespaces')] = 'physical'
        self.assertIn(b'Codespaces must be labelled', subprocess.run(command, env=env, capture_output=True).stderr)


if __name__ == '__main__':
    unittest.main()


FILE scripts/tests/test_unit_cleanup.py
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Deterministic evidence fixtures; never invoke systemd or touch cgroupfs."""
import contextlib
import copy
import errno
import importlib.util
import io
import json
import os
from pathlib import Path
import subprocess
import tempfile
import unittest
from unittest.mock import patch

spec = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location("cleanup", Path(__file__).parents[1] / "xynoos-unit-preflight.py")
m = importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec)
spec.loader.exec_module(m)
UNIT = "xynoos-018d-fixture.service"
INV = "12" * 16
STATE = dict(Id=UNIT, LoadState="loaded", ActiveState="active", InvocationID=INV,
             MainPID="123", ControlGroup="/user.slice/" + UNIT, Transient="yes")
RECORD = dict(record="manager_preflight", unit=UNIT, binding_revalidation="passed",
              manager_pid=123, binding=dict(unit_id=UNIT, invocation_id=INV, main_pid=123,
              control_group=STATE["ControlGroup"], actual_membership=STATE["ControlGroup"] + "/manager"))


class Fixtures(unittest.TestCase):
    def error(self, reason, fn, status="UNVERIFIED"):
        with self.assertRaises(m.EvidenceError) as caught:
            fn()
        self.assertEqual((caught.exception.status, caught.exception.reason), (status, reason))

    def test_normal_snapshot(self):
        self.assertIsNone(m.capture_unit(RECORD, UNIT, STATE))
        self.assertEqual(m.parse_state("\n".join(k + "=" + v for k, v in STATE.items())), STATE)

    def test_inactive_awaiting_collection(self):
        s = dict(STATE, ActiveState="inactive", MainPID="0")
        self.assertEqual(m.terminal(s, STATE), "inactive_awaiting_collection")

    def test_collected_preserved_identity(self):
        s = dict(STATE, LoadState="not-found", ActiveState="inactive", MainPID="0", InvocationID="", ControlGroup="")
        self.assertEqual(m.terminal(s, STATE), "collected")

    def test_active(self):
        self.error("service_not_inactive", lambda: m.terminal(STATE, STATE), "FAIL")

    def test_contradictory(self):
        self.error("contradictory_collected_state", lambda: m.terminal(dict(STATE, LoadState="not-found"), STATE), "FAIL")

    def test_missing_malformed_bus(self):
        for text in ("", "LoadState=loaded\nLoadState=loaded", "garbage", "Other=x"):
            self.error("missing_unit_information" if not text else "malformed_unit_information", lambda: m.parse_state(text))
        text = "\n".join(k + "=" + ("bad" if k == "InvocationID" else v) for k, v in STATE.items())
        self.error("malformed_invocation", lambda: m.parse_state(text))

    def test_bus_unavailable_and_command_timeout(self):
        for outcome in (subprocess.CompletedProcess([], 1, "", "no bus"),
                        subprocess.TimeoutExpired("systemctl", 2)):
            with patch.object(m.subprocess, "run", **({"side_effect": outcome} if isinstance(outcome, Exception) else {"return_value": outcome})):
                self.error("unit_information_unavailable", lambda: m.show(UNIT))

    def test_stale_record_and_missing_inactive_invocation(self):
        record = copy.deepcopy(RECORD)
        record["binding"]["invocation_id"] = "0" * 32
        self.error("missing_invocation_identity", lambda: m.capture_unit(record, UNIT, STATE))
        self.error("inactive_identity_unavailable", lambda: m.terminal(dict(STATE, ActiveState="inactive", MainPID="0", InvocationID=""), STATE))

    def test_invocation_and_name_reuse(self):
        self.error("invocation_mismatch", lambda: m.capture_unit(RECORD, UNIT, dict(STATE, InvocationID="34" * 16)), "FAIL")
        self.error("unit_name_reuse", lambda: m.terminal(dict(STATE, Id="other.service"), STATE), "FAIL")
        self.error("invocation_mismatch", lambda: m.terminal(dict(STATE, InvocationID="34" * 16), STATE), "FAIL")

    def hierarchy(self, root):
        parent = Path(root) / UNIT
        manager = parent / "manager"
        manager.mkdir(parents=True)
        for directory in (parent, manager):
            (directory / "cgroup.events").write_text("populated 0\nfrozen 0\n")
        record = copy.deepcopy(RECORD)
        record.update(parent_path=str(parent), manager_path=str(manager))
        for name, directory in (("parent", parent), ("manager", manager)):
            st = directory.stat()
            record["binding"][name + "_descriptor"] = dict(device=st.st_dev, inode=st.st_ino)
        h = m.Hierarchy()
        with patch.object(m, "cgroupfs"):
            evidence = h.capture(record, UNIT)
        self.addCleanup(h.close)
        return h, parent, manager, evidence

    def remove_fixture(self, parent, manager):
        (manager / "cgroup.events").unlink()
        manager.rmdir()
        (parent / "cgroup.events").unlink()
        parent.rmdir()

    def test_present_and_removed_exact_evidence(self):
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as root:
            h, parent, manager, evidence = self.hierarchy(root)
            self.assertEqual(set(evidence), {"ancestor", "parent", "manager"})
            self.assertFalse(h.removed())
            self.remove_fixture(parent, manager)
            # Ordinary filesystem EOF/data cannot stand in for kernfs deactivation.
            self.assertFalse(h.removed())
            with patch.object(m.os, "pread", side_effect=OSError(errno.ENODEV, "fixture deactivated")):
                self.assertTrue(h.removed())
            with patch.object(m.os, "pread", side_effect=OSError(errno.EIO, "fixture")):
                self.error("events_removal_ambiguous", h.removed)

    def test_replaced_parent_and_child(self):
        for target in ("parent", "manager"):
            with self.subTest(target=target), tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as root:
                h, parent, manager, _ = self.hierarchy(root)
                path = parent if target == "parent" else manager
                path.rename(str(path) + ".old")
                path.mkdir()
                self.error(target + "_replaced", h.removed, "FAIL")

    def test_uncertain_or_replaced_ancestor(self):
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as root:
            base = Path(root) / "ancestor"
            base.mkdir()
            h, parent, manager, _ = self.hierarchy(base)
            base.rename(str(base) + ".old")
            base.mkdir()
            self.error("ancestor_replaced", h.removed, "FAIL")
            base.rmdir()
            with self.assertRaises(FileNotFoundError):
                h.removed()

    def run_supervisor(self, record=True, exit_status=0, removed=True, state=None, capture_error=None, raw_output=None):
        class Stdout(io.StringIO):
            def __init__(self):
                super().__init__()
                self.buffer = io.BytesIO()
        output = Stdout()
        with tempfile.TemporaryFile() as stream:
            if raw_output is not None:
                stream.write(raw_output)
            elif record:
                stream.write((json.dumps(RECORD) + "\n").encode())
            stream.seek(0)
            process = unittest.mock.Mock(stdout=stream, stdin=io.BytesIO(), returncode=exit_status)
            process.communicate.return_value = (b"", None)
            hierarchy = unittest.mock.Mock()
            hierarchy.capture.return_value = {"ancestor": [1, 2, "3"], "parent": [1, 4, "3"], "manager": [1, 5, "3"]}
            hierarchy.capture.side_effect = capture_error
            clock = [0]
            def removal():
                clock[0] = 6
                return removed
            hierarchy.removed.side_effect = removal
            end = state or dict(STATE, ActiveState="inactive", MainPID="0")
            with patch.object(m.selectors, "DefaultSelector", m.selectors.SelectSelector), patch.object(m.uuid, "uuid4", return_value=unittest.mock.Mock(hex="fixture")), patch.object(m.subprocess, "Popen", return_value=process), patch.object(m, "Hierarchy", return_value=hierarchy), patch.object(m, "show", side_effect=[STATE, STATE, end, end]), patch.object(m.time, "monotonic", side_effect=lambda: clock[0]), contextlib.redirect_stdout(output), contextlib.redirect_stderr(io.StringIO()):
                code = m.supervise("/fixture/binary")
            return code, json.loads(output.getvalue())

    def test_supervised_pass_inactive_and_collected(self):
        for state, termination in ((None, "inactive_awaiting_collection"), (dict(STATE, LoadState="not-found", ActiveState="inactive", MainPID="0", InvocationID="", ControlGroup=""), "collected")):
            code, r = self.run_supervisor(state=state)
            self.assertEqual((code, r["status"], r["reason"]), (0, "PASS", "cleanup_verified"))
            self.assertEqual(r["evidence"]["termination"], termination)
            self.assertEqual(r["evidence"]["removal"], "retained_events_ENODEV_and_bound_absence")
            self.assertEqual(r["evidence"]["invocation"], INV)

    def test_cleanup_timeout(self):
        code, r = self.run_supervisor(removed=False)
        self.assertEqual((code, r["status"], r["reason"]), (1, "UNVERIFIED", "cleanup_timeout"))
        self.assertNotIn("removal", r["evidence"])

    def test_missing_output_and_diagnostic_failure(self):
        for status in (0, 37):
            code, r = self.run_supervisor(record=False, exit_status=status)
            self.assertEqual((code, r["status"], r["reason"], r["diagnostic_exit"]), (status or 1, "UNVERIFIED", "missing_manager_record", status))
            self.assertEqual(r["evidence"], {})

    def test_malformed_manager_json(self):
        code, r = self.run_supervisor(raw_output=b"{bad json}\n")
        self.assertEqual((code, r["status"], r["reason"]), (1, "UNVERIFIED", "observer_error"))
        self.assertEqual(r["evidence"], {})

    def test_duplicate_manager_record(self):
        code, r = self.run_supervisor(raw_output=((json.dumps(RECORD) + "\n") * 2).encode())
        self.assertEqual((code, r["status"], r["reason"]), (1, "UNVERIFIED", "duplicate_manager_record"))
        self.assertEqual(r["evidence"], {})

    def test_partial_trailing_record(self):
        code, r = self.run_supervisor(raw_output=(json.dumps(RECORD) + "\n{partial").encode())
        self.assertEqual((code, r["status"], r["reason"]), (1, "UNVERIFIED", "unexpected_trailing_output"))
        self.assertEqual(r["evidence"], {})

    def test_diagnostic_failure_cannot_pass(self):
        code, r = self.run_supervisor(exit_status=37)
        self.assertEqual((code, r["status"], r["reason"]), (37, "FAIL", "diagnostic_failed"))

    def test_absence_without_identity_never_passes(self):
        code, r = self.run_supervisor(capture_error=m.EvidenceError("ancestor_uncertain"))
        self.assertEqual((code, r["status"], r["reason"]), (1, "UNVERIFIED", "ancestor_uncertain"))
        self.assertNotIn("removal", r["evidence"])


if __name__ == "__main__":
    unittest.main()


FILE scripts/xynoos-unit-preflight.py
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Read-only observer. No cgroup writes/deletions; see TASK-018E-CLEANUP.md."""
import ctypes
import errno
import json
import os
from pathlib import Path
import re
import selectors
import subprocess
import sys
import time
import uuid


class EvidenceError(Exception):
    def __init__(self, reason, status="UNVERIFIED"):
        self.reason, self.status = reason, status
        super().__init__(reason)


def require(ok, reason, status="UNVERIFIED"):
    if not ok:
        raise EvidenceError(reason, status)


PROPERTIES = ("Id", "LoadState", "ActiveState", "InvocationID", "MainPID",
              "ControlGroup", "Transient")


def parse_state(text):
    state = {}
    for line in text.splitlines():
        key, sep, value = line.partition("=")
        require(sep and key in PROPERTIES and key not in state, "malformed_unit_information")
        state[key] = value
    require(set(state) == set(PROPERTIES), "missing_unit_information")
    require(state["MainPID"].isdigit(), "malformed_main_pid")
    invocation = state["InvocationID"]
    require(not invocation or re.fullmatch(r"[0-9a-f]{32}", invocation), "malformed_invocation")
    return state


def show(unit):
    try:
        p = subprocess.run(["systemctl", "--user", "show", unit,
                            "--property=" + ",".join(PROPERTIES)],
                           capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=2)
    except (OSError, subprocess.TimeoutExpired) as e:
        raise EvidenceError("unit_information_unavailable") from e
    require(p.returncode == 0, "unit_information_unavailable")
    return parse_state(p.stdout)


def terminal(state, original):
    require(state["Id"] == original["Id"], "unit_name_reuse", "FAIL")
    invocation = state["InvocationID"]
    require(not invocation or invocation == original["InvocationID"], "invocation_mismatch", "FAIL")
    load, active = state["LoadState"], state["ActiveState"]
    if load == "not-found":
        require(active == "inactive" and state["MainPID"] == "0"
                and not invocation and not state["ControlGroup"], "contradictory_collected_state", "FAIL")
        return "collected"
    require(load == "loaded", "unsupported_load_state")
    require(invocation == original["InvocationID"] and state["Transient"] == "yes",
            "inactive_identity_unavailable")
    require(state["ControlGroup"] in ("", original["ControlGroup"]), "unit_cgroup_changed", "FAIL")
    require(active == "inactive" and state["MainPID"] == "0", "service_not_inactive", "FAIL")
    return "inactive_awaiting_collection"


def identity(fd):
    st = os.fstat(fd)
    with open(f"/proc/self/fdinfo/{fd}", encoding="ascii") as stream:
        mounts = [line.split()[1] for line in stream if line.startswith("mnt_id:")]
    require(len(mounts) == 1, "mount_identity_unavailable")
    return (st.st_dev, st.st_ino, mounts[0])


def cgroupfs(fd):
    # An oversized aligned buffer avoids depending on libc's statfs field layout.
    buffer = (ctypes.c_long * 64)()
    libc = ctypes.CDLL(None, use_errno=True)
    require(libc.fstatfs(fd, ctypes.byref(buffer)) == 0, "filesystem_identity_unavailable")
    require(buffer[0] == 0x63677270, "not_cgroup_v2")


class Hierarchy:
    def __init__(self):
        self.fds = []
        self.chain = []

    def open(self, name, parent=None, directory=True):
        flags = os.O_RDONLY | os.O_CLOEXEC | os.O_NOFOLLOW
        if directory:
            flags |= os.O_DIRECTORY
        fd = os.open(name, flags, dir_fd=parent)
        self.fds.append(fd)
        return fd

    def capture(self, record, unit):
        b = record["binding"]
        path = record["parent_path"]
        require(isinstance(path, str) and path.startswith("/") and
                all(p not in ("", ".", "..") for p in path.split("/")[1:]) and
                Path(path).name == unit, "unexpected_parent_path")
        require(record["manager_path"] == path + "/manager", "unexpected_manager_path")
        fd = self.open("/")
        self.chain.append((None, "/", fd, identity(fd)))
        for component in path.split("/")[1:-1]:
            child = self.open(component, fd)
            self.chain.append((fd, component, child, identity(child)))
            fd = child
        cgroupfs(fd)
        self.ancestor = fd
        self.name = unit
        self.parent = self.open(unit, fd)
        self.manager = self.open("manager", self.parent)
        self.ids = {}
        self.events = {}
        for name, directory in (("parent", self.parent), ("manager", self.manager)):
            cgroupfs(directory)
            ident = identity(directory)
            require(list(ident[:2]) == [b[name + "_descriptor"][k] for k in ("device", "inode")],
                    name + "_identity_mismatch", "FAIL")
            self.ids[name] = ident
            events = self.open("cgroup.events", directory, False)
            require(b"populated " in os.pread(events, 4096, 0), "events_unreadable")
            self.events[name] = events
        require(self.ids["parent"] != self.ids["manager"], "duplicate_cgroup_identity", "FAIL")
        require(self.ids["parent"][2] == self.ids["manager"][2] == identity(self.ancestor)[2],
                "mount_identity_mismatch")
        self.check_chain()
        return {"ancestor": list(identity(self.ancestor)),
                "parent": list(self.ids["parent"]), "manager": list(self.ids["manager"])}

    def check_chain(self):
        for parent, name, fd, expected in self.chain:
            current = self.open(name, parent)
            try:
                require(identity(fd) == expected and identity(current) == expected,
                        "ancestor_replaced", "FAIL")
            finally:
                os.close(current)
                self.fds.remove(current)

    def removed(self):
        self.check_chain()
        present = False
        for name, parent, entry in (("parent", self.ancestor, self.name),
                                    ("manager", self.parent, "manager")):
            try:
                fd = self.open(entry, parent)
            except OSError as e:
                require(e.errno in (errno.ENOENT, errno.ENODEV), "cgroup_lookup_ambiguous")
            else:
                try:
                    require(identity(fd) == self.ids[name], name + "_replaced", "FAIL")
                finally:
                    os.close(fd)
                    self.fds.remove(fd)
                present = True
        if present:
            return False
        # ENODEV from a fresh offset-zero read of each original cgroup.events
        # is required. EOF, ENOENT, nlink and pathname absence are not substitutes.
        for name in ("parent", "manager"):
            require(identity(getattr(self, name)) == self.ids[name], "held_identity_changed", "FAIL")
            try:
                os.pread(self.events[name], 4096, 0)
            except OSError as e:
                require(e.errno == errno.ENODEV, "events_removal_ambiguous")
            else:
                return False
        self.check_chain()
        return True

    def close(self):
        for fd in reversed(self.fds):
            os.close(fd)
        self.fds.clear()


def capture_unit(record, unit, state):
    b = record["binding"]
    require(record["record"] == "manager_preflight" and record["unit"] == unit
            and record["binding_revalidation"] == "passed", "invalid_manager_record")
    require(b["unit_id"] == unit and state["Id"] == unit, "unit_name_reuse", "FAIL")
    invocation = b["invocation_id"]
    require(isinstance(invocation, str) and re.fullmatch(r"[0-9a-f]{32}", invocation)
            and invocation != "0" * 32, "missing_invocation_identity")
    require(state["InvocationID"] == invocation, "invocation_mismatch", "FAIL")
    require(state["LoadState"] == "loaded" and state["ActiveState"] == "active"
            and state["Transient"] == "yes", "snapshot_not_active", "FAIL")
    require(state["MainPID"] == str(record["manager_pid"]) == str(b["main_pid"])
            and int(state["MainPID"]) > 0, "main_pid_mismatch", "FAIL")
    require(state["ControlGroup"] == b["control_group"] and
            b["actual_membership"] == state["ControlGroup"] + "/manager", "membership_mismatch", "FAIL")


def supervise(binary):
    unit = "xynoos-018d-" + uuid.uuid4().hex + ".service"
    print("transient unit: " + unit, file=sys.stderr, flush=True)
    result = {"record": "unit_cleanup", "unit": unit, "status": "UNVERIFIED", "evidence": {}}
    hierarchy, process = Hierarchy(), None
    status = None
    try:
        process = subprocess.Popen([
            "systemd-run", "--user", "--wait", "--pipe", "--collect", "--unit=" + unit,
            "--property=Type=exec", "--property=Restart=no", "--property=RuntimeMaxSec=20s",
            "--property=Delegate=cpu memory pids", "--property=DelegateSubgroup=manager",
            "--setenv=XYNOOS_CLEANUP_HANDSHAKE=1", binary, "manager-preflight", unit],
            stdin=subprocess.PIPE, stdout=subprocess.PIPE)
        record, data = None, b""
        deadline = time.monotonic() + 10
        with selectors.DefaultSelector() as selector:
            selector.register(process.stdout, selectors.EVENT_READ)
            while time.monotonic() < deadline and record is None:
                if not selector.select(max(0, deadline - time.monotonic())):
                    break
                chunk = os.read(process.stdout.fileno(), 65536)
                if not chunk:
                    break
                sys.stdout.buffer.write(chunk)
                sys.stdout.buffer.flush()
                data += chunk
                require(len(data) <= 1024 * 1024, "diagnostic_output_excessive")
                while b"\n" in data:
                    line, data = data.split(b"\n", 1)
                    if line.startswith(b"{"):
                        parsed = json.loads(line)
                        if isinstance(parsed, dict) and parsed.get("record") == "manager_preflight":
                            require(record is None, "duplicate_manager_record")
                            record = parsed
        require(record is not None, "missing_manager_record")
        require(not data.strip(), "unexpected_trailing_output")
        original = show(unit)
        capture_unit(record, unit, original)
        result["evidence"]["invocation"] = original["InvocationID"]
        result["evidence"]["hierarchy"] = hierarchy.capture(record, unit)
        require(show(unit) == original, "snapshot_changed", "FAIL")
        result["evidence"]["snapshot"] = original
        process.stdin.write(b"1")
        process.stdin.flush()
        process.stdin.close()
        process.stdin = None
        tail, _ = process.communicate(timeout=25)
        sys.stdout.buffer.write(tail)
        sys.stdout.buffer.flush()
        # Any additional JSON manager record makes the diagnostic ambiguous.
        for line in tail.splitlines():
            if line.startswith(b"{"):
                parsed = json.loads(line)
                require(not isinstance(parsed, dict) or parsed.get("record") != "manager_preflight",
                        "duplicate_manager_record")
        status = process.returncode
        deadline = time.monotonic() + 5
        while True:
            end = show(unit)
            result["evidence"]["termination"] = terminal(end, original)
            if hierarchy.removed():
                require(show(unit) == end, "terminal_snapshot_changed", "FAIL")
                result["evidence"]["removal"] = "retained_events_ENODEV_and_bound_absence"
                result["status"] = "PASS" if status == 0 else "FAIL"
                result["reason"] = "cleanup_verified" if status == 0 else "diagnostic_failed"
                break
            require(time.monotonic() < deadline, "cleanup_timeout")
            time.sleep(0.1)
    except EvidenceError as e:
        result.update(status=e.status, reason=e.reason)
    except (OSError, ValueError, KeyError, TypeError, subprocess.TimeoutExpired) as e:
        result.update(status="UNVERIFIED", reason="observer_error", detail=str(e))
    finally:
        if process is not None and status is None:
            # EOF rejects handshake; never stop/kill the service or delete cgroups.
            if process.stdin is not None:
                try:
                    process.stdin.close()
                except OSError:
                    pass
                process.stdin = None
            try:
                tail, _ = process.communicate(timeout=25)
                sys.stdout.buffer.write(tail)
                status = process.returncode
            except (OSError, subprocess.TimeoutExpired):
                result["evidence"]["runner"] = "wait_failed_service_state_unverified"
        hierarchy.close()
    result["diagnostic_exit"] = status
    print(json.dumps(result), flush=True)
    return status if status is not None and status > 0 else (0 if result["status"] == "PASS" else 1)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    sys.exit(supervise(sys.argv[1]))
